“ivaḷ piṟinutal pacappūra, tōḷ nalaṉ iḻappa, ivaḷ kāmanōyāl peruntuyaruṟumāṟu nī ivaḷip puṟakkaṇit tiruttal takkataṉṟu” eṉṟāṟ pōlac cāṉṟōr taliva ṉiṭam taliviniliyi viḷakkik kūṟutal.	(kali. 99)
aracu -
paṇṭi vēḷāḷartam paṭṭappeyar. ‘vēḷ eṉavum aracu eṉavum urimi eytiṉōrum’	(to. po. 30. nac.)
araṭṭam -
pālinilam (cin.ni.272)	(ṭu)
`ariviyi iṉṟuyāṉ aṟintēṉ eṉṟal' -
“talivi nammip pōlat tuṉpattut tuvaḷum māṉiṭa maka ḷātali iṉṟē aṟintēṉ” eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal. tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattup pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟikaḷ, allakuṟi - varum toḻiṟku arumi - mutaliyavaṟṟāl muṭṭuppāṭuṟṟapōtu, talivi vēṭki mikutaliṉ, kaḷaviṉit tavirntu talivaṉ taliviyi maṇantu eṉṟum piriyāk kaṟpiṉ aṟaneṟiyil vāḻtalik karutiya tōḻi, “itaṟku muṉṉellām yāṉ taliviyi malivāḻ kaṭavuḷākaallatu māṉuṭamakaḷākak karutavilli; ippoḻutu niṉpirivāl avaḷ peritum tuyaruṟuvatu koṇṭē avaḷi māṉuṭamakaḷākat teḷintēṉ” eṉṟāṟ pōlat taliviyiṉ pirivāṟṟāmiyik kūṟit talivaṉi varivu kaṭāyatu.
‘āṟṟāt taṉmi āṟṟak kūṟal’ (na. a. 166) eṉṉum kūṟṟiṉkaṇ iḵtu aṭaṅkum.
itu tōḻi varivu kaṭātal eṉṟa tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ta. ne. vi pak. 34)

aruṭkuṇam urittal -
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṟutiyil talivaṉ taṉatu pirivu kuṟittu varuntiya taliviyi amitiyuṟac ceyyum mukattāṉ, “yām uṭampāl avvappōtu pirintu ceṉṟu piṉ kāṇpōmāyiṉum nam uḷḷam eṉṟum piriyāmal oṉṟākavē irukkum” (tañci. kō. 22) eṉṟu tammik kūṭṭivitta teyvattiṉ aruṭ paṇpik kūṟutal.
itu ‘teyvattiṟam pēcal’ eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. 
	(na. a. 129, i. vi. 497)
itu tirukkōviyāril ‘iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (kōvi 14.)
arunilam -
aḵtāvatu pāli pōlum vaṟaṇṭa nilam. ‘naṉimiku curat tiṭi’ (to. po. 79) eṉṉum toṭarkku mikutimikka aru nilattē eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urittami kāṇka. arumi - vaḷam iṉmi.
arumporuḷ viṉi -
iḵtu eṭṭuvaki maṉṟalkaḷil āṟāvatu: ācuram eṉap paṭuvatu. ēṟu taḻuvutal, kuṟi tappātu ampu eytal, nāṇēṟṟal, kuṟippiṭṭa perumporuḷ tarutal ākiya ariya ceyal purintōṉukkē taṉ makaḷik koṭuppatākak kūṟum. tantiyiṉ kuṟikkōḷi niṟivēṟṟip peṇṇi maṇantu koḷḷal. poruḷ, viṉi ivikaḷiṉ arumi perumikaḷ nōkki ippeyar amintatu. irācatakuṇa mēlīṭāṉa acurattaṉmi vāynta ceyalāl nikaḻkiṉṟami yiṉ iḵtu ācuramaṇam eṉappaṭṭatu. (to. po. 92 nac.)  
itu vaṉmaṇa mātaliṉ poruntāk kāmamāyiṟṟu. (ta. ne. vi. 14 uri)
arumporuḷ viṉinili -
acuramaṇam. (iṟi. a., uri. pak. 29)
`arumaṟic caṭaṅkiṟku aminta taruppiyi
marumalart tārōṉ maṉattuṟa nakutal' -
taruppip pullip pārttut talivaṉ puṉmuṟuval ceytal.
akkiṉi cāṭciyākat talivaṉ taliviyik kippiṭittut tīvalam ceytu, vaciṭṭaṉiyum aruntatiyiyum iruvarum kaṇṭapiṉṉar, maṇavaṟikkaṇ paḷḷiyiṭattiruntu, kaḷavuk kālattil pūppu nikaḻum kālattum tīyarāciyilum tīya nāḷilum tīya poḻutilum taliviyik kūṭiyataṟkuk kaḻuvāyākiya mantiraṅkaḷik kūṟavēṇṭiya nērattē arumaṟic caṭaṅkiṟku iṭappaṭṭa taruppippulli nōkki, “ittaliviyiṉ koṅkikaḷākiya yāṉikaḷ eṉṉit tākka varum innērattil inta eḷiya taruppip pul atit taṭukkum āṟṟal peṟṟirukkiṟatē!” eṉṟu muṟuvalittukkoṇṭu taṉṉuḷ kūṟutal (tiruppatik. 399.)
itu ‘varintu kōṭal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟām.	(mā. aka. 82)
arumi kēṭṭaḻital -
talivikku aḷavukku mīṟip paricap poruḷ etirpārppati aṟinta talimakaṉ taṉ maṉattiṭam “maṉamē! nī taliviyatu matippiṉi aṟiyātu avaḷaḻakiṉi māttiram virumpu kiṟāy. uṉatu nili ‘vāṉattirukkum matiyattiṉip paṟṟik koṭu’ eṉṟu aḻukiṉṟa kuḻantinili pōṉṟuḷḷatu kāṇ” eṉṟu uḷḷam aḻintu kūṟutal.
‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. itu.	(kōvi. 197)
`arumi cāṉṟa nāliraṇṭu vaki' -
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyi aṭutta nāḷil, talivi muṉpu arumi amintu niṉṟa niliyāl, talivaṉ talivikaṇ nikaḻttiya meytoṭṭup payiṟal, poy pārāṭṭal, iṭam peṟṟut taḻāal, iṭiyūṟu kiḷattal, nīṭu niṉintu iraṅkal, kūṭutaluṟutal, nukarcci peṟutal, tīrāttēṟṟam eṉṟa kuṟiyāc ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya eṭṭum.			(to. po. 111 nac.)
talivaṉ varitaṟkuk kuṟiyuṟukiṉṟataṉit teḷinta talivi, ceytaṟku arumi vāynta muṭṭuvayiṟ kaḻaṟal, muṉivumey niṟuttal, accattiṉ akaṟal, avaṉ puṇarvu maṟuttal, tūtumuṉiviṉmi, tuñcic cērtal, kātal kim mikal, kaṭṭuri yiṉmi eṉṟa eṭṭaṉiyum piṉpaṟṟal. (109 iḷa.)
arumi ceytu ayarttal -
talivaṉ varutalukkuk kāvalākiya arumi (-iṭiyūṟu) ēṟpaṭṭataṉāl avaṉ varutalit tavirtal. itu kaḷavuk kālattu nikaḻvatu.	(to. po. 109, iḷa.)
kaḷavukkālattil talivaṉ taṉṉi ariyāṉāka (-varutaṟku nēram illātavaki)c ceytukoṇṭu taliviyiyum tōḻiyiyum maṟattal. (111 nac.)
iraṇṭorunāḷ talivaṉ taliviyi maṟantavaṉ pōlak kuṟiyiṭattu vārātiruttal.	(161 kuḻa.)
piṟkālattār arumi ceytayarttalit ‘oruvaḻit taṇattal’ eṉṟa pakutiyil aṭakkuvar.	(kali. 53. nac.)
taliviyatu kūṟṟu nikaḻum oḻukalāṟukaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ iḵtu oṉṟu. 
puṟampōntu viḷiyāṭaṟku iṭiyūṟāka āyakkūṭṭam cūḻntu kiṭattalāl, talivi talivaṟku ariyaḷāki iṭan talippaṭutalit tavirntiruttal.	(to. kaḷa. 21 ca.pāla.)
arumiyaṟital (1) -
iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ talimakaḷi āṟṟuvittup pirinta talivaṉ avaḷuṭiya peruñciṟappiṉi aṟital.
talivaṉ, avaḷ taṉṉik kāṇāmal tāṉ avaḷik kāṇpatōr aṇimikkaṇ, maḻik koṇṭalil miṉṉal pukku oḷittāṟpōla, oru taḻippotumpariṭi maṟintuniṉṟāṉ. talivi tōḻiyarkūṭṭattic ceṉṟu cārntu tārakinaṭuvaṇ taṇmati pōla viḷaṅkiṉāḷ. avaḷ tōḻiyar palarum, kuṟuṅkaṇṇiyum neṭuṅkōti yum viravuttaḻiyum cūṭṭukkattikiyum mōṭṭu valayamum piṟavumākap puṉintu aṭiyuṟiyēnti avaḷmuṉ pal lāṇṭu kūṟi niṉṟaṉar. avaḷi anniliyiṟ kaṇṭa talivaṉ, “ivaḷi yāṉ eytiṉēṉ eṉṟu karutiṉēṉ. eytiya tuṇṭēl kaṉāppōlum! kaṉavē āyiṉum, ivaḷ eṉakku eytaṟku ariyaḷām!” eṉṟu accelvamakaḷit taṉittuk kāṇṭaṟkaṇ uḷḷa arumiyiṉi aṟital. (iṟi.a.2 uri)
arumiyaṟital (2) -
iyaṟkippuṇarcci iṟutiyil pirivu paṟṟiya kalakkam tīrat taliviyi amitiyuṟuttit talivaṉ avaḷi āyattā riṭam cellumāṟu viṭuttu, avaḷic cuṟṟiyirukkum tōḻiyari maṟintuniṉṟu pārttu, iṉit taṉittu eytutal arumi eṉavum, avaḷik kūṭiyatu kaṉavu pōla uḷḷatu eṉavum uṇartal 	(kōvi. 17)
iḵtu ‘āya veḷḷam vaḻipaṭak kaṇṭu itu māyamō eṉṟal’ eṉṟum kūṟappeṟum.	(na. a. 133.)
iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarcci eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru  kūṟṟu.	(kōvi 17.)
arumiyurittal -
uṭaṉpōkku uṇarttiya tōḻikkut talivaṉ “talivi melliyal; yāṉ pōkak karutiya pāli koṭitu; em ūrum cēyttu; ātaliṉ avaḷi uṭaṉkoṇṭu cēṟal eḷiya ceyalaṉṟu” eṉṟu uṭaṉpōtal naṭakka iyalāta ceyal eṉpatākak kūṟutal. itaṉit ‘talivaṉ uṭaṉ pōkku maṟuttal’ eṉṟum kūṟuvar. 	(na. a. 182)
‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. itu. 	(kōvi. 201.)

aruvi -
aḵtāvatu peyar kūṟappaṭāta kiḷavittalivaṉ. ‘uruvi yākiya oru peruṅkiḻavaṉi, aruvi kūṟutal āṉan tammē’. uruvi eṉpataṟku maṟutaliccol aruvi eṉpatu.			(yā. vi. pak. 665)
aruvili urittal -
tōḻi, talivaṉ taliviyi virivil maṇantukōṭal vēṇṭum eṉṟa karuttāl, palarum taliviyi maṇam pēca mulivili koṇṭu vantu taliviyiṉ peṟṟōrik kāṇum ceytiyip paṭittuk kūṟa, atu kēṭṭa talivaṉ taliviyi maṇappataṟkuriya paricapporuḷiṉ aḷaviṉi viṉava, tōḻi, “eṉ taliviyiṉ ciṟiya iṭikkē ellā ulakaṅkaḷiyum koṭuppiṉum emar viliyāka ēlār. aṅṅaṉamāka, avaḷ periya nakilkaḷukku vili kūṟu eṉṟu eṉṉik kēṭpatu viyappāka uḷḷatu” eṉat taliviyiṉ viliyarumi kūṟutal.
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 197)
aruḷ koṇṭāṭutal -
pāṅki aruḷiyal kiḷattal 	(cāmi. 94)
`aruḷ muntuṟutta aṉpupoti kiḷavi' -
talivi ūṭal koṇṭavaḻik kāmam mītūrnta talivaṉ avaḷiṭam paṇinta moḻiyāl pēcuvāṉ. appōtu avaḷ ūṭal tīrntuviṭum. āṉāl avaḷ ataṉi veḷip paṭiyākak kūṟāmal, talivaṉukkut tāṉ paṇinta karuttiṉi veḷiyiṭāmal, taṉ neñcu taṉṉiyum kaṭantu talivaṉiṭam ceṉṟuviṭṭatu eṉṟāṟ pōlat taṉ maṉakkarutti maṟittup pēcuvāḷ.
aruḷāvatu piṟartuṉpam kaṇṭu tāmum varuntum nēyam; aṉpāvatu aruḷ piṟattaṟku ētuvāki eññāṉṟum maṉattil aminta nekiḻcci.
talivaṉiṭattut talivikku aruḷit tōṉṟac ceyta aṉpiṉi maṟittu vēṟoru poruḷ payappac collu vatē aruḷ muntuṟutta aṉpu poti kiḷaviyām.			(to. po. 161 nac.)
al iṭiyīṭu -
iravukkuṟi iṭiyīṭu. 	(cāmi. 86)
allakuṟi -
talimakaṉālaṉṟip piṟitoṉṟāl nikaḻum kuṟi. (na. a. 159.)
allakuṟi aṟivittal -
talivi, talivaṉ kuṟippaṉa iyaṟkiyāṉ nikaḻntavaḻi, avaṉ vantu kuṟiceytatākak karutip poḻiliṭam ceṉṟu ciṟitu nēram taṅki avaṉ vārāmiyiṉ mīṇṭapiṉṉar, talivaṉ vantu kuṟi ceyyavē, talivi vārāmiyāl avaṉ mīṇṭupōka, maṟunāḷ tōḻi, tām kuṟiyallāta taṉik kuṟiyāka mayaṅki muṉṉarc ceṉṟu tirumpiyami yāl, mīṇṭum iṭamviṭṭup peyarntu pōtal attuṇi eḷiya ceyalaṉṟu eṉpatit talivaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉāka avaṉ kēṭkumāṟṟāl kūṟuvatu. itaṉi ‘iṟivikku ikuḷi iṟivaravuṇarttal’ eṉṟa kiḷaviyil aṭakkik koḷvar. 	(na. a. 160)
iḵtu iravukkuṟi eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 			(kōvi. 172)
allakuṟippaṭṭatit taṉpiḻippākat taḻīit tēṟiya taliviyatu nilimiyit tōḻi talivaṟkuk kūṟi, “iv viṭiyīṭu niṉ tōḻiyiṉāṉ āyiṟṟu eṉa avaḷiṭam kūṟu” eṉa, avaḷatu āṟṟāmi kūṟi varivu kaṭāyatu.
“taliva! uṉṉik kūṭum vēṭkiyiṉālē nī iravukkuṟi nikaḻttum oliyic cevicāyttuk kēṭṭum, nī virivil taṉṉi maṇakkavēṇṭum eṉpataṟkut teyvam aruḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu palavāṟu vēṇṭi, nī kuṟi piḻittataṉāl niṉakku ēṟpaṭṭirukkum varuttatti niṉittu maṉam nontum, maḻinīriṭattuḷḷa vēṭkiyāl vāṉattiyē pārttirukkum vāṉampāṭi pōla niṉṉiṭattu vēṭki yāṉ niṉṉiyē niṉittum irukkum taliviyiṭam ‘allakuṟippaṭṭami niṉ kuṟṟamaṉṟu; niṉtōḻi ceyta tavaṟē’ eṉṟu eṉmēl piḻiyi ēṟṟi, avaḷi amiti yāka irukkumāṟu ceyka. nī kūṟuvatē avaḷukku āṟutal tarum” (kali. 46) eṉṟāṟ pōlat tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal.
allakuṟippaṭutal -
talivaṉ iravukkuṟiyiṭattut tāṉ vantuḷḷa ceytiyip puṉal olippaṭuttal, puḷ eḻupputal mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ terivippāṉ. puṉaliyoṭṭiyuḷḷa maraṅkaḷiṉ kāykaḷ mūkku ūḻttu iyaṟkiyāka nīril vīḻntu puṉal olippaṭu talum uṇṭu.
paṟavikaḷ iravil tiṭīreṉṟu paṟantu olittukkoṇṭu puṟappaṭutalum uṇṭu. ivvāṟu talivaṉ nikaḻttum ceyalkaḷ iyaṟkiyāṉ nikaḻntavaḻit talivaṉ vantu viṭṭatākak karutit tōḻiyum taliviyum iravukkuṟi yiṭattu vantu ciṟitu nēram tāmatittup piṉ avaṉ vārāmiyāl mīṇṭum illam aṭivar. talivaṉ piṉṉark kuṟiyiṭattu variṉum, maṟupaṭiyum illattiṉ elli kaṭantu varutal ariya ceyal ātaliṉ, mīṇṭum taliviyum tōḻiyum iravukkuṟiyiṭattu vārār. aṉṟu kuṟi payaṉaṟṟuviṭum. ituvē alla kuṟippaṭutalām.			(to. po. 133 nac., iṟi. 17 uri)
alar -
ūr makkaḷ cilar maṟṟavar ceytiyākat tām takka cāṉṟu kaḷuṭaṉ uṟuti ceytukoṇṭa piṟaku iṉṉāṉōṭu iṉṉāḷiṭi itu pōṉṟa ceyti nikaḻntatu eṉṟu piṟa rum kēṭkumāṟu uratta kuralil tamakkuḷ pēcik koḷvatu.		(iṟi. a. 22)
kaḷavoḻukkam veḷippaṭṭatu kāraṇamākak kaḷavuk kālattum, talivaṉuṭiya puṟattoḻukkam kāraṇamākak kaṟpuk kālattum alar eḻum. icceytiyit tōḻiyum taliviyum talivaṉukku kūṟuvar.
kaḷavukkālattu alarippaṟṟik kūṟutal pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟi ivaṟṟi vilakkutaṟkākavum, kaṟpukkālattu alarippaṟṟik kūṟutal talivaṉatu puṟattoḻukkatti nīkkutaṟkākavum eṉak koḷḷappaṭum. kaḷavu alarāyavaḻip puṇarcci iṭiyīṭupaṭumē eṉṟa accattāl, iruvarkkum kāmam mikum. kaṟpuk kālattil talivaṉatu parattimiyāl alar tōṉṟiyavaḻit talivi varuntuvāḷē eṉṟu talivaṟkuk kāmam ciṟattalum, talivaṉ piriviṉāl talivikkuk kāmam ciṟattalum nikaḻum. mēlum “talivaṉ, kāmakiḻattiyar allāta parattiyarōṭu āṭalum pāṭalum kaṇṭum kēṭṭum, avaruṭaṉ āṟu mutaliyaṉa āṭiyum iṉpam nukarntāṉ” eṉṟu kēṭkuntōṟum talivikkup pulatta lum ūṭalum piṟantu kāmam ciṟakkum.
ivaṟṟāl maṟṟavar puṟattē kūṟum paḻiyoṭu paṭṭaṉavākiya ceytikaḷ talivaṉ taliviyariṭik kāmatti mikuvittaṟ kup payaṉpaṭum eṉṟavāṟu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 (to. po. 162, 163, 164 nac.)
alar aṟivuṟuttal (1) -
talivaṉukkut tōḻi ūrār pēcum alarurikaḷik kūṟutal.
“aiya! eṅkaḷ taliviyip paṟṟi ūril avar paravukiṟatu. ati mēlum vaḷaraviṭātu avaḷi nī varintu kōṭaṟ kāṉa muyaṟciyiṉi mēṟkoḷḷuka” eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻiyiṉ kūṟṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭām.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘paḻivaravurittup pakal varavu vilakkal’ eṉṉum (254). 
itu kaḷaviyalil ‘varivu kaṭātal’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 166)
alar aṟivuṟuttal (2) -
talivi talivaṉ pirivāl ēṟpaṭṭa taṉ tuyarik kaṭaliṭam kūṟi varuntiyatik kēṭṭa talivaṉ kuṟiyiṭic ceṉṟu niṟpat tōḻi avaṉi etirppaṭṭu “niṉ aruḷ ūr muḻutum paravi avarākiviṭṭatu. (iṉi iṅṅaṉam varutali viṭuttut taliviyi maṇantu iṉpam nukartaṟku āvaṉa ceyka)” eṉṟu aṟivuṟuttuvatu.
itaṉi ‘alar pārttuṟṟa accakkiḷavi’ eṉṟum kūṟuvar. (na. a. 164) iḵtu ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatu oru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 180)
alar aṟivuṟuttal (3) -
kaḷavoḻukkattit toṭarntu nikaḻtta virumpum talivaṉ piṟar taṉṉiyum taliviyiyum iṇittuk kūṟum paḻimoḻikaḷ tammuṭiya toṭarpiṉi māṇpuṟac ceyvatāl avvalariṉāl tāṉ makiḻvatit tōḻikkuk kūṟutalum, tōḻiyum taliviyum  ūravar kūṟum paḻimoḻi kaḷit talivaṉukku eṭuttukkūṟi maṇattaliyō uṭaṉ pōkkiṉiyō aṟivuṟuttalumām.	(kuṟaḷ ati. 115 parimē.)
alarpārttuṟṟa accakkiḷavi -
talivi taṉṉippaṟṟi ūrār kūṟum alarmoḻi kēṭṭu, ataṉāl tāṉ aṭiyum accattit tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
“talivaṉiṭam, ‘nī eṅkaṭkuc ceyta aruḷ ampalāy mukiḻmukiḻkkap paṭṭirunta niliyik kaṭantu alarāka ūreṅkum paraviviṭṭatu’ eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟutal vēṇṭum” (ampikā. 250) eṉat talivi karututal.
itu kaḷaviyalil ‘varital vēṭki’ eṉṉum tokutik - kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 164)
`alipuṉal ūraṉ āṟṟāṉ pukutal' -
talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintu vantu taliviyip pirintirukka maṉam poṟātavaṉāki illattup pukutal (maruta naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 95 uri mēṟ.)
`avvakip poruḷukku āṟṟātu urittal' -
talivaṉ piriviṉāl iraṅkum talivi kaṭaṟkariyākiya neytal nilatti aṭintu āṇṭuḷḷa poruḷkaḷiṭam taṉ tuyaram tāṅkātu palavāṟu kūṟutal. (neytal naṭi yiyal) 	(vī. cō. 96 uri mēṟ.).
avvaḻip perukiya ciṟappu -
talivaṉukkuriya muppattu mūṉṟu kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
ōtal mutaliya piriviṉkaṇ āṟṟiya aruñceyalkaḷiṭa mākap perukkamuṟṟeytiya ciṟappiṉiṭamākat talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum. avvaruñceyalkaḷāvaṉa : vēṟṟu nāṭṭakal vayiṉ ceṉṟu ōtiyum uṇarttiyum pulamikāṭṭal, pakiyi veṉṟu taṇittal, paṇintāriṭam tiṟipeṟutal, tūturittal, poruḷ muṟṟi īṭṭutal, kāvalviṉi, iṉṉa piṟa.	(to. kaṟ. 5 ca. pāla.)
avviṭattuk kāṇuṅkol eṉat talivaṉ aiyuṟal -
taṉatu kātal vēṭkiyaṟintu talivi iyal iṭam ivaṟṟi viṉāvic ceṉṟa pāṅkaṉ avaḷi avviṭattuk kāṇ pāṉō kāṇamāṭṭāṉō eṉṟu talivaṉ aiyappaṭṭuk koṇṭu kavaliyōṭiruttal.
ikkūṟṟu ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇ uriyiṟ koṇṭa toṉṟu.	(i. vi. 505 uri)
`avviyal oṭṭāḷ moḻital' -
tōḻi taliviyatu kaḷavoḻukkac ceytiyic cevilikku māṟukōḷ illā moḻiyāl aṟattoṭu niṟṟal. (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal) itu tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟṟal (na. a. 177) eṉavum paṭum.	(vī. cō. 92 uri)
avatti -
kāṭci, vēṭki, orutaliyuḷḷutal, melital, ākkam ceppal, nāṇuvariyiṟattal, nōkkuva ellām aviyē pōṟal, maṟattal, mayakkam, cākkāṭu - eṉṟu kātalarkku ēṟpaṭum pattuvaki nilikaḷum avatti eṉappaṭum. 			(i. vi. 405)
ivaṟṟit talivaṉukkē yuriya eṉak koḷvārum uḷar. 	(na. a. 36 uri)
avayavam kūṟal (1) -
taliviyiṉ uṟuppu aḻakit talivaṉ tōḻikku aṭi yāḷamākak kūṟutal.
pāṅki matiyuṭampāṭṭiṉkaṇ, talivaṉōṭu uriyāṭiya tōḻi, tāṉ talivaṉ virumpum peṇ yār eṉpati aṟiyātavaḷip pōla naṭikka, talivaṉ tāṉ kuṟippiṭum peṇ iṉṉāḷ eṉpati avaḷatu vaṭivaḻaki varuṇikku mukattāṉ toṭaṅki, “nāṉ kuṟippiṭum avaḷ aḻakāl irati pōlvāḷ; avaḷuṭiya kaṇkaḷ maṉmataṉuṭiya aintu ampukaḷil uyiri vāṅkum ampākiya karuṅ- kuvaḷip pūp pōlvaṉa” eṉṟāṟ pōla avaḷ uṟuppu nalaṉi varuṇaṉi ceytal. itu ‘cēṭpaṭi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟām.	(mā. a. pā. 585)
avayavam kūṟal (2) -
tōḻi, talivaṉāl virumpappaṭṭavaḷ taṉ kūṭṭattic cārnta palaruḷ yāvaḷ eṉa viṉava, talivaṉ tāṉ taliviyi iṉṉāḷeṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭāl tōḻi tavaṟātu taṉakku utavuvāḷ eṉṉum karuttāl, tāṉ virumpum taliviyiṉ uṟuppu nalaṉkaḷi urittut tōḻi attakiya nalaṉuṭiyavaḷ iṉṉāḷ eṉṟu aṟiyumāṟu ceyyat taliviyiṉ uṟuppaṭiyāḷam kūṟal.
itaṉi ‘iṟiyōṉ iṟivitaṉmi iyampal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa. 	(na. a. 144)
itu ‘cēṭpaṭi’ eṉṉumtokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  (kōvi. 108.)
avayavam tēṟutal -
pāṅkaṉ kūṟiya uṟuppaḻaku kēṭṭut talivaṉ teḷital. taliviyirunta iruppiṭam nōkkic ceṉṟu avaḷi appoḻiṟkaṇ, taṉittut talivaṉvarukiyi nāṭi eṇṭiciyum alamarum kaṇṇiṉaḷāy iruttalik kaṇṇuṟṟu mīṇṭa pāṅkaṉ, vaṇṭēṟiya kuḻalum kātaḷavu  nīṇṭa kaṇkaḷum tōṭaṇinta vaḷḷikkoṭi pōṉṟa kātukaḷum vēypōṉṟa tōḷkaḷum uṭiya ēntiḻiyip paṟṟik kūṟa, avaṉ kūṟiya uṟuppaḻaku  koṇṭu avaṉ taliviyik kaṇṭu vantiruppatākat talivaṉ teḷital. (kōvi. 36).
ikkūṟṟu ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatu. 			(ka. kā. pak. 34, 35.)
avayavam tōṟṟal -
talivaṉ taliviyiṉuṭiya uṭal uṟuppukkaḷi uruveḷit tōṟṟamākak kāṇutal. antak kāṭciyit talivaṉ tāṉē kūṟuvatāka varuvatu ittuṟi. “inta oḷivīcum veyilē avaḷuṭiya kuḻikaḷ; vēlē avaḷ viḻikaḷ; ivaḷ naṭiyum aṉṉamē; ivaḷ iṉcol kuyilē; ivaḷ aḻakē ivaḷ niṟam;  cāmaramē ivaḷkuḻal; mayilē ivaḷcāyal; miṉṉalē ivaḷ iṭi; matiyē ivaḷmukam. ittakiya uṟuppunalam keḻumiya ivaḷit tāṉ eṅkum kāṇkiṟēṉ” eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal. iḵtu ampikāpatik kōviyil ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu. (ampikā. 64)
avar ceṉṟu irattal -
pāṅkaṉ tōḻi pāṇaṉ mutaliyōr parattiyiṟ pirintuvanta talivaṉi ēṟkumāṟu taliviyi vēṇṭal. vāyil vēṇṭutal eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. (marutanaṭiyiyal)			(vī. cō. 95 urimēṟ.)
avarvayiṉ vitumpal -
kaṟpuk kālattil cēyiṭip pirivākiya nāṭum kāṭum iṭi yiṭṭuc cila tiṅkaḷ kālavariyaṟi collit talivaṉ taliviyi viṭṭu nīṅkic cellum pirivil, talivaṉum taliviyum vēṭki mikutiyāl oruvari oruvar kāṇpa taṟku virital (parimē. 2) talivaṉi niṉintu talivi vituppuṟal (-aṉpāl naṭuṅkutal) (maṇak.)	(kuṟaḷ ati. 127)
avar varaviṟku nimittamāyiṉa kaṇṭu, āṟṟaḷāya talimakaṭkut tōḻi kūṟiyatu -
“talivi! nārikaḷ vāṉattē uyarap paṟakkiṉṟaṉa. putarkaḷi luḷḷa pūkkaḷum vaṇṭukaḷ ūtuvatāl malarntuḷḷaṉa. niṉ caṅkuvaḷiyalkaḷum nekiḻcci nīṅki muṉkikaḷil ceṟintuḷḷaṉa. innimittaṅkaḷ kūtirkkāla varaviṉi aṟivikkiṉṟaṉa. niṉṉi nīttup pālinilattuc ceṉṟa talivar virivil varuvār. nī kavalaṟka” eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉ varaviṟku nimittamāyiṉa kaṇṭu taliviyi āṟṟuvittatu.	(kuṟun. 260)
`avaravar uṟupiṇi tamavākac cērttal' -
talivaṉpāl mikka kātal koṇṭa talivi melintum mēṉipacantum iravu tuyil koḷḷātum tāṉ paṭum tuṉpaṅkaḷip paṟavi pōṉṟa piṟaporuḷmēl ēṟṟi, avaṟṟukku iyalpāka amintavaṟṟi avi taṉṉip pōlat tirumāliṭam kātal koṇṭaṉamayāl viḷintaṉa vākak kūṟutal.
“uppaṅkaḻiyē! iravellām tuñcāmal nīyum tuyar uṟukiṟāyē. nīyum eṉṉip pōlat tirumāliṭam niṉ neñciṉip paṟi koṭuttāyō” eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟutal
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvāy. 2- 1- 8)
avaḷ uṭaṉpaṭuttal -
taṉṉūr aṇittaṉṟu eṉavum, talivi  taṉiyē curavaḻiyil taṉṉuṭaṉ varutal iyalātu eṉavum kūṟi, uṭaṉpōkkiṉi maṟutta talivaṉiṭam, tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟi,  avaṉi uṭaṉpaṭuvittal.
“taliva!  niṉtuṇi irukkiyil, talivikkup piṟa tuṇiyum vēṇṭumō? pālivaḻi ivaṭkuc cuṭātavāṟu niṉ aruḷ kuḷirvikkum. evvāṟēṉum ivaḷi nī uṭaṉ koṇṭu pōtalē takkatu” (tañci. kō. 307) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa tōḻiyiṉ kūṟṟu eṭuttukkāṭṭu. tirukkōvi yār itaṉi ‘ātaram kūṟal’ eṉṉum (202).
itu variviyaṟkaṇ kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭil uṭaṉpōk kiṉkaṇṇatu oru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 182)
avaḷ etirppaṭṭa iṭatti avaḷākak kūṟutal -
vēṭki mītūrnta niliyil taliviyik kāṇat tuṭikkum talivaṉ avaḷoṭu kūṭit tāṉ iṉpuṟṟa iṭatti avaḷākavē kāṇum maṉaniliyuṭaṉ pēcutal.
“poḻilil uḷḷa mūṅkil avaḷ tōḷi niṉivūṭṭa, āṭum mayil avaḷcāyalik kāṭṭa, avaḷiṭiyi ottuk koṭikaḷ tuvaḷa, inta iṭamē eṉ uyiraṉiya talivi pōla eṉ uḷḷattiṟku ēṟpat tōṉṟukiṟatu” eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu (kōvi. 38) eṭuttukkāṭṭām.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘poḻilkaṇṭu makiḻtal’ eṉṉum.  pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatu ikkūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaṟkaṇ ‘iṭam talippāṭu’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟām.	(i. vi. 503 uri)
avaḷ kuṟippaṟital -
talivaṉ viruppam talivikaṇṇatē eṉpati uṟutiyāka aṟinta tōḻi, talivaṉ taliviyi virumpumāṟu pōlat taliviyum avaṉi virumpukiṉṟāḷā eṉṟu avaḷik kuṟikkoṇṭu nōkki, avaḷukkum avaṉpāl mikunta vēṭki uḷatāya ceytiyi avaḷuṭiya kuṟippukkaḷāṉ aṟital. itu ‘kuṟiyuṟa uṇartal’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (kōvi. 65)
avaḷ payam urittal -
kaṟpukkālattil ōtal tūtu poruḷ mutaliyaṉa karutip pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ taṉatu niṉippāl eṭuttuk koṇṭa ceyali muṭṭiṉṟu muṭikkātu tirumpiviṭu vāṉō eṉṟu tāṉ añciya accattit talivi tōḻi yiṭam kūṟal (mulli naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 94 uri mēṟ.)
avaḷ paḻitturittal -
talivaṉ poruḷvayiṟ pirintu mīṇṭum varak kālam tāḻkkavē, avaṉpirivu kuṟittut talivi varunta, tōḻi avaḷi nōkki “miṉṉē! talivaṉatu maṉam irumpāka uḷḷatu. avaṉi virumpi uṉ uṭal pacali pāyntu poṉvaṭivamākiyatu. taṉṉic cērntavarkkē ivvaḷavu tuyaram viḷikkum talivaṉ taṉ pakivarkaḷi ettakiya iṭarkaṭku akappaṭuppāṉō? aṟiyēṉ” eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟuvatu.
itu tiruvārūrk kōviyil ‘aṟattoṭu niṟṟal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatorukūṟṟu. (346)

avaṉ avaṇ pulampal -
variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ, tāṉ muṉpu coṉṉavāṟē kārkālat toṭak kattiṟkuḷ taliviyik kāṇac cella iyalātu pōṉami eṇṇit tāṉ ceṉṟa iṭattē tuyaruṟutal.
“kaṇ kuḻiya eṉtēr varum vaḻiyi nōkkiya vaṇṇam, kūntal cōra, uḷḷaṅkiyil kaṇṇīr veḷḷam pōl pāya, varuntāniṟkum taliviyi, ‘muṉ ceṉṟu talivaṉi varavēṟka varuvāyāka’ eṉṟu kūṟutaṟkup pōṉa eṉmaṉam, ‘avar eṅkē’ eṉṟu avaḷ taṉṉik kēṭpiṉ, yātu viṭi kūṟumō?” (ampikā. 319) eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivaṉ variviṭivittup poruḷ tēṭac ceṉṟa iṭattē taliviyi niṉintu varuntal.
itu kaḷaviyalil ‘variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉṟum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 170)
avaṉ kuṟippaṟital -
talivaṉ taṉṉi irantu kuṟiyuṟṟuk kiyuṟi ēnti niṉṟavaḻi, avaṉ taṉṉuṭaṉ niṉṟa talimakaḷi aṭikkaṭi nōkkutali yuṇarnta tōḻi, talivaṉatu kuṟipput taliviyiṭattatu eṉavum avaṉ taliviyatu kūṭṭam viḻikiṉṟāṉ eṉavum taṉ ārāycciyāṉ aṟital.
itu ‘kuṟiyuṟa uṇartal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 64)
avaṉvayiṉ paratti(mi) -
kaṟpukkālattut talivi talivaṉ taṉṉi maṟantu ayalāṉ pōl oḻukuvatākat taṉ ovvoru pēccilum veḷi yiṭutal. 	(to. po. 109 iḷa.)
kaḷavilum kaṟpukkaṭampūṇṭu oḻukum talivi taṉkātal mikutiyāl talivaṉ taṉṉi maṟantu ayalavaṉ pōla oḻukukiṟāṉ eṉṟu karuti uriyāṭum peṇmikkuriya ceyti.	(111 nac.)
talivi, talivaṉ taṉṉiviṭṭu vēṟumakaḷiriyum kātalikkiṉṟāṉ pōlum eṉṟu eṇṇutal; talivaṉiṭam attakiya ayalavaṉām taṉmi illātiruppiṉum, kātal mikutiyāl aṅṅaṉam karututal peṇtaṉmiyākum. (161 kuḻa.)
avaṉvayiṉ vēṭṭal -
parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉ taliviyiṭam taṉakkup puṟattoḻukkam etuvumiṉṟu eṉṟu maṟittup poy kūṟa vum, aṅṅaṉam poy kūṟiya talivaṉiṭam talivi virumpal. 	(to. po. 205 nac.)
avaṉ varivu maṟuttaṟkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal -
1) talivicuṟṟattār talivaṟku varivu maṟuttavaḻiyum tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niliyāṟ kūṟuvāḷ.
“aṉṉiyē! maḻi peytu aruvinīrāka, mūṅkil vaḷarn tuḷḷa maliccarivu vaḻiyē iṟaṅkum talivaṉuṭiya maliyi otta mēmpaṭṭa mārpiṉit taḻuvāta nāḷel lām ittaliviyiṉ kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷ kalaṅkik kaṇṇīr vaṭikkum.”	(aiṅ. 220)
“ittaliviyip periya malināṭaṉākiya talivaṉ varivu vēṇṭi variṉ, maṟukkāmal koṭuttāltāṉ, ivaḷukku naṉmiyic ceytatākum. talivaṉ varivu vēṇṭit tamari viṭuttatik kaṇṭapiṉṉum, ivaḷ tuyaram iṉṉum kuṟiya villi”.
ivi pōlat tōḻi aṟattoṭu niṟpāḷ. 	(aiṅ. 258)
2) tamar varivu maṟupparō  eṉak kavaliyuṟṟa talivikkut tōḻi kūṟiyatu.
“talivi! nam cuṟṟattār, kiyil taṇṭūṉṟit taliyil talippāki yaṇintu ‘naṉṟu naṉṟu’ eṉṟu vanta talivaṉ tamaroṭu mukamaṉ kūṟi aḷavaḷāvukiṉṟaṉar. ātaliṉ nam mūril pirintavarkaḷic cērttu vippavarkaḷum irukki ṟārkaḷ eṉṟu koḷ” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.	(kuṟun. 146)
3) tamar varivumaṟuttuḻi āṟṟāta talivikkut tōḻi tīya kuṟi nīṅki naṟkuṟi taṉakkuc ceyyak kaṇṭu “kaṭitiṉ varivar” eṉṟu kūṟiyatu.
“talivi! eṉ iṭakkaṇ tuṭikkiṉṟatu; eṉ muṉki kaḷiluḷḷa vaḷikaḷum nekiḻāmal ceṟikiṉṟaṉa; kaḷiṟṟiṉ tākkutaliṉiṉṟu tappiya ciṉam ciṟanta puli iṭi pōla uṟumum. talivaṉ virivil variya varuvāṉ eṉṟu naṉṉimittam kaṇṭu kūṟukiṟēṉ” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu. (aiṅ. 218)	(to. po. 114 nac.)
“aḻakum nāṇum aḻiyāmal nī āṟṟal vēṇṭum” eṉṟu coṉṉa tōḻikkut talivi kūṟutal -
“kātalar eṉṉip pirintu ceṉṟapōtē eṉ cāyaliyum nāṇattiyum tām koṇṭu, māṟāka, pacaliyiyum kāma nōyiyum tantu ceṉṟuviṭṭār” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu. 	(kuṟaḷ 1183)
aḻiñcukkāṭu -
pāli nilam - ‘peruviṭāyaṉāṉavaṉ aḻiñcuk kāṭu ēṟappōka’ īṭu  6 - 2 piravēcam	(ṭu)
aḻuṅku tāykkurittal -
vaḻiyiṭik kaṇṭōr ulaka iyalpiṉikkūṟi, “makaḷir paruvam vantavuṭaṉ tammip peṟṟavarkkup payaṉpaṭātu, tammi virumpum kātalarkkē payaṉpaṭuvar” eṉṟu kūṟiyatiyum maṉaṅkoḷḷātu varuntum cevili kēṭpa, “inta ammitāṉ appeṇṇi vaḷarttavaḷ pōlum. avaḷum avaḷ kātalaṉum avaṉūri innērattuḷ aṭintiruppārkaḷē! ittāy pōyc ceyyakkaṭava kāriyam oṉṟumilli” eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟi, avarkaḷip piṉtoṭartali viṭuttu mīṇṭu pōmāṟu aṟivuṟuttal. 
itu tirukkōviyāril ‘uṭaṉ pōkku’ eṉṉum kiḷavik - kaṇṇatoru kiḷavi.	(kōvi. 249.)

aḻuṅkuṟṟu uṇarttal -
talivaṉ kaṟpukkālattup piriyum kuṟippiṉaṉ ātalit talivi aṟintu appiriviṟku varuntip piritaltuṉpam taṉakkut tāṅkoṇāt tuyaram tarum eṉpatit taliva ṉiṭam kūṟal.
itu kāma nukarcciyiṭattu vēṭkiyāṉ ceyyum kuṟip pāṉa ceyalkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. vī.cō. (‘kuṟippu’ eṉpatu paṟṟiya) uri viḷakkam.
iḵtu akattiṇi uri irupattēḻaṉuḷ kuṟippu eṉpataṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(vī. cō. 90)
aḷatti -
neytal nilattup peṇpāṟpeyar.	(cūṭā. aiai 72.)
aḷam -
neytal nilam (aka.ni. 159)	(ṭu)
aṟciram -
muṉpaṉikkālam; `aṟcira maṟakkuna rallar' (aiṅ. 464) kuṟiñci yākiya uripporuḷukkuc ciṟanta perumpoḻutu itu.
aṟciri -
aṟciram; `aṟciri veyya veppat taṇṇīr' 	(kuṟun. 277)
aṟakkaḻivu -
aṟattiṟku aḻivutarum ceyal. aṟattiṟku aḻivu tarum akapporuḷāvatu piṟaṉmaṉik kūṭṭam.			(to. po. 214 iḷa.)
aṟakkaḻivāvatu ulaka vaḻakkiṟkup poruttamiṉmi. atu tōḻi, “eṉṉit talivaṉ nayantāṉ” eṉavum, ” avaṉ mārpil poyyāka vīḻntēṉ” eṉavum kūṟuvaṉavum, talivaṉuṭiya iruppiṭam nōkkit tāṉ celvatākat talivi kūṟuvaṉavum pōlvaṉa.	(218 nac.)

aṟakkaḻivuṭiyaṉa poruṭpayam paṭavaral -
aṟakkaḻivuṭiyaṉa - aṟattiṟku māṟupaṭṭa ceyalkaḷ; poruṭpayaṉpaṭutalāvatu iṉpamum poruḷum tarutal. poruḷāvatu akapporuḷum puṟapporuḷum.
akapporuḷil, piṟaṉmaṉik kūṭṭattāl iṉpamum poruḷum kiṭṭuvaṉāyiṉum, avaṟṟi ēlātu vilakka vēṇṭum; puṟapporuḷil pakivaruṭiya niri kōṭalum aḻittalum pōla, naṭṭōruṭiya nirikōṭalum aḻittalum pōlvaṉa poruḷtarumēṉum avaṟṟi vilakka vēṇṭum.	(to. po. 214 iḷa.)
ulakavaḻakkiṟkup poruttamillāta kūṟṟukkaḷ akap poruḷukkum payaṉpaṭa varuvaṉavāyiṉ, avaṟṟiyum vaḻakkamākak koṇṭu ceyyuḷ ceytal.
talivaṉ taṉṉiṭam kuṟi nayattalit tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟumpōtu avaṉ taṉṉi nayantāṉ pōlat talivi yiṭam  kūṟutal, talivi tōḻikkut taṉ kaḷavoḻuk kattip pulappaṭutta muṭiyāmal varuntuṅkālattu ataṉit taṉakkup pulappaṭuvittukkoṇṭē avaḷi āṟṟuvit taṟporuṭṭu aṟakkaḻivuṭiyaṉa kūṟuvatātaliṉ, aḵtu akapporuḷukku ēṟṟatu. talivi talivaṉatu ūrkkup puṟappaṭṭup pōkalām eṉṟu kūṟuvatu ulakavaḻakkiṟku māṟupaṭṭatēṉum, ataṉit talivaṉ ciṟippuṟattā ṉākak kēṭkumāṟu kūṟutaliṉ, taliviyiṉ pirivāṟṟāmi yit talivaṉ uṇarvataṟkuk kūṟum akkūṟṟāl avaṉ virivil variya muyalvāṉ eṉpatu amitaliṉ, aṟak kaḻivu uṭiyatāyiṉum akapporuḷukku ēṟṟatu. 	(218 nac.)
e-ṭu : `nerunal elli ēṉal tōṉṟi'  (akanā. 32)
“nerunal yāṉ kākkiṉṟa puṉattu vantu oru talivaṉ taṉ perumik kēlāc ciṟucoṟ collit taṉṉi yāṉ varuttiṉēṉākak kūṟi eṉṉi muyaṅkiṉāṉ; yāṉ ataṟku muṉ nekiḻnta maṉanekiḻcci avaṉ aṟiyāmal maṟittu vaṉcoṟ colli nīṅkiṉēṉ; avvaḻi eṉ vaṉkaṇmi yāṟ piṟitoṉṟu kūṟavallaṉ āyiṟṟilaṉ. avvāṟu pōṉavaṉ iṉṟu namakkut tōlāttaṉmiyiṉmiyi ṉiṉṟu  iḷivantoḻuvaṉ; taṉakkē namtōḷ uriya ākalum aṟiyāṉāy, eṉṉip puṟanili muyalum kaṇṇōṭṭam uṭiyavaṉi niṉāyamum yāṉum nīyum kaṇṭu nakuvōmāka; nī avaṉ varumiṭattē celvāyāka” eṉak kūṟiyavaḻi, “em perumāṉi ivaḷ puṟattāṟṟiṟ koṇṭāḷkollō?” eṉavum, “avaṉ taṉakku iṉiya ceytaṉavellām eṉ poruṭṭeṉṟu koḷḷātu, piṟaḻakkoṇṭāḷ kollō?” eṉavum talivi karutumāṟṟāṉē kūṟiṉāḷ eṉiṉum, ataṉuḷḷē, “ivaḷ eṉakkuc ciṟantāḷ” eṉpatu (avaṉ) uṇartaliṉ, “eṉ varuttam tīrkkiṉṟili” eṉṟāṉ eṉavum, ataṟku mukamaṉāka ivaḷit taḻīik koṇṭataṉṟi ivaḷ piṟaḻak koṇṭa taṉmi ivaḷkaṇ uḷatāyiṉ ivaḷik kuṟippaṟiyātu pullāṉ eṉavum, ivvoḻukalāṟu ciṟituṇartaliṉ ikkuṟi muṭittaṟku maṉañekiḻntāḷ eṉavum, avaṉi eṉṉoṭu kūṭṭutaṟku eṉṉi vēṟuniṟuttit tāṉum āyamum vēṟuniṉṟu nakuvēmeṉak kūṟiṉāḷ eṉavum, talivi nāṇ nīṅkā mikkuk kāraṇamākiya poruḷi uḷḷaṭakkip puṇarttuk kūṟiyavāṟu kāṇka.	(to. po. 218 nac.)
aṟattoṭu nili -
aṟattoṭu māṟukoḷḷāta nili; takkataṉic colli niṟṟal; kaṟpiṉ taliniṟṟal. aḵtāvatu talivi taṉakkum talivaṉukkum vitivayattāl ēṟpaṭṭa toṭarpiṉic cevviyaṟintu tōḻikkut terivittu, avaḷ vāyilākac cevilikkum, cevili vāyilāka naṟṟāykkum, naṟṟāy vāyilākat tanti taṉṉiyarkkum terivittut taṉatu tirumaṇam talivaṉuṭaṉēyē naṭipeṟuvataṟku ēṟpaṉa ceytal.
aṟam eṉpatu takkatu. aṟattoṭu nili eṉpatu takkataṉic colli niṟṟal eṉṟavāṟu. allatūum, peṇṭirkku aṟam eṉpatu kaṟpu; kaṟpiṉ taliniṟṟal eṉpatumām. 	(iṟi. a. 29 uri; na.a. 48)
aṟattoṭu niliyiṉ eḻuvaki (1) -
1) eḷittal - ‘talivaṉ nammāṭṭu eḷiyaṉ’ eṉṟu kūṟutal. makaḷuṭiya tāy marumakaṉ tam viruppappaṭi naṭappavaṉāka iruttal vēṇṭum eṉṟu karututaliṉ, talivaṉi eḷiyaṉ eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟi aṟattoṭu niṟṟal.
2) ēttal : makaḷuṭiya tāy talivaṉ uyarntavaṉ eṉṟu kūṟiyavaḻi maṉam makiḻvāḷ ātaliṉ, tōḻi talivaṉi uyarttik kūṟal.
3) vēṭkiyurittal : talivaṉmāṭṭut talivi vēṭkiyum, talivimāṭṭut talivaṉ vēṭkiyum kūṟutal.
4) kūṟutal : taliviyit talivaṟkuk koṭuttal vēṇṭum eṉpatupaṭak kūṟal.
5) ucāvutal : veṟiyāṭṭum kaḻaṅkum iṭṭurittuḻi, vēlaṉōṭāvatu piṟarōṭāvatu tōḻi uriyāṭutal.
6) ētīṭu talippāṭu : puṉaliṭi utaviṉāṉ, kaḷiṟṟiṭi utaviṉāṉ, taḻi tantāṉ, kaṇṇi tantāṉ eṉa yātāṉum oru kāraṇatti muṉṉiṭṭu iruvarum cantittaṉar eṉpatu.
7) uṇmi ceppum kiḷavi : nikaḻntati nikaḻntapaṭi kūṟutal.	(to. po. 112 iḷa. uri)
aṟattoṭu niliyiṉ eḻuvaki (2) -
1) talivaṉi eḷiyaṉākak kūṟutal, 2) talivaṉi uyarttuk kūṟal, 3) talivaṉ taliviyiṭam koṇṭa vēṭkiyi mikuttuk kūṟutal, 4) taliviyum tōḻiyum veṟiyāṭṭiṭat tum piṟaviṭattum cila kūṟutaṟkaṇṇē piṟaruṭaṉēyum uriyāṭutal (kūṟutal ucātal), 5) oruvaṉ kaḷiṟum puliyum nāyum pōlvaṉa kāttu emmik kikkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, pūttantāṉ, taḻitantāṉ eṉavum ivi mutalāya kāraṇam iṭṭu uṇarttal (ētīṭu), 6) iruvarum tāmē etirppaṭṭaṉar; yāṉ aṟintilēṉ eṉṟal, 7) paṭittu moḻiyātu nikaḻntati nikaḻntavāṟu kūṟutal eṉpaṉa. 	(to. po. 207 nac.)
aṟattoṭu niṟkum neṟi -
talivi taṉatu kaḷaviyaṟ kaṟpoḻukkattiṟku ētam varumō eṉṟu añciyavaḻik kaṟpaṟam aḻiyāmikkāka nikaḻnta kaḷavoḻukkac ceytiyi oruvāṟṟāṉ tōḻikkuṇartta, tōḻi cevilikku muṉṉilip puṟamoḻiyākavum, muṉ ṉili moḻiyākavum uṇartta, cevili naṟṟāykku veḷippaṭiyāka uṇartta, naṟṟāy tanti taṉṉiyarkkuk kuṟippāka uṇarttal	(na. a. 48, 50-52)
aṟattoṭu niṟpārkku viṉā nikaḻum iṭam -
taliviyi uṟṟu nōkki avaḷuṭal vēṟupāṭum uḷḷa vēṟupāṭum kaṇṭavaḻi, pāṅki taliviyi viṉavuvāḷ; cevili pāṅkiyi viṉavuvāḷ; naṟṟāyum avaḷi viṉavu vāḷ. talivi talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōyavaḻip pāṅki ceviliyiṭamum, cevili naṟṟāyiṭamum, naṟṟāy tanti taṉṉiyariṭamum aṟattoṭu niṟpar.	(na. a. 53, 54)
aṟattoṭu niṟṟal -
aṟattoṭu niṟṟalāvatu kaḷavoḻukkatti muṟiyāka veḷippaṭuttiniṟṟal. aḵtāvatu kaḷavoḻukkattip poruntiya talivi, piṉṉar varintukoṇṭu illa- ṟatti mēvutal kuṟikkōḷ ātaliṉ, ataṟku iṭiyūṟāka veṟiyāṭṭeṭuttal piṟaṉvarivu āytal mutaliyavi nikaḻumāyiṉ taṉatukaṟpiṟku ūṟu nēruṅkol eṉa añcit tāṉ kaḷaviṉ maṇanta talivaṉiyē varinteytutal poruṭṭut tōḻivāyilākat taṉ maṉaniliyit tamark kuk kuṟippāṉ aṟivikkac ceytal. atu kaṟpoḻukkamākiya maṉiyaṟattoṭupaṭutali virumpi niṟkum niliyām. aṅṅaṉam taliviyatu karuttiṉi uṇarnta tōḻi cevi likkuk kuṟippāṉ uṇarttalum, cevili naṟṟāykku veḷip paṭiyāṉ uṇarttalum, naṟṟāy taṉ tamarkku urittalum ām.	(to. po. 11 ca.pāla.)
aṟattoṭu niṟṟal (talivi) (1) -
kaṟpu aḻiyātavāṟu talivi talivaṉoṭu tāṉ oḻuki vanta kaḷavoḻukkattit tamarkku muṟiyē aṟivuṟutti veḷippaṭuttalē aṟattoṭu niṟṟalām. aṅṅaṉam veḷippaṭuttumpōtu kaṭaṟkariyil vaṇṭalāṭik koṇṭiruntakāli, tōḻi vēṟṟiṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟa annērattē, kaṭaliṉ pēraliyoṉṟu taṉṉi aṭittuc cella, etirpārāvakiyil aṅku vanta talivaṉ taṉṉik kaṭaliṉiṉṟum eṭuttuk kāttatākavum, avaṉē taṉṉāl maṇakkat takkavaṉ eṉṟum, talivi tāyaṟiviṉoṭum tōḻikāvaloṭum ulakiyaloṭum kaṟpiṉoṭum māṟu koḷḷātavāṟu kūṟuvāḷ. itu ‘variporuṭ pirital’ eṉṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatorukūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 290)
aṟattoṭu niṟṟal (tōḻi) (2) -
taliviyippaṟṟi viṉaviya cevilikkut tōḻi “aṉṉiyē!’ talivi pētipparuvattaḷāka irunta kālattut taṉ pāvikku aṇiya malar koṭutta at talivaṉiyē maṇattal vēṇṭum eṉṟa uṟuti paruvamaṭinta ik kālattu avaḷukku viḷintamiyāl, avaṉip piṉ toṭarntu pōyiṉāḷ pōlum” (kōvi. 225) eṉṟāṟ pōlac cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟṟal.
itaṉip ‘pāṅki cevilikku uṇarttal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa. kiḷavi : kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi.	(i. vi. 538 uri)
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟām.	(kōvi. 225)
aṟattoṭu niṟṟal vaki -
muṉṉilimoḻi (muṉṉiṟpārkku nērē kūṟutal), muṉṉilip puṟamoḻi eṉpaṉa. muṉṉilip puṟamoḻi - tām uriyāṭa vēṇṭuvār nēril iruntum avari aḻittup pēcātu avar cevimaṭukkumāṟu ceytiyiṉi aṟivuṟuttal.	(na. a. 175)
aṟattoṭu niṟṟali yurittal -
kaḷavukkālattē talivaṉit talivi kāṇum poḻutiṉum kāṇāppoḻutu peritākalāṉ, pirivāṟṟāmiyāl uṭalum maṉamum meliya, taliviyiṉ melivu kaṇṭa tāyar atu teyvattāṉ āyiṟṟu eṉṟu teyvattukku makiḻcci yūṭṭa veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻttak karuti vēlaṉi aḻikka, ataṉik kēṭṭa talivi veṟiyāṭṭi vilakkak karutit tōḻiyi nōkki, “tōḻi! tāy  eṉṉi muṉintālum muṉika;  ūravar ēciṉum ēcuka; nīyē vekuḷiṉum vekuḷuka; yāṉ oḻukkan tavaṟāta tūyēṉ eṉṟu uṉakku uṟutiyākak kūṟi oru nikaḻcciyic collap pōkiṟēṉ” eṉṟu aṟattoṭu niṟka āyattam ātal.
itu ‘variporuṭpirital’ eṉṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 289)
aṟattoṭu niṉṟapiṉ, “yāṉ niṟkumāṟu eṉṉi?” eṉṟu nakiyāṭiya tōḻiyoṭu pulantu talivi taṉṉuḷḷē colliyatu -
“aṟivaṟṟavarkaḷ eṉṉip pōlak kāmanōykku āḷā kāta kāraṇattāl yāṉ kaṇṇāṟ kāṇavum kātāṟ kēṭpavum eṉṉi nakittu eḷḷukiṉṟaṉar!” eṉṟu talivi tōḻi kēṭpak kūṟutal.	(kuṟaḷ 1140)
‘aṟintōḷ ayarppiṉ avvaḻi maruṅkiṉ kēṭum pīṭum kūṟal’ -
tōḻi talivaṉiṭam “nīrkūṟiya ceytiyi yāṉ maṟantēṉ” eṉak kūṟumiṭattu, talivi taṉṉoṭu kūṭāmiyiṉ avaḷiṭam piṟanta tuyarattiyum avaḷ ataṉip poṟuttuk koṇṭirunta perumiyiyum talivaṉ kūṟutal.
e-ṭu :	“makaḷirōṭu ōriyum āṭāmal neytalmāliyum toṭukkāmal pūṅkāṉalil orupakkal niṉṟa nīyār? niṉṉit toḻutu viṉavuvōm. pēraḻakiṉi yuṭiyāy! nī kaṭaṟparappil mēvum aṇaṅkō? karuṅkaḻip pakkalil niliniṉṟa teyvamō? colluka” eṉṟēṉāka, ataṉetir avaḷuṭiya puṉmuṟuvalum pūttatu; kaṇkaḷum paṉittaṉa” (naṟ. 155) eṉavum,
“talivi tāṉuṟṟa varuttattit taṇṇiya taḻiyāṭi uṭuttum perumūcceṟintum kaṇṇīr ukuttum māṟṟik koḷkiṟāḷ eṉpatik kēḷviyuṟṟu varuntukiṟēṉ. ittalivi taṅkiyirukkum innalla ūril eṉṉiyum avaḷiyum pirittut tuṉpuṟuttum koṭiya makkaḷum uḷḷārē!” eṉavum,
“tōḻi! nī eṉṉi virumpi eṉakku aruḷāviṭiṉ, talivi yiṉ tōḷkaḷiyum kūntaliyum palavākap pārāṭṭi avaḷuṭaṉ makiḻcciyāka vāḻtal iyalātu; eṉ pirivi ṉāl avaḷ nalaṉ iḻattal uṟuti” (aiṅ. 178) eṉavum tōḻiyiṭam talivaṉ kūṟutal. 	(to. po. 102 nac.)
aṟanili -
itu maṉṟal eṭṭaṉuḷ mutalāvatu. ituvē piramam eṉavum pirama maṇam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭuvatu. tarumacāttiram kūṟum aṟanili cāṉṟa maṇam ātaliṉ, ‘aṟanili’ eṉappaṭṭatu.
peṇṇiṉ tanti, taṉ kōttirattiṟkup poruttamāṉa takka kōttirattic cērntavaṉum nāṟpattueṭṭuyāṇṭu piramacariyam kāttavaṉum ākiya maṇamakaṉukkut taṉ makaḷip pūppeytiya piṟaku maṟupūppu eytumuṉ, poṉṉum maṇiyum aṇivittu nīrttāri vārttut tāṉamākak koṭuttuc ceyyum tirumaṇam.	(to. po. 92 nac.)
iḵtu oppil kūṭṭamātaliṉ poruntāk kāmattiṉ - pāṟpaṭum (ta. ne. vi. 14). peṇ pūppeytiya piṟaku ōr irutuk kāṭci oruvaṉic cārātu kaḻiyumāyiṉ oru pārppaṉak koliyōṭu okkum eṉpatu.	(iṟi. a. 1 uri)
aṟanili iṉpam -
kulamum oḻukkamum kuṇamum paruvamum otta kaṉṉiyi aṅkiyaṅkaṭavuḷ aṟikariyāka maṇantu illaṟattu vāḻtal. 	(piṅ. 765)
aṟappuṟaṅkāval -
tarumattukku viṭappaṭṭa pūmikaḷip pātukākki. ‘aṟap puṟaṅkāval nāṭu kāvaleṉṟu’	(na. a. 72)  (ṭu)
aṟapporuṭ paṭuttal -
talivaṉ taliviyi aṟamākiya poruḷil poruttutal. parattiyiṟ pirinta talivaṉ, talivi pūppeytiyatic cevvaṇi aṇintuvarum cēṭivāyilāka uṇarntu illam vantu, avaḷ pūppuṟṟa mūṉṟu nāḷkaḷum taṉ coṟkaḷi avaḷ cevimaṭukkum aṇmiyil iruttalē aṟam; aṅṅaṉam ceyyātu pōyiṉum, pūppu nikaḻnta nāṉkām nāḷ avaḷ nīrāṭiyavaḻip parattiyiṉiṉṟum mīṇṭuvantu avaḷi aṟamākiya poruḷil poruttutalum kuṟṟa miṉṟu. eṉavē, talimakaḷi vāyilkaḷāl kōpam taṇivittu avaḷik kūṭutalum aṟapporuṭ paṭuttalām. aḵtu attuṇic ciṟappiṉṟu āyiṉum kuṟṟam eṉap paṭātu eṉpatu.	(iṟi. 44 uri)
aṟimaṭacciṟappu -
talivaṉiṭam, avaṉuṭiya tīyakuṇaṅkaḷ paṟṟic ciṟitum niṉiyātu avaṉic cāṉṟōṉ eṉavē uṟutiyākat tuṇinta taṅkaḷatu aṟiviṉatu kuṟipāṭṭi eṭuttuk kūṟit tam kātaṟ ciṟappiṉit tōḻi viḷakkutal.
e-ṭu :	“yām nummoṭu makiḻntu ciritta tūya veḷḷiya paṟkaḷ pālinilattil cellum yāṉiyiṉatu maliyik kuttiya kompu pōla virivāka muṟivaṉa vākuka! emmuyir, pāṇar tām piṭitta pacci mīṉip peyta maṇṭiyip pōla, emakkup periya veṟuppit taruvatāki, ummiyum yām peṟa iyalāta niliyil iruppativiṭa aḻika!” (kuṟun.  169) eṉṟāṟ pōla, toṭakkattil avaṉi uḷḷavāṟu uṇarāta tam aṟiyāmikku varuntik kūṟumiṭattē, avaṉoṭu nakutaṟkut tōṉṟiya uṇarvu iṉṟiyamiyāmi kūṟit tam kātaṟ ciṟappurittal.	(to. po. 114 nac.)
aṟivil kuṟipāṭu uṟṟa kātaṟ ciṟappāvatu talivaṉ kuṟi ceyyātaṉavaṟṟi avaṉ kuṟi ceytaṉavākat tiripupaṭak koṇṭu alla kuṟippaṭutal eṉpar iḷampūraṇar. (112)
aṟiyāḷ pōṟal -
talivaṉukkuk kuṟinērnta tōḻi talivaṉ niliyit taliviyiṭam kūṟa, avaḷ nāṇattāl ataṉi aṟiyātavaḷ pōla vēṟoru ceytiyik kūṟit taṉ maṉakkaruttik kuṟippāl veḷippaṭuttal.
“caṅkam tarum mutti nām peṟak kaṭal periya uppaṅkaḻiyi vantu poruntiyatu” eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivi kūṟutal eṭuttukkāṭṭām. itaṉkaṇ, “talivaṉ kūṭṭattāl ēṟpaṭum muttatti yāṉ peṟa avaṉ taṉ iruppiṭatti ṉiṉṟu peyarntu ippuṉattukku vantuḷḷāṉ” eṉṉum kuṟippupporuḷ tōṉṟumāṟu kāṇka.
itaṉi ‘iṟivi aṟiyāḷ pōṉṟu kuṟiyāḷ kūṟal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa. 	(na. a. 147).
itu ‘kuṟinayappuk kūṟal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 85)
aṟivar -
kāmam vekuḷi mayakkam eṉṉum mukkuṟṟamum kaṭantu mukkāla nikaḻccikaḷiyum aṟiyavalla ñāṉiyar. ivarkaḷ kūṟum kūṟṟukkaḷic cevimaṭuttut talivaṉum taliviyum paṇintu naṭappar. ātaliṉ ivarkaḷum talivikku, nalla vaṟṟic ceyya vēṇṭum eṉavum, allavaṟṟi vilakka vēṇṭum eṉavum, mukkālac cāṉṟōrkaḷiṉ ceyyuḷ kaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭi viḷakkaṅkūṟi nalvaḻippaṭuttuvar. talivaṉō taliviyō uṇarppuvayiṉ vārā ūṭal koḷḷiṉ avarkaṭkut tamvāḻkkiyiṉ etirkālam paṟṟi iṭittuk kūṟi avarkaḷatu ūṭalip pōkkuvar. 	(to. po. 154 nac.)
“aṟivillātavarāṉa makaḷiriviṭa aṟivuṭiyarāṉa āṭavarē mikutiyākap poṟukkum iyalpuṭiyar” eṉṟa tōḻikkut talivaṉ kūṟiyatu -
“kaṭal pōlak kariyaṟṟa kāmattāl tuyaruṟṟālum maṭalēṟātu poṟumiyōṭirukkum peṇpiṟappiṉip pōla mikka takutiyuṭiya piṟappu ulakil illi” eṉṟu, āṭavarkkuk kāmattip poṟukkum āṟṟal iṉmiyit talivaṉ tōḻikkuk kūṟutal.	(kuṟaḷ 1137)
aṟivu aṟivuṟuttal (1)
taliviyiṉatu pēraṟivit tōḻi talivaṟkuk kūṟutal.
“taliva! em talivi, taṉṉiyum taṉ vāṭiya tōḷkaḷi yum kaḻalum vaḷikaḷiyum niṉṉi ivvūrār tūṟṟum paḻiyiyum kaṇṭu nāṇit taṉṉuḷḷattuḷḷa varuttatti em aṉṉiyum cēriyum āyattārum ciṟantēṉākiya yāṉum uṭpaṭa aṟiyātu maṟittaṉaḷ” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.  (kali. 44).
itu ‘varital vēṭki eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.’	(ampikā. 266)

aṟivu aṟivuṟuttal (2) -
taliviyatu mūtaṟivit tōḻi talivaṟku eṭutturittal.
“taliva! talivi taṉṉuṭiya tāy, cēriyōr, ayalōr, āyattavar ivariyē aṉṟi eṉṉiyum kūṭa ‘uṉṉip paḻitūṟṟuvōmō’ eṉpataṟku nāṇit taṉ maṉaniliyi yum tōḷmeliviyum aṟiyātapaṭi maṟittuviṭṭāḷ” (tañci. kō. 235) eṉṟu tōḻi taliviyatu mūtaṟiviṉit talivaṉiṭam kūṟal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘varivu kaṭātal’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 166)
aṟivu nāṭal -
talivi tōḻi eṉṉum iruvarum irunta iṭattiṟkut talivaṉ vantu, mutaṟkaṇ ūr peyar mutaliyaṉa viṉavip piṉṉart tāṉ koṇarnta taḻiyuṭiyi avarkaṭkuk kiyuṟiyākak koṭuttali virumpa, tōḻi avaṉ coṟkaḷuṭ potinta karuttiṉi ārāyntu, avaṉ niṉivu iṉṉatu eṉpataṉi aṟital.
itaṉi ‘yārē ivarmaṉat teṇṇam yāteṉat teḷital’ eṉpa.	(na. a. 140.)
iḵtu ‘iruvarum uḷvaḻi avaṉ varavuṇartal’ eṉṉum kiḷavikkaṇ aminta irukūṟṟukkaḷuḷ iraṇṭāvatu. 			(kōvi. 61)
`aṟivum niṟiyum aṇṇali eytal' -
pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattil taliviyik kūṭiyapiṉ, talivaṉ taṉ paṇṭiyuṇarcci eytappeṟavē, muṉ maṭaṅkiyirunta avaṉ aṟivum niṟiyum mīṇṭum avaṉpāl veḷippaṭu tal. itaṉāṟ payaṉ, kaḷavoḻukkatti nīṭṭiyātu avaṉ virivil taliviyi maṇantukoḷvāṉ eṉpatu. (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal) 	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)

aṟuparuvam -
kār, kūtir, muṉpaṉi, piṉpaṉi, iḷavēṉil, mutuvēṉil eṉṉum perumpoḻutu āṟum aṟuparuvamām. ivi āvaṇittiṅkaḷ toṭaṅki ovvoṉṟum ivviraṇṭu tiṅkaḷ elliyaṉa. ivi mutaṟporuḷiṉ oru kūṟā kiya poḻutiṉ orupakutiyākiya perumpoḻutu eṉappaṭuvaṉa.	(i.vi. 384)
aṟupoḻutu -
‘aṟu paruvam’ kāṇka. perumpoḻutu āṟum, ciṟu poḻutu āṟum ākiya mutaṟporuḷiṉ oru kūṟu.
aṉpariṟ kūṭṭu -
pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam. 	(cāmi. po. 80, 86)
`aṉpili koṭiyi eṉṟal' -
tōḻi talivaṉ tammiṭam aṉpaṟṟa ceyalāṉ koṭiya ṉāka uḷḷami kūṟal.
“aiya! muṉpellām talivi vēppaṅkāyik koṭut tālum ‘vellakkaṭṭipōla uḷḷatu’ eṉṟīr; ti mātattē paṟampumalic cuṉinīrik koṭuttālum annīr veppamāka uḷḷatu eṉṟum, uvarkkiṉṟatu eṉṟum ippoḻutu kūṟukiṟīr! numatu aṉpiṭattu ēṟpaṭṭa tiripiṉ payaṉē itu” (kuṟun. 196) eṉṟāṟ pōlat tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal.
talivaṉatu puṟattoḻukkattip pōkkutal kāraṇat tāṉum, talivi avaṉatu parattimi aṟintum aṟiyā tāḷ pōla maṭaṉ eṉṉum kuṇattiṟku ēṟṟaṉa koṇṭu oḻukum eḷiyaḷāy irutatalāṉum, tōḻi talivaṉi “nī aṉpillāy koṭiyāy” eṉṟu collattakāta kiḷavi yum orōvaḻic collutalum uriyāḷ.(to. po. 158 nac.)
itu ‘parattiyiṟpirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. iḵtu uṇartta uṇarā ūṭal 	(na. a. 206; i.vi. 555)

aṉpiṉatu akalam oṉṟāmi -
talivaṉ kaṟpuk kālattil taliviyi ōtal kāval poruḷ mutaliya paṟṟip pirintuḻi nikaḻum aṉpiṉatu akalam kāraṇamākap pirivu kuṟittavaḻi, taliviyatu pirivāṟṟāmi iṭiniṉṟu taṭuttup piritaṟkaṇ maṉam poruntātavāṟu ceytal.	(to. po. 41 nac.)
taṉṉiyē ellāmākak karuti aṉpu ceyyum ciṟanta variṭattut tāṉum kuṟivaṟṟa aṉpu celutta vēṇṭum eṉṟu  uṇarum uṇarcciyip poruntāmi.			(to. po. 44 iḷa.)
aṉpiṉatu akalamum akaṟciyatu arumiyum oṉṟāmikkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟutal -
taliviyiṭattuḷḷa aṉpiṉ mikutiyāl avaḷ celvac ceḻippoṭu vāḻa vēṇṭum eṉavum, cilanāḷ talivi yip pirintiruntu mīṇṭum kūṭiṉāl aṉpu mikutippaṭum eṉavum koṇṭa eṇṇatti, taliviyip pirintāl avaḷ pirivāṟṟātu iṟantupaṭavum kūṭumātaliṉ avaḷ iṟantapiṉ tēṭivarum poruḷāl payaṉ etuvumilli eṉṟa eṇṇam taṭuttaṟkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“neñcē! mārikkālattu malarkiṟa nīr oḻukum picci arumpukaḷip periya paciya paṉaṅkuṭiyil vittu mūṭi viṭiyalil ataṉi virittuviṭṭāṟ pōṉṟa naṟumaṇamum taṇmiyum uṭiyaḷ eṉ talivi. puṉalil viṭum teppam pōṉṟa avaḷuṭiya paṇittōḷkaḷi maṇattalum ilam; piritalum ilam; piriyiṉ uyirvāḻtal ataṉiṉum ilam” (kuṟun. 168) eṉavum,
“neñcē! cuṉi vaṟṟippōṉa pālivaḻiyiṭattē iḷiya vākimarakkiḷiyil pūtta naṟumalar kariya mayiliṉatu uccik koṇṭipōlat tōṉṟum neṭiya kāṭṭuvaḻiyil, ittalivi tāṉum nammoṭu vantu poruntum muyakkat tiṉit taruvāḷāyiṉ, poruḷtēṭa virumpiya niṉatu tuṇivu naṉṟē!” (kuṟun. 347) eṉavum varum.(to. po. 41 nac.)

aṉpuṭikkāmam -
otta aṉpāṉ iyinta talivaṉum taliviyum tammuḷ kūṭik kāmiṉpam nukartaṟkuriya kuṟiñci pāli mulli marutam neytal eṉṉum aintiṇikkuriya kāmam.	(na. a. 4)
`aṉpu taliyeṭutta vaṉpuṟi' -
talivaṉ talivimāṭṭu aṉpuṭimiyiṉ avaḷukkuk karuṇi ceyvāṉ eṉṟu tōḻi avaḷi vaṟpuṟutti āṟṟuvittal. itaṉkaṇ, pakaṟkuṟi vantu pōkiṉṟa talivaṉ celluvataṉip piṉpuṟamāka nōkki āṟṟāta taliviyatu kuṟippaṟintu māviṉmēl vittu vaṟpuṟutti yatu (naṟ. 58), “varivu nīṭa āṟṟāta talivi vēṟupāṭu puṟattārkkup pulaṉākāmiyum iyiyum, iṅṅaṉam kūṟuvārip peṟiṉ” eṉṟu kūṟi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu (naṟ. 68), piriviṭi āṟṟāta taliviyit tōḻi naṉṉimittam kūṟi vaṟpuṟuttiyatu mutaliyaṉa koḷḷappaṭum.(to. po. 114 nac.)
`aṉpuṟu takuna iṟicciyuḷ cuṭṭal’ (1) -
talivaṉ kaṟpukkālattut taliviyip pirintu vēṟṟu nāṭṭil taṅkiyapōtu talivi talivaṉuṭiya piriviṉip poṟuttal iyalātu varuntuvāḷ.  appoḻutu tōḻi “talivar iyalpākavē neñcattil aṉpuṭiyavar. ataṉ mēlum avar niṉṉip pirintu celvaḻiyil kaḷiṟu taṉ piṭiyiṉ perumpaciyip pōkkutaṟku melliya tōliṉi yuṭiya āccāmarattip piḷantūṭṭum ceyaliyum pārt tukkoṇṭē ceṉṟiruppār ātaliṉ, niṉ niṉippuk kuṟiyātu virivil eṭutta ceyali muṭittu mīḷvar” eṉṟāṟ pōlak karupporuḷkaḷuḷ talivaṉ aṉpu ceytaṟkut takuvaṉavaṟṟik karutik kūṟutalumuṭiyaḷ. (kuṟun. 37) iṟicci - karupporuḷ.  (to. po. 231 nac.)
`aṉpuṟu takuna iṟicciyuḷ cuṭṭal' (2) -
iḵtu iṟicci aṇi vakikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. talivaṉuṭiya kaṟpukkālap piriviṉkaṇ āṟṟāḷ ākiya taliviyi āṟṟavittaṟ poruṭṭut tōḻi, talivaṉ poruḷ tēṭap pirintu ceṉṟapōtu iṭivaḻiyil avaṉ talivi yiṭattut tāṉ koṇṭa aṉpiṉi mikuttuṇarum vaki yil karupporuḷkaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷ kāṇappaṭum ātaliṉ, kālattāḻppiṉṟi mīḷvāṉ eṉṟu avaḷi vaṟpuṟutti āṟṟuvikkap payaṉpaṭum ceyti kūṟuvatu iṟicciyaṇiyiṉ ivvaki.
`naciperi tuṭiyar nalkalum nalkuvar
piṭipaci kaḷiiya peruṅki vēḻam
meṉciṉi yāam poḷikkum
aṉpiṉa tōḻiavar ceṉṟa āṟē'  (kuṟun. 37)
iyaṟkiyākavē taliviyiṭattup pēraṉpuṭiyarāy avaṭku aruḷ ceyyum talivar tām pirintu ceṉṟa vaḻiyil, peṇyāṉiyatu paciyip pōkka āṇyāṉi āccāmarattip poḷittu ūṭṭum ceyaliyum kāṇpār ātaliṉ, aṉpu mītūrntu virivil ceyalmuṟṟi mīḷvar eṉṟa karutti uṭkiṭiyākak koṇṭatu iv viṟicciyaṇivaki.	(mā. a. 176)
aṉṉattoli yurittal -
aṉṉam iravu muḻutum olitta ceyti kūṟal. tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattu iravukkuṟiyiṭattu alla kuṟippaṭṭa ceytiyit talivaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat tōḻi tali makaṭku urippāḷāy, ‘puṉṉimarattil kūṭṭil aṉṉaṅkaḷ iravu muḻutum uṟaṅkātu varunti olittuk koṇṭiruntaṉa’ (kōvi. 172) eṉṟu kūṟutal vāyilākat tām iravu muḻutum talivaṉ niṉippāl uṟaṅkātirunta ceytiyi avaṉ kēṭpak kūṟal.
itu tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattu ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ka. kā. pak. 95)
aṉṉamōṭāytal -
kaḷavuk kālattil talivaṉ oru nimittam kuṟittut taliviyip pirintu ceṉṟu orunāḷ avaḷic cantikka vārāta niliyil, avaḷ māliyil kaṭaṟkariyi aṭintu kaṭaluṭaṉē varunti, puṉṉi marattoṭu pulantu, taṉṉip pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉiṭam ammaram taṉ varuttattik kūṟāmiyāl avaṉ iṉi vārāṉō eṉa aṉṉatti nōkki viṉātal.
iḵtu ‘oruvaḻit taṇattal, eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 184)
aṉṉi, eṉṉi eṉpaṉa -
akapporuḷil aṉṉi, eṉṉi eṉpaṉa uṟavu muṟiyik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷ ātalē yaṉṟi, aṉpiṉi veḷippaṭuk kum coṟkaḷākavum varum. ataṉāl tōḻi taliviyi ‘aṉṉi’ eṉṟalum, talivi tōḻiyi ‘aṉṉi’ eṉṟa lum, iruvarum talivaṉi ‘eṉṉi’ eṉṟalum ciṟu pāṉmi talivaṉ taliviyi ‘aṉṉi’ eṉṟalum uḷa.			(to. po. 246 nac.)
iccoṟkaḷip pirittuc coṟporuḷ kāṇātu marapu paṟṟi ivvāṟu vaḻaṅkuvaṉavākak kōṭal vēṇṭum.			(242 iḷa.)
e-ṭu :
`aṉṉāy ivaṉōr iḷamāṇākkaṉ'  (kuṟun. 33)
`pulliṉ māyvatu evaṉkol aṉṉāy'  (kuṟun. 150)
ivi tōḻiyit talivi ‘aṉṉāy’ eṉṟaṉa.
`aṉṉāy vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉi nammūr'  (aiṅ. 202)
`aṉṉāy vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉi uvakkāṇ'  (aiṅ. 206)
ivi tōḻi taliviyi ‘aṉṉāy’ eṉṟaṉa.
`eṉakkum ākātu eṉṉikkum utavātu'  (kuṟun. 27)
`'orīi oḻukum eṉṉikkup
`pariyalaṉ maṉyāṉ paṇṭoru kālē.'  (kuṟun. 203)
ivi talivi, talivaṉi ‘eṉṉi’ eṉṟaṉa.
`aṉam pulampu akaṟṟal' -
pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattiṉkaṇ pāṅkaṉ talivi irunta iṭam kaṇṭu vantu talivaṉiṭam iyampa, talivaṉ avaḷ irup piṭam cērntu, avaḷ kuṟippaṟiyātu avaḷi aṇukutal kūṭātākaliṉ, avaḷ kāṇumāṟu varunti niṟpa, avaḷ ārvanōkkattōṭu avaṉi nōkki avaṉ taṉimit tuyarit tīrtta ceyti vīracōḻiyam kuṟiñci naṭiyiyalil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. (aṉṉam - ākupeyarāl, aṉṉa naṭiyiṉaḷākiya talivi) 	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
aṉaṉilam -
aṉal nilam - veppa mikka pāli nilam.	 (ṭu)
aṉurākam -
viruppam; perumpālum itu kāmaviḻiviṉik kuṟikkum. ‘iruvaruṭiya aṉurākamum kūṟiyavāṟu’			(kōvi. 33 uri) (ṭu)
aṣṭa vivākam -
‘maṉṟal eṭṭu’k kāṇka.	 (ṭu)

ā
ākkam ceppal -
meyyuṟu puṇarcci nikaḻumuṉ talivaṉukkum talivikkum ēṟpaṭum pattu vaki nilikaḷuḷ aintāvatu.
ākkam ceppalāvatu uṟaṅkāmi kūṟutalum, taṉ niliyi uṟṟāriṭam kūṟutalum mutalāyiṉa.	(to. po. 97 iḷa.)
meyyuṟu puṇarcci nikaḻumuṉ talivaṉukku ēṟpaṭum pattu avattikaḷuḷ aintāvatu ākkam ceppal. aḵtāvatu vāṭiyapiṉ taṉ neñcil varuttam mikukiṉṟapaṭiyit talivaṉ piṟarkku urittal.	(i. vi. 405)
iyaṟkip puṇarcci mutal kaḷavu veḷippaṭuntuṇiyum talivaṉ talivi eṉṉum iruvarkkum uḷavām ilakkaṇam oṉpataṉuḷ itu nāṉkāvatu.
aḵtāvatu yātāṉum ōr iṭiyūṟu kēṭṭavaḻi ataṉit tam naṉmikku nikaḻntatāka neñciṟkuk kūṟikkōṭal.
tōḻi, taliviyiṉ puṇarcci vēṇṭi irantu kuṟiyuṟṟa talivaṉi akaṟṟiyavaḻi, ataṉi avaṉ aṉpu eṉṟu koḷḷutalum, talivaṉ iyaṟkippuṇarcci puṇarntu nīṅkiyavaḻit talivi ataṉi aṉpu eṉṟu koḷḷutalum pōlvaṉa.	(to. po. 100 nac.)
ākkam ceppal : poruḷ -
kaḷavoḻukkattiṟkuriya uṇarvup pakutikaḷ pattaṉuḷ iḵtu oṉṟu. ākkam ceppalāvatu : oruvari oruvar eytutaṟkum piriviṉṟi iṉpuṟutaṟkum āvaṉa ivi eṉat tamakkut tāmē kūṟikkōṭal.	(to. kaḷa. 9 ca.pāla.)
`ākit tōṉṟum pāṅkōr pāṅkiṉ' kaṇ talivaṉ kūṟutal -
taṉakku naṇparāyiṉārkku iṭar vantiṭattē avarka ḷukku utavutaṟkākap piriyumiṭattut talivaṉ kūṟutal.
taṉ naṇparkku utaviṭap pirintu mīḷum talivaṉ taṉ neñciṟkuk  kūṟuvatāka amintuḷḷa “iruperuvēntar tammuḷ māṟupaṭum pōrkkaḷattē orupaṭikoṇṭu etirttu varum paṭiyip puṟaṅkāṇum veṟṟiccelvamē perumi niliniṉṟatu eṉakkaruti, pūkkōḷiṉiyum ēlātu tavirttuc cellum namatu nili aṟiyātavaḷāyt talivi, kāliyil mēkam kaṭuṅkuralāl muḻaṅkumtōṟum, nammi veṟuttuk kiyaṟṟu oṭuṅkit tuṉpamuṟṟa pacali pāynta mēṉiyōṭu enniliyil uḷḷāḷō?” (akanā. 174) eṉṟa kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 41 nac.)


`āṅkataṉ taṉmiyiṉ vaṉpuṟi'kkaṇ tōḻi kūṟutal -
tōḻi talivaṉi varivu kaṭāyavaḻi,  talivaṉ varintu kōṭal meyyāyiṉamiyiṉ vatuvi muṭiyumaḷavum āṟṟutaṟku vaṟpuṟuttum kūṟṟu; ippoḻutu meyyāṉa vaṟpuṟuttal. (muṉpu poyyāṉa vaṟpuṟuttalumuṇṭu.)
“talivi! talivaṉ varivukkuriya muyaṟcikaḷi mēṟ koṇṭāṉāka, avaṉuṭiya ēvalmakaṉi nōkki, yāṉ ’nalamā?’ eṉṟu viṉava, avaṉ ‘nalamē!’ eṉṟāṉ. avaṉ ney mika ūṟiya kuṟumpūḻ camitta kaṟiyōṭu uṇavu peṟuvāṉāka!” eṉṟu, tōḻi talivaṉuṭiya kuṟṟēval makaṉāl tāṉ aṟinta ceytiyit talivikkuk kūṟutal. (kuṟun. 389)
ivvāṟu talivaṉuṭiya kuṟṟēvalmakaṉāl varivu malinta tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟutalēyaṉṟi, varivumalivu kūṟutalum (kuṟun. 51 naṟ. 22), “talivaṉ varivoṭu varukiṉ ṟami kāṇa vammō!” (naṟ. 235) eṉṟalum, piṟavum ik kūṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkum.	(to. po. 114 nac.)
`āṅkataṉ puṟattup puripaṭa vanta maṟuttal' -
talivaṉ varivu vēṇṭi uṟaviṉari viṭuttavaḻit talivi yiṉ uṟaviṉar tirumaṇattukku maṟuttup piṟaṉ oruvaṉukkut taliviyi maṇam ceytu koṭukka muyalumpōtu talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟutal.
talivaṉ varivu vēṇṭiyavaḻit tamar maṟuttup piṟar varivukku muyala, talivi taṉ kuṭippiṟappum kaṟpum mutaliya uyarccikku ēṟpa, “ataṉi maṟuttut talivaṉ variyumāṟu nī kūṟuvāyāka” eṉṟu tōḻiyiṭam collumiṭattē,
“tōḻi! talivaṉiṭam, notumalar variya muntukiṉṟa taṉik kūṟi ataṟku muṉ avaṉ variviṟku muyalātirukkum pētiyaṉāyiruttalic cuṭṭik kāṭṭu. virivil avaṉit taṉ irumutukuravariṭam ittiruṇattiṟku ēṟpāṭu ceyya muṭukkāviṭiṉ, piṟar varintukoḷḷa muntiviṭuvar ātaliṉ, viriya varivoṭu varumāṟu kūṟu.” eṉṟu collip piṉṉum, “tōḻi! nāṉ talivaṉōṭu oṉṟiyati eṉ neñcu aṟiyum. ippoḻutō vēṟṟuvar vantu variviṟku muntukiṉṟaṉar. āyar kulamakaḷir oru maṇappaṭuvarēyaṉṟi, irumaṇam paṭār. irumaṇampaṭutal  namkaṟpiṟkum kuṭikkum ētam ātaliṉ virivil āvaṉa ceyvāyāka!” eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟutal.	(kali. 114)
ayalār taṉṉi maṇakka muyaṉṟapōtu talivi, “nāṉum āṭukaḷamakaḷ; eṉṉoṭu naṭpuc ceytu akaṉṟa eṉ talivaṉum ōr āṭukaḷamakaṉ. avaṉ eṉ tuṇaṅkik kūt tukkut talikki tantavaṉ. ippōtu avaṉ eṅkuḷ ḷāṉō? palaviṭattut tēṭiyum avaṉik kaṇṭēṉ allēṉ” eṉṟu tōḻikku uṇmiyip pulappaṭutti ayalvaravu nīkkutalum ām. (kuṟun. 31) (to. po. 107 nac.)
`āṅkati iṟivikku avaṉ teḷitturittal' -
tirumaṇamāṉa piṉ mūṉṟunāḷ kūṭṭamiṉmikkuriya kāraṇattit talivaṉ talivikkuk kūṟal.
tirumaṇam muṭinta piṉ mūṉṟunāḷ kūṭṭamiṉṟiyiruntu nāṉkām nāḷ taliviyik kūṭum talivaṉ avaḷiṭam, “uṉṉi mutalnāḷ cantiraṉ, iraṇṭām nāḷ kantaruvar, mūṉṟām nāḷ akkiṉi ivarkaṭku aḷittu, avarkaḷ koṭuppa, yāṉ kōṭal vēṇṭum eṉṟa vētamuṟippaṭi aminta mūṉṟu nāḷkaḷum eṉakku mūṉṟu yukaṅkaḷāyk kaḻintaṉa” (tiruppatik. 400) eṉṟu kūṟutal.
itu ‘varintukōṭal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(mā. aka. 82)
āṅkavaḷ valittal -
talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōy, talivi talivaṉi viṭuttup piṉ toṭarntuvantu uṭaṉpōkkiṉit taṭutta taṉ uṟaviṉaruṭaṉ cērātu, talivaṉ vayattaḷākavē iruttal (pāli naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 93 uri mēṟ.)
`āṅkavaṉ nikaḻa niṉṟavi kaḷiyum karuvi' -
1. payiṉṟataṉmēlallatu cellāta tātu ūtum vaṇṭu, 
2. oruvarāl iḻikkappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ, 3. muliyilum tōḷilum eḻutum toyyiṟkoṭi, 4. kikkoṇṭu mōntu uyirkkum kaḻunīrppū, 5. imikkum kaṇkaḷ, 6. kaṇṭaṟiyāta vaṭivu kaṇṭavaḻi accattāl piṟanta taṭumāṟ ṟam, 7. āṇmakaṉik kaṇṭuḻi maṉattil piṟakkum accam, 8. kāl nilam tōytal, 9. niḻal uṇṭātal, 10. viyarttal ivi mutaliyaṉa talivaṉ taliviyi mutaṟkaṇ etirp paṭṭaviṭattu, “ivaḷ māṉuṭamakaḷō, tēvarulaka makaḷō?” eṉṟu aiyuṟṟa aiyattik kaḷintu, ‘ivaḷ māṉuṭa makaḷē’ eṉṟu uṟuticeyyum karuviyām.	(to. po. 95 nac.)
kūṟi mācuuṇṇutaliyum ilakkaṇaviḷakkam kuṟippiṭum .	(i. vi. 490 uri)
`āṅkāṅku oḻukum oḻukkam' -
kaḷavuk kālattē alla kuṟippaṭṭu iravil taliviyum tōḻiyum varuntum ceyal. itu talivaṟkum uṇṭu. toliviṉiṉṟu iṭiyūṟukaḷiyum poruṭpaṭuttātu iravukkuṟikkaṇ vantu payaṉiṉṟākap pōkum talivaṉum varuntutaṟku vāyppuṇṭu eṉpatu.	(to. po. 134 nac.)
maṉam moḻi mey eṉṉum mūṉṟāṉum kaṟpuṭi makaḷir oḻukum oḻukkattil, talivi maṉattāṉ talivaṉatu nalaṉiyē nāṭi oḻukum oḻukkam.	(132 iḷa.)
ācuram -
‘acuram’ kāṇka.
āci mikal colal -
kāmam mikavum urittal; kaḷaviyaluḷ oruvaḻittaṇattal eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(cāmi. 105)
āṭiṭattu uyttal -
iyaṟkippuṇarcci muṭivil taṉpirivu paṟṟit talivi kavaliyuṟātavāṟu avaḷi amitippaṭutti avaḷit tōḻiyar kūṭṭattil viṭuttut talivaṉ tāṉ pirintu pōtal.
itu ‘pirintu varuku eṉṟal’ eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. 	(na. a. 129 )
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kiḷavi.	(kōvi. 16)
āṭiṭam paṭartal -
talivaṟkuk kuṟiyiṭam kūṟiya tōḻi, taliviyip puṉattiṟ kuc ceṉṟu ūcalāṭi aruvi nīriṟ kuḷittu viḷiyāṭaṟku varumāṟu aḻittut tōḻiyaruṭaṉ viḷiyāṭum iṭam nōkki varutal.
itu ‘pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kiḷavi. 	(kōvi. 117)
āṭūuk kuṇam -
perumiyum uraṉum. aṟivu, niṟi, ōrppu, kaṭippiṭi eṉpaṉa nāṉkum ām. āṭūu-āṇpāl; īṇṭut talimakaṉ. 	(piṅ. 357)
aṟivum āṟṟalum pukaḻum koṭiyum ārāytalum  paṇpum naṇpum paḻipāvam añcutalum mutaliyaṉavāy mēṟpaṭum perumip pakutiyum, kaṭippiṭiyum niṟiyum kalaṅkātu tuṇitalum mutaliya valiyiṉpakutiyum talivaṉ kuṇaṅkaḷ. ivi uḷḷappuṇarcciyoṭu, meyyuṟu puṇarcci nikaḻttātu, taliviyi maṇantu kaṟpaṟam naṭattat talivaṉukku utavum āṭūuk kuṇaṅkaḷām.			(to.po. 98 nac.)
āṟṟutal, pōṟṟutal, paṇpu, aṉpu, aṟivu, ceṟivu, niṟi, muṟi, poṟi eṉpaṉa āṭūuk kuṇaṅkaḷ eṉa neytaṟkali kuṟippiṭum.	(kali. 133)
āṭūumēṉa iyalpu -
perumiyum uraṉum āṇmakaṉ iyalpukaḷām. perumi yāvatu paḻiyum pāvamum añcutal; uraṉ - aṟivu.
ivviyalpukaḷāl talivaṉ taliviyi uḷḷap puṇarcci yoṭu, kaḷavil kūṭak karutātu varintu eytavē muyalum eṉpatu.	(to. po. 95 iḷa.)
aṟivum āṟṟalum pukaḻum koṭiyum ārāytalum paṇpum naṇpum paḻipāvam añcutalum mutaliya perumip pakutiyum, kaṭippiṭiyum niṟiyum kalaṅkātu tuṇitalum mutaliya valiyiṉ pakutiyum āṇmakaṉ iyalpu. itaṉāl uḷḷap puṇarcciyi uṭkoṇṭu talivaṉ virivil taliviyi maṇantukōṭalum, meyyuṟu puṇarcci nikaḻnta vaḻiyum kaḷavu nīṭṭiyātu varintukōṭalum, uḷḷam ceṉṟa vaḻiyellām nekiḻntu ōṭātu etaṉiyum ārāyntu ceytalum, taṉṉuḷḷattu melivi veḷittōṉṟātu maṟittalum, tīviṉic ceyalkaḷil virumpum uḷḷatti mīṭṭu nalvaḻip paṭuttalum talivaṟku uriya eṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum.	(to. po. 98 nac.)
avi perumiyum uraṉum. avi avaṟṟukkuriya paṇpu kaḷiyum āṟṟalkaḷiyum kuṟittu niṉṟaṉa. perumik kuriyaṉa: kalvi, taṟukaṇ, icimi, koṭi, ārāycci, oppuravu, naṭuvunili, kaṇṇōṭṭam mutaliyaṉa. uraṉuk kuriyaṉa: añcāmi, aṟivu, tiṇmi, niṟi, kaṭippiṭi, tuṇivu, ūrāṇmi mutaliyaṉa.	(to. kaḷa. 7 ca.pāla)
āṇpāṟ kiḷavi -
talivaṉ vēṭki mikum āci elli kaṭappa ēkka muṟṟup pulamputal; kāmam mikappeṟṟut talivipaṟṟiya niṉivāl pulampum talivaṉ matiyum malarum pōṉṟa aḻakiya poruḷkaḷip pārttālaṉṟit tāṉ uyyalākāmiyik kūṟutal.
ittuṟi puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālikkaṇ irupāṟ peruntiṇiyuḷ nikaḻvatu.	(pu. ve. mā. 17-7)
āṇpāṟkūṟṟuk kikkiḷit tuṟikaḷ -
kāṭci, aiyam, tuṇivu, uṭkōḷ, payantōrp paḻiccal, nalam pārāṭṭal, nayappuṟṟu iraṅkal, puṇarā irakkam, veḷippaṭa irattal eṉa ivvoṉpatum āṇpāṟ kūṟṟuk kikkiḷi eṉṉum puṟapporuṭ kiḷaviyām. (pu. ve. mā. 14)
itu taliviyatu viruppam uṇarāmalēyē talivaṉ taṉ viruppattiyē aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu ceyyum ceyal paṟṟiyatu ātaliṉ, akattiṇi oḻipākap puṟap poruḷiṟutiyil kūṟappaṭum āṇpāṟkūṟṟuk kikkiḷi eṉappaṭṭatu.
ātaram kūṟal -
taliviyatu uṭalmeṉmi paṟṟik kūṟi uṭaṉpōkki maṟutta talivaṟkut tōḻi, “niṉṉoṭu celkiyil talivikkuk koṭiya pāliyum kuḷircōliyākum; nī pāliyiṉ kaṭumi niṉittuk kalaṅkātu ivaḷi uṭaṉ koṇṭu pōvāyāka” eṉṟu taliviyatu aṉpuniliyik kūṟutal. 	(kōvi. 202)
itaṉip ‘pāṅki talivaṉi uṭaṉpaṭuttal’ eṉṉum tuṟiyākak kūṟupa.	(na. a. 182)
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṟa tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
`āyperuñciṟappiṉ tāy' -
parampari parampariyākat talivikuṭumpattil cevilit tāyāka varukiṉṟāḷ ātaliṉ, taliviyiṉ enta irakaci yattiyum avaḷiṭam veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟalām eṉṟu āyntu tuṇiyappaṭṭa peruñciṟappiṉiyuṭiya cevili yākiya tāy.	(to. po. 124 nac.)
āyattār -
aḵtāvatu tōḻiyar kūṭṭattār. ‘oṇṇutal āyattār ōrāṅkut tiḷippiṉum’ 	(kali. 142 - 6) (ṭu)
taliviyiṉ tōḻiyarām āyattār uṭaṉpiṟantu uṭaṉ vaḷarntu, nīr uṭaṉāṭi, cīr uṭaṉperuki,  oru cērat tālāṭṭappaṭṭu, pāl uṭaṉuṇṭu, orē kālattup pal muḷikkappeṟṟu orē kālattup pēccup pēcak kaṟṟup paḻamiyum muṟikaḷum paṇpum naṇpum viḻuppamum oḻukkamum māṭciyum uṭiyavarāyk kaṇṇum maṉamum kavarum oṇṇutal makaḷirāyp paṟpaṉṉūṟāyiravarāyt taliviyōṭu uṭaṉ viḷiyāṭupavar.	(iṟi. a. 2 uri)
āyattār talivi uṭaṉpōyavaḻik kūṟiyatu -
“māṉ cellum vaḻikaḷ talimayaṅkiya maliyaṭivārattuc ciṟupātikkaṇ, taṉṉuṭiya pañciṉ mellaṭikaḷ paral kuttutalāl vaṭuk koḷḷumāṟu aḵtu āṟṟātu añci añci otuṅki em talivi naṭantu celvāḷ” eṉṟu āyattār talivi uṭaṉpōyakālattuk kūṟumāṟu kāṇka. (to. po.42 nac.)
āyattu uyttal -
talivaṉ taliviyi avaḷuṭiya tōḻiyaruṭaṉ cēru māṟu tāṉ aḻittukkoṭupōy (t taṉṉi avarkaḷ kāṇātavāṟu) viṭuppatu.
iḵtu akapporuḷ viḷakkattuḷ ‘iṭantalippāṭu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kiḷavi.	(na. a. 135)
āyam -
taliviyiṉ tōḻiyar kūṭṭam, ‘āyattār’ kāṇka. ‘āyamum maṟittāḷ eṉ tōḻi’ 	(kali. 44-15)
“āyamakaḷiruḷ niṉṉāl nayakkappaṭṭāḷ yāvaḷ?” eṉṟu viṉaviya tōḻikkut talivaṉ colliyatu -
“tōḻi! eṉṉāl virumpappaṭṭa kuṟumakaḷ, koṟkit tuṟi muttiṉi otta veṇpaṟkaḷ niralāka aminta cev vāyiṉiyum, arattāl piḷakkappaṭṭa aḻakiya vaḷiyal kaḷiyum, ici narampiṉatu olipōṉṟa iṉiya kuraliṉiyu muṭiyavaḷ” eṉṟu talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal. 	(aiṅ. 185)
`āyaveḷḷam vaḻipaṭakkaṇṭu itu māyamō eṉṟal' -
tāṉ kaṇṭu kātalittup puṇarntu iṉpuṟṟa naṅkiyic cūḻa nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa tōḻiyartam kūṭṭam neruṅkik kuṟṟēval ceytu ucāttuṇiyāka iruntu vaḻipaṭutalik kaṇṭa talivaṉ, “ittuṇiyōratu kāvalatu naṭuva ṇuḷḷa ivaḷi nāṉ kūṭi iṉpuṟṟatu kaṉavō, naṉavō?” (tañci. kō. 29) eṉa viyappoṭu niṉittal.
itaṉi ‘arumi aṟital’ eṉṉum tirukkōviyār (17)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, iyaṟkippuṇarcci nikaḻntapiṉ, ‘pirivuḻik kalaṅkal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 133)
āyaṉ talivaṉāy ēṟu taḻuviyami cuṟṟattār kaṇṭu niṉṟu kūṟiyatu -
ēṟu taḻuviṉavaṉukkē peṇkoṭukkum āyar marapiṉi oṭṭit talivaṉ toḻuvattil viṭappaṭṭa kāḷiyit taḻuvi ataṉi aṭakkiṉāṉ. avvūr maṉṟattil āṭum kuravik kūttiṉuḷḷē, kariyaṉum cenniṟa āṭi uṭuttava ṉumākiya avvāyarkulat talivaṉiyum, avaṉ maṇakka irukkum taliviyiyum pārāṭṭip pāṭiṉar; “virivil iruvarukkum tirumaṇam nikaḻvatākuka” eṉṟu uṟaviṉar kūṟiṉar.	(kali. 102)
`āyiṭi avarkaḷ amarntu etirkōṭal' -
tam illattut talivi vantavaḻit talivaṉuṭiya iraṇ ṭām maṉiviyum kāmakkiḻattiyum maṉam virumpit taliviyi etirkoṇṭaḻittu upacarittal. (marutanaṭi yiyal) 	(vī. cō. 95 urimēṟ.)
āyiṭippirivum, cēyiṭippirivum -
āyiṭi - avviṭam; cēyiṭi - cēymittākiya iṭam.
kaḷavuk kālattil talivaṉ iṭṭuppirivu, arumi ceytu ayarttal eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum oruvaḻit taṇattalum (kali. 44 nac.) kaṟpukkālattu ōrūril vēṟṟutteruvilirukkum parat tiyarik karutip piriyum pirivum ‘āyiṭip pirivu’ eṉappaṭum.
kaḷavuk kālattil talivaṉ variviṭi vittup poruḷ-vayiṉ piritalum, kaṟpuk kālattu ōtal kāval tūtu paki poruḷ eṉṉum ivipaṟṟip piritalum, nāṭiṭiyiṭṭum kāṭiṭi yiṭṭum piriyum pirivātaliṉ, ‘cēyiṭip pirivu’ eṉappaṭum.	(ta. ne. vi. 25,24 ka. kā. 43 uri)
āyiḻi, mintaṉum āṟṟāmiyumē vāyilāka varavu etir kōṭal -
putalvaṉi eṭuttukkoṇṭu āṟṟāmiyuṭaṉ vanta talivaṉit talivi pulavi taṇintu ēṟṟukkoḷḷutal.
parattiyari nāṭit taṉtēriṟ puṟappaṭṭa talivaṉ, tēr pōm vaḻiyil teruvil niṉṟa aḻakiya taṉputalvaṉit taḻīi eṭuttukkoṇṭu taliviyiṉpāl vara, avaḷ pulavi tīrntu talivaṉi ēṟkum ceytiyē ikkūṟṟu.	(akanā. 66)
“ūra! pulliyakāmam virumpattakkataṉṟu. veḷḷam paraviṉāṟ pōṉṟa vēṭki tōṉṟiṉum cāṉṟōruḷḷam ciṟiyavarkaḷiṭattē paṭarntu cellalāmā?” (tañci kō. 404) eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivaṉiṭam talivi kūṟi ūṭal tīrtal.
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ, ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uṇartta uṇarā ūṭaliṉ oḻipu. 			(na. a. 207)
ārva nōkkam -
iṭantalippāṭṭiṉkaṇ taliviyatu kuṟippu aṟiyātu avaḷic cārtal kūṭāmiyiṉ, talivaṉ taṉ uḷḷat tuḷḷa viruppiṉit taṉatu pārviyāl veḷiyiṭṭu avaḷ kuṟippiṉi etirnōkkiyiruttal (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal) (vī. cō. 92 uri)
iḵtu ‘uyireṉa viyattal’ (kōvi. 39) eṉpataṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum.
āraṇaṅku -
avvan nilattukkuriya ariya teyvam. itu nilakkarup poruḷkaḷuḷ  mutalāvatākak kūṟappaṭum. kuṟiñcikku murukaṉum, mullikku māyōṉum, marutattukku intira ṉum, neytalukku varuṇaṉum, pālikkuk koṟṟaviyum āraṇaṅkukaḷām. pālikkuc cūriyaṉit teyvamākak kūṟuvar cilar. cūriyaṉ ellā nilattukkum potukkaṭavuḷ eṉṉum vētacceyti paṟṟi, akkarutti viṭuttuk koṟ ṟaviyē pālikku āraṇaṅkākak koḷvar palar.	 (to. po. 5 nac.)
āraṇaṅku ovvoru nilak karupporuḷkaḷilum mutalāva tām. karupporuḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu amik kum uḷḷuṟiuvamattil āraṇaṅki viṭuttu eṉiya karupporuḷkaḷiyē koḷḷa vēṇṭum.	(to. po. 18, 47)
ārā aṉpiṉ avaṉnili kūṟal
talivaṉatu peruṅkātalik kaṇṭa tōḻi, “muṉpel lām nī evvāṟu āṟṟi yiruntāy?” eṉṟu avaṉi viṉavutal.
“taliva! taliviyuṭaṉ nī ettaṉi muṟi muyaṅki iṉpuṟ ṟālum aluppuc calippiṉṟik kaṇaṅkūṭap piriyāmal irukkiṟāyē! ivaḷ ariyaḷāy irunta kaḷavuk kālattē evvāṟu āṟṟi yiruntaṉiyō?” eṉa viṉavutal.
iḵtu ampikāpatik kōviyuḷ ‘ilvāḻkki’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ampikā. 438)
‘ayiri paranta’ kuṟun. 178 eṉṉum ceyyuḷum atu.
āriṭam -
āviyum āṉēṟṟiyum poṟkōṭṭup poṟkuḷam-piṉavāka alaṅkarittu avaṟṟiṭi naṅkiyi niṟuttip poṉ aṇintu takkāṉ oruvaṟku avaḷi “ivi pōla nīrum polintu vāḻka!” eṉṟu nīrvārttuk koṭuppatu; eṇvaki maṇaṅkaḷuḷ ituvum oṉṟu. iruṭikaḷ marapil vanta muṟiyātaliṉ, ippeyarttāyiṟṟu.(to.po. 92 nac.)
āḻi -
kaṇavaṉip pirinta maṉivi iḻikkum kūṭaṟcuḻi; ‘āḻi yāṟ kāṇāmō yām’ (ain.aim. 43) ‘āḻi iḻittal’ kāṇka.
āḻi iḻittal -
talivaṉuṭiya piriviṉāl āṟṟāḷāya talivi avaṉ virivil taṉpāl vantaṇivāṉā eṉpati aṟitaṟku āḻiyiḻittup pārttal eṉṟa paṇṭi vaḻakkam iruntu vantatu. aḵtāvatu avaḷ taṉ kaṇkaḷi mūṭiyavāṟu maṇalil oru vaṭṭam iḻippāḷ; ataṉ irumuṉikaḷum kūṭiviṭṭāl talivaṉ tavaṟātu virivil varuvāṉ eṉṟa nampikkiyil ceyaṟpaṭuvāḷ. ivvāṟu āḻi iḻittal ‘kūṭaliḻittal’ (cīvaka. 1037) eṉavum paṭum. ‘kūṭalukku vaṭṭatti iḻittāḷ’ eṉpatu nac. uri.
āḻi iḻittalāvatu vaṭṭamākak kōṭu kīṟi, ataṉuḷḷē cuḻi cuḻiyākac cuṟṟum cuḻittu iraṇṭiraṇṭu cuḻiyākak kūṭṭip pārkkumpōtu, iraṭṭikkaṇakkāka muṭivu peṟṟāl kūṭuki, oṟṟikkaṇakkāka muṭivu peṟṟāl kūṭātuoḻiki eṉṟa oru kuṟippi maṉattukkoṇṭu kuṟipārttalum ām. ‘āḻi tiruttic cuḻikkaṇakku ōti’ 			(kōvi. 186)
`āṟṟāttaṉmi āṟṟak kūṟal' -
taliviyiṉ pirivāṟṟāmiyit tōḻi talivaṉiṭam mikavum valiyuṟuttik kūṟal.
“eṅkaḷ talivi niṉ pirivāṟṟāmiyāl peritum varunti melintuviṭṭāḷ; iṉiyum nī muṟiyākat tirumaṇam ceytu koḷḷāviṭil, avaḷ uyir vāḻvatē aritāki viṭum” (tañci. kō. 243) eṉpatu pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
‘varuttam kūṟi varivu kaṭātal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇ itaṉit tirukkōviyār aṭakkum (131)
itu kaḷaviyalil ‘varivu kaṭātal’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 166)
āṟṟātu urittal (1) -
talivaṉ, tōḻiyum taliviyum cērntu niṉṟukoṇ ṭirunta iṭatti aṇuki, “nīṅkaḷ eṉakku aruḷāmiyāl eṉṉuyir aḻikiṉṟatu. itaṉi aṟiyuṅkaḷ” eṉṟu taṉ tāṅka muṭiyāta vēṭkiyi avarkaḷukku urittal. (kōvi. 73)
itaṉit ‘talivaṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmi iyampal’ eṉpa. (na. a. 144) itu ‘maṭaṟṟiṟam’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
āṟṟātu urittal (2) -
iravukkuṟi iṟutikkaṇ talivaṉi eytit tōḻi iravuk kuṟiyi maṟuttu varintukoḷḷumāṟu vēṇṭa, “ittakiya iṉpam nukartali viṭuttu virivil maṟṟava rip pōṉṟu maṇantu illaṟam nikaḻttum vakiyil taliviyi eḷiyavaḷākak koṇṭu nī kūṟuvatu ēṉ?” eṉṟu talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam viṉaval.
itaṉi ‘perumakaṉ mayaṅkal’ eṉpatum uṇṭu. (na. a. 158)
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutik-kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 169)
āṟṟātu pulampal -
kaṟpukkālattil talivaṉ, tāṉ poruḷvayiṉ piriyap pōvatit talivikku uṇarttiṉ avaḷ āṟṟāḷākit taṉ celavit taṭuppāḷē eṉṟañcit tōḻikku urittup poruḷvayiṉ piriya, talivi talivaṉpirivu paṟṟi aṟintu, “ittōḻiyākiya koṭiyavaḷ eṉ pirivāṟ ṟāmiyi aṟintiruntum, talivaratu pirivu paṟṟik kūṟuta liṉ, yāṉ yātu kūṟavallēṉ?” eṉṟu āṟṟāmiyāl pulamputal. 	(kōvi. 334)
itu ‘poruḷvayiṉ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.
āṟṟā neñciṉōṭu avaṉ pulattal -
tōḻi kiyuṟi ēṟka maṟuttatik kaṇṭa talivaṉ atip poṟukkāmal tuyaruṟṟuk kūṟutal.
“neñcē! pāṅki eṉ kuṟiyiyum kiyuṟiyiyum ēṟkātu maṟuttamiyāl, nāṉ varunti vāṭuvatu māttiramaṉṟi, intap pacuntaḻiyum kaṇṇiyum kūṭa ivvāṟu vāṭavē tavam ceytirukkiṉṟaṉapōlum” (tañci. kō. 99) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyalil ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokuti kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 144)
āṟṟāmi kūṟal -
kaṟpuk kālattut talivaṉ poruḷvayiṉ piritalik karutit tōḻiyiṭam kūṟa, tōḻi, “talivaṉ pāliyik kaṭantu poruḷ tēṭa niṉintuḷḷāṉ eṉṟu kūṟiya aḷavilēyē taliviyiṉ mārpu poṉ pūttatu. kaṇkaḷ muttukkaḷic corintaṉa. iṉi avaḷip pirintu tēṭum poruḷ yātō?” eṉat talivaṉiṭam taliviyatu pirivāṟṟā miyik kūṟal.
itu ‘poruḷvayiṉ pirivu’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 335)
āṟṟāmi vāyil -
talivaṉ taṉ āṟṟāmiyē vāyilāka, ūṭal koṇṭa taliviyiṭam ceṉṟurittal.
“uṉ puruvavil vaḷiya, uṉ piṟinutal viyarkka, uṉ kariya kaṇkaḷ nīr curakka, eṉṉuṭaṉ kalantu oru vārttikūṭap pēcāta uṉ utaṭukaḷ tuṭikka niṟkum eṉ nāyakiyē! uṉakku ivvaḷavu ciṉam tōṉṟa nāṉ ceyta kuṟṟamtāṉ yātō?” eṉṟu kūṟutal.
iḵtu ampikāpati kōviyil ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(pāṭal. 510.)
āṟṟāmi vāyilākap pukka talivaṉ tāṉ putuppuṉalāṭit tāḻttami kūṟat talivi iṉṉa putuppuṉalē āṭiyatu eṉṉa neruṅkik kūṟiyatu -
putuppuṉalāṭi vantatākak kūṟiya talivaṉiṭam talivi, “ōo! nīr putuppuṉal āṭiṉīrō! appuṉalukkuk karumaṇal, neritta kūntalē. puṉalil ōṭum kayalmīṉkaḷ, kaṇkaḷē; cūṭiya pūkkaḷē, puṉalil aṭittuvarappaṭṭa pūkkaḷ. nāṇa mākiya kariyi aḻitta puṉal atu. pāṇaṉit teppa mākak koṇṭu apputuppuṉalāṭiyataṉiyum apputup puṉal nuṅkaḷit taṉṉiruppiṭattiṟku iḻuttuc ceṉṟa taṉiyum kaṇṭavaruḷar. numatu uḷḷam apputup puṉalil tōyntuviṭṭapaṭiyāl apputuppuṉaliṉiṉṟum nīvir karikāṇātu tavikkiṟīr. appuṉal āṭumpōtu viḻippāka irumiṉ; iṉṟēl, iḷamaṇaluḷ kāl nili taḷarntu akappaṭṭuviṭum” eṉṟu kūṟiyatu. 	(kali. 98.)
āṟṟāmi vāyilākap pukka talivaṉ, teyvamakaḷir poytal ayarvatoru kaṉāk kaṇṭami kūṟi, atu vāyākap paruvam vantiṟuttatu cuṭṭit talivi ūṭal tīrvatu payaṉākak kūṟiyatu -
“aṟattiyum poruḷiyum tārāviṭiṉum, ūṭalāl piritalum, appoḻutē ūṭal tīrntu puṇartalumākiya iṉpattit taruvataṉik kaṉavu vilakkuvatilli. yāṉ kaṇṭa kaṉavu kūṟuvēṉ, kēḷ. 
“imayamaliyiṉ oru pakkattil, viṇṇil paṟakkum aṉṉattiraḷ antik kālattil vantu taṅkiṉāṟpōla, maṇaṟ kuṉṟil nallār cilar (makaḷir)  tammuṭiya āyattā ruṭaṉē mēvi niṟintiruntaṉar. aḻakiya annaṅkiyar accōliyil niṉṟa pūṅkoṭiyi vaḷittup pūṅkot tukkaḷip paṟikkaluṟavē, avaṟṟil paṭintirunta vaṇṭiṉ kūṭṭam uṭiyalāyiṟṟu. aviyellām am makaḷiric cūḻntu moykkavē, annallār avi tammītu paṭiyāta vāṟākap palavāṟu pēṇik koḷvārāyiṉar. pulavi nīṅki, paṇinta taṉ kaṇavaṉuṭiya mālimārpiṉi muyaṅkaluṟ ṟāḷ orutti. orutti pūvēynta kuḷattiṟ pāyntāḷ; orutti ōṭattē pāyntāḷ; orutti vaṇṭukaḷi ōṭṭumiṭam aṟiyātu ki cōrntāḷ. ivvāṟu vaṇṭu moyttaṟku āṟṟāmal, viḷiyāṭṭiṉi yuṭiya ammakaḷir, koṭikaḷ kāṟṟaṭikka āṟṟāmal vaḷintu tammil piṇaṅkiyavi pōla, tammil mayaṅki iṅku maṅkumākak keṭṭōṭiṉar”.
ivvāṟu tāṉ kaṉavu kaṇṭami kūṟi, “iḷavēṉiṟ paruvam vantuṟṟatu; kāmaṉukku viruntiṭutal vēṇṭik kūṭal makaḷirum āṭavarum kāvil viḷiyāṭṭayarum nīṅkā virup pōṭu aṇikaḷip pūṇpar.” eṉṟu cuṭṭit taliviyatu ūṭalit tīrkkalāṉāṉ talivaṉ.	(kali. 92.)
āṟṟāḷ eṉak kavaṉṟa tōḻikku “āṟṟuvēṉ” eṉpatu tōṉṟat talivi kūṟiyatu -
“naṉavil vantu eṉṉuṭaṉ kūṭi iṉpam nalkāta eṉ kātalar, kaṉavil vantu eṉakku iṉpam nalkukiṟār; antak kaṉavuk kāṭciyālē eṉ uyir tarittu niṟkiṟatu” eṉṟu talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
“naṉaveṉṟu collappaṭum antak koṭiya pāvi illi yeṉil, kaṉavil vantu kūṭiya eṉ kātalar eṉṉi viṭṭup piriyamāṭṭār” eṉṟu talivi tāṉ talivaṉik kaṉavil kāṇpatāl āṟṟiyirukkum ceytiyit tōḻikkuk kūṟal.	(kuṟaḷ 1213, 1216)
āṟṟāḷāya talimakaṭkut tōḻi pirivu nayappac colliyatu -
“talivi! nīr niṟinta kaṇkaḷiyuṭiyiyāy nī illattut taṅkiyiruppat taṉittut talivaṉ celvāṉallaṉ. veṇ kaṭampampūviṉ maṇam kamaḻum neṟṟiyi yuṭiya uṉṉi yum aḻittukkoṇṭē talivaṉ celvāṉ” eṉṟu tōḻi talimakaṭkuk kūṟiyatu.	(kuṟun. 22.)
`āṟṟāṉ āki avaḷetir ūṭal' -
parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉ, ūṭiya taliviyip palavāṟu teḷivittu vēṇṭavum, avaḷ pulavi taṇiyā ḷāka, talivaṉ avaḷiṭattē ciṉam koṇṭu varunti yurittal (maruta naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 95 uri mēṟ.)
āṟṟāṉ moḻital -
iṭantalippāṭṭiṉkaṇ talivaṉ pukaḻuri kēṭṭut talivi nāṇik kaṇputittavaḻi, avaḷ kuṟippaṟiyātu ceṉṟu cārtal ākāmiyiṉ, avaḷatu uḷḷakkaruttiṉi aṟitaṟkut taṉ vēṭki mikutiyāl talivaṉ cila kūṟutal.	(kuṟiñcinaṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
itu ‘moḻipeṟa varuntal’ eṉavumpaṭum.	(kōvi. 41)
`āṟṟiṭi uṟumvaḻit talivaṉ kūṟal' -
talivaṉ iravukkuṟiyiṭit tirumpic cellum vaḻiyil tōṉṟum iṭiyūṟu paṟṟit taliviyum tōḻiyum kavaliyuṟṟa vaḻit talivaṉ kūṟal.
“arivi! kollippāvi iḷaveyilil tōṉṟiṉāṟ pōṉṟa niṉ māṇnalam nukarak karuti yāṉ variṉ, ‘vayappuli maḻakaḷiṟṟiriyi vēṭṭiyāṭat tiriyum maraccōli niṟiya āṭukaḷ pōla mēytaliyuṭiya karaṭikkūṭṭam malinta maliccura neṭuveḷiyil nī eṉṉi nayantu varutal eṟṟukku?’ eṉṟu pala pulantu aḻum niṉatu tuyar nīṅka, ammalimutalneṟi uṉ uruveḷit tōṟṟattu oḷi yiṉāl eṉakkup pātukāppākum” (naṟ. 192) eṉṟivvāṟu, talivi talivaṉ varum āṟṟiṉatu arumi kūṟiyataṟku avaṉ kūṟutal.	(to. po. 103 nac.)
āṟṟiṭit talivaṉ taliviyik kiyatu viṉāyc cērntatu -
talivaṉ	:	“naṅkāy! niṉkiyiluḷḷatu yātu?”
talivi	:	“yāṉō iṭikkulappeṇ. eṉ kiyiliruppatu 						pulitti muṭintu koṭutta paṉaṅkurut tiṉ vakirāl 					ceyyappaṭṭa puṭṭil.”
talivaṉ	:	“ippuṭṭilil eṉṉa paṇṭaṅkaḷ uḷa? kāṭṭu.”
talivi	:	“itaṉkaṇ uḷḷaṉa mullip pūkkaḷē.”
talivaṉ 	:	“immullip pūkkaḷit toṭuttu niṉakku aṇivittu niṉṉik kūṭutali viḻikiṉṟēṉ. uṉakku virivil illam cella vēṇṭum eṉpatu karuttāyiṉ, virivil uṉṉi viṭut tiṭuvēṉ. ippoḻutu kūṭṭattiṟku uṭaṉ paṭṭu nil.”	
ivvāṟākat talivaṉ taliviyi vaḻiyil talippeytu kiyiluḷḷa paṇṭatti viṉavuvāṉip pōla avaḷit taṉ vayappaṭuttiyatu. iḻintōr kāmappakutiyākiya peruntiṇiyic cārntatu itu.	(kali. 117)
āṟṟiṭi mukkōṟpakavari viṉātal -
uṭaṉpōkkil ceṉṟuviṭṭa taliviyi mīṭṭu aḻittu varutaṟkākac curattiṭi vanta cevili, aṅku etirppaṭṭa antaṇattuṟaviyariṭam viṉavutal.
“mukkōl koṇṭa muṉivarē! ivvaḻiyil eṉ pētip peṇ orutti cuṭarili vēli ēntiya kāḷiyuṭaṉ cella nīvir kaṇṭatuṇṭō?” (tañci. kō. 341) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa cevili kūṟṟu.
itaṉi ‘vētiyari viṉāval’ eṉṉum tirukkōvi
yār. (243)
itu variviyalil ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 188)
āṟṟiṉatu arumi -
talivaṉ iravukkuṟikkaṇ taliviyi nāṭi iruḷil varum vaḻiyil nikaḻak kūṭum iṭarkaḷ paṟṟiya niṉippu : tāṉ varumvaḻi kaṭumiyāka uḷatu eṉṟu kūṟi iravukkuṟiyi vilakkum  maṉanili talivaṟku nikaḻātu. avaṉ varum vaḻi yiṉatu kaṭumi mutaliyaṉa kūṟi iravukkuṟiyi vilakkum maṉanili taliviyum tōḻiyumākiya ivarkaḷiṭattēyē nikaḻvatu.	(to. po. 136 nac.)
āṟṟuk kālāṭṭiyar -
marutanilap peṇṭir.	(tivā. pak. 41)
āṟṟuvittiruntami cāṟṟal -
poruḷvayiṉ pirintu mīṇṭa talivaṉiṭam tōḻi, avaṉip pirintu mikavum āṟṟāmiyuṟṟa taliviyi mikavum aritiṉ muyaṉṟu tāṉ āṟutal peṟuvittamiyic cāṟṟutal.
“varuntiya taliviyi niṉ ūriyum, maliyiyum, mali yiṉ  varum aruviyiyum, niṉtēr ceṉṟa putuccuvaṭṭi yum, niṉ kāṭukaḷiyum kāṭṭit tuyariṉi āṟṟuvittuk koṇṭiruntēṉ.” (ampikā. 328) eṉṟāṟ pōlat tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 170.)

āṟu iṉṉāmi -
iravukkuṟiyiṭit talivaṉ varum vaḻiyiṭi nikaḻaṟpāla pollāṅkukaḷ kuṟittut talivaṉ orupōtum kavalip paṭa māṭṭāṉ eṉpatu avaṉ ilakkaṇam.	(iṟi. a. 31.)
āṟu pārttuṟṟa accak kiḷavi -
iravukkuṟik kālattuk kaṭantuvarum vaḻiyiṭiyuḷḷa tuṉpaṅkaḷi niṉittup pārttu ataṉāl tāṉ uṟum accattit talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟutal.
“iṭi ōcikku añcātu, kāṭṭāṟukaḷiyum kaṭantu varum nam talivaṉukku iccōlikkaṇ uḷḷa tīṇṭi varuttum teyvaṅkaḷ iravu varukiyil tuṉpam ceyyumō eṉa añcukiṟēṉ” eṉṟu talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘varital vēṭki’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 164.)
āṟu pārttuṟṟa accam kūṟal -
varintu koḷḷāmal kaḷavoḻukkatti nīṭṭittu iravil koṭuntuṉpam viḷiyak kūṭiya kāṭṭuvaḻiyil talivaṉ varuvati niṉintu niṉintu añci naṭuṅkum taliviyiṉ niliyik kūṟit tōḻi talivaṉi virivil varintu koḷḷumāṟu kūṟutal.
vaḻiyiṭi nikaḻum ūṟu paṟṟit tām añcuvataṉit talivaṉiṭam tōḻi kūṟal eṉpatu kiḷavipporuḷ.
itaṉi ‘nilikaṇṭurittal’ eṉpa. (kōvi. 178)
itu kaḷaviyalil ‘varivu kaṭātal’ eṉṉum tokutik-kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 166.)


āṟu vaḻuvutaliṉākiya kuṟṟam kāṭṭal -
talivaṉ iravukkuṟiyil varum vaḻiyiṭi ēṟpaṭum ētaṅkaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟit taliviyum tōḻiyum avaṉi iravukkuṟi varutali vilakkutal.
iṅṅaṉam kūṟutal ‘varital vēṭkiyi’ uṭkoṇṭu kūṟiyatām.	(to. po. 210 nac.)
i
“iḵtu avar tūtāki vantaṭintatu ippoḻutu eṉal”
poruḷvayiṉ pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ kārkālattē vārātatu kaṇṭu tuyaruṟum talivikkut tōḻi,“avar virivil varuvār eṉṟu namakku aṟivuṟukkum tūtākavē ikkārkālam vantatu” eṉṟu kūṟutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na.a. 170)
“iḵtu eṅkiyar kāṇiṉ naṉṟaṉṟu eṉṟal”
taliviyatu ūṭalit taṇivikkat talivaṉ avaḷ cīṟaṭi toḻap pukkapōtu, talivi atit taṭuttu “uṅkaḷatu icceyali eṉ taṅkiyarāṉa parattiyar kāṇa nēriṉ atu nallatākātu; vēṇṭā; itaṉic ceyyaṟka” eṉṟu kūṟutal. eṅkiyar kāṇiṉ naṉṟu eṉṟu eḷḷi uritta lum uṇṭu.
“nī eṉṉaṭikkaṇ paṇitalip parattiyar kaṇṭāl, uṉakkut tīṅku viḷivippar” eṉṟu talivi kūṟal.
“taliva! nī vaṇaṅkuvataṟku uṉmuṭimēl kuvitta kikaḷi yum eṉ mārpit taḻuva vantu eṉ aṭimēl vaṇaṅkiya uṉ kikaḷiyum parattiyar kāṇiṉ niṉakkut tīṅku nēriṭum” eṉṟu talivi kūṟutal.	(ampikā. 495)
ittuṟi kaṟpiyaliṉkaṇ ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. iḵtu uṇartta uṇarā ūṭal.	(na. a. 206)

ikaḻcci niṉintu aḻital -
kaṟpuk kālattut talivaṉ poruḷvayiṉ piriya ataṉi aṟinta talivi, “nam talivar muṉṉellām poruṭ pirivu paṟṟik  kuṟittapōtu viḷiyāṭṭiṟkākak kūṟukiṟār” eṉṟu, avar uṟutiyākap piriyamāṭṭār eṉṟa eṇṇat tōṭu iruntēṉ: āṉāl eṉ maṉanili aṟinta talivar uṇmiyil piritali nēriṭiyāka ivaḷiṭam kūṟiṉ ivaḷ peritum āṟṟāḷ āvāḷ eṉṟu karuti eṉṉiṭam kūṟāmalēyē pirintār” eṉṟu talivaṉuṭiya kūṟṟi ikaḻntu iruntataṉ aṟiyāmiyiṉi niṉintu varuntutal.
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ ‘poruḷvayiṉ pirivu’ eṉum kiḷavik kaṇṇatorukūṟṟu	(kōvi. 340.)
ikaḻntataṟku iraṅkal -
tōḻaṉ tāṉ talivaṉi ikaḻntu pēciyati niṉittu varuntutal.
“it taliviyiṉ eḻilil taṉṉip paṟikoṭuttu eṉ talivaṉ vāṭi varuntutal muṟṟilum iyalpāṉatē (taliviyik kaṇṭu mīṇṭa nāṉ avaṉi ikaḻntu pēciyatu peruntavaṟē?”) (tañci. kō. 53) eṉpatu taliviyik kaṇṭu mīṇṭa tōḻaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum kiḷavik-kaṇṇatorukūṟṟu.	(na. a. 137)
ikuḷik kūṭṭu -
pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam.	(cāmi. 80)
ici tirintu icittal -
colloṭu col toṭarpupaṭum vāypāṭṭāṉ toṭarātu piṟitoru vāypāṭṭāṉ toṭarutal.
“tāriṉaṉāy māliyaṉāyk kaṇṇiyaṉāy” eṉṟu toṭarpu paṭum vāypāṭṭāṉ toṭarātu, ‘tāraṉ māliyaṉ malinta kaṇṇiyaṉ’ (akanā. kaṭavuḷ) eṉṟu varutal pōlvaṉa.	(to. po. 193 iḷa.)
coṟkaḷ tamakku iyaṟkiyāka uḷḷa poruḷukku māṟāka akapporuḷmarapil vēṟuporuḷ uṇarttal.
‘pacali pāyntatāl paḻiya aḻaku keṭṭuviṭṭatu’ eṉpa taṉip ‘pacaliyāṉ uṇappaṭṭup paṇṭinīr oḻintak kāl’ (kali. 15) eṉṟāṟ pōla, uṇṇum āṟṟalillāta pacali eṉṟa niṟavēṟupāṭṭiṉi avvāṟṟal uṭiyatu pōlak kūṟutal pōlvaṉa.	(to. po. 195 nac.)
iciyāmi kūṟi maṟuttal -
tōḻi, cēṭpaṭikkaṇ, taḻiyāṭiyi viṭuttuk kaḻunīr malarik kaṇṇiyākak koṇṭuvanta talivaṉik kaṇṭu taliviyatu nāṇpaṟṟik kūṟi, “yāṅkaḷ vēṅki malarit tavirat teyvattukkuriya, naṟumalarkaḷic cūṭa añcutum; ikkaṇṇi em kulattukkup poruntātu” eṉa maṟuttuk kūṟutal.
itu cēṭpaṭi eṉṉum kiḷavikkaṇṇatorukūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 96)
iṭṭuppirivu -
kaḷavukkālattil tolivāṉa iṭam nōkkip piriyāmal aṇimiyākap pirital. kaṟpiṉuḷ, collātu aṇimi yākap piritaliyum iṭṭuppirivu eṉpa.	(to. po.111 nac.)
talivaṉ taliviyit taṉiyē taṅkavittut tāṉmaṭṭum pirintu pōtal 	(to. po. 109 iḷa.)
kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻumpōtu talivaṉ ciṟitu kālam taṉ ūrukkup pirintu pōtal. iṭṭu - aṇimi. aḵtāvatu itu kuṟukiya kālap pirivu.	(161. kuḻa)
piṟkālattār itaṉi ‘oruvaḻit taṇattal’ eṉpa. (kali. 53 nac.) aṇimiyiṟ ṟalivaṉ piritali iṭṭuppirital eṉpar. 	(kali. 121 uri)
iṭṭuppirivu iraṅkutal : poruḷ -
taliviyatu kūṟṟu nikaḻum oḻukalāṟukaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ 
iḵtu oṉṟu.
‘colliya nukarcci vallē peṟṟa’ piṉṉar, talivaṉ perunayappu urittu ‘iṭam aṇittu’ eṉṟalum piṟavum kūṟit teḷivuṟac ceytu, oruvaḻit taṇattalākap piriyu miṭattē talivi uḷam melintu iraṅkutal. (to. kaḷa. 21 ca. pāla.)
iṭattuyttu nīṅkal -
talivaṉik kuṟiyiṭattu niṟutti vanta tōḻi, taliviyit taṉittu avviṭattukku arukil niṟuttit tāṉ taṉiyē ceṉṟu avaḷukku viruppamāṉa pūkkaḷik koytu varuvatākak kūṟi avviṭattu nīṅkal.
itaṉik ‘kuṟiyiṭattuyttu nīṅkal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.  		(na. a. 149)
itu pakaṟkuṟi eṉṉum tokutikkaṇ oru kūṟṟu. 		(kōvi. 119.)
iṭantali -
iṭantali eṉpatu taliiṭam. aḵtāvatu etirpārāta vakiyil naṇparkaḷip pirintu taṉittu orupoḻilukku vanta talivaṉ, etirpārāvaṇṇam tōḻiyarip pirintu ōriṭattē taṉittu niṉṟukoṇṭirunta taliviyi viti vacattāl cantittu aṉpu koṇṭu kūṭiya ammutalnāḷ kūṭṭam nikaḻnta iṭam.	(to.po. 498. pē)
iṭantalippāṭṭil, talivaṉ varuvāṉkol eṉṟa accamum vārāṉkol eṉṟa kātalum kūrnta talivi kūṟutal -
“eṇṇiyavāṟē nukarcci peṟuka eṉa veṟpiṉkaṇ eṉṉi aruḷi, ‘niṉṉiṟ piriyēṉ piriyiṉ uyir vāḻēṉ’ eṉavum avalattoṭu maṉam aḻinturittu, ‘ematiṭam mikavum aṇittēyuḷatu’ eṉavum kūṟiya talivaṉiṉ moḻi yit tēṟi, ataṉāl pirintu vāḻtalip poruntiya eṉatu taṉimiyi nōkkit talivaṉ kavaliyuṟuvāṉō eṉavum, varuvāṉō eṉavum eṉṉuḷḷattē taḷarcciyuṟukiṉṟēṉ. yāṉ eṇṇiya nukarcci eytāta tākumō” eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivi kūṟutal.
(‘eṇṇiyatu iyiyā tāṅkol’ eṉat toṭaṅkum paḻampāṭal ikkūṟṟuppaṭa nikaḻkiṟatu.)	(to. po. 102 nac.)
iṭantalippāṭu -
iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarnta talivaṉ taliviyi maṟunāḷ avviṭattilēyē cantittal.
ivviṭantalippāṭu iruvakippaṭum. pāṅkaṉ vāyilākat taliviyirukkum iṭam terintu vantu avviṭattil cantittal; pāṅkaṉiṭam kūṟāmalē, mutal nāḷ kūṭṭuvitta viti maṟunāḷum kūṭṭuvikkum eṉṟa nampikkiyil talivaṉ tāṉē vantu taliviyic cantittal - eṉpaṉa. iṭantalippāṭṭiṉkaṇ ivviraṇṭaṉuḷ oṉṟē nikaḻum eṉpar cilar. talivaṉ tāṉē iraṇṭāmnāḷ cantippataṉiyē iṭantalippāṭu eṉak koṇṭu, pāṅkaṉ vāyilākac cantippataṉiyē mūṉṟām nāḷ cantippākiya pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉpar. iṭam talippāṭu - iyaṟkip puṇarcci puṇarntu ceṉṟa talivaṉ aṭutta nāḷ avviṭattilēyē vantu taliviyik kūṭutal.  talippāṭu - kūṭutal.	(iṟi. a. 3; na.a. 124)
iṭantalippāṭu - mūṉṟuvaki -
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ āṟām tokuti. itaṉ mūvakiyāvaṉa : (1) teyvam teḷital - muṉṉē taṉakkut taliviyik kūṭṭuvitta nalviṉi maṟupaṭiyum kūṭṭuvikkum eṉṟa teḷivuṭaṉ talivaṉ taliviyi mutalnāḷ kaṇṭa iṭattiṟkē cēṟal. 
2) kūṭal - talivaṉ taliviyik kūṭutal.
3) viṭuttal - talivaṉ taliviyit tōḻiyar kūṭṭat tiṭiyē cellumāṟu aṉupputal eṉpaṉa.	(na. a. 134)
iṭappuṟattu akaṟṟal -
iravukkuṟikkaṇ tōḻi taliviyi iravukkuṟiyiṭattu arukil taṉiyē viṭuttuc cēṟal. (iḵtu ‘avaṭ koṇṭu cēṟalum kuṟi uyttu akaṟalum’ (na. a. 158) eṉavum kūṟap paṭum.) (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
iṭam aṇittu eṉṟal -
‘eṉatu iruppiṭam ivviṭattiṟku mika arukilēyē uḷḷatu’ eṉṟu talivaṉ iyaṟkippuṇarcciyiṉ iṟuti yil talivikkuk kūṟutal.
“nāṉ pirintu neṭuntolivu celvēṉ eṉa nī kavalal vēṇṭā; itō, mika arukil tāṉ uḷḷatu eṉatūr” eṉṟu valiyuṟuttum talivaṉ, “eṅkaḷ ūriṉ veṇ māḷikikaḷatu oḷi paṭivatāl nuṅkaḷ ūrk karuṅkuṉṟam veṇṇiṟac caṭṭi aṇivatu pōlak kāṭciyaḷikkum attuṇi aṇimiyil emmūr uḷatu!” (kōvi. 15) eṉṟu kūṟutal pōlvaṉa.
itaṉi ‘iṭam aṇittuk kūṟi vaṟpuṟuttal’ eṉṟum kūṟuvar. 	(kōvi. 15)
iḵtu ‘iyaṟkippuṇarcci’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟām.	(na. a. 129)
iṭam aṇittuk kūṟi vaṟpuṟuttal -
iyaṟkip puṇarcci iṟutiyil talivaṉ taṉ pirivu kuṟittu varuntiya taliviyit teḷivittut taṅkaḷ iruvaruṭiya iruppiṭamum aṇimiyil uḷḷaṉavēyātaliṉ, tavaṟā mal cantittal vāykkum eṉṟu kūṟit taṉ piriviṟku uṭaṉ paṭumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttal.
iḵtu iṭamaṇit teṉṟal eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. (na. a. 129) atu kāṇka.
iḵtu iyaṟkippuṇarccikkaṇṇatu oru kūṟṟām. 			(kōvi. 15)
iṭam peṟṟu aṇital - 
iṭam peṟṟut taḻāal.	(cāmi. 87)
iṭam peṟṟut taḻāal - 
cantaruppam kaṇṭu taliviyit talivaṉ taḻuval.
“ivaḷ kaṇkaḷ tanta aruṭkuṟippuṇṭu; oruvarumillāta taṉiyiṭamāṉa ikkuḷirpūñcōli yuṇṭu; ivaḷi iṅkē nāṉ taḻuvuvēṉ” eṉa niṉikkum talivaṉatu kūṟṟu. 		(maturikkala. 39)
itu ‘taliviyiṉ eytum iyaṟkippuṇarcci’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 127)
iṭaṉ -
iḵtu akapporuḷ uri irupattēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu. 
(vī.cō. 96) iṭaṉāvatu akiṭaṉum puṟiṭaṉum eṉa iruvakippaṭum. aka iṭaṉāvatu maṉiyum vaḷākamum paṟṟi varuvatu; puṟiṭaṉāvatu maliyum paḻaṉamum cōliyum kaṭalum neṟiyum āṟum paṟṟi varuvatu. ‘iṭam’ kāṇka. iṭaṉ - cantaruppam (to. po. 513 pē.)			(vī. cō. 96 uri)
iṭittu vari niṟuttal -
maṉattup paṭumāṟu kaṭuñcoṟkaḷāl kaḻaṟiyurittu nēriya vāḻkkineṟi eṉṉum ellikkaṇ niṟuttutal. ic ceyalit talivaṉ talivi eṉṉum iruvariṭiyēyum aṟivar eṉṟa vāyilōr ceyvar.
taliva! iṉṟu uṭuttum toṭuttum pūṇṭum cerukiyum makaḷir palarik kūṭṭamākak koṇṭu viḻāc ceyyum aḷaviṟku nī peṟṟuṭiya peruñcelvam, orē pacuvik koṇṭu eḷiya vāḻkki naṭattiya niṉ illattiṟku, ittalivi vantapiṉṉarē viḷintatu eṉpati niṉittup pārttu ataṟku ēṟpa naṭantukoḷka” (kuṟun. 295) eṉṟāṟ pōla, aṟivar talivaṟkuk kūṟi avaṉi neṟippaṭuttal.			(tol. po. 155 nac.)
iṭiccurattu oḻiyak karutiya neñciṟkut talivaṉ colliyatu -
“neñcē! vaṇṭukaḷ ceṟinta kūntaliyum parutta meṉ tōḷkaḷiyum uṭiya eṉ talivi vikum, terukkaḷ palavuṭiya kuṉṟam, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattiyuṭiya nalla cōḻanāṭṭiṉkaṇ uḷḷatu. cellutaṟkariya kāṭṭiṉik kaṭantu ceṉṟu eṭutta ceyali veṟṟiyoṭu muṟṟuvik kum āṟṟaliṉṟip piṉṉē niṉṟu mīṇṭu cellak karutu vāyāyiṉ, eṉṉuṭiya inniliyiṉi eṉ taliviyiṭam ceṉṟu urippāyāka!” (akanā.  181) eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivaṉ iṭiccurattiṉkaṇ neñciṟkuk kūṟutal.
iṭiccurattut talivaṉ talivikuṇam niṉittaliṉ taṉkaṇ uṟṟa vemmi nīṅkiyatu kaṇṭu colliyatu  -
“mūṅkil piḷakkumāṟu mutuvēṉiliṉ veppam mika ñāyiṟu malikaḷum piḷakkumāṟu kāytalāl muṉpu veppamāka irunta pakutikaḷ, ippoḻutu taliviyiṉ kuṇaṅkaḷi yāṉ niṉikkuntōṟum mikak kuḷircciyuṭiyavākat tōṟṟam aḷikkiṉṟaṉa” eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟal.	(aiṅ. 322)
iṭiccurattut talivaṉ taliviyip pukaḻa, avaḷ nāṇik kaṇputittavaḻic colliyatu -
“uyarnta karikaḷiyuṭiya kāṭṭāṟṟiṉ maṇal mikka akaṉṟa tuṟiyilē vēṉiṟkālattiṟ pūkkum pātirimalar kaḷik kuvittu māli toṭuttukkoṇṭirukkum, maṭam eṉṉum paṇpu cāṉṟa makaḷē! nī nāṇik kaṇkaḷip pottik koṇṭirukkiṟāy. niṉ kaṇkaḷiviṭa niṉ nakilkaḷ eṉṉi varuttukiṉṟaṉa! avaṟṟiṉum niṉparutta meṉtōḷkaḷ eṉṉit tuṉpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa!” eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal.	(aiṅ. 361)
