`iṭiccura maruṅkiṉ avaḷtamar eytik, kaṭikkoṇṭu peyartaliṉ kalaṅkañar eytik, kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi yuḷappaṭa, appāṟpaṭṭa oru tiṟattāṉ' talivaṉ kūṟal -
talivaṉ taliviyi uṭaṉkoṇṭu cellumpōtu pālinilattu naṭuvaḻiyil taliviyiṉuṭiya tantiyum taṉṉiyarum piṉ ceṉṟu poruntit taliviyi aḻittuc cella muyalumpōtu, talivi mikavum varuntit taṉ uṟaviṉarpakkam cērntu uriyāṭātu talivaṉ pakkattilēyē niṟṟaliṉ, talivi kaṟpoṭu kūṭiyiruppati avaḷuṭiya cuṟṟattārum appāli nilittiṟ kaṇṭava rum uṇarnta kaṟpu veḷippāṭu uḷappaṭat talivaṉ taliviyi uṭaṉkoṇṭu cellum pakutikkaṇ kūṟṟu nikaḻttutal.	(to. po. 41 nac.)
‘tamaruṭaṉ celpavaḷ avaṉpuṟam nōkkik kavaṉṟu araṟṟal’ eṉa, tamar mīṭṭuc cellum aḷavil talivi talivaṉip piritali eṇṇik kavaliyuṟṟu araṟṟutali, ‘uṭaṉpōk kiṭiyīṭu’ eṉṉum variviṟkuriya tokutik- kaṇ amintatoru kūṟṟāka akapporuḷ viḷakkam cuṭṭum.	(na. a. 198)

“iyantirappāvi pōla naṭantu nummaṉi elli kaṭantu vantuḷḷāy. ikkāṉattē parantu tōṉṟum cevviya intirakōpap pūccikaḷik kaṇṭukoṇṭu caṟṟē viḷiyāṭi makiḻntukoṇṭiru; yāṉ āṇṭut tōṉ ṟum parutta aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya vēṅkimaram niṟkum maṇal ekkaril avvaṭimarap puṟattē maṟintu niṟpēṉ; pōr varumāyiṉ añcātu etirttu māṟṟārip puṟaṅkāṭṭi yōṭac ceyvēṉ; āṇṭu numar varuvarēl pōriṭātu maṟivēṉ” eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟuvaṉa.	(naṟ. 362)
“iṭiyaṟavupaṭāta kaṟpiṉ kūṭṭamē iṉpap payaṉuṭittu” eṉṟukūṟi varivukaṭāviya tōḻikkut talivaṉ kūṟuvatu -
“varintukoṇṭu iṭiyaṟāta iṉpatti eytiyavar, ūṭalum ataṉi aḷavaṟintu nīṅkalum piṉṉark kūṭutalum ākiya ivaṟṟiyē peṟṟaṉar; irutalippuḷḷup pōla, īruṭalum ōruyirum ākiya iruvēmukkum attakiya iṉpam vēṇṭā; yāṅkaḷ eppoḻutumē kūṭip piriyā mal iṉpuṟuvōm” eṉṟu talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.		(kuṟaḷ 1109)
“iṭiyīṭiṉṟi niṉiyātē, ciṟitu maṟantiru” eṉṟa tōḻikkut talivi kūṟal -
“tōḻī! eṉkātalari iṉṟu yāṉ maṟantāl mēlum eṉ aḻaku keṭṭu eṉvaḷikaḷum kaḻaṉṟuviṭum. annili nērāmal kāppatu avaratu niṉippē. avari yām yāṅkaṉam maṟattal kūṭum?” eṉṟu talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟiyatu. (kuṟaḷ 1262)
iṭiyūṟu kiḷattal -
taṉakku nērum tuyarattit talivaṉ talivikkuk kūṟutal.
‘nāṇattāl nī kaṇkaḷi māttiramē putittu maṟittuk koḷkiṟāy; āyiṉ niṉ maṟṟa uṟuppukkaḷ eṉṉi varuttuvati yāṅkaṉam taṭuttal iyiyum?’ eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu ‘taliviyiṉ eytum iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
‘kaṇputikka varuntal’ eṉpatu tirukkōviyāruḷ nikaḻum kiḷavi (43). itaṉic cāminātam ‘iṭiyūṟu collal’ eṉṟu collum. (cāmi. 87) (na. a. 127)
iṭi viṉātal -
iyaṟkippuṇarcci iṭantalippāṭu pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam ivaṟṟāl taliviyik kūṭiya talivaṉ, iṉit talivi yip pāṅki vāyilākak kūṭutalē takkatu eṉṟa eṇṇat tuṭaṉ avaḷ aṟivit taṉpakkal īrppataṟkut taḻiyum kaṇṇiyum kiyuṟiyāka ēnti vantu, avaḷuṭiya ūr peyar mutaliyavaṟṟi viṉaviya piṟaku, “uṉakku iṭi iruk kiṟatā, illiyā eṉṟa eṉ aiyattip pōkkuvāyāka” eṉṟāṟ pōla viṉavutal.
itu pāṅkimati uṭaṉpāṭu eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu. ‘oḻintavum viṉāvuḻi’ eṉpataṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum. 	(na. a. 140 uri, i. vi. 507 uri)
intiraṉ -
intiraṉ āvāṉ tēvarkaḷukku aracaṉ; maruta nilak kaṭavuḷ. intiraṉit tolkāppiyar ‘vēntaṉ’ eṉpār.
vayalum vayal cārnta iṭamumākiya marutanilattu vāḻum uḻavar mutaliyōr ūṭalum kūṭalumākiya kāmacciṟappu nikaḻtaṟku marutanilattukkut teyvamāka ‘āṭalum pāṭalum ūṭalum uṇarttalum’ uḷḷiṭṭa iṉpaviḷiyāṭṭu iṉitu nukarum imiyōrkkum iṉkural eḻilikkum iṟivaṉākiya intiraṉi nōkki viḻācceytu aḻitta liṉ marutanila makkaḷukku avaṉaruḷ veḷippaṭum.	
intiraṉ maḻivaḷaṉum āṟṟuvaḷaṉum tarum. 	(to. po. 5 nac.)
iyalpuri -
iṉṉa iṭattu iṉṉa ceyal nikaḻntatu eṉa oruceyali atu nikaḻnta cantarppattiyum kūṭṭiyurippatu iyal puriyām. 27 akapporuḷ urikaḷuḷ iyalpuri oṉṟu. 	(vī. cō. 90 uri)
iyalvaḷi muṟṟutal -
kaṟpukkālattē taliviyip pirintu ōtal mutaliya karutic ceṉṟa talivaṉ maṉam taḷarumāṟu vāṭikkāṟṟuk kaṭumiyāka vīcutal (mulli naṭiyiyal)		(vī. cō. 94 uri mēṟ.)
iyaṟki aṉpu -
kāraṇamiṉṟit tōṉṟum aṉpām. talivaṉ taliviyik kaṇṇuṟṟapōtē, “ivaḷi maṉikkiḻattiyākak kōṭal vēṇṭum” eṉṟu karututaṟkuk kāraṇamāka vitivayattāl tōṉṟum aṉpu.	(iṟi. a. 2 uri)
iyaṟkiyaṉpu vaṭivupaṟṟiyallatu tōṉṟātu. 	(to. po. 273 pērā.)
iyaṟki iṉpam -
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyākiya iṉpam 	(cīvaka. 2063)
iyaṟki nilam -
mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal ākiya kāṭu mali vayal kaṭaṟkari eṉṉum nālvaki nilaṉkaḷ.	(to. po. 4 nac.)
iyaṟkippuṇarcci -
ūḻ vayattāl talivaṉ taliviyi etirppaṭa avviruva rum maṉam iyintu tāmē kūṭutal iyaṟkip puṇarcci. itu kaḷaviyaluḷ mutaṟ kiḷavi.(na. a. 123)
iyaṟkippuṇarcciyil taliviyiṉ eytum puṇarccivaki nāṉkāvaṉa -
(1) vēṭkiyuṇarttal - talivaṉ taṉ vēṭkiyit talivikku uṇarttal.
(2) maṟuttal - talivi nāṇattāl maṟuttal.
(3) uṭaṉpaṭal - talivi kūṭṭattiṟku icital
(4) kūṭal - taliviyit talivaṉ kūṭutal - eṉpaṉa. 	(na.a. 126)
iyaṟkippuṇarcciyiṉ eḻuvaki -
nayappu, pirivuṇarttal, iṭam aṇitteṉṟal, eytaṟ karumi, uyireṉa viyattal, pāṅkiyi uṇartal, pāṅkaṉi niṉital- eṉa iyaṟkippuṇarcci eḻuvakippaṭum. itaṉiyē tamiḻneṟiviḷakkam nayappu, teruṭṭal, pirivac cam, vaṉpoṟi, eytutal arumi, uyireṉak kūṟal, āṟṟiṉaṉ peyartal - eṉa ēḻuvakittākak kūṟum.			(ka.kā. 25, ta.ne. vi. 15)
iyaṟkippuṇarcciyiṉ kiḷavikaḷ -
kāṭci, aiyam, teḷital, nayappu, uṭkōḷ, teyvatti makiḻtal, puṇarcci tuṇital, kalavi urittal, iruvayiṉ ottal, kiḷavi vēṭṭal, nalam puṉinturittal, pirivu uṇarttal, paruvaral aṟital, aruṭkuṇam urittal, iṭam aṇittuk kūṟi vaṟpuṟuttal, āṭiṭattu uyttal, arumiyaṟital, pāṅkiyi aṟital - eṉṟa patiṉeṭṭum tirukkōviyāruḷ iyaṟkippuṇarcci eṉṉum kiḷa vikkaṇ amiyum kūṟṟukkaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ kikkiḷiyic cārnta mutal ēḻkūṟṟukkaḷum kūṭa, iyaṟkippuṇarccikku upakārappaṭutalāṉ iyaṟkippuṇarccikkaṇṇēyē koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.(kōvi. cū. 1)
iyaṟkippuṇarcci vaki -
muyaṟciyiṉṟi muṭiyum teyvattāṉiyaṉṟa iyaṟkip puṇarcci eṉavum, talivaṉatu muyaṟciyāṉ muṭiyum iyaṟkip puṇarcci eṉavum iyaṟkippuṇarcci iru vakittu.	(na. a. 32)
iyaṟkippoḻutu -
mullikku vakukkappaṭṭa kārum māliyum, kuṟiñcikku vakukkappaṭṭa kūtirum muṉpaṉiyum iṭiyāmamum, marutat tiṟku vakukkappaṭṭa āṇṭu muḻumiyum  vikaṟiyum viṭiyalum, neytalukku vakukkappaṭṭa āṇṭu muḻumiyum eṟpāṭākiya piṟpakalum, pālikku vakukkappaṭṭa vēṉi lum piṉpaṉiyum naṇpakalum eṉa ivi iyaṟkip poḻutukaḷām.	(to. po. 4, 6 - 10 nac.)
iyaṟpaṭa moḻital -
parattiyariṭam pirinta talivaṉit tōḻi mutaliya vāyilōr iyaṟpaḻittalip poṟāta talivi, “yāṉ uṟaṅkumpōtu kaṉavil eṉ mārpiṉit taḻuvi makiḻum emperumāṉi nīṅkaḷ kuṟi kūṟuvatu takātu” eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivaṉi iyaṟpaṭat kūṟutal. 
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 378.)
iyaṟpaḻittal -
talivaṉ taliviyi varintu koḷḷāmal kālam kaṭattum koṭumiyic cuṭṭit tōḻi avaṉuṭiya iyalpiṉip paḻittuk kūṟutal. ‘pāṅki iṟivaṉip paḻittal’ eṉpatu kūṟṟu. (avaḷ avvāṟu avaṉip paḻittavaḻi, atu poṟātu talivi iyaṟpaṭa moḻivāḷ.)
“oḷimikka veṇṇakiyāy! iṟāmīṉkaḷi orucēra uṇṇum nārikaḷ taṅkum paciya kaṭaṟkaric cōliyil tēmalarkaḷiyuṭiya puṉṉimara niḻalil nammi virumpi vantavar talivar. avar vanta ac cevviyi annārikaḷ maṟakkamāṭṭā. nammi maṟanta avar pōla vaṉṉeñcuṭi yār oruvarum irār!” (ampikā. 243)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘varitalvēṭki’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 164; i. vi. akat. 149)

iyipu eṭutturittal -
talivi talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōyavaḻit taliviyic cevili tēṭic cellukiyil avvaḻic celvōri “pēraḻakiyuṭaṉ kāḷi oruvaṉ ceṉṟatik kaṇṭīrō?” eṉṟu viṉāva, etirvaruvōr taliviyatu iyalpiṉiyum talivaṉuṭiya āṇmiyiṉiyum eṭuttukkūṟi, avarkaḷ oru tuṉpamum iṉṟi iyintu ceṉṟati eṭuttuk kūṟic ceviliyi āṟṟuvittal. iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (kōvi. 246)
irakkattoṭu maṟuttal -
talivaṉ oru kiyil villum oru kiyil taḻiyāṭiyum ēnti niṟṟalik kaṇṭa tōḻi avaṉip pārttu, uṭal naṭuṅkak kiyil taḻiyāṭiyoṭu villiyum ēntip pittēṟiyavaṉip pōlak kāṇappaṭum avaṉatu nili irakkappaṭutaṟku uriyatu eṉṟu collaḷavoṭu, karuṇi kāṭṭik kiyuṟi peṟātu viṭuttal.
itaṉit ‘tōḻi kiḻavōṉ tuyarnili kiḷattal’ eṉavum koḷvar. 	(na. a. 148)
itu cēṭpaṭi eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 102)
irakkam -
piṟar tuṉpam kaṇṭu varuntum avalam eṉṉum meyppāṭu. karuṇi eṉiṉum okkum. iḵtu akapporuḷ neytal tiṇikkuriya uripporuḷum ākum. kuṟitta paruvattut talivaṉ mīṇṭu taliviyi aṇiyākkāl, avaḷ uḷḷam eytum mikka varuttanili itu. ‘iraṅkal’ kāṇka. 	(to. po. 226 nac.)
irakkam kūṟi varivu kaṭātal -
kaḷavu virumpi varivu uṭaṉpaṭātu iravukkuṟiyi nīṭṭik kak karutiya talivaṉukkut tōḻi, “nī cellum neṟikkaṇ iraviṭi niṉakku iṭiyūṟu uṇṭākum eṉṉum accat tāl talivi aḻutu varuntukiṉṟāḷ. avaḷ āṟutal peṟa nī niṉṉūr aṭintatum caṅkiṉi ūti niṉkuṟi kāṭṭuvāy” eṉṟu taliviyatu irakkam paṟṟik kūṟi varivu kaṭāvutal.	(kōvi. 170)
iḵtu iravukkuṟi eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
irakkamuṟṟu varivu kaṭātal -
pakaṟkuṟiyil vēṅki pūttut tiṉimutirvurittut tiṉiyi aṟukkacceytu tiṉikkāvalip pōkkiya taṉi urittu varivu kaṭāva muṟpaṭṭa tōḻi talivaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat taliviyiṭam urippōḷ pōṉṟu, “yām avaṉi etirppaṭalām eṉṟu makiḻvoṭu vaḷartta tiṉittiraḷ koyyappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa” eṉṟu talivaṉ pāl irakkam kāṭṭupavaḷip pōla varivu kaṭātal. itu pakaṟkuṟikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (kōvi. 142)
iraṅkal -
talivaṉ kaḷavilō kaṟpilō kuṟitta kālattut taṉṉik kāṇa vārātaviṭattu ēṟpaṭṭa varuttattāṉ talivi kaṭalum kāṉalum kaḻiyum kāṇṭoṟum iraṅkalum, poḻutum puṇartuṇippuḷḷum kaṇṭu iraṅkalum, talivaṉ etirppaṭṭu nīṅkiyavaḻi iraṅkalum pōlvaṉa iraṅkalām. itu neytal eṉṉum tiṇikku uripporuḷ ākiya oḻukkamām. itu kaḷaviṟkuc ciṟantatu. 	(to. po. 14 nac.)
iraṅkal nimittam -
talivi varuntum kaṭaṟkariyum, talivaṉ taliviyi nīṅkalum pōlvaṉa taliviyiṉ iraṅkaṟkuc cūḻniliyum kāraṇamum ām.	(to. po. 14 nac)
iraṅki moḻital -
pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭattil talivaṉukkuk kuṟi muṭippatāka ēṟṟuk koṇṭa tōḻi, talivaṉuṭiya tuyaraniliyit talivikku eṭuttukkūṟi, avaḷik kuṟinayakkumāṟu vēṇṭutal. (itu ‘tōḻi kiḻavōṉ tuyarnili kiḷattal’ na. a. 148 eṉavumpaṭum.) (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal)			(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
irattalum teḷittalum -
parattiyariṭattup pirintu mīṇṭu vanta talivaṉ taliviyi eyti avaḷkūṭṭatti vēṇṭi niṟkiyil, avaḷ avaṉuṭiya tavaṟāṉa oḻukkam karuti avaṉiṭam kōpam koṇṭu vilaki niṉṟaviṭattē, taṉṉiṭam kōpam koḷḷāmal taṉakku avaḷ aruḷceytu iṉmukam kāṭṭit taṉṉoṭu kūṭutal vēṇṭumeṉat talivaṉ taliviyip paṇivoṭu vēṇṭutalum, talivi karutuvatu pōṉṟu taṉṉiṭattē tavaṟu etuvumiṉṟu eṉpatit taṉ pēcciṉālum uṟutimoḻiyālum avaḷ maṉattē patiyumāṟu ceytalum. (kali. 88)	(to. po. 41 nac.)
`irantu kuṟipeṟātu varuntiya kiḻavōṉ maṭalē poruḷeṉa matittal' -
pāṅkiyi irantum kuṟi niṟivuṟāmal varuntiya talivaṉ maṭalēṟuvatē iṉit taṉkuṟi niṟivēṟaṟku vaḻiyeṉat tuṇital.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 145)
irantu kuṟiyuṟṟup piṉ niṉṟa talivaṉ āṟṟāṉāyt taliviyi nōkki, “iṅṅaṉam varuttuviyāyiṉ nī ceytavam iṉṟām” eṉak kūṟiyatu -
“taliviyē! niṉ kūntal aḻakik kaṇṭa aḷavil eṉ paṇpukaḷākiya aṟivu, niṟi, ōrppu, kaṭippiṭi eṉpaṉa eṉṉiviṭṭup pōmpaṭi ceytu eṉṉi nīṅkic cel kiṟāy. eṉ nōy paṟṟi nī kavaliyuṟāmaṟ ceṉṟāl nī tinnīrāṭiya nalviṉiyum, nōṉpākiya viḷiyāṭṭi mēṟkoṇṭu piṟarmaṉikkaṇ aiyamēṟṟup pāṭi āṇṭup peṟṟavaṟṟip piṟarkkuk koṭutta nalviṉippayaṉum, kuḻamakaṉukkut tirumaṇam ceytu nōṟṟa nōṉpiṉ payaṉum, niṉakkuk kiṭṭāmal pōyviṭum. ātaliṉ eṉmāṭṭu aruḷceyka” (kalit. 59) eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivaṉ kūṟutal.
irantu kuṟiyuṟutal -
irattal - kuṟiyuṭiyār ceyyum ceyki ceytu oḻukuvatu.
kuṟiyuṟutal - ‘ivaṉ ikkuṟiyi iṉṟiyamiyātavaṉ’ eṉpatu paṭa uriyāṭuvatu.
talivaṉ pāṅkaṉālāvatu taṉiyē etirppaṭṭāvatu taliviyi iraṇṭāmnāḷ kūṭiyapiṉ, iṉi ikkūṭṭam tōḻiyiṉ utaviyiṉṟi nikaḻātu eṉṉum karuttāṉ, iḻintārkkē yuriya “ikkaṇṇi nalla, ittaḻi nalla” eṉṟāṟ pōlak kiyuṟiyip pārāṭṭi niṟkum coṟka ḷōṭu, taliviyiṭam irattalum kuṟiyuṟutalum ceytu niṟṟal.	(iṟi. a. 5)
irantu piṉnili niṟṟal - 
talivaṉ taliviyiṭam taṉkuṟiyiṉik kūṟit tāḻntu 
niṟpatu .
”peṇṇē! uṉ kaṇṇāl eṉṉi nōyuṟac ceytu eṉ uyirukkē ūṟu viḷivittu viṭṭu nī vāḷā iruppatu takumā? ” (tañci. kō. 6 ) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu taliviyiṉ eytum iyaṟkip puṇarcci eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 127)
irantu piṉ niṟṟaṟku eṇṇal - 
taliviyiṭam taṉkuṟiyik kūṟi irakkalām eṉṟu talivaṉ niṉital.
“maṉamē!avaḷāl nām uṟṟu varuntum ikkāmanōykku avaḷē maruntāvataṉṟi vēṟu vaḻiyilli; ākavē, tāḻmi karutāmal avaḷiyē ceṉṟu irappōm” (kuṇanāṟpatu) eṉat talivaṉ eṇṇutal.	(na. a. 127)
iraviṭit tammaṉip pukka talivaṉi aṉṉi viruntāka ēṟṟukkoṇṭataṉit talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟal -
talivikkuriya kiḷavikaḷuḷ ituvum oṉṟu.
“ematu kōtiyi aṟuttup pantu koṇṭōṭi nōtakka ceyyum ciṟupaṭṭi pōṉṟirunta talivaṉ yāṉum aṉṉiyum irunta illattē pukku uṇṇum nīr kēṭpavē, eṉṉip poṟkarakattāl uṇṇu nī ūṭṭi varumāṟu aṉṉi paṇippa, yāṉum ceṉṟu koṭukkaluṟṟa aḷavil, eṉatu vaḷi muṉkiyip paṟṟi nalintāṉāka, yāṉ terumantu aṉṉiyi viḷittu ‘ivaṉ ceytatu kāṇ!’ eṉṟēṉāka, aṉṉiyum avviṭam alaṟip paṭartaravē, yāṉ ataṉi maṟittu ‘ivaṉ uṇṇum nīr vikkiṉāṉ’ eṉṟu poykkavē, aṉṉiyum avaṉatu mutukil palakāl taṭavik koṭuppa, aṉṟē eṉṉik kaṭikkaṇṇāl kolvāṉ pōla nōkki makiḻtaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa kūṭṭattiṟku vittiṭṭāṉ akkaḷvaṉ makaṉ” 	(kali. 51)
iḵtu uṇavuk kālattut tammaṉikkaṇ pukka talivaṉi aṉṉi viruntēṟṟuk koṇṭa ceytiyip piṉṉoru kālattut talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟiyavāṟu.		(to. po. 107 nac.)
iraviṉ nīṭṭam -
talivaṉ toṭakkattē taliviyiṭam, “niṉṉiṟ piriyēṉ” eṉṟu uṟutimoḻi kūṟip piṉ  ataṉi maṟantu pirintatu kuṟittut talivi varuntukiṟāḷ. avaḷukku irāppoḻutu mika nīṇṭu tōṉṟukiṟatu. avaḷ “talivaṉ eṉṉip pirintu ceṉṟa nāḷ mutal, cūriyaṉuṭiya paccik kutiri kaḷum pākaṉum iratamum kālam tāḻttuvati vaḻakkamākak koṇṭuviṭṭaṉa” (tiruvārūrk. 345) eṉṟāṟ pōla, irāppoḻutu nīḷitāyt taṉakkut tuṉpam tarutalit tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
‘aṟattoṭu niṟṟal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu itu.	
iraviṉum eytā uravōṟku iraṅkal -
iravuppoḻutilum illam eytāta talivaṉi eṇṇit talivi varuntal.
“talivaṉatu tēr iravilum varavilliyē; talivaṉip paṟṟi ūrār kūṟum pukaḻ mika naṉṟu; (naṉṟaṉṟu eṉpatu kuṟippu)” (ampikā. 450) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟi varuntal.
iḵtu ampikāpati kōviyil ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
iravukkuṟi -
talivaṉ taliviyi iravukkuṟiyiṭattē kūṭutal. kaḷavuk kālattē talivaṉ viruppatti īṭēṟṟutalit taṉ aṟamākak karutum talivi, avaṉ virumpiyavāṟē iravil vantu taṉṉic cantippataṟku variyaṟutturikkum iṭam iravukkuṟiyām. avviṭam toṭakkattil illattiṉ kaṭṭaṭamāṉa uḷ maṉikkuḷ iruttal kūṭātu; āyiṉ illattu matil ellikkuḷ illattavar pēcum pēc ciṉi veḷiyiliruntu kēṭkum aṇimikkaṇ amin tiruttal vēṇṭum. tōḻiyum taliviyum maṉiyiṉuḷ ḷiruntu oruvāṟṟāṉ pēcuvataṉik kuṟiyiṭattu vanta talivaṉ kēṭṭu āṟṟumpaṭiyāka iravukkuṟiyiṭamāṉatu akamaṉikkum puṟamaṉikkum naṭuvē amintirukkum. cilanāḷ paḻakiyapiṉ accamiṉṟi uḷmaṉiyiṟ ceṉṟu kūṭutalum nikaḻum.	(to. po. 131 nac.)
iravukkuṟi maṉi ellikkuḷ iruttal vēṇṭum; āyiṉ maṉik kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷuḷ iruttal kūṭātu. aṭṭil, koṭṭakāram, paṇṭacāli, kūṭakāram, paḷḷi ampalam, urimi iṭam, kūttappaḷḷi eṉa ivaṟṟuḷ nīṅkic ceykuṉ ṟum iḷamarakkāvum pūmpantarum viḷiyāṭumiṭamum ivaṟṟic cārntavaṟṟuḷḷum piṟavaṟṟuḷḷum ākavēṇ ṭum eṉpatu. kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ kuravar uṟiyum iṭamākaliṉ teyvattāṉam eṉṟu karutappaṭum. mēlum talivaṉ varu taṟkum mīṇṭu cellutaṟkum kaṭṭaṭappakuti ētamuṭittu ātaliṉ iravukkuṟi maṉikkaṭṭaṭa elliyik kaṭantē amital vēṇṭum. (iṟi. 21 uri)
iravukkuṟi iṭiyīṭu -
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ patiṉmūṉṟām kiḷavi. (eṭṭāmnāḷ) iravukkuṟikkaṇ vanta talivaṉ allakuṟippaṭutalāl iṭiyīṭupaṭṭup pōtal.	(na. a. 123)

iravukkuṟi iṭiyīṭum ataṉ vakikaḷum -
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ patiṉmūṉṟāvatāka nikaḻum tokuti. itaṉ iraṇṭu vakiyum varumāṟu :
1. allakuṟi - iravukkuṟiyil kuṟikaḷ allātaṉa kuṟikaḷ pōla nikaḻtal. talivaṉ, talivi tōḻi ākiyōr tām māttiram aṟiyum vakiyāl, kuṟiyīṭu vakiyil ēṟpaṭuttik koṇṭa puḷ eḻupputal, nīril kalleṟital pōṉṟa nikaḻccikaḷ vēṟu ētō kāraṇattāl nikaḻntuviṭa, appōtu kuṟiyiṭattiṟku vanta talivi talivaṉik kāṇātu mīṇṭuviṭa, atupōlavē talivaṉum ak kuṟikaḷic ceytapōtu talivi vārāmiyāl tāṉum tuyaramuṟṟuc cellutal alla kuṟippaṭutalām.
2. varuntoḻiṟ karumi - talivaṉ iravukkuṟi varutaṟku iṭiyūṟu nērtal.	(na. a. 159)
iravukkuṟikkaṇ talivaṉ taliviyip peṟṟu makiḻntu kūṟutal -
“viṇṇakatti viḷakkutal virumpi ipperuntōḷ koṭicci nammiṉiṉṟu pirivāḷō? piriyamāṭṭāḷ. vāṉam cūṭiyuḷḷa tilakam pōla, ivaḷnutal pōṉṟa piṟiyi āṇṭum kāṇpōm; īṇṭum ivaḷatu ciṟu nutalik kāṇpōm” eṉṟāṟ pōla, iravukkuṟiyil taliviyik kūṭiya talivaṉ makiḻntu kūṟal.
iccūttirattuḷ ‘avaṭpeṟṟu malital’ eṉṟataṟku iraṭṭuṟa moḻitalāl koḷḷappaṭṭa kūṟṟu itu.		(to. po. 103 nac.)
iravukkuṟikkaṇ talivaṉ mati kaṇṭu kūṟiyatu -
“antō, pāvam! inta viṇmīṉkaḷ eṉtaliviyiṉ mukattiyum vāṉattuc cantiraṉiyum pirittu aṟiya māṭ ṭāmal nili kalaṅkit tirikiṉṟaṉa” eṉṟu talivaṉ talivi vaṉappip pukaḻtal.	(kuṟaḷ 1116)

iravukkuṟikkaṇ varukiṉṟa talivaṉ taliviyi aiyuṟṟup pāṅkaṟkuk kūṟutal -
taliviyiṉ pēraḻaku teyvamakaḷir aḻakiṉi nikarppatu ātaliṉ, tāṉ avaḷit teyvamakaḷō eṉṟu aiyuṟṟa ceytiyip pāṅkaṉiṭam talivaṉ kūṟutal.		(tiṇimoḻi. 49)
iḵtu iccūttirattup ‘parivuṟṟu meliyiṉum’ eṉṟatāl koḷḷappaṭṭa kiḷavikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.	(to. po. 103 nac.)
iravukkuṟik kūṟṟukaḷ -
1. iravukkuṟi vēṇṭal, 2. vaḻiyarumi kūṟi maṟuttal, 3. niṉṟu neñcuṭital, 4. iravukkuṟi nērtal. 5. uṭkōḷ viṉātal, 6. uṭkoṇṭu viṉātal, 7. kuṟiyiṭam kūṟal, 8. iravukkuṟi ēṟpittal, 9. ira varavurittal, 10. ētam kūṟi maṟuttal, 11. kuṟi nērtal, 12. kuṟi nērntami kūṟal, 13. varavuṇarnturittal, 14. tāy tuyilaṟital, 15. tuyileṭuttuc cēṟal, 16. iṭattuyttu nīṅkal, 17. taḷarvakaṉṟurittal, 18. maruṅkaṇital, 19. mukaṅ kaṇṭu makiḻtal, 20. paḷḷiyiṭattu uyttal, 21. varavu vilakkal, 22. āṟṟāturittal, 23. irakkam kūṟi varivu kaṭātal, 24. nilavu veḷippaṭa varuntal, 25. allakuṟi aṟivittal, 26. kaṭaliṭi vittut tuyar aṟivit tal. 27. kāma mikka kaḻipaṭarkiḷavi, 28. kāppuc ciṟimikka kaḻipaṭarkiḷavi, 29. āṟupārttuṟṟa accakkiḷavi, 30. taṉṉuṭ kiyāṟu eytiṭu kiḷavi, 31. nili kaṇṭurittal, 32. iravuṟu tuyaram kaṭaloṭu cērttal, 33. alar aṟivuṟut tal - eṉpaṉa muppattu mūṉṟum tirukkōviyār cuṭṭum iravukkuṟik kūṟṟukkaḷ. (kōvi. 148 - 180 cū. 14)
iravuk kuṟiyiṭit taliviyi nukarnta talivaṉ pakaliṭi avaḷ āṟṟali viyantu kūṟal -
“maliyaṉatu muḷḷūrk kāṉattiṉkaṇ naḷḷeṉ yāmattē nammi naṇṇuṟa vantu nammavaḷāyuḷḷāḷ; iraviṉkaṇ yām vēynta malarkaḷi yutirttuk kūntali eṇṇeypūci nīvi vikaṟikkaṇ nammoṭu mēvāta mukattaḷāyt taṉṉic cārntavariṭiyēyum uḷḷāḷ. ātaliṉ nam kātalavaḷ iraṇṭu tiṟamum oruṅkē aṟinta kaḷvi!” (kuṟun. 312) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
iccūttirattuḷ, ‘collavaṭ cārttip pulliya vakiyi ṉum’ eṉpuḻi, ‘vaki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ ikkūṟṟut taḻuvik koḷḷappaṭṭatu.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
iravukkuṟivaki -
kaḷaviyaluḷ paṉṉiraṇṭāvatāka nikaḻum iravukkuṟi eṉṉum tokuti oṉpatu vakittām. iravukkuṟiyāvatu talivaṉ taliviyi iraviṉkaṇ kaṇṭu maruvaluṟum iṭam. itu talivi illattiṟku aṇimiyāṉatu.
vēṇṭal	:	talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam iravukkuṟi vēṇṭa, avaḷ			 taliviyiṭam kēṭṭal.
maṟuttal	:	talivaṉ vēṇṭiyatit tōḻiyum talivi yum 			maṟuttal.
uṭaṉpaṭal	:	tōḻi talivaṉ kūṟiyatiyum, talivi tōḻi			 kūṟiyatiyum ēṟṟu uṭaṉpaṭutal.
kūṭṭal	:	pāṅki taliviyi aḻittuc ceṉṟu kuṟiyiṭattu 			viṭuttal.
kūṭal	:	talivaṉ taliviyi iravukkuṟiyil kūṭutal.
pārāṭṭal	:	talivaṉ taliviyip pukaḻtalum, tōḻi 				talivaṉ tanta kiyuṟiyip pukaḻtalum.				pāṅkiyiṟ
kūṭṭal	:	talivaṉ taliviyip pāṅkiyuṭaṉ cērppit tal.
uyaṅkal	:	iravukkuṟikkaṇ talivaṉ varum vaḻiyiṭi yuḷḷa			 tuṉpaṅkaḷi niṉintu talivi varuntutal.
nīṅkal	:	tōḻi taliviyik kuṟiyiṭattuyttu nīṅkuta lum			 talivaṉ taliviyik kūṭinīṅkutalum eṉa 				ivi.	(na. a. 157)
iravukkuṟi vēṇṭiya kiḻavaṟkut tōḻi nērnta 
vāypāṭṭāṉ maṟuttatu -
“naḷḷiravil peyta maḻiyāl maṟunāḷilum aruvi malik kukikaḷil olikkum malināṭaṉē! kāmam nīṅkuvatāka iruppiṉum niṉṉiṭattu emakkuḷḷa naṭpu nīṅkātu” (kuṟun. 42) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟiya kūṟṟil, ‘kāmam nīṅkuvatāyiṉum’ eṉṟataṉāl meyyuṟu puṇarccikku vāyppāṉa iravukkuṟi kiṭṭātu eṉpataṉiyum, mutal nāḷ peyta maḻi niṉṟa piṉṉum maṟunāḷum aruvi ōṭivaruvatu pōlap puṇarcci nīṅkiṉum aṉpu aṟātu eṉpataṉiyum cuṭṭiyavāṟu.	(kuṟun. 42)
iravuttalic cēṟal -
peruntiṇit talivi talivaṉik kāṇa viḻintu iruḷ neṟiyē cellutal.
“kāmamē tuṇiyāka eṉṉit tūṇṭavum ceytalāl, eṉatu uḷḷattu vemmi akala, eṉkātalaṉit taḻuva virumpi iruḷil cellaluṟṟēṉ; vāṉam miṉṉimiṉṉi eṉakku vaḻi kāṭṭuka” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmālikkaṇ peruntiṇiyil oru pakutiyāṉa peṇpāṟ kūṟṟil oru kiḷavi.			(pu. ve. mā. 16-6)
iravu nili uṇarttal -
oruvaḻit taṇanta talivaṉi aṉṟiravu kaḻinta maṟunāḷ kāli tavaṟātu mīṇṭu varumāṟu tōḻi kūṟal.
“taliva! ēḻu kutirikaḷ pūṭṭiya oṟṟiccakkarat tēri irukālum illāp pākaṉ iyakka ēḻu kaṭalkaḷiyum eṭṭuk kuṉṟaṅkaḷiyum cuṟṟip piṉ mīṇṭum kiḻakkil utittuk katiravaṉ tōṉṟum nērattē nī mīṇṭu vantu viṭuka!” (ampikā. 283) eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟal. 
iḵtu oruvaḻittaṇattal eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uriyil koḷḷappaṭṭatu.	(i. vi. 525 uri)
iravu nīṭu paruvaral -
irāppoḻutu nīḷutalāl kikkiḷit talivi uṟṟa tuṉpam. “iravē! oru peṇṇi ivvāṟu koṭumip paṭuttal niṉakkut takumō? immuḻumatiyiyum vēṟu viḷaṅkac ceytu eṉṉik kolluti” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyil peṇpāṟ kūṟṟuk kikkiḷikkaṇṇatoru kiḷavi.	(pu. ve. mā. 15-8)
iravum pakalum varavu vilakkal -
variyātu vantoḻukum talimakaṉiṭam iravu uṭaṉ paṭṭāḷ pōlap pakalvaral vilakkiya tōḻi, “nī pakalil variṉ alarmikutiyāl eṅkaṭkup paḻi varum; iravu variṉ kāval mikutiyāl yāṅkaḷ niṉṉi etirppaṭal iyalātu” eṉṟu kūṟit taliviyi maṇakkumāṟu kuṟippāka vaṟpuṟut tal.
itaṉi ‘varivu kaṭātal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇ ‘pakali lum iraviṉum akalivaṇ eṉṟal’ eṉṟu kūṟuvar.
itu ‘varivu muṭukkam’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 261)
iravu varavu urittal -
talivaṟku iravukkuṟi nērnta tōḻi taliviyiṭam, “vēṭki mikutiyāṉ talivaṉ yāṉikaḷ añcumāṟu ciṅkaṅkaḷ tiriyum maliccāralkaḷik kaṭantu niṉṉik kāṇa varutali viḻikiṟāṉ. yām yātu ceyvōm?” eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivaṉ iravukkuṟiyiṭattu varuvatit terivittal.	(kōvi. 156)
itaṉip ‘pāṅki iṟivikku iṟiyōṉ kuṟi aṟivuṟut
tal’ eṉpar.
iḵtu ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
iravu varuvāṉip “pakal varuka” eṉal -
ayalār aiyuṟātavāṟu maṟi kākkavē, ivvāṟu pakal iravukkuṟikaḷ māṟṟi amikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
“taliva! kāṭukaḷil vēṅki yāṉiyit tākkit tiriyum iravil nī taṉittu varuvatu kaṇṭu emmaṉam peritum varuntukiṉṟatu. nāḷi yāṉum taliviyum tiṉippuṉam kāvalil irukkiṉṟōm. kāntaḷ malarntuḷḷa aruviyiṉ pakkattē ivaḷi nī peṟṟu makiḻka” (akanā. 92) eṉṟu tōḻi kūṟiyatu.
itaṉi ‘ētam kūṟi ira(vu) varavu vilakkal’ eṉpataṉkaṇ aṭakkuvar (kōvi. 253). itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘varivu kaṭātal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na.a. 166)
iravu valiyuṟuttal -
“uyirttōḻiyiṭam ceṉṟu kuṟi irantu vaṟpuṟuttu vēṉ” eṉṟu talivaṉ tuṇital.
itaṉik ‘kuṟiyuṟat tuṇital’ eṉṉum tirukkōvi
yār (51). itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkimati uṭaṉpāṭu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatu oru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 140)
iravuṟu tuyarattiṟku iraṅki urittal -
kaṟpuk kālattil talivaṉ taliviyiṭam kūṟātu poruḷ vayiṉ piriya, talivaṉuṭiya pirivu kēṭṭa talivi āṟṟātu varunti melitalik kaṇṭa tōḻi, “katiravaṉ tōṉṟap pakal varuvatu eppoḻutu? atuvariyil taliviyi āṟṟuvittal yāṅkaṉam?” eṉat talivi iravellām talivaṉpirivāl aṭinta tuyarattiṟkut tāṉ irakka muṟṟup pakal varumvari iravil avaḷi āṟṟuvittaliṉ arumi paṟṟit taṉṉuḷ aḻintu kūṟutal.
itu ‘poruḷvayiṉ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 339)
iravuṟu tuyaram kaṭaloṭu cērttal -
iravukkuṟikkaṇ talivaṉi etirppaṭa māṭṭātu varunti niṉṟa talivi, “iṉṟi iravellām eṉṉip pōṉṟu nīyum tuyaramuṟṟuk kalaṅkit teḷintāyalli; eṉṉip pōla niṉṉiyum pirintu ceṉṟār uḷarō?” eṉṟu tāṉuṟṟa tuṉpattik kaṭaloṭu cērttuk kūṟutal.
iḵtu iravukkuṟi eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.			(kōvi. 179)
irākkatam -
valinili; atu kāṇka.
iruttal
taliviyiṭam talivaṉ taṉpirivu paṟṟi uṇarttiyavaḻiyum tōḻi talivaṉpirivupaṟṟi uṇarttiyavaḻiyum talivaṉ piriyāṉ eṉṟu iruttalum, talivaṉ pirintuḻi avaṉ mīṇṭu vārātapōtu avaṉ kuṟitta paruvam vantavaḻiyum atu talivaṉ kuṟitta paruvam aṉṟu eṉṟu tāṉē kūṟalum, paruvam varumaḷavum tāṉ āṟṟiyirunta ceytiyit talivi mīṇṭu vanta talivaṉiṭam kūṟalum pōlvaṉa iruttalām. itu mulli eṉṉum oḻukkamām. talivaṉ mīṇṭu varuvatākak kuṟitta paruvam varuvataṟkumuṉ talivi varuntik kūṟuvaṉa mulli cāṉṟa kaṟpu aṉmiyiṉ pāliyām. itu kaṟpuk kālattiṟkē uriyatu.	(to. po. 14 nac.)
iruttal nimittam -
pirintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ mīṇṭuvaruvatākak kuṟitta  paruvam toṭaṅkiyum talivaṉ vārātatu kuṟittut tōḻi āṟṟātu kūṟuvaṉavum, paruvam aṉṟu eṉṟu taliviyi vaṟpuṟuttuvaṉavum, talivaṉ virivil varuvāṉ eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttuvaṉavum, talivaṉ pācaṟikkaṇ iruntu urippaṉavum, avi pōlvaṉavum iruttal eṉṉum uripporuḷ nikaḻtaṟkuriya kāraṇamum cūḻniliyum ām.			(to. po. 14 nac.)
irutu -
iraṇṭu tiṅkaṭ kālamākiya perumpoḻutu ‘irutu iḷavēṉil eṟikatir iṭapattu’ (maṇi. 11 : 40)
kār, kūtir, muṉpaṉi, piṉpaṉi, iḷavēṉil, mutuvēṉil eṉa irutu āṟām.	(vī. cō. 181 uri)
irutu nukarvu -
kār mutaliya perum poḻutiṟkuriya nukarcci.	(cīvaka. 2677)
iruppu -
iruttal eṉṉum uripporuḷ	(cāmi. 80)
irupaṇpu kūṟal -
paṇpu pārāṭṭal	(cāmi. 89.)
irupatamum vēṇṭal -
talivaṉ iṟivaṉ irutiruvaṭikaḷiyum vaḻipaṭal. iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ taliviyik kūṭi avaḷit teḷivuṭaṉ viṭutta talivaṉ, taliviyi mīṇṭum tāṉ kūṭutaṟku vāypput tarumāṟu avaḷik kūṭṭivitta teyvattiṉ tiruvaṭiyiraṇṭaṉiyum vēṇṭutal.
iḵtu iyaṟkippuṇarcci eṉṉum tokutikkaṇ aminta iṟutik kūṟṟu.	(mā. akap. 21)
irupāl kuṭipporuḷ -
talivaṉum taliviyum tōṉṟiya iruvakik kuṭiyum, piṟappu, kuṭimi, āṇmi, āṇṭu, uruvu, niṟutta kāmavā- yil, niṟi, aruḷ, uṇarvu, tiru - eṉṉum pattum porutta māka varutal. itaṉic cevili uḷaṅkoṇṭu aṟattoṭu niṟpāḷ.	(to. po. 115 nac.)


irupāṟ peruntiṇik kūṟṟukkaḷ -
1. cīr celavu aḻuṅkal, 2) ceḻumaṭalūrtal, (3) tūtu iṭiyāṭal, (4) tuyar avaṟku urittal, (5) kaṇṭu ki cōrtal, (6) paruvam mayaṅkal, (7) āṇpāṟ kiḷavi, 
(8) peṇpāṟ kiḷavi, (9) tēṅkamaḻ kūntal terivi veṟiyāṭṭu, 10) arivikku avaḷ tuṇi pāṇ varavu urittal, 11) paripurac cīṟaṭip paratti kūṟal, 12) viṟali kēṭpat tōḻi kūṟal, 13) veḷvaḷi viṟali tōḻikku viḷampal, 14) paratti vāyil pāṅki kaṇṭurittal, 
15) piṟarmaṉit tuyiṉṟami viṟali kūṟal, 16) kuṟṟici, 
17) kuṟuṅkali eṉpaṉa.
ikkūṟṟup patiṉēḻum talivaṉ talivi eṉṉum iruvariṭiyēyum  nikaḻnta poruntāk kāmacceyal paṟṟiyaṉa ātaliṉ, aṉpiṉ aintiṇip puṟattavāyp puṟattiṇi āyiṉa.	(pu. ve. mā. 17)
`irupeyar mūṉṟum uriya' vātal -
irupeyar - uvamip peyarum uvamikkum peyarum; mūṉṟum uriya ātal - toḻilum paṇpum payaṉum ākiya mūvakippaṭṭa poruṭkum uriyavāmāṟu uvamatti amittal. aḵtāvatu uvamip poruḷ upamēyamāka mayaṅkik koḷḷutal.
e-ṭu :	
 koṭiyit taliviyiṉ iṭiyeṉavum, kāntaḷik ki
eṉavum karuviḷiyik kaṇ eṉavum mayaṅkik koḷḷal.	(to. po. 194 iḷa.)
aḵṟiṇikkuriya oṉṟaṉpāl palaviṉpāl eṉṉum  iraṇṭaṉkaṇṇum uyartiṇikkuriya āṇ peṇ palar eṉṉum muppāṟ ceytikaḷi ēṟṟi, neñcam mutaliyavaṟṟi uṟuppuṭiyatu pōlavum uṇarvuṭiyatu pōlavum kūṟutal pōlvaṉa.	(196 nac.)
iruvakik kaṟpiṉum talivaṉ puṇarcci -
kaḷaviṉvaḻi vanta kaṟpu, kaḷaviṉvaḻi vārāk kaṟpu eṉṉum iruvakik kaṟpiṉkaṇṇum talivaṉukkuk kātaṟparatti yoṭu kaḷavup puṇarcciyum, kāmakkiḻattiyum piṉṉar maṇanta taliviyum eṉṟa ivarkaḷoṭu vatuvip puṇarcciyum nikaḻum.	(na. a. 58)
iruvakik kuṟi piḻittal -
pakaṟkuṟiyum iravukkuṟiyum tavaṟutal. kuṟi piḻittalāvatu puṉal olippaṭutalum, puḷ eḻupputalum mutaliyaṉa. kuṟiyeṉak kuṟitta nikaḻcci talivaṉāṉ aṉṟi vēṟoru kāraṇattāṉ nikaḻntuḻi, ataṉik kuṟi eṉa niṉintu ceṉṟu aḵtu avaṉ ceyta kuṟi aṉmiyiṉ taliviyum tōḻiyum akaṉṟoḻital.	(to. po. 107 nac.)
iruvakip pāṅkar -
talivaṉukkup pārppaṉappāṅkaṉ vēḷāḷappāṅkaṉ eṉa iruvakip pāṅkar uḷar.	(na. a. 100, 101)
iruvakip pirivu -
talivaṉ taliviyip piritalum, taliviyi uṭaṉ koṇṭu avaḷuṟaviṉarākiya tōḻi mutaliyōrip piritalum pālittiṇikkuriya poruḷākum.			(to. po. 13 iḷa)
nālvaki varuṇattārkkum naṭantum vaṇṭi vāyilākavum pirintu cellutaṟkum, antaṇar allātārkku marakkalat til pirintu cellutaṟkum paṉikkālam ciṟantatu.	(11 nac.)
kāliṟ pirivu - nilattu vaḻiyākat tēr mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ pirintu cellutal.
kalattiṟ pirivu - kaṭal vaḻiyākak kappalil pirintu cellutal. 	(11 kuḻa.) 
iruvakippirivu vēṉiṟpirivum piṉpaṉip pirivum eṉpar 			(pārati 11).
kāliṉāl naṭantu cellutal, marakkalattil cellutal, vaṇṭi mutaliya ūrtikaḷiṟ cellutal eṉap piriviṉi mūṉṟākkuvar piṉṉūlōr (na.a. 83, i.vi. 451).  antaṇar kalattiṟ cellutal kūṭātu eṉpar (na.a. 84, i.vi. 452)
iruvayiṉ ottal (1) -
talivaṉ taliviyik kaṇṇuṟṟu avaḷ kuṟippaṟintu, avaḷip puṇara virumpip puṇarātamuṉ niṉṟa vēṭki puṇarnta piṉṉum aṅṅaṉamē kuṟivaṟa niṟintu niṟṟal kaṇṭu, talimakaḷi makiḻntu kūṟal.
itaṉi ‘nalam pārāṭṭal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa (na. a. 125 i. vi. 493) iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarcci eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 9.)
iruvayiṉ ottal (2) -
talivaṉtaliviyariṭip puṇarāta muṉ niṉṟa aṉpu puṇarnta piṉṉum appeṟṟiyē niṟṟal.
uṇṇātamuṉ niṉṟa vēṭki uṇṭa piṉṉarum appeṟ ṟiyē niṟkumāyiṉ uṇṭataṉāṟ payaṉ illātavāṟu pōlap puṇarātamuṉ niṉṟa vēṭki puṇarnta piṉṉum appeṟṟiyē niṟkumāyiṉ puṇarcciyāl payaṉ illi eṉiṉ, puṇarāta muṉ niṉṟa vēṭki puṇarcciyuḷ kuṟi paṭum; akkuṟipāṭṭik kūṭṭattiṉkaṇ tammuḷ peṟṟa ceykuṇaṅkaḷāṉ āya aṉpu niṟivikkum; piṉṉum muṉ ṉiṉṟa aṉpu kūṭṭattil kuṟipaṭum;  ataṟku iṭiyiṉ ṟiyē kuṇattiṉāṉāya aṉpu niṟivikkum; niṟivitta piṉṉar muṉṉiṉṟa aṉpu kūṭṭattil kuṟiyātu eññāṉ ṟum orupeṟṟiyēyāy niṟṟalē iruvayiṉ ottalām.	(iṟi. a.u.)
iruvarkātaliyum aṟivittal -
maṇamaṉi ceṉṟu mīṇṭa cevili naṟṟāyiṭam talivaṉ taliviyar oruvarpāl maṟṟavar tīrāk kātalum teviṭṭā iṉpamum peṟṟu makiḻkiṉṟavāṟṟik kūṟal.
“talivaṉ aracaṉ paṇiyāka veḷiyē ēkiṉum avaṉtāṉ taṉ illattil vantu taṅkumēyaṉṟi vēṟṟiṭattē taṅkuta lilli. taliviyum talivaṉit tavira vēṟṟut teyvam etaṉiyum teyvamākak karutuvatilli”. (kōvi. 306) eṉṟu cevili naṟṟāyiṭam kūṟal.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘maruvutal urittal’ eṉṉum. (306). kaṟpiyaluḷ iḵtu ‘ilvāḻkki’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (na. a. 203)
iruvarum uḷvaḻi avaṉ vara uṇartal -
iyaṟkippuṇarcciyiṉ piṉṉart talivikku ēṟpaṭṭa ivvēṟupāṭu teyvattāṉ āyiṟṟā aṉṟi maṟṟoru vāṟṟāṉ āyiṟṟā eṉṟu muṉṉuṟa uṇarntum, talivaṉ taḻiyum kaṇṇiyum koṇṭu piṉniṟṟalik kuṟivākak karutāmal irantu piṉniṉṟavaḻi, “ivaṉ irantu piṉṉiṟ patu etaṟkāka?” eṉak kuṟiyuṟa uṇarntum aiya uṇarvuṭaṉ irunta tōḻi, tāṉum taliviyum irunta iṭattut talivaṉ vantu patiyum peyarum piṟavum viṉāyavaḻi, muṉṉuṟat taliviyip paṟṟiyum kuṟiyuṟat talivaṉip paṟṟiyum koṇṭa aiyavuṇarvu nīṅki, “ivaḷ vēṟupaṭṭatu ivaṉāṟpōlum; ivaṉ kuṟiyirappatu ivaḷip pōlum” eṉṟu, tāṉum taliviyum uṭaṉ iruntapōtu vanta talivaṉ varukiyāl tuṇivāka uṇartal.	(iṟi. a. 7)
iruvarum uḷvaḻi avaṉ vara uṇartaliṉ kūṟṟukkaḷ -
aiyuṟutal, aṟivunāṭal eṉṉum iraṇṭu kūṟṟukkaḷiyē ittokuti uṭiyatu.	(kōvi. 60, 61)
iruvarum  uḷvaḻit talivaṉ, talivikaṇṇatē
taṉ vēṭki eṉat tōḻi uṇarak kūṟutal -
“ivaḷ tantiyatu malippakkattē piñcuṉikkaṇ pūtta periya kuvaḷiyum ivaḷ kaṇpōla malartal aritu; ivaḷ pōlum cāyali uṭittātal mayilukkum aritu” (aiṅ. 299) eṉavum,
“muḷ pōṉṟa paṟkaḷiyum, amutam ūṟum cevvāyiṉi yum, akilum cantaṉamum kamaḻum kūntaliyum, periya amartta maḻikkaṇkaḷiyumuṭiya koṭicciyatu muṟuva loṭu mayaṅkiya annōkkiṉi eṇṇi uḷḷattē kāṇpēṉ” (kuṟun. 286)  eṉavum varum talivaṉ kūṟṟup pōlvaṉa.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
iruvarum oruṅku niṉṟuḻi, “ivaḷ eṉṉi varuttutaṟku yāṉ ceyta tavaṟu eṉ?” eṉṟu viṉāya talivaṟkut tōḻi nakiyāṭic colliyatu -
“kaṭalalikaḷ koḻitta maṇalmēṭṭilē kaḻimuḷḷic ceṭi malar maṇam kamaḻum toṇṭi eṉṟa ūriṉip pōṉṟa iyaṟki vaṉappuṭiya talivi tōḷi virumpu pavarkaḷ tammāṭṭut tavaṟoṉṟilliyāyiṉum orutali yāka naṭuṅkuvārkaḷ” eṉṟāṟ pōlat tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟal. (aiṅ. 177)
iruḷkaṇṭu iraṅkiya talimakaḷ tōḻiyarkku
varivu viruppurittal -
irāppoḻutil taṉṉuṭiya pirivāṟṟāmi mikuntu ceyyum tuṉpattiṟku añciya talivi tōḻiyariṭam taṉṉit talivaṉukku virivil maṇam ceytu koṭukka āvaṉa ceyyumāṟu kēṭṭal.
“pakal (-ñāyiṟu) eṉṉum orēyōr yāṉi maliyiṟ ceṉṟu maṟintavuṭaṉēyē, ariya iravu eṉṉum yāṉikaḷ palavum kūṭṭamāka eḻuntu vantu eṉṉi varutta neruṅkiviṭṭaṉa; eṉ maṇāḷaṉuṭiya naṟiya tuḻāy māliyi nāṉ eṉatu kuḻaliṟ cūṭi uyir tarittirukkum vaki ceytu eṉ aṉṉimār eṉṉik kāppatu eppōtō?“
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvi. 40)
illatu uṇarttal -
aḵtāvatu illātatik kūṟutal. variyātu iravukkuṟikku vantukoṇṭirukkum talivaṉiṭam tōḻi, “talivi nēṟṟup poḻilil, āṇkuraṅku taṉatu peṇkuraṅkiṉ vāyil māṅkaṉiyit tēṉil tōyttu uṇṇak koṭukkum kāṭciyik kaṇ vāṅkātu pārttukkoṇṭu iruntāḷ“(kōvi. 257) eṉṟāṟ pōlap paṭittu moḻintu taliviyiṉ illaṟa vēṭkiyi veḷippaṭut tit talivaṉi varivu kaṭāyatu.
itu ‘varivu kaṭāvutal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟām.	(ka. kā. pak. 111)
illavi nakutal -
peruntiṇit talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ kūṭi makiḻntapōtu illāta oṉṟit tāṉē colli nakittal.
“nī parattiyarip puṇarntu taḻuviyatāl uṉ malar mālikaḷ vāṭik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟip pārttāl eṉakkuḷ cirippu varukiṟatu. āyiṉ atupaṟṟi nāṉ niṉpāl ūṭal ceyyēṉ; eṉ kāmanōy poṟuttal aritu.” eṉpatu pōṉṟa kūṟṟu.	(pu.ve.mā. 312)
‘illiyal maṭantiyar iyalpu uḷam kēṭṭal’ -
kaṟpukkālattup pirintu mīṇṭu varum talivaṉ taṉṉip pirintu vāṭiyirukkum taliviyatu niliyit taṉ uḷḷat tōṭu ucāvi aṟital. (mulli naṭiyiyal) 			(vī. cō. 94 uri mēṟ.)
ilvayiṟ ceṟittami collal -
talivi tōḻiyiṭam tāyar taṉṉi illattuḷḷēyē irukkumāṟu ceytuviṭṭamiyic collutal.
“tōḻi! eṉ meyvēṟupāṭu, yāṉ uḷḷattē aḻuṅki yuḷḷami pōṉṟavi kaṇṭum, ūrār pēcum alarik kēṭṭum eṉtāy uḷḷam vēṟupaṭṭu eṉṉi maṉi yakattē kāval ceytaṉaḷ” (ampikā. 344) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu. iḵtu aṟattoṭu niṟṟal eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatu. (i.vi.po. 161)
ilvāḻkki vaki nāṉku -
kaṟpiyal tokutikaḷuḷ mutalāvatākiya ilvāḻkki nāṉku kūṟṟiṉatām.
	1. kiḻavōṉ makiḻcci
talivaṉ taṉatu ilvāḻkkiyil makiḻcci koḷḷutal. itu avaṉ talivi muṉṉiliyil tōḻiyip pukaḻvatāl pulappaṭum.
	2. kiḻatti makiḻcci
illaṟam toṭaṅki vāḻum taliviyatu makiḻcci. itu variyum nāḷkāṟum tāṉ varuntāmal iruntati avaḷ kāraṇam kāṭṭit tōḻikku urittalāl pulappaṭum.
	3. pāṅki makiḻcci
tōḻiyatu makiḻcci. iḵtu ivaḷ talivaṉiyum taliviyiyum variyum nāḷvari āṟṟiyiruntami paṟṟik kēṭṭaṟitalālum, maṇamaṉikku vanta ceviliyiṭattē talivaṉ taliviyariṉ aṉpiṉ āḻattic colli avarkaḷatu illaṟattip pukaḻtalālum pulappaṭum.
	4. cevili makiḻcci
maṇamaṉi vantu taṉmakaḷ vāḻum uyariya celva niliyic cevili kaṇṭu tāṉ makiḻntu, ataṉi naṟṟāyiṭam kūṟum tiṟattiṉāl itu pulappaṭum. (na. a. 202)
ilakkaṇam -
akapporuḷ uri 27 il oṉṟu. (vī.cō. 91) caṭṭakam mutaliya akapporuḷ urikaḷukkellām ilakkaṇam collik kāṭṭutal.  (96 uri)
ilaṅkiḻi uṇartal -
pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattu iṟutiyil, aṟivum niṟiyum mēlōṅ kappaṭṭa niliyil talivaṉ kaḷavu oḻukkatti ataṉoṭu tavirttu iṉit taṉṉi muṟiyiṉ maṇanta piṉṉarē kūṭuvatākak kūṟiya avaṉatu peruntaṉmiyit talivi uṇartal. (kuṟiñcinaṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)

‘ivaḷ iṟantupaṭum’ eṉṟu, tōḻi talivaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākak kūṟiyatu -
“icciṟiya nalla ūr, uppaṅkaḻikaḷiluḷḷa nīlamaṇi pōṉṟa malarkaḷ kuviyumāṟu alittuḷiyoṭu porunti, pirintiruppōr ceyalaṟṟu varuntumāṟu vāṭik kāṟṟi ṉāl tuṉpattit tarum cilanāḷkaḷi yuṭiyatu (aḵtāvatu talivi tuṉpattit tāṅki vāṭikku varuntic cilanāḷ kaḷē uyir vāḻtal kūṭum.)” eṉṟu kūṟi, kaḷavu tavirttu viriviṉ talivaṉ varintu koḷḷal vēṇṭum eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉ ciṟappuṟattāṉākak kuṟippil peṟappaṭu vittavāṟu.	(kuṟun. 55)
“ivaḷpōlum talivaṉi varuttiṉāḷ!” eṉṟu 
pāṅkaṉ aiyuṟṟatu -
“kaṇṇākiya kuvaḷimalar, pātamākiya taṇṇeṉum taṭamalart tāmari, nakilkaḷākiya kōṅkarumpu, iṭi yākiya nuṭaṅkum miṉṉal ivaṟṟiyuṭiya palluruviṉ kāṇṭaku kamaḻ koṭiyākiya ivvaṇaṅkupōlum, eṉ āṇṭakiyām talivaṉatu aṟivit tolittatu!” eṉṟu pāṅkaṉ viyantu aiyuṟṟavāṟu pōlvaṉa.
iccūttirattuḷ ‘kuṟṟam kāṭṭiya’ eṉṟataṉāl, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattiṟkuriya kūṟṟākic cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṇ vēṟupaṭa varuvaṉavellām taḻuvappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oru kūṟṟē itu.	(to. po. 102 nac.)
“ivaḷi varintukoṇṭu nī illaṟam naṭattuka”  eṉṟa tōḻikkut talivaṉ kūṟutal -
“ivvaḻakiyi nāṉ taḻuvikkoḷḷum iṉpam, tamatu illil iruntu illaṟam naṭattit tām tēṭiya poru ḷāl teṉpulattār teyvam viruntu okkal ākiyōrk kup pakuttu aḷittut tamatu paṅkiṉit tāmuṇṇum iṉpatti okkum”  eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟuvatu.	(kuṟaḷ 1107)
‘iḻintuḻi iḻivē cuṭṭal’ -
taliviyatu  vaṭivamum avaḷi etirppaṭṭa iṭamum takkaṉa allavāyiṉ avaḷ tāḻviyē karuti uṇarappaṭum.	(to. po. 91 iḷa.)
talivaṉiṉ iḻintavaḷākiya talivikkut talivaṉip paṟṟi murukaṉō iyakkaṉō makaṉō eṉṟu aiyam nikaḻu māyiṉ avviyatti nīkkiyuṇarak karuviyilaḷ ātaliṉ avaḷukku ēṟpaṭum aiyam  iṉpattiṟku iḻiviyē tarum.			(94 nac.)
iḻintōrkkuriya pirivukaḷ -
nāṉku  kulattavarkkum, iḻintavar eṉappaṭum nilamakkaḷuḷ talimakkaḷāvārkkum, aṟappuṟaṅkāval pirivu - poruḷ vayiṉpirivu - tuṇivayiṉ pirivu - parattiyiṟ pirivu - eṉṟa pirivukaḷ urimiyuṭiyaṉa. (na. a. 79)
iḷanāḷ -
iḷavēṉil; ‘ikaḻunar ikaḻā iḷanāḷ amiyam’ 	(akanā. 25-12)
‘iḷamit taṉmikku uḷam melintu iraṅkal’ -
naṟṟāy taṉmakaḷ talivaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟuviṭṭati aṟinta piṉ ‘ivaḷ iṉṉum pētimi māṟāta kuḻantiyāy uḷḷāḷē; eṅṅaṉam itaṟkut tuṇintāḷ?’ eṉṟu kūṟit tuyar uṟutal.
“nī viḷiyāṭa vittukkoṇṭirukkum patumiyiṉi ‘aḻātē’ eṉṟu colli arumpāta uṉ nakili ataṟku ūṭṭum eṉpētik kuḻantāy! eṉṉi viṭṭup pōka evvāṟu tuṇintāy?”  (tañci kō. 337) eṉpatu pōṉṟa naṟṟāy kūṟṟu.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘paruvam niṉintu kavaṟal’ (233) eṉṉum.
ituvariviyaluḷ ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 186)
iḷami tīr tiṟattuk kaṇṭōr kūṟuvatu -
talivaṉō taliviyō iruvarumō iḷamipparuvam nīṅkiya piṉṉum kāma nukarcciyil īṭupaṭṭiruppatu paṟṟik kaṇṭōr kūṟuvatu.
‘atirum puṉalūraṟku āramirtam aṉṟō,
mutirum muliyār muyakku’   (pu. ve. mā. 17-13)
iḷamiyatu arumi oṉṟāmi  -
iḷamipparuvam cila āṇṭukaḷē nilittirukkum eṉṟa uṇarcci talivaṉi iṉpattiṉkaṇṇē īrttatu, vāḻnāḷ cilavātaliṉ avaṟṟiṟkuḷ taliviyip pirintu poruḷ tēṭivaral vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇattoṭu poruntātiruttal.	(to. po. 41 nac.)
iḷiya kalām -
iḵtu uṇartta uṇarum ūṭalām. 	(na. a. 205)
iḷiyar -
‘iḷiyōr’ kāṇka. 	(to. po. 193 nac.)
iḷiyōr -
talivaṉi iravum pakalum nīṅkātu avaṉukkuk kavacam pōṉṟu irukkum kuṟṟēval niliyiṉarākiya cilar. ivarkaḷum vāyil ātaṟku uriyarāvar. 	(na. a. 108)
iḷiyōr āvār talimakkaḷiṉ nalam pēṇi meypuku karuvipōla avarik kāttu avar ēvalvaḻi viṉipuri vōr. perumpāṉmiyum ivar talimakkaḷiṉ tantikkuk kāmakkiḻatti vaḻivanta makkaḷāvār; ciṟupāṉmi piṟaru māvār.	(te. taṟ. 29 ca. pāla.)
iḷiyōrkku uriyaṉa -
1.	talivaṉ taliviyuṭaṉāyiṉum taṉittāyiṉum pōkku oruppaṭṭiṭattē avaṉuṭaṉ cella vēṇṭiya iḷiyōr “cellumvaḻi taṇṇitu, veyyatu, cēyttu, aṇittu”  eṉṟāṟ pōla āṟṟatu nilimi kūṟal.
2.	tuṇintu ceṉṟu eṭutta karumam muṭikkumāṟu kūṟutal,
3.	‘iṉṉuḻi iṉṉatu ceyka’ eṉṟu ēviyakkāl, ataṉi muṭittu vantu kūṟutal,
4.	talivaṉ ēvalit tām kēṭṭal,
5.	talivaṉ viṉavātavaḻiyum, talivikkākavāvatu collat takka aṟivurikaḷit talivaṉiṭam kūṟal,
6. pōkum pālivaḻikkaṇ kaṇṭa nimittam mutaliya poruḷkaḷit talivaṟkum talivikkum uṟuti payakkumāṟu kūṟutal.
7.	pōkum vaḻiyuḷ māvum puḷḷum puṇarntu viḷi yāṭuvaṉavaṟṟi avviruvarkkumāyiṉum talivaṟkē yāyiṉum kāṭṭutalum, ūṟu ceyyum koṭiya vilaṅku kaḷi akaṟṟik kūṟutalum,
8.	talivaṉ varuvaṉ eṉat talivimāṭṭut tūtāy varutal,
9.	talivaṟkut talivi niliyi aṟintu ceṉṟu kūṟal,
10.	mīḷuṅkāl “viruntu peṟuvaḷkol!” eṉat talivi nili kūṟal,
11.	kuṟṟēval ceytu talivaṉtaliviyarip pātukāttal - iviyāvum iḷiyōr toḻilāka uriyaṉa. 	(to. po. 170, 171 nac.)
iḷiyōr kūṟṟu -
talivaṉ pōkaluṟṟa vaḻiyatu niliyiṉi āyntu atu taṇṇitu veytu cēyttu aṇittu eṉṟu kūṟutal. tuṇivākac ceṉṟu vantu ceyporuḷ muṭikkumāṟu aṟintu kūṟutal, iṉṉaviṭattu iṉṉatu ceyka eṉṟu ēviyakkāl ataṉi muṭittu vantami kūṟutal, talivaṉ ēvalit tām kēṭṭal, talivaṉ viṉavātaviṭattum talivikkākavāyiṉum ceppattakuvaṉa talivaṟkuk kūṟutal, celcurattuk kaṇṭa nimittam mutaliya poruḷ kaḷit talivaṉtaliviyarkku uṟuti payakkumāṟu kūṟutal, āṇṭu māvum puḷḷum puṇarntu viḷiyāṭuvaṉavaṟṟi avviruvarkkumāyiṉum talivaṟkēyāyiṉum kāṭṭiyum ūṟu ceyyum kōṇmākkaḷi akaṟṟiyum kūṟutal eṉ ṉum iviyum ivipōlvaṉa piṟavum (talivaṉ varuvaṉ eṉat taliviyiṭam tūtāy varutal, aṟintu ceṉṟu talivikkut talivaṉnili uṇarttal, mīḷuṅkāl viruntu peṟukuvaḷkol eṉat talivinili urittal) iḷiyōrkkuriya kūṟṟu ām.	(to. po. 170 nac)
taliviyit talivaṉ varukikku vāyil vēṇṭutal, avaḷi avaṉ varavu uṭaṉpaṭuvittal, avaḷ ūṭalit tīrttal, avaḷ ūṭal tīrntavāṟṟit talivaṟku uṇart tal, talivaṉ taliviyarkkuk kuṟṟēval purital, viṉi vayiṟ pirintōṉ viṉiyiṉ mīṇṭu varutali muṉceṉṟu talivikku urittal, avaṉ viṉittiṟaṅ kaḷip porunta avaṭku urittal, viṉi veṟṟiyuṟa muṭinta tiṟatti urittal, avaṉ varum vaḻiyiyalpiṉik kūṟal, vaḻiyiṭit tām kaṇṭa kāṭcikaḷi urittal ivi ellām iḷiyōrkku uriyaṉa.	(na. a. 98)
iṟkoṇṭēkal -
iravukkuṟi nikaḻnta puṟamaṉiyiṉiṉṟum tuyil kōṭaṟkuriya akamaṉikkut taliviyit tōḻi aḻittuc cellutal.
“talivi! nām kuvaḷip pūkkaḷum mullippūkkaḷum koytuviṭṭōm. aṉṉiyiṉ tīkkaṇkaḷ uṟakkam nīṅkāmuṉ peṇkaḷ kāvalpuriyum nam kaṭimaṉiyic ceṉṟaṭivōm” (ampikā. 212) eṉṟu tōḻi talivi yiṭam kūṟi avaḷi aḻittuc cellutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 158)
iṟceṟittal -
taliviyatu tōṟṟap polivu kaṇṭum, avaḷippaṟṟi ūravar kuṟippākap pēcikkoḷvatik kēṭṭum talivi taṉ illattiviṭṭu viḷiyāṭal mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkup puṟam cella iyalātavāṟu cevili avaḷi illattiṟkuḷ ḷēyē taṅkac ceytal.
“kāppum pūṇṭiciṉ; kaṭiyum pōkali
pēti alli; mētiaṅ kuṟumakaḷ”  (akanā. 7)
eṉṟāṟ pōlac cevili kūṟi iṟceṟippāḷ.
iṟceṟippu, iṟ ceṟivu eṉpaṉavum oruporuḷa. (na.a. 154)
iṟceṟivu aṟivittu varivu kaṭātal -
pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ talivaṉiṭam tāyaccam kūṟi varivu kaṭāya tōḻi, “em aṉṉi taliviyi uṟṟu nōkkip ‘puṟattē pōy viḷiyāṭal vēṇṭā’ eṉak kūṟiṉāḷ; iṉi illattēyē taliviyic ceṟittu viṭuvāḷpōlum” eṉat taliviyi iṟceṟittali aṟivittu virivil avaḷi maṇam ceytu koḷḷumāṟu talivaṉi vēṇṭal.
itu ‘pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 133)
iṟparatti -
kaṭaṉaṟiyum vāḻkkiyi uṭiyaḷākit talivaṉi maṇantu kāmakkiḻami pūṇṭu illaṟam naṭattum kāmak kiḻatti iṟparatti eṉappaṭuvāḷ. (ivaḷ kātaṟparatti yiṉ mēmpaṭṭavaḷ; taliviyiṉ tāḻntavaḷ.)		 	(to. po. 151 nac.)
cēripparattiyar makaḷirāyt talivaṉāl varintu koḷḷappaṭṭu avaṉuṭaṉ kātalil puṇarum kātaṟ paratti iṟparattiyāvāḷ. (na. a. 114, 115)
iṟantataṉ payaṉ -
talivaṉum taliviyum viti kūṭṭak kaṇṭu iyaṟkip puṇarcciyāṉ oṉṟupaṭṭuk kaḷavoḻukkam pūṇṭu, piṉ karaṇamoṭu puṇarat tirumaṇam ceytukoṇṭu kaṟ poḻukkam pūṇṭu, illaṟatti nallaṟamāka naṭattik kāmatti maṉam veṟukkumaḷavum tuyttal, vāḻkkiyil ituvari vāḻnta illaṟattiṉ payaṉ. appayaṉāvatu, aṟam poruḷ iṉpam eṉṉum ivaṟṟiṉum ciṟanta vīṭ ṭiṉpam peṟutaṟku ēmam cāṉṟavaṟṟi aṭippaṭuttalām. aḵtāvatu vīṭṭiṉpattuḷḷa vēṭkiyāṉ muṉ paṟṟi niṉṟavaṟṟi yellām paṟṟaṟat tuṟantu, meyyuṇarntu vīṭupeṟa muyaṟalām. 	(to. po. 192 nac.)
iṟanta pōlak kiḷakkum kiḷavi -
kaḻintuviṭṭatu pōlak kūṟutal.
talivaṉ piriyumvaḻi mīṇṭu varuvatāka avaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa paruvam irutiṅkaḷi elliyāka uṭiyatu āyiṉum, apparuvam toṭaṅkiya aḷavilēyē atu kaḻintuviṭṭatu pōlat talivi kūṟutal. iṅṅaṉam kūṟutaṟkuk kāraṇam avaḷ aṟiyāmiyum varuttamum mayakkamum akkālattiṟ kuriya karupporuḷ mikat tōṉṟutalumām.			(to. po. 232 iḷa.)
talivi talivaṉ kaḷaviṭit taṉakkut teriviyāmal vāḷāṉ etirum piriviṉkaṇ pirintu mīṭaṟkuk kālam tāḻttiya viṭattu, maṭappamum āṟṟāmiyum viyappum taṉvaṉappu mikutiyum taṉṉik kikaṭantaṉa pōlak kūṟum kūṟṟu. e-ṭu : (kali. 142)	(to. po. 236 nac.)
iṟantapōlak kiḷakkum kiḷavi nikaḻum poruḷkaḷ -
talivaṉatu pirivāṟṟāmiyāl talivi ceyalaṟṟut tuyar kūrntu, viṉivayiṟ pirinta talivaṉ tāṉ mīṇṭum varuvatākak kuṟitta paruvam kaḻintaṉa pōlak kalaṅkik kūṟum kūṟṟukkaḷ, maṭaṉum varuttamum maruṭkiyum mikutiyu mākiya annāṉku poruṭkaṇṇum nikaḻum eṉpa. maṭaṉ : ārāytaliṉṟi nikaḻum aṟiyāmi. varuttam : pirivāṟṟāmi yāl varum tuyaram.  maruṭki : oṉṟi maṟṟoṉṟāk karu tum mayakkam. mikuti: apparuva kālattiṟkuriya poruḷkaḷ mikkaṉavākat tōṉṟutal.	(to. poru. 41 ca. pāla)
iṟantupāṭurittal -
uṭaṉpōkkiṟkut talivaṉit tōḻi oruppaṭutta, talivaṉ tāṉ cella vēṇṭiya vaḻiyatu arumi kūṟi melliyalākiya taliviyi uṭaṉkoṇṭu pōtal aritu eṉṟu kūṟiyavaḻi, talivi avaṉpāl koṇṭuḷḷa aṉpu mikutiyit tōḻi eṭuttuk kūṟi, “nī taliviyi uṭaṉ koṇṭu pōkātu viṭuppiṉ ūravar tūṟṟum alarāṉum kāval mikutiyāṉum niṉṉit talivi kāṇpatu aritāka liṉ avaḷ iṟantupaṭum” eṉat taliviyatu iṟantupāṭu kūṟutal.
iḵtu uṭaṉpōkku eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟām.	(kōvi. 203)

iṟappa nuvaṟal -
talivaṉuṭiya piriviṉit talivi āṟṟāḷ eṉpatupaṭat tōḻi talivaṟku mikutiyum eṭuttuk kūṟutal.
iravukkuṟikkaṇ talivaṉ kēṭkumāṟu tōḻi vēṅki maratti niṉintu kūṟuvāḷāy, “vēṅkiyē! eṉtalivi tiṉiyiṟ kiḷi kaṭitali vilakkumāṟu tiṉi muṟṟiya ceytiyit terivittu niṉ uṭal muḻutum pūkkaḷ malarap polivāka uḷḷāy; eṉ talivi avvāṟu pū aṇiyum kālam eṉṟō?”  eṉṟu kūṟip piṉ talivaṉiṭam, “nāṭa! palaviṉ ciṟukiḷiyil perumpaḻam toṅkuvatu pōla ivaḷ ciṟiya uyir periya kāmattic cumantu varuntum niliyil uḷḷatu. ivaḷ kavali tīra virivil nī ivaḷi varika”  (kuṟun. 18) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟiyatu.
itu tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattu ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ta. ne. vi. 18)
iṟukkar -
pāli nila makkaḷ (cūṭā. pak. 47)	(ṭu)
“iṟiūrpaṭartum”  eṉṟal -
talivi talimakaṉatu ūrkkuc celavu oruppaṭutal; ‘varital vēṭki’kkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(cāmi. 102)
iṟicci -
iḵtu akapporuḷuri 27-il oṉṟu.	(vī. cō. 90)
iṟicciyāvatu ovvoru nilattiṟkumuriya karupporuḷkaḷ. akkarupporuḷkaḷi aṅkiyaṟporuḷ, tiṇi nilip poruḷ, vaṉpulap potupporuḷ, meṉpulap potup poruḷ, perumip potupporuḷ, viruntup potup poruḷ eṉṟu aṟuvakiyākap pirittuk koḷvar. (vī. cō. 96 uri mēṟ.)

iṟicci : ataṟkōr eṭuttukkāṭṭu -
iṟicciyāvatu, makkaḷum teyvamum allāta ēṉiya karupporuḷkaḷiṉ ceyalum paṇpumākiya naṭakki paṟṟi varum. nāṭakavaḻakkattāṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkattāṉum ōtappeṟṟa oḻukalāṟukaḷ ellām uyartiṇiyākiya makkaṭkē uriya poruḷākum eṉpatu naṉku viḷaṅka ‘uri’ eṉṟu nūlōr kuṟiyīṭu ceytu koṇṭāṟpōla, aḵṟiṇik karupporuḷkaḷiṉ paṇpum ceyalkaḷumākiya naṭakkikaḷi ‘iṟicci’ eṉak kuṟiyīṭu ceytaṉar. iṟicci yiṭattākat tōṉṟum uḷḷuṟi ‘uṭaṉili’ eṉappaṭṭatu.
e-ṭu : vēṅki tolitta...... tiyalāy pāṭuvām nām’(kuṟiñcik. 7)
itaṉkaṇ, ‘vēṅki tolitta vāraṇam’ eṉṟataṉāṉ talivaṉ taliviyi varintukoṇṭu ūrār tūṟṟum alarit tolippāṉ eṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu. ‘iṉa vaṇṭu imirpu ūtum cāntamaram’ eṉṟataṉāṉ talivaṉ tamar maṇam pēca varuvar eṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu. ivvāṟu tōḻi karutiyaṉavāka iṟicciyiṟ poruḷ piṟantavāṟu.
yāṉi vēṅkiyit tolittal, vaṇṭu imirpu ūtal ākiya karupporuḷkaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷākiya naṭakki paṟṟi ivviṟiccip poruḷ piṟantu niṟṟal kāṇka.
e-ṭu :	‘kaṉṟutaṉ payamuli mānta’ 	(kuṟun. 225)
itaṉkaṇ, ‘kaṭṭil vīṟu peṟṟa maṟanta maṉṉaṉ pōla naṉṟi maṟantu amiyāmal naṉṟiyuṭiyāyāyiṉ, ivaḷatu oli meṉ kūntalum uṉakku uriyavām’ eṉa ēṉiyuvamattāṉ tiṇipporuḷ viḷaṅkik kiṭattalāṉ, “mali nāṭṭut taliva!” eṉṉum (mutaliraṇṭaṭi) viḷiyuḷ, karupporuḷ kaḷiṉ ceyalkaḷākiya iṟicci, vaṇṇaṉi māttiriyāy nāṭṭic ciṟappittu niṉṟavāṟu kāṇka.			(to. poru. 35 ca. pāla.)
iṟicci eṉṟa uḷḷuṟi (1) -
iṟicci eṉpatu karupporuḷ. iṟiccip poruḷāṉatu uripporuḷiṉ puṟattatākik karupporuḷākiya nāṭṭiṟ kum ūrkkum tuṟikkum aṭiyāvatu
e-ṭu :
‘nilattiṉum peritē vāṉiṉum uyarntaṉṟu
nīriṉum āraḷa(vu) iṉṟē cāral
karuṅkōl kuṟiñcip pūkkoṇṭu
peruntēṉ iḻikkum nāṭaṉoṭu naṭpē’   (kuṟun. 3)
eṉṟa pāṭalil nāṭṭukku aṭiyāki vanta kuṟiñcippūvum tēṉum iṟiccipporuḷ.	(to. po. 225 iḷa.)
“ciṟiya kuṟiñcip pūkkaḷiluḷḷa tēṉit tēṉīkkaḷ palavum palanāḷum uṟiñci vantu palanāḷ muyaṟciyāl periya tēṉkūṭu kaṭṭum nāṭaṉāka iruntuvittum, avaṉōṭu yāṉ ceyta naṭpu nilattiṉatu akalam pōlavum, vāṉiṉatu uyarcci pōlavum, nīriṉatu āḻam pōlavum  kaṇṭavuṭa ṉēyē kaṇakkiṭamuṭiyāta aḷaviṟṟākap perukiviṭṭatē eṉpatu viyappit tarukiṟatu” eṉak karupporuḷkaḷākiya kuṟiñcippūviyum tēṉiyum koṇṭu ippāṭalil iṟiccip poruḷ amintuḷatu.	(225. iḷa.)
iṟicci eṉṟa uḷḷuṟi (2) -
oruvar tām kuṟippiṭak karutum ceytikaḷi nēriṭiyākak kūṟutaṟku iyalātavaḻi ōr uṭkarutti aṭakkip pēcutalu muṇṭu. aṅṅaṉam ōr uṭkarutti uḷḷaṭakkip pēcu muṟiyil oṉṟu iṟicci eṉpatu. iṟicciyāvatu oru nilattiṉ karupporuḷik koṇṭu tām veḷippaṭi yāka oru ceytiyik kūṟiyapiṉ, ataṉi oṭṭiyē vēṟu oru ceytiyiyum aṟiyavittal.
talivaṉ “niṉṉiṟ piriyēṉ; piriyiṉ āṟṟēṉ; piriyiṉ aṟaṉallatu ceytēṉākuvēṉ” eṉṟu taliviyiṭam tey vatti muṉṉiṭṭu uṟutimoḻi kūṟiyōṉ. attakiyavaṉ kaḷavuk kālattē ōriru nāḷkaḷ taliviyik kāṇa vārāmalirukkiṟāṉ. avaṉ cūḷ poytta koṭiyaṉ eṉṟu tōḻi avaṉip paṟṟik kuṟivākap pēcukiṟāḷ. avvāṟu pēcukiyil avaṉatu malivaḷattiyum kuṟip piṭukiṟāḷ.
‘ilaṅkum aruvittē ilaṅkum aruvittē
vāṉiṉ ilaṅkum aruvittē tāṉuṟṟa
cūḷpēṇāṉ poytta mali’   (kali. 41)
“talivaṉō tāṉcoṉṉa uṟutimoḻiyit tavaṟa viṭṭavaṉ. attakiya aṟaṉaṟṟavaṉatu nāṭṭil iyaṟkikku māṟāṉa ceytikaḷ nikaḻtal vēṇṭum; maḻivaḷam kuṟital vēṇṭum; āyiṉ paruvamaḻi tavaṟātu peytalāṉ avaṉ maliyaruviyiṉiṉṟu nīr iṭiyaṟātu iḻintukoṇṭiruk kiṟatu. talivaṉ uṇmiyil aṟaṉ tavaṟiyavaṉāyiruppiṉ avaṉ maliyil maḻivaḷam irātaṉṟō? maḻivaḷam tavaṟātiruttaliṉ avaṉ uṇmiyil aṟam tavaṟiyavaṉ allaṉ. cūḻniliyi oṭṭi uṭalāṉ avaṉ nammiṟ pirin tiruppiṉum uḷḷattāṉ nammi oru pōtum piriyātavaṉ pōlum eṉṟāṟpōla, collappaṭṭa ceytiyik koṇṭu ataṉ uḷḷārnta karuttāka vēṟu ceytikaḷi yum uyttuṇarntu koḷvatu iṟicci eṉṟa uḷḷuṟi yām“.	(to. po. 229 nac.)
iṟiccip poruḷ -
karupporuḷiṉuḷḷē koḷḷum poruḷ. 	
	(to. po. 229 nac.)
iṟicciyiṟ piṟakkum poruḷ (1) -
karupporuḷiṉiṉṟu peṟappaṭum toṉipporuḷum uṇṭu. aḵtu uḷḷuṟi uvamam pōlvatē aṉṟi uḷḷuṟi uvamam aṉṟu. “iḵtu uḷḷuṟi aṉṟu, iṟicci” eṉṟuṇarum teḷivuṭiyārkkē itu naṉku pulappaṭum. ātaliṉ, iṟiccip poruḷiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟum iraṇṭām iṟiccip poruḷum uṇṭu eṉpatu.
(1) ‘kaṉṟutaṉ payamuli mānta’ eṉṟa kuṟuntokip pāṭalil (225) iṟicciyiṟ piṟakkum poruḷiṉi viḷakku vār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.	(to. po. 230 nac.)
(1) tāṉ keṭṭaviṭattu utaviṉa utaviyi araca urimi eytiya maṉṉaṉ maṟantāṟ pōla, nī irantu tuyaruṟṟa kālattu yāṉ taliviyi niṉṉoṭu kūṭṭiya ceynaṉṟiyi maṟavātu iṉṟu nī varintu koḷvāyāyiṉ ivaḷ kūntal niṉakku uriya” eṉṟa viṭattē uvamiyum poruḷum ottu muṭintamiyiṉ, muṉniṉṟa ‘nāṭa’ eṉpatu uḷḷuṟi uvamam aṉṟāy iṟiccip poruḷām. “taṉ kaṉṟiṟkup payaṉpaṭṭup piṟarkku uyirik koṭukkiṉṟa tiṉiyip piṭi tāṉ uṇṭu aḻivu ceykiṉṟāṟ pōla, nī niṉ kāriyam citiyātavāṟu pārttukkoṇṭu emakkuyirākiya ivaḷit tuyaruṟutti emmiyum iṟantupaṭuvittal ākātu” eṉṟu uvami eytiṟṟāyiṉum, piṉṉar niṉṟa upamēyattōṭu iyiyātu ivvuvamam uḷḷuṟiyākap poruḷ tārātu, iṟicciyākiya ‘nāṭaṉ’ eṉpataṉuḷḷē vēṟōr poruḷit tōṟṟuvittu niṉṟatu ātaliṉ iḵtu iṟicciyēyām.	(to. po. 230 nac.)
(2) ‘oṉṟēṉ allēṉ oṉṟuveṉ kuṉṟattup
		 porukaḷiṟu mititta neṟitāḷ vēṅki
		 kuṟavar makaḷir kūntal peymār
		  niṉṟakoya malarum nāṭaṉoṭu
		 oṉṟēṉ tōḻi oṉṟa ṉāṉē!    (kuṟun. 208)
2) ippāṭal varivu etirkoḷḷātu tamar maṟutta viṭattē, “talivaṉōṭu ivaḷ oṉṟum vaḻi yātu?” eṉak kavaliyuṟṟa tōḻikku uṭaṉpōtal kuṟippiṉaḷāyt talimakaḷ kūṟiyatu. itaṉuḷ ‘poru kaḷiṟu’ eṉṟamiyāṉ, talimakaḷtamar talimakaṉ variviṟku uṭaṉpāṭuvārum maṟuppārum āki māṟupaṭṭami tōṉṟukiṟatu. ‘poru kaḷiṟu mititta vēṅki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ porukiṉṟa iraṇṭu kaḷiṟṟāṉum mitikkappaṭuvatu oṉṟākaliṉ varivuṭaṉpaṭā tār talimakaṉi avamatittavāṟu tōṉṟiṟṟu. vēṅki niṉṟu koyya malarum’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, muṉpu ēṟip paṟittal vēṇṭuvatu ippoḻutu niṉṟu paṟikkalāmpaṭi eḷitu. āyiṟṟu eṉpatu poruḷāyiṟṟu; itaṉāl paṇṭu namakku ariyaṉākiya talimakaṉ ippoḻutu taṉṉi avamatikka vum namakku eḷiyaṉāki aruḷ ceykiṉṟāṉ eṉap poruḷ koḷḷak kiṭantavāṟu. (226)	(to. po. 230 nac.)
iṟicciyiṟ piṟakkum poruḷ (2) -
veḷippaṭiyāṉa kūṟṟiṉāṉ toṉipporuḷ oṉṟu tōṉṟutal kūṭum. attoṉipporuḷiṉiṉṟu piṟitoru toṉipporuḷum tōṉṟuvatu iṟicciyiṟ piṟakkum poruḷ ākum.
coṟṟoṭar terivikkum mūvakipporuḷ veḷip paṭip poruḷ, ilakkaṇip poruḷ, kuṟippup poruḷ eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ kuṟippup poruḷē iṟicci. akkuṟip piṉiṉṟu piṟitoru kuṟippum tōṉṟalām eṉpatu.
kaḷavoḻukkatti nīṭṭittuvarum talivaṉ virivil taliviyi maṇattal vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇattāṉ tōḻi avaṉiṭam “vēṅkiyum pūkkat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu; cantiraṉiyum parivēṭam cuṟṟat toṭaṅkiyatu” eṉṉum poruḷpaṭa,
‘pimputal vēṅkiyum oḷḷiṇar virintaṉa
neṭuveṇ tiṅkaḷum ūrkoṇ ṭaṉṟē’  (akanā. 2)
eṉkiṟāḷ. itaṉkaṇ,
‘vēṅki pūttuviṭṭatu; cantiraṉi ūrkōḷ cuṟṟi viṭṭatu’ eṉpatu veḷippaṭip poruḷ.
‘vēṅki pūkkum kālattil tiṉi muṟṟiviṭum ātaliṉ tiṉiyi arintuviṭuvārkaḷ; cantiraṉi ūrkōḷ cuṟṟuvatu vaḷarpiṟiyiṉkaṇ ātaliṉ, iravil matiyiṉ oḷi pakalpōla mikunta veḷiccam tarukiṟatu’ eṉpatu iṟiccip poruḷākiya kuṟippup poruḷ.
tiṉiyi arintu viṭuvatāl tiṉikkāvalukku iṉi vāyppilli yātaliṉ talivi tiṉippuṉattup pakkattu varuvatu iyalātu eṉavē pakaṟkuṟi nikaḻa vāyppilli eṉpatum, cantiraṉ oḷi pakalpōla vīcutaliṉ talivaṉ ūrkkāvalari maṟittu iravukkuṟikkaṇ varutaṟku vāyppilli eṉpatum, niṟi matināḷ tirumaṇattiṟku mika ēṟṟatu ātaliṉ ataṟkuc cila nāḷkaḷē iruttaliṉ, virivil talivaṉ taliviyi maṇattaṟ poruṭṭākap peṇkēṭṭal mutaliya ēṟpāṭukaḷic ceytal vēṇṭum eṉpatum, kuṟippiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟum piṟitoru kuṟippākiya iṟicciyiṟ piṟakkum poruḷ.	(po. 230. nac)
iṟiyōṉ iṟivi taṉmi iyampal -
talivaṉ kātalukkuriya taliviyiṉ aḻakiṉic ciṟappit tuk kūṟal.
“yāṉ virumpum peṇṇiṉ aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷik kūṟuki ṟēṉ, kēṭpāyāka: koṅki kurumpi; kuḻal koṉṟi; cevvāy kovvi; paṟkaḷ muttu; kaṇkaḷ kaḻunīr, mukam niṟimati eṉṉum vaṉappiṉi uṭiyavaḷ avaḷ” eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.	(kōvi. 108)
itaṉit tirukkōviyār avayavaṅkūṟal (108), kaṇ nayanturittal (109) eṉṟu kūṟum.
kaḷaviyalil itu ‘pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 144)
‘iṟivaṉ piriya illiruntāṟṟi niṉivu naṉikāttal’ -
kaṟpuk kālattil talivaṉ ōtal kāval mutaliyaṉa kuṟittup piriya, avaṉ mīṇṭu varuvatākak kūṟiya paruvam varum aḷavum talivi illattiruntu nallaṟam kāttu avaṉ niṉi vākavē avaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa ceyali muṭṭiṉṟu muṭittu mīḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu niṉittu iruttal (mulli naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 94 uri mēṟ.)
iṟiviyi eḷitil kāṭṭiya kaṭavuḷik kaṇṇuṟṟu iṟiñcal -
taliviyik kāṇacceyta kaṭavuḷip pāṅkaṉ vaṇaṅku tal.
iyaṟkip puṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu ivaṟṟiṉ piṉṉarp pāṅkaṉik kaṇṇuṟṟa talivaṉ avaṉukkut taṉnilimi kūṟit talivi paḻiya iṭattil iruppati aṟintu varumāṟu aṉuppa, avaḷ talivaṉ kuṟippiṭṭa iṭattiṟ kuc ceṉṟu taṉittu niṉṟu koṇṭirunta taliviyik kaṇṭu, “eṉṉit talivaṟku naṇpaṉākak kūṭṭi vitta teyvamē muyaṟciyiṉṟi ittaliviyik kaṇṭu cellu māṟu eṉakku eḷitiṉ aruḷpālittuḷḷatu. ataṟku nāṉ eṉṟum aṭimi” (tiruppatik. 67) eṉṟu taṉ kulatey vatti vaṇaṅkutal.
itu pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟām.	(mā. aka. 28)
iṟiviyi eḷitil kāṭṭiya teyvatti vaṇaṅkal -
talivaṟkākat taliviyik kāṇac ceṉṟa tōḻaṉ, āya veḷḷattiṭi avaḷi nāṭit taṉimiyil kāṇamuṭiyā mal varuntum nili nērātu, eḷitiṉ avaḷit tāṉ kāṇumāṟu avaḷit taṉiyē niṟkac ceyta teyvatti vaṇaṅkik kūṟutal.
kaḷaviyaluḷ itu pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uriyil koḷḷappaṭṭatu. 	(i. vi. 505 uri)
iṉcol irakkam -
tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattil talivaṟkuk kuṟi nērnta tōḻi, taliviyiṭam iṉcoṟkaḷāl uriyāṭi avaḷik kuṟi nayappikka accoṟkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttutal. situ ‘meṉ moḻiyāṟ kūṟal’ (kōvi. 83) eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.] (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
iṉpam -
orutalik kāmamākiya kikkiḷiyum aṉpuṭikkāmam ākiya aintiṇiyum, poruntāk kāmamākiya peruntiṇi yum, iṉpattiṉ pakuti. uḷattuṭaṉ uḷam porunti oṉṟupaṭṭu niṟkum kātal aintiṇippāṟpaṭum. (nāṭaka. 24, 25)
iṉpam mēvaṟṟātal -
aṟaṉum poruḷum oḻiya eñci niṉṟa iṉpam eṉappaṭuvatu āṇum peṇṇum eṉa aṭukkik kūṟaluṭittāy nukarcci nikaḻvatu.	(to. po. 223 nac.)
iṉpamum iṉpak kāraṇamum -
iṉpamum iṉpakkāraṇamum akattiṇiyic cārntaṉa.
	(vī. cō. 106 uri)
iṉmiyatu iḷivu oṉṟāmi -
‘iṉmiyiṉ iṉṉātatu yāteṉiṉ iṉmiyiṉ, iṉmiyē iṉṉātatu’ (ku. 1041) ātaliṉ, poruḷillār ivvulakil piṟarāṉ ikaḻappaṭṭu eḷiyarāvar eṉa uṇarum uṇarcciyip poruntāmi. tāṉ iṉpam nukarum talivi yiṉ ciṟappiṉi nōkka, avaḷip pirintu ceṉṟu iṉmi yiṉ iḷivu tīrap poruḷ tēṭi varutal eṉṉum eṇṇam ceyaṟpaṭāmal oḻital.	(to. po. 41 nac.)
iṉmiyatu iḷivum uṭimiyatu uyarcciyum oṉṟāmikkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟal -
taṉṉiṭam celvam illāviṭil nikaḻak kūṭiya iḻivu karutip poruḷ tēṭa niṉikkum uḷḷattic celvatti ṉum mikka taliviyatu uṇmik kātalukkut taṉ pirivu ūṟutarum eṉṟa eṇṇam iṭiyūṟākat taṭuttavaḻit talivaṉ kūṟutal.
e-ṭu :
“neñcē ‘iravalarkku ītalum tām iṉpaṅkaḷi nukartalum
poruḷ illātārkku illi’ eṉṟu karutip poruḷ cey
taṟkuriya viṉikaḷi mika eṇṇukiṟāy. avviṉikkut
tuṇiyāka aḻakiya māmi ciṟattavaḷākiya taliviyum uṭaṉ
varuvāḷō? emmi māttiram celuttukiṉṟāyō? col”
eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu. (kuṟun. 63)	(to. po. 41 nac.)
iṉṟiyamiyāmi kūṟal -
taliviyiṉatu iṉṟiyamiyāmiyit talivaṉ kūṟutal. ivaḷip pirintu ivaḷ illāmal eṉṉāl oru kaṇamum uyirvāḻtal iyalātu eṉṟa talivaṉ iyaṟkip puṇarcci iṟutiyil taṉṉuḷ kūṟik koḷvatu. itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘vaṉpuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatorukūṟṟu. itaṉit tirukkōviyār pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattup puṇarcci iṟutik kaṇ talivaṉ taṉṉuḷ kūṟiya kūṟṟākak kūṟik koḷḷum.		(kōvi. 46)
itaṉi ilakkaṇa viḷakkam uriyiṟ koḷḷum. 		(497 uri)
“iṉṟu ivvāṟu varuttamuṟum nī pirivukku uṭaṉpaṭṭami ēṉ?” eṉṟa tōḻikkut talivi kūṟiyatu -
muṉpellām (kātalaruṭaṉ kūṭi iṉpuṟṟakāli) eṉakku  naṭpāki iṉpa mūṭṭiya mālippoḻutu iṉṟu eṉakkup pakiyāki ivvāṟu nōyceytu varuttum eṉṟu eṉ talivar piriyātirunta kālattil nāṉ terintukoḷḷa villiyē! ataṉālēyē avar piriviṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭēṉ” eṉ talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟutal.	(kuṟaḷ 1226)
iṉṉa nāḷil varival eṉak kūṟi aṉṉa nāḷil variyātu piṉ avvāṟu kūṟum talimakaṟkut tōḻi kūṟiyatu -
tamakkōr iṭiyūṟum ceyyāta malivāḻ varuṭi māṉit tiṉiyi mēyum kiḷikaḷ añcumāṟu pōla, niṉvariviṟku iṭiyūṟu ceyyāta em uṟaviṉari añcukiṟāy. nī poyyurittalil vaṉmiyuṭiyāy. uṇmi pēcuvataṉkaṇ vaṉmiyuṭiyi alli” aiṅ. 287 eṉṟāṟ pōlat tōḻi kūṟutal.
iṉṉal eytal -
talivaṉ kaḷavukkālattē variporuṭkup pirintāṉāka, avaṉ pirivāṟṟāmiyāl melinta taliviyatu nili kaṇṭu am melivu teyvattāṉāyiṟṟu eṉṟu karutit tāy teyvattiṟku uvappāṉa ceyya veṟiyāṭaṟku vēlaṉi aḻippa, atu kēṭṭa talivi “nam illattil eṉ poruṭṭāka veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻumāyiṉ iṉi yāṉ uyir vāḻum neṟi illi” eṉat taṉṉuḷ kūṟi varuntal.
itu ‘variporuṭ pirital’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 287)
ī
“īṅku itu eṉ?” eṉap pāṅki viṉātal -
talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirinta maṟunāṭkāli taliviyik kāṇum tōḻi, “iḵteṉṉa? nāḷum niṉkaṇavaṉ putiya iṉpam tantu niṉṉi makiḻvikkum nāḷkaḷil ivvāṟu nī aḻutu kaṇ kalaṅkiyatu eṉ?” (tañci. kō. 379) eṉṟu viṉāvutal.
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. iḵtu uṇartta uṇarum ūṭal. 			(na. a. 205)
u
ucāttuṇi nilimiyiṉ cūḻtal -
talivaṉum taliviyum taṉṉiṭam uriyāṭum aḷavu avarkaḷoṭu paḻakum vāyppup peṟṟa niliyālē, talivaṟkum talivikkum puṇarcci nikaḻnta ceytiyit tōḻi, nāṟṟam tōṟṟam oḻukkam uṇṭi ceyviṉi maṟippu celavu payilvu eṉṟa eḻuvakiyum taliviyiṭam vēṟupaṭṭa nilikoṇṭu ārāytal.	(to. po. 126 nac.)
ucāvutal -
itu tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟkum eḻuvakikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. (204 iḷa.) veṟiyāṭṭum kaḻaṅkum iṭṭu urittuḻi vēlaṉoṭum piṟaroṭum tōḻi uriyāṭit talivaṉ taliviyariṉ kūṭṭam paṟṟiya ceytiyi aṟiyac ceytal. ik kūṟṟik kēṭṭa cevili uṇmiyi uṇarvāḷ.	(to. po. 112 iḷa. uri)
uṭkoṇṭu viṉātal -
iravukkuṟi nērnta tōḻi talivaṉ nāṭṭu aṇiyiyali viṉava, avaḷ viṉaviyataṉ karutti uṭkoṇṭu, avaṉ taṉ ūr makkaḷ aṇiyiyal kūṟiyatoṭu, tāṉ avaḷ ūrkku varutaṟkāka avaḷiṭam, “niṉ ūriṭattu makkaḷ iravil emmalar cūṭi eccāntu aṇintu emmaraniḻalil viḷiyāṭupa?” eṉṟu viṉavuvatu.
itaṉik ‘kiḻavōṉ avaḷnāṭṭu aṇiyiyal viṉātal’ eṉpa.	(na. a. 158)
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇ amintatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 153)
uṭkoḷa viṉātal
talivaṉukku iravukkuṟi nērnta tōḻi taṉṉāṭṭu makkaḷip pōla avaṉ varal vēṇṭum eṉpataṉi avaṉ aṟivataṟkāka, “taliva! niṉ ūriṭattu makkaḷ emmalarcūṭi enniṟac cāntu aṇintu emmarattiṉkīḻ viḷiyāṭupa?” eṉṟu talivaṉi viṉavutal.
itaṉip ‘pāṅki avaṉ nāṭṭu aṇiyiyal viṉātal’ eṉpa. 	(i. vi. 517)
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum  tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 158; )
uṭkōḷ (1) -
kikkiḷit talivi taṉmaṉattu eṇṇutal.
“ivaṉik kaṇṭatu mutal eṉṉuḷḷam uruki uṭalum iḷittuviṭṭatu. āyiṉ ivaṉi iṉṉum kūṭappeṟṟi lēṉ. iṉi eṉṉuyir tariyātu” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyuḷ peṇpāṟ kūṟṟuk kikkiḷikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(pu. ve. mā. 15 - 3)
uṭkōḷ (2) -
kikkiḷiyiṉ talivaṉ taliviyik kaṇṭu aiyuṟṟut tuṇinta piṉṉart taṉṉuḷḷattil niṉittal.
“ivaḷi nāṉ kaṇṭu āci koṇṭu tavikkiṉṟa niliyil ivaḷ eṉṉip pārkkavumāṭṭātu vēṟeṅkō cinti yāḷāy irukkiṉṟāḷē!” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyuḷ āṇpāṟ kūṟṟuk kikkiḷikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(pu. ve. mā. 14-4)
uṭkōḷ (3) -
taliviyik kaṇṭa talivaṉ avaḷ māṉuṭa makaḷē eṉṟu tuṇinta piṉṉart taṉṉiṭattu avaḷukku uṇṭāya kātali avaḷ kaṇṇil kaṇṭu taṉ maṉattu ataṉik koḷḷutal.
itaṉik ‘kuṟippaṟital’ eṉavum kūṟupa. 	(na. a. 122.)
iḵtu ‘iyaṟkip puṇarcci, eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 5)
uṭampaṭṭu vilakkal -
tōḻiyiṭam talivaṉ tāṉ taliviyi aṭitaṟku maṭalēṟa muṭivu ceyta ceytiyik kūṟa, tōḻi taliva ṉiṭam talivikkum taṉakkumiṭiyēyuḷḷa naṭpurimiyi eṭuttuk kūṟit tāṉ avaṉkuṟiyi muṭittut taruvatākak kūṟi avaṉ maṭalēṟuvati nīkkutal.
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘maṭaṟṟiṟam’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇ aminta iṟutik kūṟṟākum.	(kōvi. 81)
uṭampaṭātu vilakkal -
talivaṉ tōḻiyiṭam tāṉ taliviyip peṟutaṟku maṭalēṟa ēṟpāṭu ceytiruppatākak kūṟa, tōḻi, “yāṉ ceṉṟu avaḷatu niṉivi aṟintu varukiṉṟēṉ; avaḷ viruppatti yāṉ aṟintu vanta piṟaku numakku  vēṇṭiya vāṟu ceyka” eṉṟu kūṟal.
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘maṭaṟṟiṟam’ eṉṟa tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 80)
uṭaṉpōkkiṭic cevili curattiṭik kāṉavarmakaḷik kaṇṭu kūṟutal -
talivi uṭaṉpōyavaḻic cevili avaḷip piṉ toṭarntu ceṉṟa pālivaḻiyil kāṉavarmakaḷoruttiyik kaṇṭu, ‘koṅkikaḷ tōṟṟap polivu peṟṟu viṭṭaṉa; paṟkaḷ viḻunteḻuntuviṭṭaṉa; talimuṭi piṉṉum aḷaviṟkuk kūṭiviṭṭatu; taḻiyāṭi uṭukkum aḷaviṟku vayatu vantuviṭṭatu. iṉi nī pēti alli; petumpip paruvattu āyattāroṭu puṟam pōntu viḷiyāṭutal vēṇṭā’ eṉṟu kūṟiya eṉ āṇiyi maṟuttu valiyiṉiṉṟu tappic cellum peṇmāṉ pōla, vēlēntiya viṭaliyoruvaṉuṭaṉ iccurattiṉ vaḻiyē eṉmakaḷ vantuviṭṭāḷ. avaḷ uṭaṉpōkkiṉi aṟintu piṉ toṭarntu vanta eṉṉāl avarkaḷik kāṇa iyala villi. nī avarkaḷ ivvaḻic ceṉṟatik kaṇṭaṉiyā?” eṉṟu viṉavutal (akanā. 7) pōlvaṉa.			(to. po. 42 nac. uri)
kāṉavar makaḷ - eyiṟṟi. ‘cevili eyiṟṟiyoṭu pulampal’  eṉṉum nampi akapporuḷ (188).
uṭaṉpōkkiṭip piṉtoṭarnta cevili kaṭattiṭit taṉ neñciṟkuc collutal -
kaṭam - kāṭu. “eṉ kālkaḷ naṭi taḷarntuviṭṭaṉa; kaṇkaḷ palakāl kūrntu nōkkutaliṉ oḷiyiḻantuviṭṭaṉa. iv vulakattē ivvaḻiyiṭi eṉmakaḷ kāṇappaṭṭilaḷ; kāṇappaṭṭavarō, viṇṇil tōṉṟum mīṉkaḷiṉum palar!” (kuṟun. 44) eṉpatu pōṉṟa cevili kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 42 nac. uri)
uṭaṉpōkkiṭip piṉtoṭarnta cevili curattiṭik kurāmaram kaṇṭu, talivi ceṉṟa vaḻiyik kāṭṭa vēṇṭal -
kurā - pāli nilattu maraṅkaḷil oṉṟu. “periya kuravē! tāṉ tāyāka iruntu kōṅku taḷarntu taṉ arumpākiya mulikoṭuppavē nī pāvi īṉṟāy. īṉṟāḷukkuriya moḻi kāṭṭa māṭṭāyāyiṉum, eṉ makaḷ ceṉṟa vaḻiyi yāvatu vantu kāṭṭuvāy” (tiṇimāli. 65) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa cevili kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 42 nac. uri)
‘cevili kuravoṭu pulampal’ eṉṉum nampiyakap poruḷ (188)
uṭaṉpōkkiṭip piṉ toṭarnta cevili curattiṭik kurāvoṭu pulamputal -
“iṭituṭik kampali mutaliya iṉṉāta ōciyiyuṭiya kaṭuviṉiyāḷar tiriyum iccura neṭu vaḻiyil, kuravē! nī kavaliyuṟṟu niṉṟāy.  muṟpiṟappil pāvañceyta emmip pōla, nīyum niṉ pāviyic ciṉiyiṉiṉṟu paṟittuk koḷḷappaṭṭāyō? kūṟuvāy” eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa cevili kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 42 nac. uri)
‘cevili kuravoṭu pulampal’ eṉṉum na.a. (188)
uṭaṉpōkkiṭip piṉ toṭarnta ceviliyi iṭiccurattu “nī yār?” eṉṟu viṉāviṉārkku, avaḷ kūṟiyatu -
“kuṭattāl nīr mukakkappaṭāmiyāl kuṭam pukāta kiṇaṟṟuc ciṉṉīrip paruka vāykkāmiyāl vilaṅkukaḷ aṭiyāta neṭiya curattiṉkaṇ, uṭaṉākak kūṭiccellum kātalāl makiḻcciyoṭu kaṭantu ceṉṟa taṭamperuṅkaṇṇiyām aḻakiya makaṭku yāṉ tāyāvēṉ” eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa cevili kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 42 nac. uri)
uṭaṉpōkkiṟku oruppaṭṭa talivi alaraccam niṅkiṉami kūṟal -
“cilarum palarum kaṭikkaṇṇāl nōkki mūkkiṉmītu cuṭṭu virali vittu vītiyiṟ peṇṭir paḻikaḷit tūṟṟa, valakkiyil ciṟukōlic cuḻaṟṟi aṉṉi eṉṉi alippa, yāṉ itukāṟum varuntiṉēṉ. iṉi, tōḻi! taṉatu neṭuntēric celutti naḷḷiravil īṇṭu varum talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōkkiṟkut tuṇintuviṭṭēṉ. ikkoṭiya ūr alar cumantoḻika!” eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 42 nac. uri)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ kaṇṭōr ‘ūratu cārvum cellum tēyamum ārva neñcamoṭu ceppiya kiḷavi’ -
talivaṉum taliviyum uṭaṉpōyavaḻik kaṇṭōr avviruvarum vaḻiyiṭit taṅkap pakkattē ūr uṇṭu eṉavum, avarkaḷ pōka vēṇṭiya iṭam  neṭuntolivi luḷḷatu eṉavum aṉpu niṟinta uḷḷattōṭu avarkaḷatu naṉmi karutik kūṟi, aṉṟu aṇmiyiluḷḷa tammūril taṅki maṟunāṭkāli puṟappaṭalām eṉak kūṟutal.
“maṇittār mārpa! ippeṇ taṉ mellaṭikaḷāl iṉi naṭattalum iyalātu; katiravaṉum maṟiyaluṟṟāṉ; emmūr aṇmiyiṟṟāṉ uḷḷatu; ātaliṉ emmūril iravutaṅki nāḷi celka” (poruḷiyal) eṉṟa kūṟṟu.	 (to. po. 40 nac. uri)
‘kaṇṭōr kātaliṉ vilakkal’ eṉṉum kūṟṟāka itaṉi nampiyakap poruḷ collum. (182)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ kaṇṭōr tāynili kaṇṭu taṭuttal -
talivaṉum taliviyum uṭaṉpōyavaḻic cuittiṭik kaṇṭōr talivaṉum taliviyum tolitūram ceṉṟu viṭṭaṉar eṉṟu kūṟic ceviliyi mīṇṭupōmāṟu col lutal.	(to. po. 40 nac.)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ kaṇṭōr tāynili kaṇṭu viṭuttal -
talivaṉ taliviyōṭu uṭaṉpōyavaḻi avarkaḷi vaḻiyiṟ kaṇṭu pēci viṭuttavarkaḷ, piṟaku avarkaḷit tēṭik koṇṭuvanta cevilittāyiṉ maṉanili kaṇṭu, avarkaḷ ceṉṟa vaḻiyik kāṭṭi avvaḻiyē ceṉṟu avarkaḷik kāṇumāṟu viṭukkumpōtu kūṟutal.	(to. po. 40 nac.)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ kaṇṭōr ‘poḻutum āṟum uṭkuvarat tōṉṟi vaḻuviṉ ākiya kuṟṟam kāṭṭi’ moḻital -
talivaṉ taliviyōṭu uṭaṉpōyavaḻic curattiṭik kaṇṭōr “mālikkālamum kaṭattaṟkariya vaḻiyum añcat takkaṉa” eṉṟu kūṟi, avaṟṟatu tīṅkiṉāṉ uṭaṉpōkum iruvarkkum ēṟpaṭakkūṭiya iṭiyūṟṟiṉi eṭuttuk kūṟutal.	(to. po. 40 nac.)
‘kaṇṭōr kātaliṉ vilakkal’ eṉpatu nampiyakap poruḷ cuṭṭum kūṟṟu. (182)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ cevili curam ceṉṟu tēṭat tuṇital -
talivi uṭaṉpōyatu uṇarnta cevili, avaḷ tāy cēri muḻutum ceṉṟu tēṭiyum avaḷik kāṇātu varunti mīṇṭamiyiṉ, tāṉ avaḷ talivaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟa pāli vaḻiyil piṉtoṭarntu ceṉṟu avaḷit tēṭi aḻittu vara uṟuti koḷḷutal.	(to. po. 37 nac.)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ tammi viṉāviya cevilikku mukkōṟ pakavar aṟivuṟuttal -
talivi talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōkkuc cella, avarkaḷit tēṭikkoṇṭu avvaḻiyē vanta cevili appakkamāka mīṇṭu vantukoṇṭirunta mukkōliyuṭiya tuṟavikaḷi viṉava, avarkaḷ, “cantaṉam, pūcikkoḷpavark kaṉṟit taṉṉit tōṟṟuvitta malikku yātu payaṉic ceyyum? veṇmuttam, aṇintu koḷpavarkkaṉṟit taṉṉip piṟappitta kaṭalukku yātu payaṉpaṭum? iṉṉici, pāṭu pavarkkaṉṟit taṉṉi yuṇṭākkiya yāḻkku yātu payaṉpaṭum? ārāyuṅkāl, nummakaḷ numakkum attaṉmi yaḷē” eṉṟāṟpōla upatēcittu, “avaḷ taṉakkuc ciṟan tāṉit toṭarntu pōyiṉaḷ; atuvē avaṭkut tali yāya aṟam; maṟumiyiṉum avaṉip piriyāta neṟiyum atu” (kali. 9) eṉa aṟivuṟuttal.	(to. po. 40 nac.)
‘mikkōr ētuk kāṭṭal’ eṉṉum nampi akapporuḷ. (188)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ talivaṉtaliviyari iṭiccurattuk kaṇṭōr kūṟiya vārttiyik kēṭṭōrākac cilar kūṟiyami -
talivaṉ taliviyōṭu uṭaṉpōyavaḻi, avarkaḷic curattiṭik kaṇṭōr avarkaḷōṭu uriyāṭiya cey tiyit tām avvaḻiyē vantapōtu cevippaṭuttavarkaḷ, tām kēṭṭa ceytiyic ceviliyiṭam kūṟal.
“oḷi paṭitta nilāmukattu āyiḻi maṭantiyum veñ cuṭar umiḻum nīḷ vēlēntiya tōṉṟalum uṭaṉiṇintu varavē, “viṇṇulakam oru cuṭaru miṉṟākap pāḻpaṭumāṟu ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ eṉṉum irucuṭarum nilavulakattu vantu viṭṭaṉavō!” eṉṟu añcik kūṟiṉar, avarkaḷi iṭic curattuk kaṇṭōr” (tiṇimāli 71) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 40 nac.)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ talivi tamar vantuḻit talivaṉi maṟitta maliyi vāḻttiyatu -
“paṟṟit tuyaruṟuttumāṟu eṉ cuṟṟattār toṭarntuvara, eṉ talivaṉi maṟitta aruvikkuṉṟam nalviṉi niṟiva tākuka! ulakam vaṟkaṭamuṟṟa kālattum ellā nalviṉiyum niṟika!” (aiṅ. 312) eṉṟāṟpōlat talivi maliyi vāḻt tum kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 42 nac.)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ talivi nāṇ nīṅkiyami kūṟutal -
“nāṇ peritum iraṅkattakkatu; nammōṭu neṭuṅkālam uṭaṉiruntu nīṅkamāṭṭātu varuntiyatu; karumpukaḷ ōṅki vaḷarum āṟṟaṅkariyiṭattē niṉṟa īntiṉ kāykaḷ nīrpperukku neruṅki vantu mōtumtōṟum kīḻē ukkāṟ pōla, nāṇam taṭukkum aḷavākat taṉṉit tāṅki niṟuttikkoṇṭu kāmam mikuntuvantu alikkuṅkālattu eṉ elliyil nillātu pōyviṭukiṉṟatu.” (kuṟun. 149) eṉṟāṟ pōla uṭaṉpōya kālattē taṉatu nāṇ nīṅka luṟṟami paṟṟiya talivi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 42 nac.)
itaṉi nampi akapporuḷ ‘nāṇ aḻipu iraṅkal’ eṉṉum (182)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ talivi, “yāṉ pōkiṉṟami āyattārkku urimiṉ” eṉṟu avvaḻiyē ūr nōkkic celvāriṭam kūṟal -
“neṭuntolivākiya iṭam cellak karutiya antaṇīr! nummi oṉṟu vēṇṭiṉēṉ : emmūril nēriṟi muṉki yuṭiya eṉ āyattōrpāl, ‘tāy virumpi aṇivitta nuṇukiya aḻaku mēlum kaviṉumāṟu num talivi kaṭattaṟ kariya vaḻikkaṇ naṭantu cellaluṟṟāḷ’ eṉṟu kūṟumiṉ” (aiṅ. 384) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.(to. po. 42 nac.)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ, talivi “yāṉ pōkiṉṟami yāykku urimiṉ” eṉṟu āṟu celvāriṭam kūṟal -
“āṟu cel makkāḷ! ‘kaṭuṅkaṭ kāḷiyoṭu neṭuntēr ēṟik koṭiya malipala piṟpaṭa vēṟākiya pala curaṅkaḷik kaṭantaṉaḷ niṉmakaḷ’ eṉṟu eṉṉi iḻantu maṉikkaṇ vāṭiyirukkum eṉ aṟaṉillā yāykkuc ceṉṟu urimiṉ” (aiṅ. 385) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 42 nac.)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ naṟṟāy ‘accam cārtal’ eṉṟu aṉṉa piṟavum nimittam moḻipporuḷ teyvam eṉṟu avaṟṟoṭu tokiip pulampal -
talivi uṭaṉpōṉaviṭattu avaḷip payanta  tāy, talivi cellum kāṭṭakattup paṟavikaḷ vilaṅkukaḷ āṟalikaḷvar mutaliya kaṇṭu avaḷ añcum accam, tanti taṉṉiyar piṉ toṭarntu ceṉṟu tīṅku cey vārō eṉṟañcum accam ākiya iruvakiyaccam - talivi ceṉṟu cārum iṭam mīṇṭu vantu cārum iṭam eṉṟa iruvakic cārtal - eṉpavaṟṟiyum, avipōlvaṉa piṟavaṟṟiyum palli mutaliya col, naṟcol nimittam, teyvam kaṭṭiṉum kaḻaṅkiṉum iṭṭurikkum atteyvap pakuti eṉpavaṟṟoṭu kūṭṭi varuntik kūṟutal.			(to. po. 36 nac.)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ naṟṟāy kaṇṭōr pāṅkiṉ pulampal -
talivi uṭaṉpōyavaḻi avaḷip peṟṟa tāy taṉṉik kāṇa vanta ayalāruṭaṉ taṉ makaḷuṭiya uṭaṉpōkkuk kuṟittu varuntik kūṟal.
“iḵtu eṉ pāvi pōṉṟavaḷukku viḷiyāṭaṟkiṉiya pāvi; iḵtu eṉ kiḷi pōṉṟavaḷ eṭutta piṅkiḷi; iḵtu eṉ pūvi pōṉṟavaḷ uriyāṭaṟku iṉiya coṟ pēcum pūvi eṉṟu yāṉ ivaṟṟik kāṇuntōṟum kāṇuntōṟum kalaṅkumāṟu, eṉ makaḷ nīṅkiṉaḷē” (aiṅ. 375) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa naṟṟāy kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 36 nac.)
‘naṟṟāy, accat taṉmikku accamuṟ ṟiraṅkal’ eṉṉum nampi akapporuḷ. (185)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ naṟṟāy ‘kālam mūṉṟuṭaṉ viḷakkip pulampal -
uṭaṉpōya taliviyiṉ naṟṟāy taṉmakaḷatu kaḻintakāla iḷamic ceytiyiyum, nikaḻkāla aṟivu oḻukkac ceytiyiyum, etirkālattu maṉiyaṟa māṭciyiyum taṉ neñciṟku viḷakki varuntik kūṟal.	(to. po. 39 iḷa.)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ naṟṟāy cēri ceṉṟurittal -
talivi uṭaṉpōyavaḻi avaḷit tēṭik koṇṭu taṉ teru muḻutum naṟṟāy, avaḷ viḷiyāṭiya iṭaṅkaḷi yellām cēriyōrkkuk kāṭṭi varuntik kūṟal.
“vemmali aruñcurattiṉkaṇ, nām ivaṇ taṉittiruppa, koṭiya kāṉam nēṟṟup pōkiya perumaṭa muṭiyāḷākiya eṉmakaḷ, taṉ iṭikkut taḻiyaṇi puṉivittuk koḷḷa utavum noccikku ayalatāyt taṉ ciṟu viralkaḷāl varitta maṇal viḷiyāṭṭiṉi, kaṇṇuṭi makkāḷ! nīr kaṇṭīrō?” (akanā. 275) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa naṟṟāy kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 37 nac.)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ naṟṟāy talivaṉtāyit tuṉpam cārttik kūṟal -
talivi uṭaṉpōyavaḻi avaḷip peṟṟa tāy, taliva ṉip peṟṟa tāy taṉṉip pōla maṉattuyaram aṭital vēṇṭumeṉa varuttik kūṟal.
“puliyiṭamiruntu uyirtappiya āṇmāṉ taṉ peṇ māṉik kuraliṭṭu aḻikkum tīmi niṟinta pālivaḻi yil, eṉ makaḷi aḻittuc ceṉṟa viṭaliyip peṟṟa tāy, niṉikkuntōṟum kaṇṇīrip perukkumpaṭiyāṉa tuṉpatti yaṭivāḷāka!” (aiṅ. 373) eṉpatu pōṉṟa naṟṟāy kūṟṟu.
iḵtu ic cūttirattuḷ ‘aṉṉa piṟavum’ eṉpataṉāl koḷḷappaṭṭatu.	(to. po. 36 nac.)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ naṟṟāy, ‘taṉṉum avaṉum avaḷum cuṭṭik kālam mūṉṟuṭaṉ maṉṉum naṉmi tīmi muṉṉiya viḷakkip pulampal’ -
talivi uṭaṉpōṉaviṭattut taliviyip peṟṟa tāy taṉṉiyum talivaṉiyum taṉ makaḷiyum kuṟittu, iṟappu nikaḻvu etirvu eṉṉum mukkālaṅkaḷilum nilipeṟṟu varum nalviṉi tīviṉikkuriya ceyalkaḷit taṉ neñciṟku viḷakki varuntik kūṟal.
“eṉ kuṟumakaḷ pōkiya curam, mayilkaḷ āṭum uyar neṭuṅkuṉṟaṅkaḷil tāḻnta mēkaṅkaḷ maḻi poḻiya vaḻi iṉiyatu ākuka!” (aiṅ. 371) “taṉatu neñcam koḷḷat teruṭṭiya kāḷiyōṭē pala kuṉṟam iṟantu uṭaṉpōya eṉ makaḷ eṉṉiyum niṉittup pārttāḷō?” (aiṅ. 372) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa naṟṟāy kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 36 nac.)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ naṟṟāy taṉṉum avaṉum avaḷum cuṭṭip pulampal -
talivi uṭaṉpōyavaḻi, naṟṟāy makaḷip payanta taṉṉi yum talivaṉiyum avaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟa taṉ makaḷiyum niṉittu niṉittu varuntik kūṟal.	(to.po. 36 nac.)
“erumikaḷ niṟinta pāl vaḷam mikka taṉ illattiyum tāyar tōḻiyariyum viṭuttut taṉṉi aḻittuc ceṉṟa kāḷi pōlvāṉuṭiya poyccoṟkaḷil maṉam kalaṅki uṭaṉpōkit toliviluḷḷa, avaṉ aḻittuc cellum ūrukku vaḻiyil viḻuntu kiṭakkum nellikkāy kaḷit tiṉṟu cuṉiyiluḷḷa ciṟitu nīrip parukic cellat toṭaṅkiviṭṭāḷē eṉ makaḷ!” (naṟ. 271) eṉpatu pōṉṟa, taṉ makaḷip pirinta taṉimiyāl varuntik kūṟum naṟṟāy kūṟṟu.
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ naṟṟāy tuṉpam cārttip pulampal -
talivi uṭaṉpōyavaḻi, avaḷip peṟṟa tāy avaḷ vaḻiyiṭi nukarak kūṭiya tuṉpatti niṉittu varuntik kūṟal.	(to. po. 36 nac.)
“cempoṟ kalattiṉkaṇ aḻakiya pori kalakkappaṭṭa pāliyum miki eṉṟu parukiyaṟiyāta eṉ taḷir aṉṉa makaḷ, nīrillāta neṭiya curavaḻiyil, kaḻalōṉ tuṇiyāka uṭaṉvara, avaṉoṭu virintu naṭantu nīr aṟṟa cuṉikkaṇ maṟuki veppa muṟṟuk kuṟintupōṉa kalaṅkalnīrit tākam taṇiyak kuṭippataṟku evvāṟu vallunaḷ āvāḷō?” eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa naṟṟāy kūṟṟu.
‘naṟṟāy taṉ makaḷ meṉmit taṉmikku iraṅkal’ eṉṉum nampiyakap poruḷ. (186)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ naṟṟāy teyvattoṭu paṭuttup pulampal -
talivi talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōyavaḻi, avaḷip peṟṟa tāy teyvatti vēṇṭip pulamputal.
“ariya curattik kaṭantu taṉ makaṉōṭu eṉ kuṟumakaḷ varuvāḷeṉa avaṉtāy taṉ perumaṉi matilil cemmaṇ pūci maṉiyiṭattu muṟṟattē putumaṇaṟ parappip palaviṭat tum pūmālikaḷit toṅkaviṭṭu makiḻcci mika viḻā ayarvāḷ eṉpa. yāṉ immakaḷip peṟṟa avvurimikku aruḷāviṭiṉum, avaḷip palanāḷ kāttōmpi nalam puṉivitta utaviyi ammakaṉ aṟiyavallāṉō? mutuvāy vēla! iravellām iṭiyaṟātu kaṇṇīr vārum eṉ kaṇkaḷ iṉitu tuyil koḷḷumāṟu avaṉ avaḷi emmaṉikkaṇ muntuṟak koṇarvaṉō? taṉ maṉikkaṇ aḻittēkuvaṉō? avaṉ kuṟippu yāteṉak kaḻaṅkiṉatu tiṭpam kūṟuka.” (akanā. 195) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa naṟṟāy kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 36 nac. )
itaṉi ‘naṟṟāy curam taṇivittal’ eṉṉum tuṟiyākak kūṟum nampiyakapporuḷ (186), akkūṟṟu oruvakiyāṉ teyvatti vēṇṭip pulamputaṟkaṇ aṭaṅkum ātaliṉ.
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ naṟṟāy tōḻi tēettu vekuṇṭu kūṟal -
talivi uṭaṉpōyavaḻi, avaḷip peṟṟa tāy tōḻi yiṭam ciṉantu uriyāṭal.
“eṉ makaḷē! niṉtōḻi - (talivi) viḷiyāṭiya varippuṉi pantum, viḷiyāṭu miṭattuḷḷaṉavākiya vayalikkoṭi yum nocciyum yāṉē taṉiyaḷāyk kāṇkiṟēṉ. ataṉ mēlum, pālivaḻiyil veppam viḷaṅkum  amiyattē avaḷ amarceyyum kaṇṇaḷāy nōkki, veṉvēl viṭaliyākiya taṉ talivaṉi, naṭattaṟkariya ammali vaḻikkaṇ tuṉpam ceyvaḷō eṉa niṉiyumtōṟum maṉattē varuttamuṟat tuṉpam mikukiṉṟatu. (innilikku niṉ tōḻi uṭaṉpōkku nērumāṟu ceytuviṭṭaṉi!)” (naṟ. 305) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa naṟṟāy kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 36 nac.)
‘naṟṟāy pāṅki taṉṉoṭu cāṟṟal’ eṉṉum nampiyakap poruḷ (185)	(to. po. 36 nac.)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ naṟṟāy nimittam cārttip pulampal -
talivi uṭaṉpōyavaḻi, avaḷip peṟṟa tāy palli mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ col ākiya nalla cakuṉaṅkaḷoṭu, taṉ kuṭumpaniliyip poruttip pulamputal.
“ciṟu karuṅkākki! viṭaliyōṭu uṭaṉpōya eṉ añcilōtiyi varak karivāyāka. niṉakkup paciya iṟic cik kaṟi viraviya pintiṉic cōṟṟip poṟkalatti liṭṭut taruvēṉ” (aiṅ. 391) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa naṟṟāy kūṟṟu.		(to. po. 36 nac.)
‘naṟṟāy nimittam pōṟṟal’ eṉpatu nampiyakap poruḷ kiḷavi. (186)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ naṟṟāy niliyum āyattu niliyum kaṇṭōr kūṟal -
talivi uṭaṉpōyavaḻi, varuntiya aruḷuṭiya tāyiṉ niliyiyum tōḻiyar niliyiyum ayaliliruntu kaṇṭōr varuntik kūṟiyatu.
“vaḷampaṭitta marutanila vippiṉik kaṭantu ceṉṟa makaḷ poruṭṭākat tōḻiyar kūṭṭamum kaviṉ iḻantu varutta muṟṟatu; avaḷ tāyum, nocci niḻalil kuvaḷiyatu vāṭumalaric cūṭi maṇaliṟ pāviyik kiyileṭuttuk kaṇṇum neṟṟiyum nīvi, ‘eṉ marumakaḷē!’ eṉṟu taḻuvi, ‘niṉṉi īṉṟa eṉ makaḷit taruka; avaḷik kāṇamāṭ ṭāta eṉṉuyir aḻika!’ eṉṟu aḻukiṟāḷ” (akanā. 164) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa kaṇṭōr kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 36 nac.)
taliviyiṉuṭiya āyamum tāyum aḻuṅkak kaṇṭu kātaliṉ iraṅkum ‘kaṇṭōr irakkam’ eṉṉum nampiyakap poruḷ (187)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ naṟṟāy pantum pāviyum kaṇṭu pulampal -
talivi uṭaṉpōyavaḻi, avaḷip peṟṟa tāy avaḷ viṭuttuc ceṉṟa viḷiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷākiya pantu pommi ākiyavaṟṟik kaṇṭu taṉmakaḷ niṉippu mēliṭa varuntik kūṟal.
“nīrvēṭkiyāl muyaṉṟa varuttamikka yāṉi tūmpu pōlap perumūcceṟiyum curaneṭuvaḻikkaṇṇē eṉ makaḷ ceṉṟaṉaḷ; tāṉ viḷiyāṭiya pantum pāviyum kaḻaṅkum īṇṭuk kiṭappat tāṉ avaṟṟi viṭuttuc ceṉṟu viṭṭāḷē!”  (aiṅ. 377) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa naṟṟāy kūṟṟu.	 (to. po. 36 nac)
‘naṟṟāy talivi payiliṭam tammoṭu pulampal’ eṉpatu nampiyakapporuḷ ceyti. (185)
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ naṟṟāy moḻipporuḷ cārttip pulampal -
talivi uṭaṉpōyavaḻi, avaḷip payanta tāy taṉ kuṭumpa niliyic cakuṉac coṟkaḷoṭu poruttip pārttup pulamputal.	(to. po. 36 nac.)
taṉ makaḷ uṭaṉpōyiṉa aṉṟu ūrelliyi eyti nelliyum mulli malariyum tūvit teyvatti vaḻipaṭṭu niṉṟa nērattil, avaḷōṭu iyipillātā roruvar, ” virivil mīṇṭum varuvar, kavali vēṇṭā”  eṉṟāṟpōla (mullip. 7 : 17) maṟṟoruvariṭam uriyā ṭum naṟcollik kēṭṭu āṟutalaṭital.
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ ‘puṇarntōr pāṅkiṉ puṇarnta neñcamōṭu aḻintu (kaṇṭōr) etir kūṟi viṭuttal
1. puṇarntu uṭaṉvarum talivaṉ talivi eṉṟa iruva riṭamum vaḻiyiṭik kaṇṭavarkaḷ aṉpu koṇṭa neñcattōṭu avarkaḷ akkoṭiya vaḻiyil varutal kaṇṭu āṟṟāmi koṇṭu, ” iṉi uṭaṉpōtal eḷiya taṉṟu; num ūrkkē mīṇṭu cellumiṉ eṉṟu kūṟi avarkaḷi mīṇṭu pōkac collutal. (e-ṭu : kuṟun. 390) 
2. uṭaṉ pōvārāy vanta talivaṉ talivi eṉṟa iruvariṭamum aṉpuṟṟa uḷḷattoṭu vaḻiyiṭik kaṇṭōr avarkaḷatu nilimikku varuntit tam maṉak karutti avviruvariṭamum kūṟi avarkaḷi viṭukku miṭattuk kūṟutal.  (e-ṭu : ‘itunum mūrē’ eṉṟa pāṭal.)			(to. po. 40 nac.)
uṭaṉpōkku -
talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ taṉittup puṟappaṭṭu vēṟṟūr cērtal.
tāṉum taliviyum oḻukiya kaḷavoḻukkam palarāṉum aṟiyappaṭuvataṉ muṉ, tāṉ taliviyi maṇakkāmal kaḷavoḻukkattip palanāḷ nikaḻttit taliviyip pirivāṟṟāmiyāṉum notumalar varivup pēccāṉum iṟceṟippāṉum varuntacceytu, avaḷ taṉṉip pirintu toṭarntu vāḻa muṭiyātu eṉṟa nili vantapōtu, iraviṭi avaḷuṟaviṉar aṟiyāmal avaḷi aḻittuk koṇṭu vēṟṟūrukkup puṟappaṭṭut tam kaḷavoḻuk katti ūr aṟiyap paravumāṟu ceyta talivaṉatu ip poruntāk kāmap poruṭ ceyti, āciriyar cilarāl aintiṇi yiṉpattukku ēlāta akapporuṭ perun tiṇik kiḷavikaḷuḷ oṉṟākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu.			(na.a. 243)
uṭaṉpōkku iṭiyīṭu : nālvaki -
1.	pōkku aṟivuṟuttal - (aimpattāṟām nāḷ) tāṉ talivaṉuṭaṉ pōvatit tōḻiyarkkum tāykkum colvataṟkākat talivi etirē vantavarkaḷi aṉuppu talum, antaṇar naṟṟāyiṭam talivi uṭaṉpōṉa ceytiyi aṟivittalum;
2.	varavu aṟivuṟuttal - taṉ cuṟṟattār piṉ toṭarntu varuvatit talivi talivaṟku iṭiccurattil aṟivittal;
3.	nīkkam - talivaṉ taliviyi avaḷuṭiya cuṟṟat tāroṭu cērumāṟu iṭiccurattil viṭṭu nīṅkutal;
4.	irakkamoṭu mīṭci - talivaṉ taṉṉi viṭṭakaṉṟa piṉṉar, taṉṉi vantaṭinta cuṟṟattāruṭaṉ talivi mikunta tuyarattōṭu illattiṟku mīṇṭu varutal eṉpaṉa.	(na. a. 197)
uṭaṉpōkkuk kiḷavikaḷ (tirukkōviyār) -
paruvaṅkūṟal mutalāka amuṅku tāykku urittal īruka uḷḷa kiḷavikaḷ aimpattāṟum ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkuriya.	(kōvi. cū. 16 (194 - 249)
1. paruvaṅkūṟal 2. makaṭpēccu urittal. 3. poṉ aṇivu urittal. 4. aruvili urittal. 5. arumikēṭṭu aḻital. 6. taḷarvu aṟintu urittal. 7. kuṟippu urittal. 8. arumi urittal. 9. ātaram kūṟal. 10. iṟantupāṭu urittal. 11. kaṟpunalaṉ urittal. 12. tuṇintami kūṟal.  13. tuṇivoṭu viṉātal. 14. pōkku aṟivittal. 15. nāṇ iḻantu varuntal. 16. tuṇivu eṭuttu urittal. 17. kuṟiyiṭam kūṟal. 18. aṭiyoṭu vaḻiniṉintu avaṉ uḷamvāṭal.  19. koṇṭuceṉṟu uyttal. 20. ōmpaṭuttu urittal.  21. vaḻippaṭuttu urittal. 22. mellak koṇṭu ēkal. 23. aṭal eṭuttu urittal. 24. ayarvu akaṟṟal. 25. neṟivilakkik kūṟal. 26. kaṇṭavar makiḻtal. 27. vaḻi viḷiyāṭal. 28. nakar aṇimi kūṟal. 29. nakar kāṭṭal. 30. patiparicu urittal. 31. cevili tēṭal. 32. aṟattoṭu niṟṟal. 33. kaṟpu nilikku iraṅkal. 34. kavaṉṟu urittal. 35. aṭiniṉintu iraṅkal. 36. naṟṟāykku urittal. 37. naṟṟāy varuntal. 38. kiḷimoḻikku iraṅkal. 39. cuṭarōṭu irattal. 40. paruvam niṉintu kavaṟal. 41. nāṭat tuṇital. 42. koṭik kuṟi pārttal. 43. cōtiṭam kēṭṭal. 44. cuvaṭukaṇṭu aṟital. 45. cuvaṭukaṇṭu iraṅkal. 46. vēṭṭamātarik kēṭṭal. 47. puṟavoṭu pulattal. 48. kuravoṭu varuntal. 49. viratiyari viṉāval. 50. vētiyari viṉāval. 51. puṇarntu uṭaṉ varuvōrip porunti viṉāval. 52. viyantu urittal. 53. iyipu eṭuttu urittal. 54. mīḷa urittal. 55. ulaka iyalpu urittal. 56. aḻuṅku tāykku urittal - eṉṉum tirukkōviyāriṉ uṭaṉpōkkuk kiḷavikaḷām. kōvi-194-
uṭaṉpōkku nayanta talivaṉ ataṉit tōḻikku uṇartta avaḷ muṭippāḷāyc collutal -
talivaṉ taliviyi aḻittukkoṇṭu uṭaṉ pōk kiṉi nikaḻttak karutiya taṉ eṇṇattit tōḻiyiṭam kūṟa, tōḻi talivaṉiṭam taṉtaliviyi uṭaṉpōkkiṟku iṇaṅkac ceyvatākak kūṟal. (to.po. 39 nac.  innūṟ pāvil, ‘pōkkaṟkaṇ’ tōḻi kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkuva toru kūṟṟu itu.)
‘veṉ vēl viṭali! eyiṉar taṅkiyām iḷamā eyiṟṟi (talivi)kku niṉ maṉaniliyi aṟiyac colli nāṉ kuṟiyirappēṉ; atukāṟum nī viriyaṟka.’ (aiṅ. 364) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
‘pāṅki talivikku uṭaṉpōkku uṇarttal’ eṉpatu nampiyakap poruḷ kūṟṟu. (182)	
uṭaṉpōkku mutaliyavaṟṟil eñciyōr kūṟṟu -
eñciyōr - eṭuttu vitantu kūṟiya naṟṟāy kaṇṭōr tōḻi talivaṉ nīṅkalāka eñciyuḷḷa cevili, talivi, āyattōr, ayalōr eṉpār.
avi, cevili taliviyi niṉintu maṉiyiṉkaṇ mayaṅku tal, teruṭṭuvārkkuk kūṟutal, curattiṭip piṉceṉṟu navvippiṇāk kaṇṭu collutal, kāṉavar makaḷik kaṇṭu collutal, taṉ neñciṟkuc collutal, kuravoṭu pulampi ataṉi vaḻikāṭṭa vēṇṭal pōlvaṉa.
pōkkuṭaṉpaṭṭa talivi tōḻikku urittal, nāṇ nīṅki ṉami kūṟal, alaraccam nīṅkiṉami kūṟal, vaḻivaruvōriṭam āyattārukkut taṉ uṭaṉpōkkuc ceyti colli aṉupputal, tāṉ varuttamiṉṟit tērēṟic celvati naṟṟāykku urimiṉ eṉṟal, mīḷkiṉṟāḷ taṉ varavu āyattārkkuk kūṟi viṭuttal, mīṇṭa talivi vaḻivaral varuttam kaṇṭu varuntiya tōḻikkuk kūṟal, aiyaṉmār piṉ toṭarntavaḻi. nikaḻntatit tōḻikkuk kūṟal pōlvaṉa.
āyattār talivipirivukku varuntutalum ayalōr talivi mīṇṭu vantuḻik kūṟuvaṉavum pōlvaṉa.	(to. po. 42 nac.)
uṭaṉpōkku vaki -
kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭiṟkuriya kiḷavi mūṉṟaṉuḷ mutalāva tākiya uṭaṉpōkku eṭṭuvakippaṭum. aviyāvaṉa
1.	pōkku aṟivuṟuttal - tōḻi talivaṉiṭam taliviyi uṭaṉkoṇṭu pōmāṟu aṟivuṟuttal;
2.	pōkku uṭaṉpaṭāmi - talivaṉum taliviyum ataṉi maṟuttal.
3.	pōkku uṭaṉpaṭuttal - uṭaṉkoṇṭu celvatit tavira vēṟuvaḻiyilli eṉṟu talivaṉukkum, kaṟpiṉ mēṉmiyit talivikkum eṭuttuk kūṟi, tōḻi iruvariyum uṭaṉpōkkukku oruppaṭuvittal,
4.	pōkku uṭaṉpaṭutal - iruvarum uṭaṉpōkkiṟku oruppaṭutal;
5.	pōkkal - talivaṉuṭaṉ taliviyit tōḻi vaḻi yaṉupputal,
6.	vilakkal - uṭaṉpōkkil talivaṉiyum taliviyi yum kaṇṭōr, avarkaḷatu kaḷippiṉik kaṇṇuṟṟu avarkaḷatu celavit taṭuttut tam illattil taṅkip pōmāṟu vēṇṭal;
7.	pukaḻtal - talivaṉ taliviyip pukaḻntu kūṟutal;
8.	tēṟṟal - kaṇṭōr, talivaṉatūr arukiliruppatik kūṟiyum, talivaṉ taṉatūr vantuviṭṭami kūṟiyum taliviyit tēṟṟutal eṉpaṉa.	(na. a. 181)
uṭaṉpōkāniṉṟa talimakaṭkut talivaṉ colliyatu -
“pātirippūkkaḷ viḻuntu maṇam vīcum vēṉiṟkālattup pakaṟpōtil, yāmarattiṉ paṭṭikaḷik kaḷiṟu cuvittup pōkaṭṭa cakki kāyntu avvaḻiyē cellum uppuvaṇi karukkut tīmūṭṭum turumpāka utavum putarkaḷ aṭarnta pakutiyil pāmpukaḷ neḷiyumpaṭi kuṭṭikkaraṭikaḷ puṟṟāñ cōṟṟik kiḷaṟum malippakutiyil, kaṭattaṟkariya vaḻi yilē cīṟaṭi civappa eṉṉoṭu naṭantu vantu uṉ kūntali naṉku vāri veṇkaṭampa malarkaḷit toṭuttuc cūṭi uṉ kūntalil moykkum vaṇṭukaḷi ōṭṭutali aṟiyātu kikaḷi vaḷiyolippa vīcik koṇṭu eṉṉip piṉ toṭarntu varum niṉ aṉpu mētakkatu”  (akanā. 257) eṉpatu pōṉṟa kūṟṟu. (to. po. 41 nac.) ‘appāṟpaṭṭa orutiṟat tāṉum’ eṉpataṉāl taḻuvappaṭum oru kūṟṟu itu.
uṭaṉpōkiya talimakaḷit talimakaṉ maruṭṭic colliyatu -
