” kuṟumakaḷē! pulinakam pōlac civanta muki virinta muḷ muruṅkip pūkkaḷ kīḻē paravik kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. makaḷir nakil pōṉṟa kōṅkamoṭṭukkaḷ alarnta pūkkaḷum puṉalip pūkkaḷum palavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pātirippūkkaḷum veṇkaṭappampūkkaḷum viravit tōṟṟam aḷikkiṉṟaṉa. teyvattiṉ muṉṉiliyil kalantu kiṭakkum pūkkaḷip pōla ikkāṭu palavakip pūkkaḷik koṇṭu kāṇṭaṟku iṉitāka uḷḷatu. inta vaṉappiṉik kāṇpāyāka. mēlum niṉ tanti pōriṭaṟkup payaṉpaṭuttum kaḷiṟukaḷoṭu piṭikaḷum kaṉṟukaḷum cūḻa iruppatu pōṉṟu ciṟiyavum periyavumāṉa kuṉṟukaḷ cūḻntiruppataṉiyum kāṇpā yāka!”  eṉṟu taliviyi iṉpuṟuttit talivaṉ uṭaṉ pōkkil aḻittuc ceṉṟatu. (akanā. 99)
‘appāṟ paṭṭa orutiṟat tāṉum’ (to. po. 41 nac.) eṉpataṉāl koḷḷappaṭuvatu itu.
uṭaṉpōyt talivi mīṇṭuḻi ayalōr avaḷ tāykkuc colliyatu -
“makaḷit tuṟantamiyāl tuyaram varuttat taḷarntu aṟakkaṭavuḷi veṟuttup paḻikkum aṅkaṇāṭṭi! tuyaram mikka niṉ maṉattiṟkup pātukāppāka, veḷvēlviṭali (- talimakaṉ) muṉ vara, niṉmaṭamakaḷ illattiṟku vanta ṉaḷō!”  (aiṅ. 398) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 42 nac.)
uṭaṉpōy mīṇṭa talivi, ” nī ceṉṟa nāṭṭu nīr iṉiya alla; eṅṅaṉam nukarntāy?”  eṉṟa tōḻikkuk kūṟutal -
“aṉṉi! kēḷ. avar nāṭṭil taḻikaḷ vīḻnta kiṇaṟṟiṉ kīḻatāy māṉuṇṭu eñciya kalaṅkal nīr, namtōṭṭatti leṭutta tēṉ kalanta pāliṉum uṇṇutaṟkuc cuvi mikkatu!”  (aiṅ. 203) eṉṟāṟpōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu. (to. po. 42 nac.)
uṭaṉpōy mīṇṭa talivi, vaḻivaral varuttam kaṇṭu varuntiya tōḻikkuk kūṟutal -
” tōḻi! nalvarināṭaṉoṭu nāṉ itupōtu vantuviṭṭa kāraṇattāl iṉi varuntaṟka; mūṅkil taṉ vaṉappiṉi iḻattaṟkuk kāraṇamāṉa eṉ tōḷkaḷiyum, veyil teṟutalāl nuṇṇiya aḻaku keṭṭa eṉ nutaliyum nōkki nī varuntaṟka. vāḻi!”  (aiṅ 392) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 42 nac.)
uṭaṉpōy mīḷum talivi taṉ varavu āyattārkkuc colli viṭuttatu -
uṭaṉpōy mīḷkiṉṟa talivi, muṉ celvōriṭam tam varukiyip pāṅkiyarkku uṇarttumāṟu colli viṭuppatu.
“muṉṉuṟa virintu cellum makkāḷ! cennāy ēṟṟi yāṉatu paṉṟikkuṭṭiyik kavvātu nīṅkum aruñcura vaḻiyiṭi ivaḷ varukiṉṟāḷ eṉṉum ceytiyi em tōḻiyarkūṭṭattiṟku muṟpaṭa viriyac ceṉṟu collu miṉ”  (aiṅ. 397) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.			(to. po. 42 nac.)
uṭaṉpōy varinta neṭuntaki mīṭci urittal -
taliviyi aḻittukkoṇṭu ceṉṟu veḷiyūrkkaṇ maṇanta talivaṉ mīṇṭum taliviyūr varutalik kūṟal. itu kavi kūṟṟu.
taṉṉūril taṉ poṉ māḷikiyil cāṉṟavar muṉ taliviyi maṇantu iṉpuṟṟu vāḻumpōtu, talivikkut taṉṉūr niṉivu varavē, talivaṉ avaḷi aḻittuk koṇṭu avaḷūrkku varukiṉṟāṉ eṉpatu. (ampikā. 409)
itu variviyaluḷ mīṭci eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; uriyil ‘oṉṟeṉa muṭittalāl’ taḻuvik koḷ ḷappaṭṭatu.	(i. vi. 540 uri)
uṭaṉpōṉa taliviyi niṉittuc cevili maṉiyiṉkaṇ mayaṅkal -
“kiḷiyum pantum kaḻaṅkum virumpiya eṉmakaḷ taṉ aḷiyum aṉpum cāyalum piṟa iyalpum muṉpiruntaṉa pōla illāta vaḷāyiṉāḷ. nāṉ orumuṟi mellacceṉṟu avaḷatu nutali nīvit taḻuvikkoṇṭēṉāka, avaḷ ākam viyarppa eṉṉip paṉmāṇ muyaṅkiṉāḷ. ataṉ poruḷ aṟiyātupōṉēṉē! itupōtu viṟal miku neṭuntaki taṉṉip palavākap pārāṭṭa, maṭamāṉ kūṭṭam peyal nīr peṟāmal maralic cuvikkum kāṉ vaḻiyi avaḷ naṭattali yāṉ paṇṭē aṟintiruppiṉ, tantiyatu kāval mikka illattē avaḷ cellumiṭa mellām uṭalniḻal pōlat toṭarntu, avaḷ viḷiyāṭumiṭamellām akalātiruppēṉmaṉ!”  (akanā. 49) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa cevili kūṟṟu.
‘cevili iṉiyal eṉpōrkku etiraḻintu moḻital’ eṉṉum nampiyakapporuḷ. (184) 	(to. po. 42 nac.)
uṭaṉuṟi -
oru nilattil uṭaṉuṟikiṉṟa karupporuḷāṟ piṟitoru poruḷ payappa maṟittuk kūṟum iṟicci (to. po. 242 nac.) ‘iṟicci’ kāṇka.
uṭimiyatu uyarcci oṉṟāmi -
poruḷ nirampa uṭiyavarkaḷukku atu pōtum eṉṟa maṉaniṟivu vēṇṭum; attaku maṉaniṟivip porun tāmi. aḵtāvatu perumporuḷuṭiyār mīṇṭum poruḷīṭṭa virumputal. itu talivaṉ taliviyip pirintu cellak kāraṇamākak koḷvaṉavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu. (to. po. 41 nac.)
uṇṭik kālattu maṉikkaṇ varutalum curuṅkit talivaṉ parattiyiṭattāṉāka, avaṟku vāyilāka vantārkkut talivi kūṟiyatu -
erumi taṉṉik kaṭṭiyirunta kayiṟṟi aṟuttuk koṇṭu pōy viṭiyalilē vayalil mēyum  ūraṉ (ātaliṉ, vilakku vārkku aṭaṅkātu puṟattoḻukkattil tiḷittu niṟkum nam talivaṉ) pakalilum uṇavuṇṇa nam illattukku vārātu eṉakkup peruntuyaram ceytuḷḷāṉ”  eṉṟu talivi kuṟippāṟ pulappaṭumāṟu kūṟutal.	(aiṅ. 95)
uṇṭiyāl aiyamuṟṟu ōrtal -
talivaṉiyē niṉintu ēṅkum talivi uṇavil mikunta nāṭṭam koḷḷāmal muṉṉiṉum kuṟinta aḷavē uṇṭalik kaṇṭu aiyuṟṟup puṇarcciyuṇṭeṉṟu pāṅki tuṇital.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅki mati yuṭaṉpāṭu’ eṉṉum toku tikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 139 i. vi. 507)
uṇṭiyiṉ uvattal -
talivi camitta uṇaviṉi uṇṭa talivaṉ ataṉi uvantu kūṟutal. “eṉṉuyir aṉiyāy! mukattil aḻakiya viyarttuḷi arumpa, aruṅkayaṟkaṇṇil civappup parava, tāḷippuppuki kamaḻa nī aṭṭa porik kaṟiyum iṉiya pāṟ cōṟum tēvar uṇṇum amutamē pōṉṟaṉa”  eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
iḵtu ampikāpati kōviyuḷ ilvāḻkkip pakutiyil orukūṟṟu.	(ampikā. 442)
uṇmi kūṟi varivu kaṭātal -
pakaṟkuṟi nikaḻtti vanta talivaṉit tōḻi virivil taliviyi variyumāṟu vēṇṭa, ataṟku uṭaṉpaṭātu, taliviyi mika uyarttik kūṟiya talivaṉiṭam avaḷ, “malināṭa! eṅkaḷ peṟṟōr kāṉavar; eḷiya makkaḷ. nī varivu virumpāmiyiṉ eṅkaḷip puṉintu kūṟutal vēṇṭā”  eṉat taṅkaḷ kuṭumpattu uṇminiliyi eṭuttuk kūṟit taliviyi virivil maṇakkumāṟu talivaṉi vēṇṭutal.
itu ‘pakaṟ kuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (kōvi. 130.)
uṇmi ceppum kiḷavi -
itu tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟkum vaki ēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu.
uṇmi cepputalāvatu nikaḻntati nikaḻntapaṭi kūṟutal. 			(112 iḷa. uri)
paṭittu moḻiyāmal, nikaḻntati nikaḻntavāṟē kūṟutal. eḷittal ēttal mutaliyavaṟṟic ciṟitu kalantu uṇmi cepputalu muṇṭu.	(to. po. 207 nac.)
uṇartta uṇartal -
kaḷavoḻukkattit tōḻi uṇarntatit talivaṉ uṇarttat talivi ataṉi aṟital.
talivaṉ puṇarcciyiṟutikkaṇ talimakaḷik kōlam ceyya avaḷ mikavum nāṇat talivaṉ, “niṉtōḻi ceyyumāṟē kōlam ceytuḷḷēṉ”  eṉṟu kūṟat talivi, talivaṉ tōḻiyic cantittirukkum ceyali uṇarvāḷ. itaṉāl payaṉ, talivi taṉ kaḷavoḻukkattiṉit tōḻi yiṭam aṟivittaṟkut talivaṉ vaḻiceytukoṭuttami.			(iṟi. a. 4)
uṇartta uṇarā ūṭal  -
parattiyiṟ pirintuvanta talivaṉ, taṉmēl tavaṟu ētu miṉṟu eṉap pala kāraṇam kāṭṭiyum, ceyta tavaṟṟiṉip poṟuttuk koḷvāyeṉa vēṇṭiyum, taliviyit teḷivitta iṭattum, avaḷ taṉ vekuḷi nīṅkāḷāyt talivaṉiṭam ūṭal nīṭittal; iṅṅaṉam taliviyiṭattē uṇarppuvayiṉ vārā ūṭal tōṉṟumāyiṉ,  talivaṉum avaḷatu paṇpiṉmi karuti veṟuttu avaḷiṭam ūṭal koḷḷutalu muṇṭu. iv ‘vuṇartta uṇarā ūṭal’ eṉṟa kaṟpukkālak kiḷavittokikkaṇṇē ‘veḷḷaṇi aṇintu viṭuttuḻit talivaṉ vāyil vēṇṭal’ mutalākat ‘pāṅki talivaṉi aṉpili koṭiyi eṉṟu ikaḻtal’ īṟākap patiṉāṉku kūṟṟukkaḷ uḷa. itu ‘mutiya kalām’ eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.	(na. a. 206; iṟi. a. 50)
uṇartta uṇarum ūṭal -
talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintuvantu vāyil vēṇṭiyavaḻit talivi vāyil maṟukkavē, talivaṉ taṉmītu kuṟṟamiṉṟu eṉṟu talivi maṉam koḷḷum vaki teḷivittum, taṉ kuṟṟattip poṟukkumāṟu avaḷi vēṇṭiyum avaḷatu ūṭalip pōkkuvittal.
iḵtu ‘iḷiya kalām’ eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.
itu ‘kātalaṉ pirivuḻik kaṇṭōr pulavikku ētu ītām ivviṟivikku eṉṟal’ mutalākap ‘puṇarcciyiṉ makiḻvu’ īṟākak kūṟappaṭṭa patiṉoru kiḷavikaḷi yuṭiyatu.			(na. a. 205)
uṇarppu -
uṇarttutal; talivaṉiṭam parattimi karuti avaṉōṭu ūṭutal koṇṭa talivikkut taṉmāṭṭu attakiya tavaṟu etuvumiṉṟu eṉṟu talivaṉ taṉ coṟkaḷālum ceyalkaḷālum teḷivittalum, taṉ parattimi maṟikka muṭiyāta aḷavu veḷippaṭṭatāyiṉ “tappiṉēṉ”  eṉa vaṇakkattuṭaṉ kūṟi avaḷi amitiyuṟuttalum (kali. 89)  ām.	(to. po. 499 pērā.)
uṇarppuvayiṉ vārā ūṭaṟkaṇ talivaṉ taṉṉuḷ kūṟiyatu -
“yāṉ uṇarttiyum taṇiyappeṟāta ivaḷ ūṭalāl yāṉ tuṉpuṟumpōtu eṉakkurimi vāynta eṉ neñcamē tuṇiyākavilliyeṉiṉ, vēṟu yārtām eṉakkut tuṇiyāvār? ātalāl ittuṉpatti yāṉ uṟṟē tīral vēṇṭum”  eṉṟu uṇarppuvayiṉ vārā ūṭaṟkaṇ talivaṉ taṉakkuḷ kūṟik koḷḷutal.	(kuṟaḷ 1299)
uṇarvu (1) -
talivaṉ taliviyatu ūṭali nīkkac ceyyum ceyalkaḷ.
ūṭal nikaḻntavaḻi ataṟku ētuvākiya kāriyamiṉmiyit talivaṉ uṇarvittal ‘uṇarvu’ eṉappaṭum. illatu kaṭutta (aiyuṟṟa) mayakkam tīra uṇarvittalāṉ, uṇarttutal eṉavum, ataṉi uṇartalāṉ ‘uṇarvu’ eṉavumpaṭum.			(to. cey. 187 pērā.)
uṇā -
aivaki nilattu uṇavukaḷākiya karupporuḷ vaki.	
kuṟiñci	-	mūṅkilnel, malinel, tuvari, tiṉi; tēṉ, miḷaku, 	iñci, mañcaḷ.	
mulli	-	varaku, cāmi, mutiri
pāli	-	vaḻippaṟi, ūrkaḷuṭ ceṉṟu koḷḷi yaṭittal						 mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ koṇarntaṉa.
marutam	-	cennel, veṇṇel; kaṉṉal, kaṉi varukkam, 						cēmpu, karuṇi, cēṉi, mañcaḷ.
neytal	- 	mīṉ, uppu, veṇcaṅku muttup pavaḷam ivaṟṟi 						viṟṟup peṟṟaṉa.			(na.a. 20-24;  cāmi. 74-79)
‘uyttuṇarvōri urimiṉ eṉṟal’ -
uṭaṉpōṉa taliviyit tēṭip pālinilattil ceṉṟa cevili etirkālatti uṇarntu kūṟum cāṉṟōrkaḷi vaḻiyiṭik kaṇṭu taṉmakaḷ etirkālam paṟṟi viṉavutal.
“nāṉmaṟi antaṇīr! iṟivaṉ aruḷāl emmaṉik kaṇṇēyē emmakaḷatu tirumaṇatti naṭattum vāyppu emakku nikaḻumō eṉṟu ārāyntu tītiṉṟik kūṟumiṉ”  (kōvi. 236) eṉṟu cevili takkōri viṉavutal.
itaṉi ‘cōtiṭam kēṭṭal’ eṉṉum tirukkōvi yār. (236)
ituvariviyaluḷ ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(i.vi. 538 uri.)
uyarntōṉ -
potumakkaḷiviṭa uyarnta kiḷavittalivaṉ. (na. a. 19) kiḷavittalivaṉiviṭa uyarnta pāṭṭuṭittalivaṉ.	(na. a. 246)
uyarmoḻi -
talivaṉiyum taliviyiyum uyarttuk kūṟum kūṟṟu. itu tōḻik kurittu. ‘uyarmoḻik kiḷavi’yum atu. 	(to. po. 240 nac.)
uyarmoḻik kiḷavi -
‘uyarmoḻi’ nōkkuka
uyarmoḻiyum uṟaḻkiḷaviyum -
kaḷavilum kaṟpilum iṉpam uyartaṟkuk kāraṇamākak kūṟṟu nikaḻttumiṭattut tōḻi uyarmoḻi kūṟumiṭattē talivi māṟupaṭak kūṟuvāḷ. talivaṉ uyarmoḻi kūṟiyaviṭattut talivi  māṟupaṭak kūṟuvāḷ. talivi uyarmoḻi kūṟiya viṭattut tōḻi māṟupaṭak kūṟuvāḷ. 
uyarmoḻi - oruvarip pukaḻntu kūṟum col; iṉpam uyartaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya kūṟṟu.
uṟaḻ kiḷavi - etirmoḻiyāka māṟupaṭak kūṟum kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 238 nac.)
uyireṉak kūṟal -
taliviyit talivaṉ taṉṉuyirākak kuṟippiṭutal.
teyvat tiruvaruḷāl, vēṭṭi mēṟ ceṉṟu uṭaṉirun tōrip pirintu, taṉiyaḷāka niṟkum taliviyi etirp paṭṭu avaḷik kūṭi avaḷ taṉakku vāḻnāḷ muḻutum iṉṟiyamiyātavaḷ eṉpati yuṇarntu, ” uyir pulaṉā kātatu eṉṟu kūṟukiṟārkaḷē! mārpil vaḷarkiṉṟa koṅkikaḷōṭu oḷi vīcum niṟimatiyam pōla atu kāṭci vaḻaṅkukiṟatē!”  eṉṟāṟ pōlat taṉakkuḷ taliviyit taṉṉuyirākavē talivaṉ kūṟi matittal.
taliviyiṭattuḷḷa ātaraviṉ mikutiyālē avaḷit talivaṉ taṉ ‘uyireṉa viyattal’ eṉṟu kūṟum kaḷaviyaṟ kāriki.	sa(25) pak. 18]
iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarcci eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ta. ne. vi. 15)
uyireṉa viyattal (1) -
iyaṟkip puṇarcci yiṟutikkaṇ taliviyiṭattuk koṇṭa ātaraviṉ mikutiyālē talivaṉ avaḷit taṉatu uyir eṉṟu viyantu kūṟal.
itaṉit tamiḻneṟiviḷakkam ‘uyireṉak kūṟal’ eṉṉum. atu kāṇka.
ittuṟi ‘iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉṟa kiḷavikkaṇṇa toṉṟu.	saka.kā. (25) pak. 18]
uyireṉa viyattal (2) -
pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattukkaṇ poḻil kaṇṭu makiḻntu poḻi liṭicceṉṟu pukkut talivaṉ taliviyik kaṇṭa aḷavil avaḷit taṉ uyirākak kūṟi avaḷ ceyalkaḷit taṉṉuyir ceyvaṉavākak kūṟutal.
itaṉi ilakkaṇaviḷakkam ‘iṭantalippāṭu’ eṉṉum tokutiyiṭi ‘muntuṟak kāṇṭal’ eṉpataṉkaṇ koḷḷum. (503)
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭattuk kaṇṇa toru kiḷavi.	(kōvi. 390)
‘uravōṉ nāṭum ūrum kulaṉum, marapum pukaḻum
vāymiyum kūṟal’ -
tōḻi talivaṉiṭam avaṉuṭiya nāṭu, ūr, kuṭimi, marapu, pukaḻ, vāymi kākkum iyalpu ākiya perumikaḷik kūṟi, attakiya mēmpāṭuṭiyavaṉ taliviyi maṇantu koḷ ḷātu kaḷaviṉi nīṭṭippatu muṟimiyaṉṟu eṉṟu kūṟutal.
‘mulivili kūṟal’ (kōvi. 266) eṉpataṉuḷ iḵtu aṭaṅkum.
“kuṟiyiṟkum, kalvikkum, celviṟkum, niṉkulattiṟkum makaṭkēṭṭuvaruvōr niṟiviṟkum mētaku nītikkum ēṟpa emar makaṭkoṭi nērvar aṉṟit talivikku ēḻulakum muliviliyākamāṭṭā”  eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟāka im ‘mulivili kūṟal’ tirukkōviyāruḷ amintuḷḷatu. ‘uravōṉ.................. kūṟal’ itaṉkaṇ aṭakkappaṭṭatu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘varivu kaṭātal’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 166)
uripporuṭṭalivaṉ -
kiḷavittalivaṉ; ‘uripporuṭṭalivaṉ oruvaṉē āṉavāṟum’ (to.po. 55 nac. uri) ivaṉukku iyaṟ peyar kūṟuvatilli.	(ṭu)
uripporuḷ -
ovvoru nilattiṟkum ciṟappāka urimiyuṭiya oḻukkam. kuṟiñcikkup puṇartalum, pālikkup piritalum, mullikku iruttalum, marutattukku ūṭalum, neytalukku iraṅkalum uripporuḷkaḷām. puṇartal mutaliya aintaṉ nimittaṅ kaḷum uripporuḷāy aṭaṅkum. oru pāṭaluḷḷēyē uripporuḷ iraṇṭu mayaṅki varalām.	(na. a. 25)
ivvuripporuḷkaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭē kuṟiñci mutaliya tiṇippeyarkaḷ amintaṉa eṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar karuttu.	(to. po.5)

uripporuḷ allaṉa -
mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal pāli eṉṉum aintiṇi kaḷukkum uriya uripporuḷkaḷākiya iruttal, puṇartal, ūṭal, iraṅkal, pirital eṉpaṉa allātaṉavākiya kikkiḷi peruntiṇic ceytikaḷ	(to. po. 3 nac.)
mulli mutaliya tiṇikaḷiṉ uripporuḷkaḷākiya iruttal mutaliyaṉa allāta mutaṟporuḷ karup poruḷkaḷ.	(15 iḷa.)
talivaṉ taliviyi uṭaṉkoṇṭu cēṟal, iṭiyē taliviyiṉ uṟaviṉar avarkaḷit taṭuttut taliviyip pirittukkoṇṭu cellutalāl pirivu paṟṟi varuntal, talivaṉ talivi iruvarum mutalil cantittal, tam uḷḷa niliyik kaṇkaḷāl terivittuk koḷḷutal mutaliyaṉa mulli mutaliya tiṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟkum nērāṉa urip poruḷ ākātaṉa. ivi ellā nilattum vantu mayaṅkum.		(akat. 13 pārati)
uruvi -
pāṭṭuṭittalivaṉ; ‘uruvi yākiya oruperuṅ kiḻavaṉi’(yā. vi. pak. 565)
uruveḷittōṟṟam -
iṭiviṭā niṉippiṉāl etiriluḷḷatu pōl tōṉ ṟum pōlittōṟṟam.	(ṭu)
uruvu veḷippaṭṭu niṟṟal -
kaṟpuk kālattil taliviyiṭam nēril uṇarttātu poruḷ tēṭap pirinta talivaṉ kāṇumiṭantōṟum taliviyiṉ vaṭivam taṉakku uruveḷiyākak kāṭciyaḷittaliṉ, kuṟitta iṭam nōkkic cella iyalātu mīṇṭu vara muṭivuceytu pālinilattē taṭumāṟi niṟṟal.
itaṉit ‘talivaṉ curattiṭit taliviyi niṉittal’ eṉpa.	(i. vi. 558 uri)
itu ‘poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 341)
uruveḷippāṭu kaṇṭa talivi talivaṉ kaṇṇaḻakukku iraṅkal -
talivaṉiṭattē iṭiyaṟātu taṉ maṉattic celuttiya talivikku anta niṉiviṉ mikutiyāl avaṉē taṉ kaṇṇetirē niṟpatu pōṉṟa uruveḷippāṭu tōṉṟa, anta aḻakaṉatu kaṇṇaḻakil īṭupaṭṭa avaḷatu kūṟṟāka varuvatu itu .
“itō teriyum emperumāṉuṭiya kaṇkaḷ iraṇṭum utayakirimītu aṉal vīca eḻunta iru ñāyiṟukaḷ pōlac ceṅkatirkaḷip parappukiṉṟaṉa. ñāyiṟṟu neruppil vīḻntu iṟakkum ‘mantēhar’ eṉṉum acurarkaḷip pōlavē emperumāṉmītu kātal koṇṭa eṉ pōṉṟavarkaḷum ataṉkaṇ vīḻntu uyirviṭattāṉō intak kaṇkaḷ eṉpārvikku eṅkum terikiṉṟaṉa!”
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉpāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvi. 82)
‘uri eṉat tōḻikku urittal’ -
1. talivaṉ tam kaḷavoḻukkattip paṟṟit tōḻiyiṭam urikkumāṟu kūṟumpōtu talivi ataṉit tōḻikkuk kūṟal.
“tōḻi! poṉpōṉṟa pūṅkottukkaḷiyuṭiya vēṅki maraṅkaḷāl aḻaku peṟṟa pūmpoḻiṟkaṇ malināṭaṉ eṉatu nalam puṉiyak kaḷaviṉāl innāḷ pōyiṟṟu.”  (ain.aim.11) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 110 iḷa. uri)
2. virivil kaḷavoḻukkam tavirttut taṉṉi maṇantu koḷḷumāṟu talivaṟkuk kūṟumāṟu talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟal.
“tōḻi! yātāmō? avariṭattum naṉmiyilli; tāy mukattum naṉmi tōṉṟiṟṟilatu. pūṅkāṉaṟ cērpparām avari ivvāṟu eṉṉi variyātu kaḷavil nīṭṭittal takkatō eṉṟu uri. niṉṉiyaṉṟi itaṉkaṇ eṉakkut tuṇiyilli”  (ain.eḻu. 58) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(112 nac. uri)
urikaḷiṉ vaki -
caṭṭakam, tiṇi, kikōḷ, naṭi, cuṭṭu, iṭaṉ, kiḷavi, kēḷvi, moḻi, kōḷ, uṭpeṟu poruḷ, coṟporuḷ, eccam, iṟicci, payaṉ, kuṟippu, meyppāṭu, kāraṇam, kālam, karuttu, iyalpu, viḷivu, uvamam, ilakkaṇam, puṭiyuri, moḻi cēr taṉmi, poruḷaṭivu eṉṉum irupattēḻum akapporuḷiṉukku uriyām. akappuṟap poruḷukkum, puṟapporuḷukkum, puṟappuṟapporuḷukkum ivaṟṟuḷ ēṟpaṉa koḷḷappaṭum.	(vī. cō. 90, 91)
urikēṭṭu nayattal -
kāmattāl tuṉpuṟṟa peruntiṇit talivi kātalaṉ collik kēṭṭu makiḻtal.
“eṉ uyirē aṉiya kātalaṉ kūṟum aṉpumoḻikaḷik kēṭkumpōtu nāṉ tuykkum iṉpam peritu, ūrār kūṟum alarum paḻiyum paṟṟi eṉakkuk kavali illi”  eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyuḷ peruntiṇiyiṉ oru pakutiyākiya peṇpāṟ kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. 	(pu. ve. mā. 16 : 16)
urittatu maṟuttal -
talivaṉiṭattēyē uḷḷattic celuttiya talivi, tōḻi pōlvār viṉāyavaṟṟiṟkut taṉmaṉam īṭupaṭāmal, urittirukkum maṟumāṟṟatti maṟantaṉaḷāyiruppavē, piṉṉart tōḻi talivi kūṟiya  coṟkaḷ paṟṟi viṉaviya kālit tāṉ ac coṟkaḷik kūṟavilli eṉṟu maṟuttuk kūṟal.
innilikkuk kāraṇam taliviyiṉ uḷḷatti uḷḷavāṟu uṇarntavarkkē pulaṉām ātaliṉ iḵtu akameyppāṭu 
(vī. cō. 90) muppattiraṇṭaṉuḷ oṉṟu. 	(vī. cō. 96 uri)
ulakiyal -
ulakattē potuvāka naṉmakkaḷiṭi nikaḻum nikaḻcci. pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkamākiya ceyyuḷvaḻakkattiṟku ulakiyal paṟṟiya ceytikaḷi iṇittuk koḷvatum iṉṟiyami yātatu.  	(na. a. 2)
‘nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum
pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam’	(to.po. 53 nac.)
ulakiyal urittal -
uṭaṉpōya taliviyit tēṭic ceṉṟa cevilikku vaḻiyiṭic cāṉṟōr ulakanaṭappik kūṟutal.
talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōṉa taliviyit tēṭikkoṇṭu cevili pālippakutic cella vaḻiyiṭik kaṇṭa cāṉṟōrit tammakaḷ paṟṟi viṉava, avarkaḷ, ” cantaṉamum muttum iciyum piṟanta iṭattup payaṉpaṭātu maṟṟavark kup payaṉpaṭumāṟu pōla, oruvar peṟṟa peṇṇum ava rukkup payaṉpaṭātu maṟṟavarukkē payaṉpaṭuvāḷ ātaliṉ, avaḷippaṟṟik kavaliyuṟāmal avaḷ ceytatu ulakiyaṟku ēṟṟatē eṉṟu uṭkoṇṭu ūrukku mīṇṭu celka”  eṉṟu avaḷukkuk kūṟal.
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. itaṉi ‘mikkōr ētuk kāṭṭal’ (na. a. 188) eṉavum kūṟupa.
ulakiyal kūṟi nīyuri eṉṟal -
tirumaṇam ceytukoḷḷum ulakamarapi eṭuttukkūṟi, talivitamari eytip paricap poruḷ paṟṟi aṟiyumāṟu tōḻi talivaṟku aṟivuṟuttal. kaḷavu nīṭṭittavaḻi aḵtu alarākiya taṉmiyit tōḻi talivaṟkuk kūṟi ulakattu vaḻakkappaṭit taliviyi maṇantukoṇṭu illaṟam nikaḻtta āvaṉa ceyyumāṟu vēṇṭa, talivaṉ ” talivik kup paricap poruḷ evvaḷavu vēṇṭuvar?”  eṉa avaḷi viṉava, avaḷ, ” aṇṇalē! “atu paṟṟi eṉakku yātu teri yum? ivvulakiyē paricapporuḷākat tariṉum, talivi tamar maṉaniṟivu aṭiyamāṭṭār! nīyē makaṭ kēṭpataṟku āvaṉa ceytu paricam mutaliya paṟṟi aṟintu virivil taliviyi maṇattaṟku muyalka” (ka.kā.mēṟ.) eṉṟatu.
itu kaḷaviyaṟ kārikiyuḷ ‘paricaṅ kiḷattal’ eṉṟa kūṟṟu. 	(pak. 115)
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku valittal’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(ta. ne. vi. 20)
ulakiyalpu urittal -
cevili uṭaṉpōṉa taṉ makaḷiyum avaḷ āṭavaṉiyum paṟṟi vaḻiyiṭi mīṇṭu varupavari viṉava, cantaṉamum muttum iciyum tām piṟanta iṭattaṉṟi vēṟiṭaṅkaḷi lēyē payaṉpaṭuvaṉa; atupōla, niṉmakaḷum avaḷi virumpiya āṭavaṉukkē payaṉpaṭutaṟku uriyaḷ; nī kavalaṟka”  eṉṟu ulaka iyaṟkiyi urittu avaḷukku āṟutal kūṟal.
itaṉik ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉṉum tokutik- kaṇ ‘mikkōr ētuk kāṭṭal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa. 	(na. a. 188)
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 248)
ulakiyal vaḻakkam -
ulakattār oḻukalāṟṟōṭu ottu varuvatu. (akat. 56 iḷa.)
ulakattōrkku naṉmi payattaṟku nallōrkku uḷḷa vaṟṟi oḻintōr aṟintoḻukutal aṟameṉak karuti an nallōrkku uḷḷavaṟṟik kūṟutal. 	(akat. 53 nac.)
makkaḷ vāḻkkiyiṉ melliyalpukaḷ 	(akat. 52 pārati.)
makkaḷ vāḻkkiyil iyalpākak kāṇappaṭum oḻukkam. 			(to. po. 42 kuḻanti.)
ulakiyal vaḻakku eṉappaṭuvatu -
ellā uyirkkum iyaṟkiyāṉa iṉpanāṭṭamum viruppum veṟuppum kulaṉum kuṭiyum nāṟpāṟ pakutiyum ēṟṟat tāḻvum uṭiyōrāy, āḷvōrum āḷappaṭuvōru māy, iṉpamum tuṉpamumākiya iṭiyūṟukaḷum paratti miyum puṇartalum piritalum iruttalum iraṅkalum ākiya paṇpum ceyalum mēṟkoṇṭu nikaḻum makkaḷiṉ oḻuka lāṟṟiṉi uḷḷavāṟē amittuk kūṟutal ulakavaḻakkām.		(to. akat. 55 ca.pāla.)
`uvantu alarcūṭṭi uḷmakiḻntu urittal' -
uṭaṉpōkkil taliviyi iḷippāṟa irukkac col lit tāṉum iḷippāṟiya talivaṉ, kāṉattup pūkkaḷip paṟittu vantu avaḷukkuc cūṭṭi uḷam niṟinta makiḻcci yoṭu taṉ maṉattē kūṟutal.
“iccurattil, eṉatu muṉṉiya nalviṉi, ivaḷuṭiya aṭittāmarikaḷip pōṟṟavum, pukaḻntu ivaḷmuṭikku āynta maṇamalarkaḷic cūṭṭavum eṉakku aruḷiyatu!” (tañci. kō. 318) eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (na. a. 182)
uvantuṭaṉ cēṟal -
uṭaṉpōya taliviyiṭamiruntu, makappēṟu vāytta piṟaku ceyti vantatākat talivitamar taliviyit tammūrkku aḻittuvarac cellutal. (pāli naṭiyiyal) (‘ceṉṟal’ eṉṟa pāṭam ‘cēṟal’ eṉak koḷḷappaṭṭuḷḷatu.)			(vī. cō. 93 urimēṟ. )
uvanturittal -
kuṟiyiṭi niṉṟa talivaṉ tōḻiyāl taṉ arukē taṉiya ḷāy niṟuttappaṭṭa taliviyi etirppaṭṭut taṉṉiṭam aṉpukoṇṭu aṅku avaḷ vanta ceyal kuṟittu makiḻntu kūṟal.
itaṉik ‘kiḻavōṉ (kuṟi) iṭattu etirppaṭutal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.	(na. a. 149, i. vi. 509)
itu ‘pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  (kōvi. 120)
uḻiyar iyaṟpaḻittal -
talivaṉatu parattimi kuṟittut tōḻi avaṉip paḻittuk kūṟa, atu kēṭṭu arukil irukkum ēṉiya vāyilavar, “taṉṉiyē niṉintu talivi varuntat talivi yiṉ aṉpiṉi uṇarātu puṟattoḻukkattil īṭupaṭ ṭirukkum talivaṉ neṟiyil niṟpavaṉ allaṉ” eṉṟu avaṉ paṇpiṉip paḻittuk kūṟutal.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 377)
uḻiyar talivinilimi urittal -
talivaṉōṭu ūṭiya taliviyip paṟṟip pakkalil uḷḷavar tammuḷ pēcikkoḷḷutal.
parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉ taliviyoṭu paḷḷi yiṭattāṉāka, avaṉmārpam taṉṉit tavira ēṉiya peṇṭirālum pārkkappaṭṭatāyiṉami kuṟittut talivi ūṭiṉāḷ. itaṉi aṟinta pakkalil uḷḷōr palarum, “nam talivaṉmārpil vēṟoru peṇṇuṭiya kaṇka ḷākiya vēl pāyccappaṭa, ataṉāl nam talivi neñcam puṇpaṭṭati nōkka, ivaḷ avaṉuṭiya uyirāvāḷ eṉpatu pulappaṭukiṉṟataṉṟē!” eṉṟu tammuḷ pēcikkoḷ ḷutal.
itu  ‘parattiyiṟ pirital’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(mā. a. pāṭal. 487)
uḷ makiḻnturittal (1) -
maṉam makiḻntu kūṟutal.
poruḷvayiṉ pirinta talivaṉ kārkālam varutaṟku muṉṉarē tāṉ etirpārtta aḷavu celvatti īṭṭi maṉikku mīṇṭāṉ; taṉ taliviyi eyti avaḷoṭu makiḻcciyāka illaṟam naṭattukiyil, “yāṉ poruḷ tēṭap pirinta kālattu eṉ nāvu vaṟaṇṭu pōṉa tuyaratti ivaḷ atarapāṉattālum, eṉ uṭalveppatti ivaḷatu paricattālum, eṉ uḷḷa veppatti ivaḷāl peṟum iṉpattālum pōkkik koṇṭēṉ” eṉṟu maṉam makiḻntu taṉṉuḷ kūṟikkōṭal.
itu ‘poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(mā. a. pāṭal. 437)
uḷmakiḻnturittal (2) -
kaṟpiṉuḷ poruḷvayiṉ pirintu mīṇṭa talivaṉ talivi yuṭaṉ paḷḷiyiṭattaṉāyiruntu, “ivaḷip pirintu poruḷ tēṭap pālinilattil yāṉ paṭṭa tuṉpamellām ivaḷit taḻuvutalāl nīṅkiviṭum” eṉṟa maṉattil makiḻvoṭu kūṟutal. 
itu ‘poruḷvayiṉ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 351)
uḷmakiḻnturittal (3) -
pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ puṇarccikkup piṉ talivaṉ taliviyatu mukattil taṉimalarcci kaṇṭu avaḷik kaḻunīrmalarākavum taṉṉi ataṉakattut tēṉip parukum vaṇṭākavum niṉittut taṉṉuḷ makiḻntu kūṟutal.	
itaṉi ‘iyital’, eṉṟum kūṟupa. na.a. 149; i.vi. 509-54. ām aṭi.
itu ‘pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (kōvi. 123)
uḷḷattuṇarcci -
tōḻiyiṉ uḷḷattiluḷḷa uṇarcci.
tōḻi talivaṉ talivi eṉṉum iruvaratu kūṭṭamum nikaḻntuviṭṭatu eṉṟu uṇarnta uṇarcci.
talivaṉ taliviyit taṉmāṭṭuc cērttu vikkum kuṟiyiṉi iṉṟiyamiyātavaṉ eṉa uṇarum uṇarcci.
“yāṉ kuṟi vēṇṭiṉ talivi ataṉi niṟivēṟṟu vāḷō” eṉṟu eṇṇum eṇṇam.
“eṉṉālākak kūṭiya ikkūṭṭam nikaḻātu pōyiṉ talivaṉ iṟantupaṭum ākaliṉ, taliviyik kuṟi nayappittuk koṇṭu muṭikkalāmkollō?” eṉṟu uṇarnta uṇarcci.
ivvāṟu ‘uḷḷattuṇarcci’ eṉpatu palavāṟu poruḷ paṭum.	(iṟi. a. 10)
uḷḷatu kūṟi varivu kaṭātal -
talivaṉ taliviyip paṟṟi uyarvākak kūṟi avaḷi virivil maṇappataṉkaṇ kālam tāḻkka, tōḻi avaṉi nōkki, “taliva! nī eṅkaḷi mikippaṭuttik kūṟutal vēṇṭā. yāṅkaḷ malināṭṭuk kuṟavarkaḷē. emmip peṟṟavaḷum koṭicciyē. eṅkaḷ toḻil tiṉippuṉam kāttal mutaliyaṉavē. nī variya niṉiyātu eṅkaḷi aḷaviṟku mīṟip pukaḻtal vēṇṭā” eṉṟu kūṟi virivil taliviyi variyumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttiyatu.
itu ‘varivumuṭukkam’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 252)
uḷḷappuṇarcci -
talivaṉum taliviyum oruvari oruvar uḷḷattāṉ virumpip piṉ irumutukuravar maṇam ceytumuṭikkum vari meyyuṟu puṇarcciyiṉṟi uḷḷak kaḷittum kaṇṭu makiḻntu miruttal.  talivaṉukkup perumiyum uraṉum talivikku nāṇum maṭaṉum accamum nilipeṟṟa kuṇaṅkaḷ ātaliṉ, meyyuṟu puṇarcci vēṇṭātu, tirumaṇam vari uḷḷap puṇarcciyālēyē iruvarum niṟivu  peṟṟiruttalumuṇṭu.		(na. a. 351)
uḷḷuṟi aintu -
uṭaṉuṟi, uvamam, cuṭṭu, naki, ciṟappu eṉṟu, nērāṉa poruḷi viṭuttu uḷḷārnta poruḷi viḷakkum pakuti aivakippaṭum.
1. 	uṭaṉuṟi - nāṉku nilattuk karupporuḷkaḷālum piṟitoru poruḷ payappa maṟittuk kūṟum iṟicci.
2.	uvamam - karupporuḷkaḷik koṇṭu nērukku nēr uṇarum uḷḷuṟiuvamam.
3.	cuṭṭu - maṉattuḷ oṉṟi niṉittup piṟitoṉṟic cuṭṭutalum, ‘aṉpuṟu takuna iṟicciyuḷ cuṭṭutalum.’
4.	naki - nakiyāṭi oṉṟi niṉittu oṉṟu kūṟutal.
5.	ciṟappu - ēṉiuvamam niṉṟu uḷḷuṟiuvamam tarum karupporuṭkuc ciṟappuk koṭuttu niṟṟal.
niṉintataṉi veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟāmal maṟittuk kūṟutaliṉ ivi aintum uḷḷuṟi āyiṉa.	(to. po. 242 nac.)
uṟṟavaḷ oḻukkam -
talivaṉ irukkum iṭam nōkkit talivi cēṟal. 
itu kāmanukarcciyiṭattu vēṭkiyāṉ ceyyum kuṟip pāṉa ceyalkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. 	(vī. cō. 96 urimēṟ.)
iḵtu akattiṇi uri irupattēḻaṉuḷ ‘kuṟippu’ eṉpataṉpāṟpaṭum.	(vī. cō. kā. 98)
uṟṟār taṭukkat talivi tiruppēreyil cērat tuṇital -
aḵtāvatu talivi talivaṉuṭiya ūrukkup pōkat tuṇital; ‘veḷḷiccuri’ (tiruvāymoḻi 7 - 3 - 1)
tiruppēreyil makaraneṭuṅkuḻikkātaṉ eṉṟa peru māṉiṭam kātalkoṇṭu varuntum talivi, avaṉ neñ cuḷḷē yiruntu varutta, taṉtōḻiyarum tāyum atu paḻikkuk kāraṇam ākumē eṉṟu taṭuttum kēḷāmal, vētavoliyum viḻā voliyum nīṅkāta tiruppērikkut tāṉ pōvataṟkut tuṇintu viṭṭatik kūṟutal.
itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷiyiṉ pāṟpaṭum.	(tiruvāy. 7 - 3 - 1)
uṟṟuḻi utavac ceṉṟavaḻip pācaṟikkaṇ talivaṉ pulampal -
talivaṉ naṇpaṉukkut tuṉpam uṟṟaviṭattē ataṉip pōkkutaṟku avaṉukku utaviyākac ceṉṟāṉāka, appo ḻutu taliviyiṭam mīṇṭu varuvatākak kūṟiya paruvam vantavaḻip pācaṟiyil tāṉ paṭum taṉimittuṉpam tōṉṟak kūṟutal.
“oṇṇutalāḷ ākiya talivi kāṭṭu nilappakuti aṭin tirunta ariya muṉikaḷiyuṭiya vaḻiyiṉuḷ cīṟūriluḷ ḷāḷ; yāmē, tiṟi koṭuppavum koḷḷāmal piṉṉarum pōrt toḻiliṉkaṇ celuttappaṭukiṉṟa cēṉiyi yuṭiya vākit tār vēntaṉatu pācaṟikkaṇ uḷḷēm. avaḷnili eṉṉiyō?” (akanā. 84) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itaṉit ‘talivaṉ pācaṟip pulampal’ eṉavum kūṟupa. (ampikā. 533)
uṟaṅkātu iravu aḻuṅkal -
kaṇpaṭi peṟātu kaṅkul nōtal; atu kāṇka. (cāmi. 89)
uṟaḻum kiḷaviyum, aiyakkiḷaviyum -
kāma iṉpam ciṟattaṟkut tōḻi talivaṉi uyarttik kūṟumiṭattut talivi tāḻttik kūṟutalum, talivaṉ uyarmoḻi kūṟiyavaḻit tōḻi tāḻttuk kūṟutalum, talivaṉ uyarmoḻi kūṟiyavaḻit talivi tāḻttuk kūṟutalum, talivi uyarmoḻi kūṟiyavaḻit tōḻi tāḻt tuk kūṟutalum uḷa. ivi uḷḷak karutti māṟṟik kūṟaliṉ vaḻuvāmēṉum, kāmatti mikuvikka utavutaliṉ amintaṉa.
taliviyō tōḻiyō taṉakku utavuvāḷō māṭ ṭāḷō eṉṟu aiyappaṭutal talivaṟku urittu.
‘colliṉ, maṟātuīvāḷ maṉṉō ivaḷ’ kali. 61 mutalākat talivaṉ aiyappaṭṭut taṉakkuḷ urittavāṟu. 	(to. po. 238 nac.)

uṟāc ciṟunōkkam -
nēriṭiyākap potunōkkāṉ nōkkātu kaṇkaḷic curukkikkoṇṭu aṉpu tōṉṟak kaṭikkaṇṇāl pārttal.
itu kāmanukarcciyiṭattu vēṭkiyāṉ ceyyum kuṟip pāṉa ceyalkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.	(vī. cō. 96 uri mēṟ.)
iḵtu akattiṇi uri irupattēḻaṉuḷ kuṟippu eṉpataṉ pāṟpaṭum. 	(vī. cō. 90)
ū
ūṭal taṇivittal -
parattiyiṟ pirintuvanta talivaṉ viṟali mutaliyavarkaḷi vāyilāka viṭa, avarkaṭku vāyil nērātu ūṭali nīṭṭitta taliviyiṭam tōḻi, “namputalvaṉit tamakkut tuṇiyākak karutit talivar avaṉuṭaṉ vantu tōṉṟu talāl niṉ ūṭali nīkkit talivaṟkup paṇṭu pōla makiḻvuṭaṉ kuṟṟēval ceytalē niṉakkut takkatu” eṉṟu kūṟit taliviyiṉ ūṭalit taṇivittatu.
itu ‘parattiyiṟpirivu’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇṇatoru 
kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 389)
ūṭal nimittam -
parattiyum pāṇaṉum pōlvār ūṭal tōṉṟaṟkuriya kāraṇamum cūḻniliyum taruvōrāvar.	(to. po. 14 nac.)
ūṭal nīṭa vāṭi urittal -
parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉ taṇikkavum taṇiyātu talivi ūṭal mikuttavaḻit talivaṉ kaḷavukkāla nikaḻcci kaḷik kuṟippiṭṭu, “maṉamē! muṉpu cōliyil eṉṉi aṉpoṭu pārttut taṉ vacappaṭuttiya peṇṇamutam allaḷ ivaḷ; ivaḷ avaḷvaṭivil māyamāka vantuḷḷa vēṟorutti!” eṉat taṉ neñciṟku varuttattoṭu collutal.
itaṉit ‘talimakaḷ taṇiyāḷākat talivaṉ ūṭal’ eṉpa. iḵtu uṇartta uṇarā ūṭalām.			(na. a. 206, i. vi. 555)
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirital’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 394)
ūṭaluvaki -
kaṟpukkālattil illaṟam nikaḻttum talivaṉum talivi yum nuṇukiya kāraṇattik kaṟpittukkoṇṭu ūṭutalāl tamakkuk kūṭal iṉpam ciṟattaliṉ, akkūṭal iṉpattic ciṟakkac ceyyum ūṭalit talivaṉum taliviyum virumpip pukaḻtal.	(kuṟaḷ ati. 133 parimē.)
ūṭaluḷ nekiḻtal -
peruntiṇit talivi ūṭalkoṇṭu varuntutal; itu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyuḷ peruntiṇiyiṉ oru pakutiyākiya peṇpāṟkūṟṟiṉkaṇ nikaḻum oru kūṟṟu.
“kāraṇam iṉṟiyē nāṉ ūṭiṉēṉ; talivaṉ nayantu teḷivittāṉ. appoḻutum ūṭal taṉiyāmal iruntu viṭṭēṉē! tuṉpamūṭṭum iruḷ ceṟinta mālip pōtum vantuṟṟatu. iraviṉkaṇṇāyiṉum avaṉik kūṭi iṉpuṟuvēṉō?” (pu.ve.mā. 16-15) eṉpatu pōṉṟa kūṟṟu.
ūtiyam eṭutturittu ūṭal tīrttal -
parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉiṭam talivi ūṭal koṇṭu vāyil nērātiruttalik kaṇṭa tōḻi, “talivaṉ ellōrukkum potuvāṉa ūtiyamāka iruppataṉāl, avaṉ parattiyarkkum aruḷceytal vēṇṭumaṉṟō? maṟṟa peṇṭirip pōla nām ava ṉoṭu pulattal takātu. talivaṉi varumpōtu etir koṇṭu muṉtoḻutum, pōmpōtu piṉtoḻutum, putalvaṉip payantu illiliruntu viruntōmpi nallaṟam ceytalaṉṟō nam kaṭami?” eṉat talivaṉatu ūtiyam eṭutturittu avaḷatu ūṭali nīkki avaṉōṭu ici vittatu.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇ aminta iṟutik kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 400)
ūtiyam payattal -
ūtiyam eṭutturittu ūṭal tīrttal; atu kāṇka.
ūr tuñcāmi -
viḻā ayarntu makiḻum ūrār viḻānāḷkaḷil uṟaṅkātu viḻittiruppatāl, talivaṉ kuṟiyiṭam vantu cēra iṭiyūṟu nērtal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘iravukkuṟi iṭiyīṭu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. itu ‘varuntoḻiṟku arumi’ eṉṉum vakiyiṉpāṟpaṭum.	(na. a. 161)
itu talivikūṟṟākavum, tōḻikūṟṟākavum varum.
ūr viṉātal -
pāṅki vikum poḻiliṭam puṉam pōṉṟa iṭattē ceṉṟa talivaṉ avaḷiṭam avaḷatu ūr yātu eṉṟu viṉavutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅki matiuṭaṉpāṭu’ eṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
‘kuṟiyuṟa uṇartal’ eṉṉum vakiyiṉ tiṟam itu. (na. a. 140.)
ūratu nilimi tōḻi urittal -
ūrār taliviyippaṟṟik koṇṭuḷḷa karuttit tōḻi talivikku urittal.
“talivi! nīṅkaḷ uṭaṉpōkkuc cellumuṉ paḻi tūṟṟiya ūr, nī kaṟpoḻukkam ciṟakkat talivaṉuṭaṉ ceṉṟatik kēṭṭu yāviṉum cālap pulampiyatu” eṉpatu pōṉṟa kūṟṟu.
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku iṭiyīṭu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(ampikā. 428)
“ūravar collum aṉṉi collum nōkki āṟṟal vēṇṭum” eṉṟa tōḻikkut talivi kūṟal -
“intak kāmanōy eṉṉum payir, ūrppeṇṭir pēcum alar eruvāka, nam aṉṉi ciṉantu kūṟum kaṭuñcol nīrāka nāḷukku nāḷ vaḷarkiṟatu. ātaliṉ ivi eṉatu kāmam vaḷarppaṉavē; aḻippaṉa alla” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
“maṟṟavar tūṟṟum alarāl namatu kāmavētaṉiyi avittut taṇippōm eṉa niṉippatu, tīyi ney ūṟṟi avippōm eṉṟu niṉippatip pōṉṟa tākum” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(kuṟaḷ 1147, 1148)
ūḻviṉi valittal -
iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ talivaṉ taṉakkut taliviyik kūṭṭuvitta teyvatti makiḻntu kūṟutal (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal) vī.cō. 92 uri. itu ‘teyvatti makiḻn turittal’ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. (kōvi. 6) ‘teyvattiṟam pēcal’ eṉṉum nampi akapporuḷ. (129)
e
eṅkiyar -
talivi parattiyarik kuṟikkap payaṉpaṭuttum ikaḻccic col. eṅkiyar - eṉ taṅkiyar. talimakaṉoṭu kūṭu taṟkaṇ urimikoṇṭavarāyt taṉakkuppiṉ avaṉoṭu toṭarpu koṇṭavarākiya parattiyarit talivi ‘eṅkiyar’ eṉkiṟāḷ.	(to. po. 147 nac.)
`eñcātu kiḷanta irunāṉku kiḷavi' -
tōḻi, nāṟṟam tōṟṟam mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ talivikkut talivaṉoṭu kūṭṭam uṇṭu eṉpataṉi muṉṉar uṇarntu, piṉ talivaṉ taṉṉik kuṟinayantavaḻi, avaṉ uḷḷakkarutti naṉṟāka aṟintu muṭivu ceyvataṟkāka avaṉ kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟiyellām cevimaṭuttu (1) perumi yiṉ peyarttal, 2) ulaku urittu oḻittal, 3) arumiyiṉ akaṟci, 4) avaḷ aṟivuṟuttup piṉ vā eṉṟal, 5) pētimi ūṭṭal, 6) muṉṉuṟu puṇarcci muṟi niṟuttu urittal, 7) añci accuṟuttal, 8) urittuḻik kūṭṭam - eṉṟa eṭṭuvakiyākat talivaṉōṭu uriyāṭit taṉakku avaṉiṭam pācamum parivum uṇmiyi veḷippaṭuttal. 	(to. po. 112 iḷa.)
talivaṉ maṉattil taliviyit tāṉ iṉṟiyamiyātavaṉ eṉṟu tōḻiyiṭam taṉkuṟi uṇarttal nikaḻavum, annērat tilēyē talivaṉ talivi ivarkaḷip paṟṟiya niṉipput tōḻi maṉattu nikaḻavum, ivviruvakippaṭṭa nikaḻccikaḷi yum tōḻi kūṟum ivveṭṭuk kūṟṟum taḻuvi niṟpaṉavām. 	(to. po. 114. nac.)
eṇṇam teḷital -
talivaṉ vantu ētētō kēṭṭapōtu, talivaṉuṭiya uḷḷakkuṟippiṉi ārāyntu avaṉatu maṉakkarutti pāṅki teḷintu koḷḷutal.	(i.vi. akat. 135)
etirkōḷ kūṟi varivu kaṭātal -
pakaṟkuṟi iṟutikkaṇ talivaṉit taṉittuk kāṇaluṟṟa tōḻi, “nī varivoṭu variṉ em aṉṉi makiḻvāḷ; emar niṉṉi varavēṟṟu etirkoḷvar. ātaliṉ virivil taliviyi varitaṟkuriya ceyaṟkaṇ īṭupaṭṭu varuvāyāka” eṉṟu kūṟi varivu kaṭātal.
itaṉi ‘varivu etirvu uṇarttal’ eṉṟum kūṟuvar.  	(na. a. 166, i. vi. 523)
itu ‘pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  (kōvi. 135)
etirntōr moḻital -
iṭiccurattukkaṇ uṭaṉpōya talivaṉ talivi iruvariyum kaṇṭōr tammūril avviravu taṅkic cellu māṟu, iraviṭi avvaḻiyē cellum ētam kūṟi avar celaviṉi vilakki moḻital (pāli naṭiyiyal)  (vī. cō. 93 uri. mēṟ.)
etirmaṟi -
“talimakaṉatu ūrkkuc celvōm” eṉṟa talivi piṉ ataṉi etirmaṟuttu moḻital.
“accam tarum pāmpukaḷum pulikaḷum yāṉikaḷum tiriyum vaḻiyil iruḷil, koṭiya em uṟaviṉartam kāvaliyum kaṭantu iṅkiruntu puṟappaṭṭut talivaṉatu ūr ceṉṟaṭivatu aritō? āyiṉ anta arumippāṭṭiṉi aṟintu aruḷ ceyvār illāta kāraṇattāltāṉ yām celvatu taṭippaṭukiṟatu” eṉṟa kūṟṟu.
itu ‘varital vēṭki’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ampikā. 242)
“empirāṉ virumpātavaṟṟāl eṉakkum payaṉilli” eṉṟu talivi iraṅkal -
“em perumāṉākiya tirumāl ukantu ēṟṟukkoḷḷāta eṉ niṟamum niṟiyum eḻilum iṉiya uṟuppukkaḷum vaḷiyum mēkaliyum eṉakku vēṇṭiyaṉa alla” eṉṟa taliviyatu maṉanili. itu neñcoṭu kūṟuvatu.
‘māṟāḷaṉ kavarāta maṇimāmi kuṟivilamē’ (1)
‘maṇimāyaṉ kavarāta maṭaneñcāl kuṟivilamē’ (2)
‘neṭumāyaṉ kavarāta niṟiviṉāl kuṟivilamē’ (3)
‘caṟiyiṉār kavarāta taḷirniṟattāl kuṟivilamē’ (4)
‘aḷimikkāṉ kavarāta aṟiviṉāl kuṟivilamē’ (5)
‘kiṟiammāṉ kavarāta kiḷaroḷiyāl kuṟivilamē’ (6)
‘vaḷaroḷiyāṉ kavarāta varivaḷiyāl kuṟivilamē’ (7)
‘viripukaḻāṉ kavarāta mēkaliyāl kuṟivilamē’ (8)
‘yōkaṇivāṉ kavarāta uṭampiṉāl kuṟivilamē’ (9)
‘uṭampuṭiyāṉ kavarāta uyiriṉāl kuṟivilamē’ (10)
eṉa varumāṟu koḷka. itu cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷappaṭṭa puṟattiṇik kikkiḷippāṟpaṭum. 	(tiruvāy. 4 : 8)
`emmeṉa varūum kiḻamit tōṟṟam' -
ovvoruvarum tamakkē urimiyuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu uṟuti yākak kūṟakkūṭiyaṉavum maṟṟavarum kaṇṇāṟ kaṇṭu oppuk koḷḷak kūṭiyaṉavum ākiya oruvaruṭiya uṟuppukkaḷ.
maṟṟa poruṭcelvam pōṉṟavaṟṟit tamakkē urimi yuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu uṟutiyākak kūṟutal iyalātu. peṟṟōr tam poruḷkaḷ makkaḷi vantaṭiyum. koṭuppāṉ poruḷ koḷvāṉ kiyi vantaṭiyum. mintarillā tārkku mintar ceyyum kaṭamikaḷic ceytāl avaratu celvam akkaṭami ceytavari vantaṭiyum. poruṭ celvam pakivar kaḷvar mutaliyōrālum kavarntu koḷḷappaṭutalām. ākavē ivaṟṟil muḻu urimi pārāṭṭutal iyalātu.
āyiṉ taṉ uṟuppukkaḷit taṉṉuṭimi eṉṟu urimi pārāṭṭutal amiyum. aṅṅaṉam talivikkē urimi yuṭiya avaḷ uṟuppukkaḷiyum naṭpiṉ mutirviṉāl tōḻi taṉ uṟuppukkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟutalum kūṭum eṉpatu. (to. po. 221 nac.)
eytal eṭutturittal -
parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉ taliviyiṉ ūṭalit tīrttu iṉpuṟac ceytu avaḷi aṭintu makiḻnta cey tiyi ‘akampukal marapiṉ vāyilōr’ tammuḷ eṭuttuk kūṟutal.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 365)
el iṭiyīṭu -
pakaṟkuṟi iṭiyīṭu. atu kāṇka.	(cāmi. 86)
`evvakip poruḷum irantu kuṟiyuṟutal' -
talivaṉpirivāl iraṅkum talivi kaṭaṟkariyākiya neytal nilatti yaṭuttu āṇṭuḷḷa kaṭal - kaḻi - kiti - kāṉal - peṇṇi - puṉku - iruḷ - mati - poḻutu - kāṟṟu - mutaliyavi taṉ coṟkaḷik kēṭpaṉa pōlak kaṟpaṉi ceytu, avaṟṟit taṉakku utavumāṟu vēṇṭal. (neytal naṭiyiyal) 	(vī. cō. 96 uri mēṟ.)
eḻuti iraṅkal -
talivaṉ taliviyiṉ uruviṉik kiḻiyil eḻutip piṉ varuntutal. tōḻiyi irantu piṉṉiṉṟa talivaṉ, avaḷ taṉakkut taliviyik kūṭṭivikkak kālam tāḻttaliṉ āṟṟātu maṭalēṟat tuṇintu, cittiram eḻutum taṉatu āṟṟalāl talivi uruviṉi oru kiḻiyil eḻutivittuk koṇṭu ataṉi nōkki, “talivi! uṉakku eṉpāl kōpam ciṟitum illiyē. nam paḻantoṭarpiṉi nī tōḻikkuk kūṟi avaḷvāyilāka eṉṉik kūṭutaṟku āvaṉa ceyyātatu eṉ ūḻviṉiyē. avvūḻ viṉiyāl iṉṟu uṉ vaṭiviṉik kiḻiyil eḻuti vittuk koṇṭu maṭalēṟum niliyil uḷḷēṉ” (tiruppatik. 133) eṉṟu varuntik kūṟal.
itu ‘tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.(mā. akap.  37)
eḻutum muṉ iraṅkal -
taliviyatu  uruviṉik kiḻiyil eḻutumuṉ talivaṉ varun tal. talivaṉ tōḻiyiṟkūṭṭattil tōḻi taliviyik kūṭṭuvittaṟkaṇ kālatāmatam ceytapōtu āṟṟāmi mītūrntu maṭalēṟat tuṇintu, acceytiyit tōḻiyiṭam kūṟic cittiram variyum taṉatu vallami tōṉṟat talivi yatu uruvi orukiḻiyil variyumuṉ, “oru peṇṇukkāka ippaṭi mayaṅkip pittaṉāki avaḷatu uruviṉi eḻuti maṭalēṟum nilikku vantuviṭṭēṉē!” (tiruppatik. 132) eṉṟu taṉṉuḷ varuttamuṟutal.
itu tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam eṉṟa tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(mā. akap. 37)
eḷḷarun tōḻi ēṟpak kūṟal -
talivi, piṉṉar maṇakkappaṭṭa iraṇṭām maṉivi kāmak kiḻattiyar ākiyōriṭam aṉpākap paḻakutalik kaṇṭa taliviyiṉ tōḻi, avaḷ ceyal nalla illaṟak kiḻattikku ēṟṟa ceyalē eṉṟu pukaḻntu kūṟal (maruta naṭiyiyal) 	(vī. cō. 95 uri. mēṟ)
eḷittal -
itu tōḻi aṟattoṭu niṟkum vaki ēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu. eḷittalāvatu talivaṉi eḷiyaṉākak kūṟutal; aḵtāvatu celvac cerukkiṉṟit tammoṭu camamākap paḻakum perun taṉmiyuṭiyaṉākak kūṟutal.	(to. po. 207 nac.)
talivaṉ tammāṭṭu eḷiyaṉ eṉṟu kūṟutal.
ataṉatu payaṉ, makaḷuṭiya tāyar tamvaḻi oḻukuvārkku makaṭkoṭi vēṇṭuvarātaliṉ eḷiyaṉ eṉṟu kūṟi tōḻi aṟattoṭuniṟṟal.	(112 iḷa.)
eṟkuṟi -
pakaṟkuṟi. atu kāṇka. 	(cāmi. 86)
eṟpāṭu neytaṟ kurimi - 
neytal eṉpatu iraṅkal eṉṟa uripporuḷām. atu talivaṉ kuṟitta nērattō paruvattō taliviyik kāṇa vārātavaḻi avaḷkaṇ nikaḻvatu. ataṟkup perumpoḻutu iṉṟu. ciṟupoḻutiṉuḷ eṟpāṭu ataṟkuriyatu.
veñcuṭar veppam tīrat taṇṇaṟuñcōli tāḻntu niḻal ceyyavum. taṇpatam paṭṭa teṇkaḻi mēyntu puḷḷelām kuṭampi nōkki uṭaṅku peyaravum, puṉṉi mutaliya pūviṉ nāṟṟam muṉṉiṉṟu kañaṟṟavum, neṭuntiri aḻuvattu nilākkatir parappavum, kātal kimmikkuk kaṭaliṭattō kaṭaṟkaric cōliyiṭattō niṟi kaṭantu vēṭki pulappaṭat talivi urittaliṉ āṇṭuk kāmakkuṟippu veḷippaṭṭu iraṅkaṟporuḷ ciṟattaliṉ eṟpāṭu neytaṟku vantatu.	(to. po. 8 nac.)
eṟpāṭu eṉpataṉi nāḷ veyiṟkāli eṉṟu poruḷ ceytu puṇarcciyiṉṟi iravu koṉṉē kaḻintatē eṉṟu talivi varuntutaṟkuriya kālam aḵtu eṉṟu kūṟuvārum uḷar.	(cū.vi. pak. 38)

`eṉkuṟi ituveṉa irantu kuṟi kūṟal' -
kaṟpukkālattil talivaṉ poruḷkarutip piriya niṉitta - vaḻit talivi taṉṉiyum uṭaṉkoṇṭu cellumāṟu talivaṉiṭam taṉ viruppattip paṇivuṭaṉ aṟivittal. (mulli naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 94 uri mēṟ.)
`eṉpiḻippu aṉṟu eṉṟu iṟivi nērtal' -
“yāṉ iravuk kuṟiyiṭattuc ceṉṟu avarik kāṇātu tirumpi mikavum tuyaruṟṟēṉ; alla kuṟippaṭṭatu eṉpiḻi yaṉṟu” eṉṟa taliviyatu kūṟṟu.
“nocciyiṉ pūkkaḷ viḻum ōciyiṉik kēṭṭavāṟē ūravar uṟaṅkiya piṉṉum yāṉ ivvōci talivaṉ ceyta kuṟiyō eṉṟu aiyuṟṟavāṟē iravu muḻutum uṟaṅkā tiruntēṉ” (tañci kō. 201) eṉṟu talivi tōḻiyiṭam kūṟal.
kaḷaviyaluḷ iḵtu ‘iravukkuṟi iṭiyīṭu’ eṉṉum toku tikkaṇṇatu oru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 160)
`eṉ poruṭpirivu ēntiḻikku uṇarttu eṉṟal' -
talivaṉ varivataṟku muṉpu, taṉ muyaṟciyāl poruḷ īṭṭit talivikkup poṉṉum maṇiyum pūṭṭutal virumpip pirintu cella vēṇṭiyiruppatit tōḻiyiṭam kūṟi, ataṉit talivikku eṭuttiyampumāṟu collutal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na.a. 170)
ē
ēku avaṇ eṉṟal -
talivi irukkum iṭattiṟkuc celka eṉal. iyaṟkip puṇarcci iṭantalippāṭu ivaṟṟāṉ taliviyik kūṭiya talivaṉ, taṉ uyirppāṅkaṉi aṭintu, taṉ nili yiṉik kūṟi, avaṉ viṉaviyavaṟṟiṟkellām maṟumoḻi pakarntu, taṉ āṟṟā niliyiṉik kūṟit taliviyit tāṉ muṉpu kaṇṇuṟṟa cūḻalit terivittu avaḷ accōli yil irukkum ceytiyit terintu varutaṟkuc cellumāṟu avaṉi vēṇṭutal.	(mā. akap. 28)
itu pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam eṉṟa tokutikkaṇ oru kūṟṟu.
ēttal (1) -
ūrmakkaḷ taliviyiṉ peruṅkaṟpiṉip pukaḻntu kūṟal. (maruta naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 95 uri. mēṟ.)
ēttal (2)
tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟkum eḻuvakikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. ēttal eṉpatu talivaṉi uyarttuk kūṟutal. atu makaḷuṭit tāyar “talivaṉ uyarntāṉ” eṉṟavaḻi maṉam makiḻvarātaliṉ avvāṟu kūṟappaṭṭatu. (to. po. 112. iḷa. uri)
talivaṉik ‘kāṉakanāṭaṉ makaṉ’ (kali. 39) eṉṟāṟ pōla uyarttuk kūṟuvatu. 	(207 nac.)
ētti moḻital - 
pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭattil talivaṉukkuk kuṟi nērnta pāṅki taliviyiṭam talivaṉuṭiya paṇpunalaṉkaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟi avaṉuṭiya kuṟiyiṉit talivi nayakkumāṟu vēṇṭal (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal). sa‘pāṅki iṟivikku avaṉkuṟi uṇarttal’ (na. a. 149) eṉpataṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum.]		(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
ētam kūṟi iravu varavu vilakkal -
taliviyi variyak karutātu iravukkuṟiyi nīṭṭikka virumpiya talivaṉiṭam, “nī varitali viṭuttuk koṭiya vilaṅkukaḷ tiriyum kāṭṭuvaḻiyē iruḷiṭit  taliviyi nāṭi  varutal eṅkaḷukkup peruntuyaram tarukiṟatu. iṉi iru ḷiṭi vārātē” eṉṟu iraviṭi nikaḻkkūṭiya tuṉpaṅ kaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟit taliviyi maṇakkumāṟu tōḻi talivaṉi vaṟpuṟuttal.	(kōvi. 253)
itaṉi ‘iravu varuvāṉip pakal varuka eṉṟal’ eṉṟum kūṟuvar.	(na. a. 166)
‘varivu muṭukkam’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇ oru kūṟṟu itu.
ētam kūṟi maṟuttal -
talivaṟku iravukkuṟi nērnta tōḻi taliviyiṭam taṉ karuttik kūṟiṉāḷākat talivi taṉakkut talivaṉ ceytuḷḷa karuṇiyi uṭkoṇṭa uḷḷattaḷāy, ‘ciṅkaṅkaḷ yāṉikaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭat tiriyum koṭiya vaḻiyil iruḷil talivaṉi varumāṟu nām vēṇṭuvatu takutiyā?’ eṉṟāṟ pōlak kūṟit taṉakku iravukkuṟi uṭaṉpāṭiṉmiyiṉik kuṟippiṭutal.	(na. a. 158)
itaṉi ‘nērātu iṟivi neñcoṭu kiḷattal’ eṉpa. 
(i. vi. 517) iḵtu ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇ oru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 157)
ētīṭu -
tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟkumvaki ēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu. ētīṭāvatu, oruvaṉ kaḷiṟum puliyum nāyum pōlvaṉa vaṟṟiṉiṉṟu kāttu emmik kikkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, taḻi tantāṉ eṉavum ivi mutaliya kāraṇam iṭṭu uṇarttal.
ētu īṭu - kāraṇamiṭṭuṇarttal.	(to. po. 207 nac.)
ētīṭu talippāṭu -
ituvum tōḻi cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟkum vaki ēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu. yātāṉum oru kāraṇampaṟṟit talivi talivaṉ ākiya iruvarum cantittami kūṟutal. atu puṉaliṭi utaviṉāṉ, kaḷiṟṟiṭi utaviṉāṉ, taḻiyum kaṇṇiyum tantāṉ eṉṟāṟ pōlak kāraṇam kāṭṭutal.
(ētīṭu, talippāṭu eṉa iraṇṭāka nacciṉārkkiṉiyar koḷvar.)	(to. po. 112 iḷa. uri)
ēntiḻi eṭuppal -
iravukkuṟikkaṇ tōḻi taliviyit tuyil eḻupputal (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal) (iḵtu iṟivikku iṟivaṉ varavu aṟivuṟuttal (na. a. 158) eṉavum kuṟikkappaṭum.			(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
ēntutār virumpal -
itu kikkiḷi eṉṉum pāṭāṇtuṟi 	(cāmi. 146)
‘ēmam cāṉṟa uvaki’ : poruḷ -
taliviyatu kūṟṟu nikaḻum oḻukalāṟukaḷ palavaṟṟuḷ iḵtu oṉṟu.
kaḷaviṉ payaṉākiya kaṟpoḻukkattiṟku viḷakkamākiya varitalit talivaṉ muyalumiṭattu, aḵtu iruvarkkum ēmam ātaliṉ av ēmattāṉ talivi mika makiḻtal.			(to. kaḷa. 21 ca.pāla.)
ēmañ cālā iṭumpi -
maruntu mutaliya parikārattāṉ pōkkamuṭiyāta peruntuyar. itu kāmañcālā iḷamiyōḷik kāmuṟṟa āṇṭil mūtta talivaṟku ēṟpaṭuvatām.	(to. po.50 nac.)
ēmanta rutu -
muṉpaṉik kālamākiya perumpoḻutu. atu mārkaḻiyum tiyumākiya iraṇṭu tiṅkaḷ.
`ēmuṟum aṟunāṉku iraṭṭi kaḻintapiṉ, kāmaru tuṟaviṟ kātalaṉ paṭartal' -
kāma vēṭki nīṅkiya talivaṉ tuṟaviṟ cēṟal. talivaṉ 48 āṇṭu āyiṉapōtu kāmattil paṟṟaṟṟut taliviyiṭam, “nām aṟattāṟ peṟṟa namputalvaṉ illaṟattil iṉitu vāḻka! iṉi, nām māṟaṉit tutittup piṟavippiṇiyi aṟukkum cāṉṟōrōṭu oṉṟum peruvāḻvu eytutaṟku illaṟatti viṭuttu vāṉappiratta āciramatti mēṟ koḷvōmāka” eṉṟu kūṟutal.
itu māṟaṉakapporuḷil kāṇappaṭum iṟutittuṟi. tiruppatik kōviyil kāṇappaṭum iṟutic ceyti. (pāṭal 527) 	(mā. akap. 106)
ēr aḻivu urittal -
kaviṉ aḻivu urittal; 	(cāmi. 104)
ēval marapiṉ ēṉōr -
piṟari ēvik koḷḷum marapiṉiyuṭiya maṟṟavar. ivarkaḷ aintiṇit talivarātalē yaṉṟi, tam ēvalarkaḷip pōlak kikkiḷi peruntiṇi iraṇṭaṟkum uriyarāvar.			(to. po. 26 iḷa.)
ēval marapiṉ ēṉōr, ākiya nilimiyavar - eṉappaṭuvār-
ēval marapiṉ ēṉōr : piṟari ēvip paṇikoḷḷum marapiṉarāṉa kuṟunilamaṉṉar, vēḷir, taṇṭattalivar mutalāṉōr. muṭiyuṭi vēntariṉkīḻ avar āṇikkuṭ paṭṭut tiṟi celutti āṭci puriyum  ivarkaḷ talimip pāṭum taṉṉurimiyum ilarāyiṉum, vēntaṉcārpāka niṉṟu piṟari ēvip paṇikoḷḷutalāṉ talimiyum urimiyum peṟṟu oḻukutaliṉ, akattiṇit talimakkaḷā taṟku uriyar.
ākiya nilimiyavar - vēntarāṉ āṭciyurimi aḷikkap peṟṟu āḷum urimi eytiya piṟar. ivarkaḷum akat tiṇik kiḻavarātaṟku uriyar. (to.akat. 26 ca.pāla.)
piṟari ēvikkoḷḷum marapiṉiyuṭiya aracar antaṇar vaṇikar eṉṟa mūvarum, kuṟunila maṉṉarum, vēḷāḷarum akaṉ aintiṇikkaṇ talimakkaḷ ātaṟku uriyar.	(to. po. 24, nac. 24 kuḻa.)
aṭiyavariyum viṉivalariyum ēvikkoḷḷum nilimi yuṭiya naṉmakkaḷum kikkiḷi peruntiṇi eṉṟa akappuṟattukku urimi uṭiyavar. 	(26 iḷa.)
aṭiyōr viṉivalar, taṉikkuṭi viṉivalar, ēṉiya ēval marapiṉar ākiyōrum aintiṇikkaṇ nikaḻum oḻukkat tukku urimi uṭiyarāvar  (24 pārati.)
ēvalarkaḷām akappuṟat talivaṉum taliviyum muṉṉorukāl kūṭip pirinta piṉ avaḷ etirppaṭṭuḻi avaṉvayiṉ parattimiyāṉ avaḷ ūṭik kuṟinērntatu -
“pēraḻakuṭiyāy! nī mōrviṟṟu mīḷumpōtu eṉṉic cantippatākak kūṟiṉāyē! niṉṉi yāṉ cantiyāmi pōṉa tevvāṟu?“
“mōrviṟpatu em kulattoḻil. nīyum ōr iṭiyaṉ tāṉē! aṉṟēl, cūriyaṉ makaṉām karṇaṉō.?”
“nī eṉṉi ikaḻntu kūṟuvatāl niṉṉuṭaṉ oṉṟum kūṟēṉ. pēraḻakiyāṉa niṉṉuṭaṉ maṟumāṟṟam pēca yārukkut tuṇivuḷatu?”
“appaṭiyāyiṉ oṉṟum colla vēṇṭā.”
“yāṉ niṉṉip pōkaviṭēṉ.”
“nāṉ eṉṉa uṉṉiṭam kaṭaṉ vāṅkiyuḷḷēṉā? eṉ kūṭiyip piṭittu niṟuttāmal pōyviṭu.”
“nī eṉṉiṭam vāṅkik koṇṭa poruḷip paṟṟik kūṟukiṟēṉ kēḷ. uṉ kaṇṇālē eṉ neñcam kavarnta 
kaḷvi nī.”
“avvāṟāyiṉ, uṉ neñcu eṉ tamiyaṉukkuc cōṟu koṭuppavum, entikkuk kalam koṇṭu cellavum, yāy coṉṉa kaṉṟukaḷi mēykkavumē payaṉpaṭum.”
“naṉṟu. iṉi nī ēviya toḻilkaḷic ceykiṟēṉ. ūr aṇimiyil uḷḷatu eṉiṉum uccip poḻutākiviṭṭatu. ippoḻutu naṭappatu aritu. iccōliyil taṅkic cuṉiyil kuḷittu mullimoṭṭukkaḷic cūṭi ip poḻilil eṉṉoṭu taṅki māliyil tari kuḷirnta vuṭaṉ ūrkkuc cellalām.”
“nāṉ ūrukku ippōtē cella vēṇṭum. nī ciṟumiyari mayakki avarkaḷiṭam ivvāṟu kūṟu. palapacukkaḷiyum teviṭṭāmal kūṭum ēṟu pōla nāṭōṟum makaḷir palarit tuykkum kaḷvaṉ nī!”
“eṉṉāl virumpappaṭuvār immaṇṇulakil niṉṉi yaṉṟi illi. tirumālaṭiyi vaṇaṅki uṟuti kūṟukiṟēṉ.“
“uṉ vārttiyi uṇmiyeṉa nampupavarkaḷē uṉ ṉoṭu kūṭutaṟku uṭaṉpaṭuvar. uṉ varuttam kaṇṭu uṭaṉ paṭukiṉṟēṉ. em puḻikkaṭip pakkam kāñcimara niḻalil varuka. varumpōtu āmpaṟ paṇṇālē kuṟiceytu varuka.”
itu mulli nilattu akappuṟat talivaṉtaliviyar kaḷiṭiyē muṟiyē nikaḻnta uriyāṭal.	(kali. 108)
ēḻiyi viṉaval -
iṭantalippāṭṭiṟkut taliviyic cantikkac cellum talivaṉ avaḷ taṉatu varaviṉi etirnōkki niṟkum nili paṟṟit taṉ maṉatti viṉaviyavāṟu cēṟal. (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal) 	(vī. cō. 92 urimēṟ.)
ēṟpuṟa aṇital -
iyaṟkip puṇarccikkup piṉ kalaviyāl nilikulintu kiṭanta taliviyiṉ āṭiyaṇikaḷit talivaṉ tiruttamuṟa aṇivittal. avaṟṟiṉ nilikuliviṉāl pāṅki mutali yōr aiyuṟa iṭaṉiṉṟi itu ceyvatu.
iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarcciyiṉ oruvakiyākiya teyvap puṇarcci yeṉṉum tokutikkaṇ oru kūṟṟu. sataliviyiṉ (eytum) iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇṇum varum kūṟṟām.] 	(na.a.125.)


`ēṟṟa uvakiyōṭu iṉiyavaḷ muṟuval' -
talivaṉ uḷḷakkaruttit tāṉ uṭaṉpaṭṭa makiḻc ciyit terivikkum vāyilākat talivi puṉmuṟuval ceytal. iḵtu iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇṇō iṭan talippāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇō nikaḻvatu. (itu ‘vaṟitunaki tōṟṟal’ eṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭum. (na. a. 127) (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
ēṟṟu makaḷ moḻital -
talivaṉ varivu vēṇṭit tamari viṭuttavaḻit taṉtamar varivu maṟukkātavāṟu tōḻiyi aṟattoṭu niṟkumāṟu talivi vēṇṭavē, tōḻi avvēṇṭukōḷi uṭ koṇṭu cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟṟal (kuṟiñci naṭi yiyal)	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
ēṟukōṭal -
kikkiḷiyil ācuramākiya ēṟu kōṭal. taliviyiṉ illattār aṭakka vēṇṭum eṉṟu viṭutta kāḷiyip paṟṟi aṭakkit talivaṉ taliviyip peṟutal.			(to. po. 92, 105 nac. kali. 105)
ēṟukōṭaṟ kikkiḷikkaṇ kuravi āṭat tōḻi taliviyi aḻittatu -
talivaṉ taliviyi varitaṟkuk koṭiya kāḷiyit taḻuvi aṭakki variyum urimi peṟum tirumaṇam ākiya oruvakik kikkiḷiyiṭattut talivaṉukku avaṉ kaṭamiyiṉi niṉippūṭṭavum, avaṉukku aruḷ ceyyat tam nilatteyva mākiya tirumāli vēṇṭavum, kuravik kūttāṭat tōḻi taliviyi aḻittal.			(to. po. 45 nac., kali. 103)
ēṟukōḷ -
āyarkula vaḻakkappaṭi orupeṇṇi varintu koḷvataṟ kāka ēṟutaḻuval 	(kōvi. 136 uri)
ēṟukōṭpaṟi. 	(piṅ. 544)
mullinilat talivaṉ tāṉ kuṟitta taliviyi maṇantu kōṭaṟku avaḷuṟaviṉar viṭutta kāḷiyi aṭakki av veṟṟikkup paricāka attaliviyik koḷḷutal. iḵtu ācuram eṉṟum, arumporuḷviṉi eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum maṉṟaluḷ aṭaṅkum.	(to. po. 92 nac.)
ēṟukōḷ kūṟi varivu kaṭātal -
talivaṉ pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ toṭarntuvara, tōḻi avaṉiṭam, “em aiyaṉmār talimakaḷiṉ uṭalvaṉappiṉik kaṇṭu avaḷ maṇapparuvam eytiviṭṭataṉi yuṇarntu tām toḻuvattil celuttum viṭiyiṉi aṭarppavaṉukkē avaḷi maṇamceytu koṭuppatāka muṭivuceytu, ciṟanta viṭiyiṉ maruppiṉiyum cīvi em ūril ataṉit toḻu vattil pukutta ēṟpāṭum ceytuviṭṭaṉar” eṉa ēṟukōḷ kūṟi varivu kaṭāyatu.	(kōvi. 136)
‘pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatu ikkūṟṟu.
ēṟukōḷ veṉṟi -
accam tarum kāḷiyi aṭakki ataṉmēl ēṟi varum āyarkulat talivaṉ, maliyik kuṭiyākap piṭitta kaṇṇaṉ nappiṉṉiyi maṇakka ēṟukaḷ ēḻaṉit taḻuviyatupōla, taliviyi aṭivataṟkut taḻuvavēṇ ṭiya ēṟṟiṉi aṭakkit taḻuvivanta ceytiyit tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟutal pōlvatoru veṟṟip perun tiṇit tuṟi.	(pu. ve. mā. 18-5)
ēṟutaḻuvutal -
valiya kāḷiyiṉi aṭakki vayappaṭuttutal. ēṟukōḷ kāṇka. (itu perumpālum talivi oruttiyi maṇappa taṟku avaḷ peṟṟōrāl kaṉyā culkamāka vikkappaṭu vatu.)	(to.po. 92 nac.)
ēṟu varavu kaṇṭu iraṅki urittal -
kaṟpuk kālattē taliviyi nīttup poruḷvayiṉ pirintu virumpiyavāṟu poruḷ tēṭi muṭittu ūrkku mīḷaluṟṟa talivaṉ, ciṟanta ēṟu nalla pacuvuṭaṉ ūriṭattē varutalik kaṇṭu, “ikkār kālattuc cevvāṉiyuṭiya mālik kālam talivi eṉ pirivip poṟuttirukkac ceyyum aḷaviṉa taṉṟē!” eṉṟu iraṅkik kūṟutal. 
itu ‘poruḷvayiṉ pirivu’ eṉṟum tokutikkaṇ oru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 346)
`ēṉōr pāṅkiṉum' eṉṉum cūttirattu ēṉōr āvār -
ēṉōr eṉṟatu, nirimēyttal pēṇal mutaliya nilat tiṟkuriya toḻili mēṟkoḷḷāmal, ōtal - ōtuvittal - āḷutal - mutaliya viṉikaḷi mēṟkoṇṭoḻuku vāri. avarkaḷ puṟattiṇi oḻukalāṟṟiṉkaṇ vēṟuvēṟu peyar peṟutalāṉ, āyar vēṭṭuvar eṉpāri nōkka, avarkaḷ ‘ēṉōr’ āyiṉar. ammuṟiyāṉ avarkaḷi ‘uyarntōr’ eṉac cuṭṭik kūṟutal nūl vaḻakkāyiṟṟu.			(tol. akat. 24 ca. pāla)
ēṉōr pāṅkiṉum kāmappakuti -
makkaṭ peṇṭir kaṭavuḷarip puṇarcci viruppāṉ nayattal. (to. po. 83 nac.)
ai
aintiṇi -
1) aivakippaṭṭa nilam 2) aivakippaṭṭa oḻukkam. 
malippakutiyākiya kuṟiñci, kāṭṭuppakutiyākiya mulli. vayaṟpakutiyākiya marutam, kaṭaṟkarip pakutiyākiya neytal, mulliyum kuṟiñciyum toṭarntu cila āṇṭu maḻi peṟāmiyāl veṅkatir veppattāl vaṟaṇṭa nilamāka māṟum vaṉpāli, marutamum neytalum toṭarntu cila āṇṭu maḻi peṟāmiyāl avvāṟē māṟum meṉ pāli ākiya iruvakittākiya pāli - eṉa nilavaki aintām.
talivaṉum taliviyum kūṭum puṇarcciyākiya kuṟiñciyum, talivaṉ taliviyip pirintucellum piritalākiya pāliyum, pirintapiṉ talivaṉ mīṇṭu varuvatākak kūṟiya paruvam varuntuṇiyum illattil talivi āṟṟiyirukkum iruttalākiya mulliyum, kuṟitta paruvattē talivaṉ vārāmiyāl talivi varuntum irakkamākiya neytalum, talivaṉtaliviyar iṭiyē kāmiṉpattiṉi mikuvik kum ūṭalākiya marutamum - eṉa oḻukkavaki aintākum.			(to.po. 14 nac.)
aintiṇikkum uripporuḷ
mulli-
iruttalum iruttal nimittamum; talivaṉ pirivu uṇarttiyavaḻit talivi avar piriyār eṉṟiruttalum, pirintuḻik kuṟitta paruvam aṉṟu eṉṟu tāṉē kūṟalum, paruvam varuntuṇiyum āṟṟiyiruntami piṉṉark kūṟuvaṉa vum pōlvaṉa iruttal. (talivaṉ kuṟitta paruvam varutaṟku muṉṉart talivi āṟṟātu kūṟuvaṉa pāliyām.) paruvaṅ kaṇṭu āṟṟātu tōḻi kūṟuvaṉavum, paruvam aṉṟu eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiyaṉavum, varuvar eṉṟu vaṟpuṟuttiṉavum, talivaṉ pācaṟikkaṇ iruntu urittaṉavum, avi pōlvaṉavum iruttal nimittamām.
kuṟiñci -
puṇartalum puṇartal nimittamum; nāṉku nilattum puṇarcci nikaḻumēṉum, aintiṇiyuḷ ciṟappikkappaṭum kaḷavup puṇarccikkaṇ iyaṟkip puṇarcciyum, iṭantalip pāṭum, pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭamum, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭamum, ataṉ pakutiyākiya pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟi paṟṟiyaṉavum puṇartal. talivaṉ tōḻiyik kuṟiyuṟum pakutiyum, āṇṭut tōḻi kūṟuvaṉavum, kuṟi nērtalum, maṟuttalum mutaliyaṉa puṇarcci nimittamām.
marutam -
ūṭalum ūṭal nimittamum; pulavi mutaliyaṉa ūṭalām. parattiyum pāṇaṉum mutaliyōr ūṭal nimittamām.
neytal -
iraṅkalum iraṅkal nimittamum; talivaṉip pirintu taṉittirukkum talivi avaṉ kuṟitta paruvattē mīṇṭu vārāmiyāl kaṭalum kaṭaṟkaric cōliyum uppaṅkaḻi yum kāṇuntōṟum iraṅkalum, mālippoḻutiṉiyum tuṇiyākak kūṭic cellum paṟavikaḷiyum kaṇṭu iraṅkalum pōlvaṉa iraṅkal. akkaṭal mutaliyaṉavum, talivaṉ kuṟitta paruvattu vārātiruttalum pōlvaṉa iraṅkal nimittamām.	(to.po. 14 nac)
pāli -
piritalum pirital nimittamum. ōtal paki tūtu avaṟṟiṉ pakutiyāṉa  poruḷ eṉpaṉa paṟṟip piritalum, uṭaṉpōk kum pirivām. uṭaṉpōkkiṟku muṉ tōḻiyoṭu kūṟutal mutaliyaṉa piritalnimittam. pirintapiṉ talivi varuntuvaṉa vum, tōḻi āṟṟuvippaṉavum pāliyātaliṉ piṉ orukāl piritaṟku nimittamām.	(to. po. 14 nac.)
aintiṇit talimakkaḷātaṟkuriyār -
aracar antaṇar vaṇikar vēḷāḷar eṉṟa marutanilattu nāṟkulamakkaḷum, kuṟiñci pāli mulli marutam neytal eṉṟa ainnilattut talimakkaḷum, aintiṇit talimakkaḷ ātaṟkuriyar.	(to. po. 24 nac.; na.a. 30,31)
aintiṇip peyarkkāraṇam -
kāṭṭiṟku mullippūvum, malikkuk kuṟiñcippūvum, vayaṟpakutikku marutamaramum, kaṭaṟkarip pakutikku neytaṟ pūvum, pāli nilattiṟkup pālimaramum ciṟattaliṉ, aintiṇikaṭkum pū maram ākiya karupporuḷ paṟṟip peyariṭappaṭṭatu eṉpār iḷampūraṇar.	(to. po. 5)
mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal pāli eṉṟa peyarkaḷ muṟiyē iruttal puṇartal ūṭal iraṅkal pirital eṉṟa uripporuḷ paṟṟiyē amintaṉa eṉpār nacciṉārk kiṉiyar.	(to. po. 5 )
ippeyarkaḷ karupporuḷ uripporuḷ eṉa iraṇṭum paṟṟi vantaṉa eṉṟu iruvarkaruttum koḷvar ilakkaṇa viḷakka nūlār.	(i. vi. po. 379)
kāṭum maliyum ūrum kaṭalumāṉa nāṉilappakutikaḷum avvan nilattiṟ ciṟanta mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal eṉṟa aṭiyāḷap pūkkaḷāl aḻikkappaṭṭaṉa. curamum pālip pūviṉ peyarāl pāli eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. (to. po.uri.pārati)
mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal pāli eṉpaṉa oḻukkat tiṉ peyarkaḷ. oḻukkattiṉ peyar atu nikaḻum nilat tiṟkup peyarāyiṟṟu.	(to. po. 5 kuḻa.)
aintiṇiyuḷḷum kaḷavu nikaḻtal -
mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal pāli eṉa aivakippaṭṭa nilaṅkaḷukkum uriya mutalum karuvum vantu uripporuḷ mayaṅkutalum uṇṭu eṉṟa marapiṉāṉ aṉpiṉ aintiṇik kaḷaveṉappaṭuvatu (iṟi.a.1) eṉa aintiṇikaḷukkum uriya nilattiṉkaṇ kaḷavu oḻukkam nikaḻalām eṉpatu. eṉavē, kaḷavu kuṟiñci nilattiṟkē uriyatu eṉṟu koḷḷutal vēṇṭā eṉpatu.	(to. po. 14, 93 nac.)
ainnila uṇavu -
kuṟiñci - tiṉi, vetirnel, kiḻaṅku; pāli - taruppaṇam (aval), āṟu alittup peṟṟaṉa; mulli - varaku; marutam - nel; neytal - uppu, viliyiṟ peṟṟaṉa.(ta. ne. vi. 10 uri)
ainnilac ceyalkaḷ -
kuṟiñci - niṟitēṉ aḻittal, tiṉi arital, kiḻaṅku akaḻtal; pāli - neṟi ceṉṟu alittal, ūr eṟital, niri kōṭal; mulli - āṭu pacu erumi eṉa mūviṉam ōmpal, varaku arital, ēṟu kōṭal; marutam - uḻavoṭu payilutal, viḻavu ayartal, puṉalāṭal; neytal - tirikkaṭa lil mīṉ piṭikkac cēṟal, uppākkutal, muttuk kuḷittal.			(ta. ne. vi. 9 uri)
ainnila nīr -
kuṟiñci - aruvi nīrum, cuṉi nīrum; pāli - vaṟuñcuṉi nīrum, uvar nīrum; mulli - kāṭṭāṟṟu nīr; marutam - kiṇaṟṟu nīrum, poyki nīrum; neytal - maṇaṟkūval nīr.			(ta. ne. vi. 10 uri)
ainnilap paṇkaḷ -
kuṟiñci - kuṟiñcippaṇ; pāli - pañcurap paṇ; mulli - tārap paṇ (cātāri) ; marutam - marutappaṇ - neytal - cevvaḻippaṇ.	(ta. ne. vi. 11)
ainnilap paṟavikaḷ -
kuṟiñci - mayil, kiḷi; pāli- paruntu; mulli - puṟā, kāṉaṅ kōḻi; marutam - aṉṉam, tārā; neytal - kampuḷ, aṉṉam, makaṉṟil.	(ta. ne. vi. 12)
ainnilap paṟikaḷ -
kuṟiñci - veṟippaṟi, toṇṭakap paṟi; pāli - tuṭippaṟi, pūcaṟpaṟi; mulli - ēṟaṅkōṭpaṟi; marutam - nellari kiṇi mutaliyaṉa; neytal - neytaṟ paṟi, nāvāyp paṟi.	(ta. ne. vi. 11)
ainnilap pūkkaḷ -
kuṟiñci - kāntaḷ, vēṅki, cuṉikkuvaḷi; pāli - marāmpū, kurāmpū, pātirampū, murukkampū; mulli - mulli, tōṉṟippū; marutam - kaḻunīr, tāmarippū; neytal - kaḻimuḷḷi, neytaṟpū.	(ta. ne. vi. 12)
ainnila makkaḷ - 
kuṟiñci - veṟpaṉ, kuṟavar; mulli -  kuṟumpoṟi nāṭaṉ, kuṭavar; pāli- viṭali, eyiṉar; marutam - makiḻnaṉ, uḻavar; neytal - cērppaṉ, paratavar - ākiyōrum āvar.
(āṇpāl uyarntōriyum, palarpāl ēṉiyōriyum kuṟikkum.) mēlum kuṟiñcikkup poruppaṉ maliyaṉ cilampaṉ koṭicci kuṟatti kuṟavar iṟavuḷar - ākiyōrum, mullikku aṇṇal mīḷi iṭicciyar āycciyar kōvalar iṭiyar - ākiyōrum, pālikkuk kāḷi eyiṉar eyiṟṟi maṟavar - ākiyōrum marutattukku ūraṉ kiḻatti maṉivi kaṭiyar kaṭiciyar ākiyōrum, neyta lukkuk koṇkaṉ  tuṟivaṉ tuṟivi paratti nuḷiyar nuḷicciyar - ākiyōrum makkaḷāvar.	(ta. ne. vi. 8 uri)
ainnila maraṅkaḷ -
kuṟiñci - vēṅki, akil, cantaṉam; pāli - ōmi; mulli - kuruntam, koṉṟi; marutam - kāñci, marutam, vañci; neytal - tāḻi, puṉṉi, ñāḻal. (ta. ne. vi. 12)

ainnila mākkaḷ (vilaṅkukaḷ) - 
kuṟiñci - ciṅkam, puli, yāṉi ; pāli - cennāy ; mulli - muyal, irali; marutam - erumi,  nīrnāy; neytal - mutalimā.	(ta. ne. vi. 12 uri)
aiyakkiḷavi -
kaḷavukkālattil oṉṟik kūṟuvōmā, kūṟal vēṇṭāvā eṉṟu aiyamuṟṟuk kūṟum col. itu talivaṉukkē uriyatu.
“colliṉ, maṟātuīvāḷ maṉṉō ivaḷ” (kali. 61) eṉpatu talivaṉ kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 238 nac.)
aiyam tīrak kūṟal -
talivaṉ variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirinta kāli, talivi melivu kaṇṭu avaḷicivu peṟṟu aṟat toṭu niṟṟaṟku muṟpaṭṭa tōḻi, talivimaṉattu nikaḻnta tōr aiyattik kuṟippāṉ uṇarntu, “talivi! nam kuṭikkup paḻi variṉum talivaṟkup paḻipaṭak kūṟēṉ” eṉṟu avaḷatu aiyam nīṅkak kūṟit tāṉ aṟattoṭu niṟṟaṟku avaḷi uṭaṉpaṭuvittal.
itu kaḷavukkālattu ‘variporuṭ pirital’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 219)
aiyam (1) -
aiyam eṉpatu oru poruḷik kaṇṭavaḻi ituveṉat tuṇiya iyalāta nilimi.
`aṇaṅkukol āymayil kollō kaṉaṅkuḻi
mātarkol'   (kuṟaḷ. 1081) 
eṉṟu taliviyik kaṇṭa aḷavil talivaṉuḷḷattil aiyam nikaḻntavāṟu.	(to. po. 94 nac.)
aiyam eṉpatu oruporuḷmēl iruporuḷ taṉmi karuti varum maṉattaṭumāṟṟam.	(to. po. 260 pērā.)
itaṉi vaṭanūlār vitarkkam eṉpar.
mutaṉmutal taliviyic cantikkum talivaṉ avaḷi nilavulaka makaḷō, tēvarulakamakaḷō eṉṟu aiyuṟum; taliviyum talivaṉi ‘ivaṉ kaṭampu amar kāḷi kollō, iyakkaṉ kollō’ eṉṟum aiyuṟum. (iṟi.a. 2 uri)
kantaruvattiṉ  vaḻīiya ulakiyalil talivikku ittakiya aiyam tōṉṟuvatuṇṭu eṉpār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. 			(cīvaka. 713)
aiyam (2) -
iyaṟkip puṇarcciyaṉṟu mutaṉmutal taliviyik kaṇṭa talivaṉ  aiyuṟutal. ivaḷ teyvamakaḷō māṉuṭa makaḷtāṉō eṉṟu aiyuṟutal. itu kikkiḷip pakutiyiṉ kiḷavi.	(na.a. 120)
ittuṟi puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyil āṇpāṟ kūṟṟuk kikkiḷiyilum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. (14-2)
aiyam tīrtal -
talivaṉ tāṉ koṇṭa aiyatti akaṟṟik koḷal. taliviyiṉ āṭi aṇikaḷiyum āṭavaṉik kaṇṭatāl avaḷiṭam tōṉṟiya meyppāṭukaḷiyum uṟṟaṟinta talivaṉ avaḷ teyvamakaḷ allaḷ māṉiṭamakaḷē eṉṟu teḷital. avaḷ cūṭiyirunta malarkaḷ vāṭutalum, kaṇkaḷ  imittalum, kālkaḷ nilattil tōytalum piṟavum aiyam akalat tuṇiyām.
itu kikkiḷip pakutittuṟi. ittuṟi puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyuḷ ‘tuṇivu’ eṉa āṇpāṟ kūṟṟuk kikkiḷiyilum kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. (14-3). nampi akapporu ḷil ‘aiyam kaṭital’ eṉappaṭum. 	(na. a. 121.)
itu teḷital (kōvi. 3) eṉavum kūṟappaṭum.
aiyam tīrtaṟku ētuvāvaṉa -
talivi tōḷilum muliyilum eḻutappaṭṭa valliyum, avaḷ aṇintuḷḷa āparaṇaṅkaḷum, vāṭippōkaluṟum malarkaḷum, avaṟṟil paṭintuḷḷa vaṇṭukaḷum, nilam tōya naṭakkum aṭikaḷum, piṟaḻntu imikkum kaṇkaḷum, avaḷukku eḻaluṟṟa accavuṇarvum, piṟavum  taliva ṉiṭattē, “ivaḷ tēvarmakaḷō, māṉuṭa makaḷō?” eṉa nikaḻāniṉṟa aiyatti nīkki ivaḷ māṉuṭamakaḷē eṉṟu tuṇitaṟku ētuvāvaṉa.	(na. a. 121)
payiṉṟataṉ mēl allatu cellāta tātu ūtum vaṇṭu, oruvarāl iḻikkappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ, muliyiṉum tōḷiṉum eḻutappaṭṭa toyyiṟ koṭi, kikkoṇṭu mōntu uyirkkum kaḻunīrppū, vāṉkaṇ allāta ūṉakkaṇ, kaṇṭaṟiyāta vaṭivu kaṇṭa accattāl piṟanta taṭumāṟṟam, akkaṇṇiṉ itaḻ imittal, āṇmakaṉik kaṇṭa viṭattup piṟakkum accam eṉṉum avveṇ vakip poruḷum avi pōlvaṉa piṟavum aiyatti nīkkum karuviyām.	(to. po. 95 nac.)
piṟa eṉṟataṉāl, kāl nilam tōytalum, niḻaliṭutalum, viyarttalum mutaliyaṉa koḷḷappaṭum.	(to. po. 95 nac. uri)
aiyuṟṟuk kalaṅkal -
kaḷavuk kālattil talivaṉ variviṭi vittup poruḷ vayiṉ pirintirunta nāḷil orunāḷ, makaṭpēca varuvā ruṭiya maṇamuracu taṉ maṉivayiṉ olikkak kēṭṭa talivi tōḻiyiṭam, “nam illattil iṉṟu olikkappaṭum im maṇa muracu eṉṉi yārukku makaṭpēci muṭikkak karutiyō eṉpatu pulaṉākavilliyē” eṉṟu uṟutiyāka aṟiya iyalātu kalaṅkik kūṟutal.
itu ‘variporuṭpirital’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatu oru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 297)
aiyuṟutal -
taliviyum tōḻiyumākiya iruvarum uḷḷa iṭattiṟkut talivaṉ vantu ūr peyar mutaliyavaṟṟi mutalil viṉavi, piṉṉart taḻiyuṭiyiṉi avarkaḷukkuk kiyuṟiyākat taruvatākak kūṟit taṉ karuttik kuṟippāṉ aṟivittavaḻi, “ivaṉ yāvaṉō? ivaṉ eṇṇam yātō” eṉṟu tōḻi aiyuṟṟuk kūṟutal.
iḵtu ‘yārē ivarmaṉat teṇṇam yāteṉat, tērtal’ eṉṟa pakutikkaṇ aminta mutalām kūṟṟu.(kōvi. 60)			(na.a. 140)
uḷḷuṟikaṭkum iṭiyē vēṟṟumikaḷ -
uḷḷuṟi aintum akattiṇi māntarkkuriya kūṟṟākac ceyyuṭkaṇ varumēṉum, ivaṟṟiṭiyē vēṟṟumikaḷ varumāṟu : 
1)	uṭaṉuṟi : karupporuḷkaḷiṉ paṇpu ceyalkaḷi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu, uvamamum poruḷumāka iyittuk kūṟutaṟku ēlāmal, ‘tiṟattiyal maruṅkiṉ teriyumōrkkē’ pulappaṭṭu varum.
2)	uḷḷuṟi uvamam : uvamamum poruḷumāka oppiṭṭuk kūṟutaṟku ēṟpuṭittāy, teyvam oḻinta karupporuḷ kaḷiṉ aṭippaṭiyil, viṉi payaṉ mey uru piṟappu eṉṉum vakippāṭu tōṉṟat teṟṟeṉap pulappaṭṭu varum.
3)	cuṭṭu : iṟiccip poruḷ pōlac coṟṟoṭarōṭu uṭaṉuṟivu ākāmal, variyaṟiyiṉṟi yātāṉum oru poruḷ paṟṟik kūṟum kūṟṟiṉuḷ, kūṟuvōr tam karuttākak koḷḷumāṟu nuṇṇitāka amintu varum.
4)	naki : mutaṟporuḷ karupporuḷ eṉṉum aṭippaṭi yik karutāmal eḷḷutali aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu nakiyāṭṭāka nikaḻum kūṟṟiṉuḷ kuṟippāka amintu varum.
5)	ciṟappu : karupporuḷi aṭippaṭiyākak karutāmal oruvaric ceyaṟkiyākac ciṟappittup pārāṭṭik kūṟum kūṟṟiṉuḷ nuṭpamāka amintu varum.	(to. poru. 47 ca. pāla.)
aivakit tāyar -
īṉṟa tāy, ūṭṭun tāy, mulittāy, kittāy, cevilittāy (piṅ. 396) eṉṉumivar. āṭṭuvāḷ, ūṭṭu vāḷ, ōluṟuttuvāḷ, noṭi payiṟṟuvāḷ, kittāy, eṉṉumivar.	(cīvaka. 363 nac.)
o
otta kiḻatti -
piṟappu, kuṭimi, āṇmi, āṇṭu, uruvu, niṟutta kāma vāyil, niṟi, aruḷ, uṇarvu, tiru eṉṟa pattāṉum talivaṉukku ottavaḷāy avaṉ maṇantu illaṟam nikaḻt tutaṟkup poruttamāṉa talivi.	(to. po. 93 nac.)
otta kiḻavaṉ -
piṟappu, kuṭimi, āṇmi, āṇṭu, uruvu, niṟutta kāma vāyil, niṟi aruḷ uṇarvu tiru eṉṟa pattāṉum taliviyi ottu avaḷi maṇantu illaṟam nikaḻttap poruttamāṉa talivaṉ.	(to. po. 93 nac.)
otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum kāṇṭaṟkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟṟu -
“karuntaṭaṅkaṇ vaṇṭāka, cevvāy taḷirāka, arumpu pōṉṟa meṉmuli tottāka, perumūṅkil pōṉṟa tōḷāṉum peṇtaṉmiyāṉum aḻaku peṟṟatoru pūṅkoṭi aṉiyāḷi nōkkiṉēm; nōkkiṉēmāka em viḻikaḷ makiḻntaṉa” eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa kūṟṟu. (pu.ve.mā. 14-1, to. po. 93 nac. )
oppu (1) -
itu maṉṟal eṭṭaṉuḷ oṉṟu. ituvē pirācāpattiyam eṉavumpaṭum. peṇṇiṉ tanti, uriya uṟavum kōttiramum uṭiya oruvaṉukku, avaṉ tarum paricap poruḷiṉ irumaṭaṅku tāṉ koṭuttu uṟaviṉar cūḻa muṟippaṭi kaṭimaṇam ceytu koṭuppatu.
piracāpati - purōkitaṉ. avaṉi muṉṉiṭalāl iḵtu ip peyarttāyiṟṟu. 
mittuṉa kōttirattāṉ makaḷ vēṇṭic ceṉṟāl maṟātu koṭuppatu. (iṟi.a. 1 uri) itu kaṟpiṉ pāṟpaṭum 		(ta. ne. vi.14)
itu peruntiṇip pāṟpaṭum nālvaki maṇaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. ituvum kantaruvattiṉpāṟpaṭum eṉpatu perum poruḷ eṉṟa paṇṭi ilakkaṇanūl kuṟippiṭum ceyti eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.	(cīvaka. 187 uri)
oppu (2) -
1. uvami yaḷavi (ci.ci. aḷavi 1)
2. pirācāpattiyam (iṟi.a.1 uri)	 (ṭu)
orukāṟ pirivu -
oruvaḻit taṇattal 	(cāmi. 82)
oruṅka varuntal -
tam makaḷ uṭaṉpōyapiṉ pala āṇṭukaḷ avaḷuṭaṉ toṭarpu koṇṭirunta tam nili kuṟittut tāyar maṉam aḻiyumāṟu varuntutal. (pāli naṭiyiyal)			(vī. cō. 93. uri mēṟ)
orucār pakaṟkuṟi -
oru kūṟṟup pakaṟkuṟi. aḵtāvatu talivaṉ taṉ vēṭki mikutiyāl pakaṟkuṟiyiṭattu vantu niṟkap pāṅki kuṟiyiṭat tut taliviyic cellaviṭāmal maṟuttuk kūṟat talivaṉ varuttattuṭaṉ cellutal. itu kaḷavukkuriya kiḷavit tokikaḷuḷ oṉṟu; pakaṟkuṟiyuḷ aṭaṅkuvatu.	(na. a. 123)
orucār pakaṟkuṟi vaki - 
1.	iraṅkal - talivaṉ pirintapōtu talivi varuntutalum, atu kaṇṭu tōḻi pulamputalum, pirintu ceṉṟuḷḷa iṭattē talivaṉ varuntutalum ākiyaṉa.
2.	vaṉpuṟi - tōḻi taliviyi vaṟpuṟuttal - iṭit turittal.
3.	iṟceṟippuṇarttal - puṟattuc cella iyalāta vaki taliviyi illattuḷ ceṟittu vittirukkum ceytiyit tōḻi talivaṟku urittal. (na. a. 153)
oruñāṉṟu talimakaṉ taṉmaṉikkaṇ ceṉṟatu kaṇṭu talivi puṟaṅkūṟiyatu kēṭṭa paratti, talivikkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpat taṉ tōḻikkuc colliyatu -
“tōḻi! nam talivaṉ viruppam tarum eṉ nakilkaḷi muṟṟak kalantu piṉ taṉ pirivāl eṉ tōḷ nekiḻap pirintu taliviyiṭam ceṉṟuḷḷāṉ āyiṉum, puṟattāl pirinta avaṉ uḷḷattāl eṉṉi eṉṟum piriyāṉ; itaṉi avaḷ aṟintilaḷ“eṉṟa parattiyiṉ kūṟṟu.	(aiṅ. 39)
`orutali urimi vēṇṭal' -
kaḷaviṉpam tuykkum talivi iṭiviṭātu iṉpam nukartalōṭu illaṟam nikaḻttum urimiyiṉi uṟuti yākap peṟutali virumpal.	(to. po. 225 nac.)
orutali uḷḷutal -
iṭiviṭātu talivaṉ taliviyi niṉittal			(to. po. 100 nac. na. a. 36 uri. i. vi. 405)
orutali uḷḷutaṟkaṇ talivaṉ taṉṉuḷ kūṟutal -
“taliviyiṉuṭiya muḷpōṉṟa paṟkaḷiyum, amiḻtam ūṟum cevvāyiyum, akilum cantaṉamum  kamaḻum aṟal pōṉṟa kūntaliyum, periya amartta kuḷirnta kaṇkaḷiyu muṭiya avaḷatu muṟuvaloṭu matarttut tōṉṟum pārviyiṉiyum niṉittuk kāṇpēṉ” (kuṟun. 286) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
orutalikkāmam -
orutali - oru pakkam; orutalikkāmam - oru pakkattu viruppam. talivaṉ talivi ākiya iruvarum otta aṉ piṉarāyiruttal iṉṟit talivaṉ māttiram taliviyiṭa mō, talivimāttiram talivaṉiṭamō aṉpu koḷḷu tal. itu kikkiḷi eṉṉum akappuṟat tiṇiyākak koḷḷappaṭum.	(na.a. 3)

orutali vēṭki -
iyaṟkip puṇarcci mutal kaḷavu veḷippaṭum tuṇiyum iruvarukkum uḷavām ilakkaṇattuḷ mutaṟkaṇṇatu orutali vēṭkiyām.
aḵtāvatu puṇarāta muṉṉum puṇarnta piṉṉum talivaṉ talivi eṉṉum iruvarkkum iṭiviṭṭu nikaḻātu oru taṉmittāki nilipeṟum vēṭki.
itaṉi ‘iruvayiṉ ottal’ eṉpar.	(to. po. 100 nac.)
`orupāṟkiḷavi eṉippāṟ kaṇṇum' varutal -
ulakiṉuḷ kiḻavarum kiḻattiyarum palarākavum, ceyyuḷuḷ kiḻavaṉ eṉavum kiḻatti eṉavum varum orumic coṟkaḷē tam āṟṟalāl ulakiluḷḷa kiḻavar palariyum kiḻattiyar palariyum cuṭṭip paṉmippāṟ poruḷavāy varutal. 			(to. po. 222 nac.)
orupālmēl vittuk kūṟiya ceyti ēṉiya nāṟpālkaḷi yum taḻuvivarutal. “nañcuṇṭāṉ cām” eṉa āṇpālmēl vittuk kūṟiya ceyti ēṉiya peṇpāl palarpāl oṉṟaṉpāl palaviṉpāl eṉṟa ēṉiya nāṟpāṟkaṇṇum varutal.	(to. co. 161 cēṉā.)
`orumi kēṇmiyiṉ uṟukuṟi, poṟiyiṉ yātta puṇarcci nōkkit teḷintōḷ, arumi cāṉṟa  nāliraṇṭu vakiyiṉ perumi cāṉṟa iyalpiṉ' kūṟutal -
tāṉ eṉṟum tōḻi eṉṟum vēṟupāṭillāta oṉṟiya naṭpiṉālēyē talivaṉ taṉṉiṭam kuṟi nayappataṉi eṭuttuk kūṟi ataṉi niṟivēṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu tōḻi taṉṉiṭam vēṇṭiya ceytiyi, muṉpē iyaṟkip puṇarcci nikaḻntataṉi uṭkoṇṭu tāṉ niṟivēṟṟuva tākat teḷinta eṇṇam koṇṭa talivi, tāṉ taliva ṉukkup putiyavaḷāyc cantittapōtu avaṉ taṉkaṇ nikaḻttiya meytoṭṭup payiṟal mutaliya eṭṭiṉālē tāṉ nāṇum maṭaṉum nīṅkavilli eṉṟu tōḻikkuk kūṟutal. 

varumāṟu :
`aṉiyaṉa palapā rāṭṭip piyeṉa
valiyar pōlac cōrpataṉ oṟṟieṉ
neñcu nekiḻnta cevvi kāṇūup
puliyar pōlap puṉkaṇ nōkkit
toḻalum toḻutāṉ; toṭalum toṭṭāṉ;
kāḻvari nillāk kaṭuṅkaḷiṟu aṉṉōṉ
toḻūum toḻūumavaṉ taṉmi
ēḻit taṉmiyō illi tōḻī!'  (kali. 55)
itaṉkaṇ, ‘pārāṭṭi’ eṉpatu poypārāṭṭal, ‘cōr pataṉ oṟṟi eṉ neñcu nekiḻnta cevvi’ kūṟiṉamiyiṉ kūṭutal uṟutal; ‘puliyar pōla nōkki’ eṉpatu nīṭu niṉintu iraṅkal; ‘toḻalum toḻutāṉ’ eṉpatu iṭam peṟṟut taḻāal; ‘toṭalum toṭṭāṉ’ eṉpatu mey toṭṭup payiṟal; ‘kaḷiṟu pōla aṟiviṉ elliyil nillātavaṉ’ eṉpatu tīrāt tēṟṟam. ivi oruvāṟṟāṉ koḷḷappaṭum.	(to. po. 111 nac.)
oruvaḻit taṇattal -
varivu kaṭāviya tōḻiyiṭam varivukku uṭaṉpaṭṭa talivaṉ taṉṉūrukku orumuṟi pōy varuvatākak kūṟic cellutal. itaṉi ‘iṭṭuppiritalum arumi ceytayart talum’ (to. po. 111, kali. 53 nac.)eṉpa. itu kaḷaviyaluḷ 16 āvatu kiḷavi. (na. a. 123)	
oruvaḻit taṇattal kūṟṟukkaḷ -
1. akaṉṟaṇivu kūṟal, 2. kaṭaloṭu varavu kēṭṭal, 3. kaṭaloṭu pulattal, 4) aṉṉamoṭu āytal, 5) tērvaḻi nōkkik kaṭaloṭu kūṟal, 6) kūṭal iḻittal, 7) cuṭaroṭu pulampal, 8) poḻutu kaṇṭu mayaṅkal, 9) paṟaviyoṭu varuntal, 10) paṅkayattoṭu parivuṟṟurittal, 11) aṉṉa mōṭaḻital, 12) varavuṇarnturittal, 13) varutta mikuti kūṟal eṉṟa patiṉmūṉṟu kiḷavikaḷiyuṭiyatu oruvaḻit taṇattal eṉṉum kaḷaviyal tokuti. (kōvi. 181- 193)

oruvaḻit taṇattal vakikaḷ -
1)	celavu aṟivuṟuttal - talivaṉ tāṉ oruvaḻit taṇantu cellutalit tōḻikku aṟivippāṉ. avaḷ ataṉit talivikku aṟivippāḷ. avviruvaki aṟivuṟuttal.
2)	celavu uṭaṉpaṭāmi - tōḻi, talivaṉ celvati ēṟṟuk koḷḷātu maṟuttal.
3)	celavu uṭaṉpaṭuttal - talivaṉ, tōḻiyiṭam tāṉ cellum celaviṉi ataṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmi kūṟi ēṟkac ceytal.
4)	celavu uṭaṉpaṭutal - tōḻi talivaṉcelaviṉi ēṟṟuk koḷḷutal.
5)	ceṉṟuḻik kalaṅkal - talivaṉ oruvaḻit taṇanta kālattē talivi tuyaruṟutal.
6)	tēṟṟi āṟṟuvittal - tōḻi taliviyit teḷivuṟutti āṟutal aṭivittal.
7)	vantuḻi nonturi - talivaṉ mīṇṭapiṉṉar avaṉiṭam tōḻi tamatu tuyarattiṉik kūṟutalum, ataṟkavaṉ varunti yurittalum. ivvāṟu oruvaḻit taṇattal ēḻu vakippaṭum.	(na. a. 167)
oruvaḻit taṇattalil talivaṉit tōḻi iyaṟpaḻikkum eṉṟu añcit talivi avaḷ kēṭpat taṉṉuḷḷē kūṟiyatu -
“(eṉ kātalar eṅkō pōyviṭṭatāka niṉippavar kiṭakka.) eṉ kātalar eṉ kaṇṇiṉiṉṟu appāl akaṉṟi lar; nāṉ imittālum ataṉāl avarukkut tuṉpam illi; avar attuṇi nuṇṇiyar (maṟṟavar avar cēymikkaṇ ceṉṟuviṭṭatākak karutukiṉṟaṉar.)” eṉṟu tōḻi iyaṟ paḻikkāta vakiyāl talivaṉ ciṟappiṉit talivi kūṟutal.	(kuṟaḷ 1126)
oruvaḻit taṇantu vanta talivaṉ “emmi uḷḷiyum aṟitirō” eṉṟa tōḻikkuk kūṟutal -
“taliviyiṉ peṇmi nalaṉkaḷi nāṉ eppōtāvatu maṟantiruntāṟṟāṉē niṉikka muṭiyum! orupōtum avaṟṟi maṟantaṟiyēṉ ātaliṉ niṉittum aṟiyēṉ!” eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟutal.	(kuṟaḷ 1125)
oḻintatu viṉātal -
itu ‘moḻiyāmi viṉātal’ (i.vi. 507 uri) eṉavum paṭum. tirukkōviyāril ‘moḻi peṟātu kūṟal’ (57) eṉṉum kūṟṟum atu.
“karuṅkuḻalīr! num taṭṭip puṭippu oliyāl kiḷikaḷ ellām pōyiṉa; mīṇṭum tam iṉattiṉ kuralām eṉṟu karuti ittiṉippayirik kavarum eṉṟu karutiyō nīvir pēcā toḻintatu?” (tañci. kō. 73) eṉpatu pōṉṟu talivaṉ tōḻiyi viṉavuvatu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkimati uṭaṉpāṭu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 140)
oḻipaṉi - 
ittoṭar paṉi nīṅkumpaṭiyāṉa piṉpaṉik kālattic cuṭṭukiṟatu. piṉpaṉit tiṅkaḷ māci paṅkuṉi iraṇṭum. ivviru tiṅkaḷum pālikkuriya perumpoḻutiṉic cārum.	(ta. ne. vi.4)
oḷitta ūṭal veḷippaṭal -
viruntiṉarik kaṇṭatum maṟintirunta ūṭal, talivikkup paḷḷiyiṭattē veḷippaṭutal.
talivikku oppaṉi ceytu avaḷip pārāṭṭit talivaṉ avaḷit tuykkum nērattil, avaḷ uḷḷattē maṟintirunta kōpam veḷippaṭum. ‘ituvari uṉakkuk kātali nāṉ oruttiyē eṉṟiruntēṉ. annili māṟiyami yāl ippaṭukkikkaṇ nīyē iruppāyāka’ (ampikā. 493) eṉṟu kūṟit talivi paṭukkiyiṉiṉṟu iḻiyum ceyti itu.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇ oru kūṟṟu. iḵtu uṇartta uṇarā ūṭal.	(i. vi. 555)
oṉṟāc curam -
ariya cēya periya karuṅkal ceṟinta vaḻi ātaliṉ, melli yal ākiya taliviyi uṭaṉkoṇṭu talivaṉ cellu taṟkup poruntāta ariya pālivaḻi.	(to. po. 41 nac.)
oṉṟāt tamar -
uṭaṉpōkkiṟkup poruntāta taliviyiṉtāyar mutaliya uṟaviṉar.	(to. po.41 nac.)
`oṉṟāt tamariṉum paruvattum curattum oṉṟiya tōḻiyoṭu talivaṉ valittal' -
oṉṟāt tamar	-	uṭaṉpōkkiṟku maṉam poruntāta tāyar mutaliya cuṟṟattār.
oṉṟāp paruvam	-	taliviyi iṟceṟittamiyāl veḷiyē puṉamkāval mutaliyavaṟ ṟiṟkuc cella muṭiyāta kālam; tali vaṉik kāṇa muṭiyāmiyāl āṟṟi yiruppataṟku vāyppu illāta kālam.
oṉṟāc curam	-	naṭattaṟku aritātalum tolivi luḷḷatum kaṟkaḷ niṟintatum ātaliṉ talivaṉuṭaṉ talivi naṭantu pōtaṟ kup poruttamillāta pālinilam.
oṉṟiya tōḻi	-	taliviyiṉ viruppamē taṉviruppa mātaliṉ, talivi pirintu pōṉapiṉ uṟaviṉar (tōḻiyākiya) taṉṉik kaṭintu pēcum coṟkaḷip poṟut tuk koḷḷutaṟku maṉam icintu, taliviyi ayalavaṉ maṇattaṟku vāypput taralākātu eṉṟa nōkkat toṭu, naṭattaṟku ariya kaṭuṅkōṭi yilum pāliyil talivi talivaṉu ṭaṉ taṉittup puṟappaṭṭup pōtaṟku maṉam poruntiya tōḻi.
talivaṉ, ārāyntu tōḻiyiṭam, tāṉ taliviyi maṇattaṟku uṭaṉpaṭāta poruttamillāta uṟaviṉari nīkki, poruttamillāta akkālattil, poruttamillāta pāli vaḻiyē taliviyi aḻittuc cellat tuṇintu kūṟutal.	(to. po. 41 nac.)
‘talivaṉ pōkku uṭaṉpaṭutal’ eṉpatu nampiyakap poruḷ kūṟum tuṟi. (182)
`oṉṟāt tamariṉum paruvattum curattum oṉṟiya tōḻiyoṭu viṭuttal' -
varivu uṭaṉpaṭāta tamariyum, iṟceṟippu mutaliya vaṟṟāṉ talivi paṭum tuyariyum, uṭaṉpōkak karutiya pālinilattiṉ kaṭumiyiyum niṉittuppārttu, talivaṉ taṉ talivi āṟṟiyiruppāḷ eṉṟu karuti, uṭaṉpōkātu viṭuttaṟkaṇ poruntiya tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.	(to. po. 41 nac.)
oṉṟāpparuvam -
iṟceṟippiṉāl puṟampōtaṟkup poruntāta paruvam (2) talivaṉuṭaṉ kūṭṭam peṟāmiyāl āṟṟiyiruppataṟkup poruntāta paruvam.	(to. po. 41 nac.)
`oṉṟāp poruḷvayiṉ ūkkiya pāl' -
vāḻnāḷ cilavē ātaliṉ virivil poruḷ tēṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu niṉittāl, ataṟku māṟāka, “iḷamip paruvam vāḻnāḷil cila āṇṭukaḷē; apparuvam kaḻintāl mīḷātu ātaliṉ iḷamip paruvattil taliviyip pirintu ceyyum kāriyam etuvum vēṇṭā” eṉṟa eṇṇam tōṉṟum.
uḻittup poruḷ tēṭātu vīṭṭil cōmpi iruttal kūṭātu eṉṟa eṇṇattoṭu poruḷtēṭa niṉittāl, “avaravar tattam takutikkēṟpac ceyaṟpaṭal vēṇṭumē yaṉṟit takutikkuk kuṟivāṉa ceyal etaṉkaṇṇum īṭu paṭal kūṭātu” eṉṟa eṇṇam ataṟku māṟākat tōṉṟum:
celvam illāviṭil varum iḻivi niṉittup poruḷ tēṭa niṉittāl, “celvaṅkaḷuḷ ciṟanta iḷamiyum kāmamum ākiyavaṟṟi viṭuttup poruḷvayiṉ pirital kūṭātu” eṉṟa eṇṇam ataṟku māṟākat tōṉṟum.
pirintu ceṉṟu mīṇṭuvantāl aṉpu perukum eṉṟu karutip poruḷ tēṭap piriya niṉittāl, ataṟku māṟākat “talivi pirivuttuyarit tāṅkamāṭṭāḷ” eṉṟa eṇṇam tōṉṟi appiriviṉit taṭuttuviṭum.
ivvāṟu nāḷatu ciṉmikku iḷamiyatu arumiyum, tāḷāṇ pakkattiṟkut takutiyatu amitiyum, iṉmiyatu iḷiviṟku uṭimiyatu uyarcciyum, aṉpiṉatu akalat tiṟku akaṟciyatu arumiyum māṟāka amintu piriyak karutum eṇṇatti māṟṟuvaṉavākavuḷḷaṉa.
oṉṟōṭoṉṟu poruntāta ceytikaḷiṭattē talivaṉ uḷḷam koṇṭa pakuti eṉpatu mēlit toṭarkkup poruḷākum.
e-ṭu :	‘arumporuḷ vēṭkiyiṉ’ eṉṉum kalippāṭal (18)		(to. po. 41, 44 nac.,)
vāḻum nāḷ cilavāka uḷavātaliyum, taṉatu tāḷāṇmi yākiya pakutiyiyum, poruḷiṉmiyāl eytum iḻivi yum, taliviyip palavāṟu iṉpuṟuttaṟku eṇṇum taṉatu aṉpiṉatu perukkattiyum karutip poruḷīṭṭali valiyuṟuttukiṟatu talivaṉatu neñcam.
iṉpa nukarccikkuriya iḷamiyatu arumippāṭṭiṉiyum, taliviyip pēṇum kaṭamiyākiya takaviṉiyum, kāta luṭimiyākiya ciṟappiṉiyum, taliviyip pirintu uṟital āṟṟāta taṉ niliyiyum karutip poruḷvayiṉ piritali avaṉ uṇarvu melivikkiṟatu.
ivvāṟu tammuḷ oṉṟāta nilimikkuriya poruḷvayiṉ piritali ūkkuvikkum pakutikkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻum. 	(to. akat. 42 ca. pāla.)
oṉṟāp poruḷvayiṉ ūkkiya pāliṉkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟiyatu -
oṉṟu oṉṟaṉōṭu poruntātu varum poruḷiṉ kūṟupāṭukaḷik karutip piritaṟkut talivaṉ maṉam koṇṭapōtu kūṟutal; poruḷvayiṉ pirivil orupuṟam poruḷ tēṭum āciyāl taliviyip piriya virumpalum, maṟupuṟam taliviyiṭattuk kātalāl piriviṉi virumpā tiruttalum ākiya tammuḷ māṟupaṭṭa iruvēṟu uṇarc cikaḷ talivaṉiṭam nikaḻumpōtu yātāṉum oṉṟaṉip paṟṟit talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻttutal.
‘poruḷvayiṉ pirivu talivaṉ aṟivuṟuttal’ eṉṟu itaṉi nampi akapporuḷa kuṟikkum. (169)(to. po. 41 nac.)
oṉṟāp poruḷvayiṉ ūkkiya pāliṉkaṇ talivaṉ pirintami -
oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu poruntātaṉavākiya nāḷatu ciṉmi mutaliyavaṟṟi niṉittup pārttu iṟutiyil poruḷ tēṭa vēṇṭiyataṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyiṉik karutit talivaṉ piriyumpōtu kūṟuvatu.
nāḷatu ciṉmikku iḷamiyatu arumi poruntāmi, tāḷāṇ pakkattiṟkut takutiyatu arumi poruntāmi, iṉmiyatu iḷiviṟku uṭimiyatu uyarcci porun tāmi, aṉpiṉatu akalattiṟku akaṟciyatu arumi poruntāmi - ākiyavaṟṟip paṟṟi naṉku eṇṇiya talivaṉ, pukaḻum iṉpamum vaṟiyavarkku ītalum maṉi yakattu muyaṟciyiṉṟit taṅkiyiruppavarkkuk kiṭṭā eṉṟa niṉivāl, “tammi aruḷ paṇṇi vanta antaṇar tāpatar mutaliyōrkku aṟamceytu vēṇṭuvaṉa koṭuttal, periya pakivarkaḷi veṉṟu perumitattuṭaṉ taṉṉi vaḻipaṭātāri aḻittal, maṉiviyuṭaṉ kuṟivaṟa illaṟam naṭattutal eṉpaṉa poruḷālēyē nikaḻum” eṉṟa karuttuṭkoṇṭu poruḷ tēṭap pirital. 	(kali. 11, nac. ) (to. po. 41 nac.)


`oṉṟi uyarnta pālatu āṇi' -
mūvakippaṭṭa kālaṅkaḷuḷ ellāp piṟappiṉum tavaṟātu uyir oṉṟāki uṭal iraṇṭāki orukālikku orukāl aṉpu mutaliyaṉa ciṟattaṟku ētuvākiya pālvari teyvat tiṉ āṇi.	(to. po. 93 nac.)
talivaṉ talivi eṉṟa iruvar uḷḷamum piṟapputtōṟum oṉṟi nalviṉikkaṇṇē nikaḻnta ūḻiṉ āṇi.(90 iḷa.)
iruvariyum oṉṟuvittu uyartaṟku ētuvākiya ūḻ.	(i. vi. 487 uri)
kuṟiviṉṟi uyarnta aṉpu tōṉṟutaṟkuriya āṇpāl peṇpāl eṉṉum iru kūṟṟiṉatu kaṭṭaḷi.			(to. po. 121 kuḻa.)
oṉṟit tōṉṟum tōḻi -
tāṉ eṉṟum talivi eṉṟum vēṟṟumiyiṉṟit taliviyi ṉuṭiya uṟuppukkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum taṉṉuṭiya vākak kūṟum urimiyuṭiya tōḻi.	(to. po. 39 nac.)
oṉṟit tōṉṟum tōḻi `nīkkaliṉ vanta tammuṟu viḻumam 
cāṟṟal' -
tāṉ avaḷ eṉṉum vēṟṟumiyiṉṟit taliviyuṭaṉ paḻakiya tōḻi, 1) uṟaviṉariyum tōḻiyariyum viṭuttup pirintu celvatāl talivikku ēṟpaṭakkūṭiya tuyaram paṟṟiyum, 2) talivi tammip pirivatāl tamakku ēṟpaṭak kūṭiya tuyaram paṟṟiyum kūṟutal. talivi pantum kaḻaṅkum pōlvaṉavaṟṟi nīkkutalāl uṇṭākiya tamatu varut tattit taliviyiṭam urittal- (naṟ. 12) (to. po. 39 nac.)
oṉṟit tōṉṟum tōḻi `nōymikap perukit taṉ neñcu kaluḻntōḷi aḻintatu kaḷiiya oḻintatu kūṟi, vaṉpuṟi neruṅki varutal' -
1) tōḻi, taliviyiṉ uṭaṉpōkkiṉi niṉittu mikavum maṉam puṇpaṭṭut taṭumāṟum avaḷtāyi avaḷuṭiya varuttattip pōkkuvataṟkāka, eḻumiyum toṭarnta aṉpiṉāl avarkaḷ illatti viṭuttuc ceṉṟaṉar eṉpati avaḷmaṉam  koḷḷumāṟu kūṟi, avaḷi vaṟpuṟuttik kavaliyaṟṟirukkumāṟu kūṟutal.
2) talivaṉ viṭuttu akaṉṟatāl mika nontu maṉam kalaṅ kiya taliviyi ‘varuntutali nīkkuvāyāka’ eṉṟu avaṉ kūṟic ceṉṟa ceytiyi eṭuttuk kūṟi vaṟpuṟutti avaḷi amitiyuṟumāṟu tōḻi kūṟutal. (to.po. 39 nac., 42 iḷa.)
oṉṟit tōṉṟum tōḻi pōkkaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
tāṉ vēṟu talivi vēṟu eṉṟa vēṟṟumiyiṉṟi iraṇṭu taliyuṭiya paṟavi pōṉṟa naṭpumuṟiyuṭiya tōḻi, uṭaṉpōkkiṉ tēviyit talivaṉ talivi eṉṟa iruvarkkum eṭuttuk kūṟi, iruvarum uṭaṉpōkkiṟku icintavaḻit taliviyit talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōka viṭukkumiṭattuk kūṟutal.
itaṉi nampiyakap poruḷ ‘pāṅki kiyaṭi koṭuttal’ eṉṉum (182).	(to. po. 39 nac.)
oṉṟit tōṉṟum tōḻi `vāymiyum poymmiyum kaṇṭōrc cuṭṭit tāynili nōkkit talippeyarttuk koḷḷu'tal -
1. ‘meyyum poyyum uṇarnta aṟivarkaḷiṉ tarumanūl tuṇivu itu’ eṉa eṭuttuk kūṟi, piṉ toṭarntu ceṉṟu taliviyiyum talivaṉiyum mīṭṭu varutaṟku niṉitta tāyiṉ maṉaniliyi aṟintu, avaḷ avarkaḷip piṉ toṭarātapaṭi ceyyumiṭattut tōḻi kūṟal.			(to. po. 39 nac.)
2. meyyiyum poyyiyum kaṟpaṉi ceytu kūṟiyum anupavappaṭṭavarkaḷatu varalāṟṟi eṭuttuk kūṟiyum, taliviyip peṟṟa tāyiṉ tuyaram karuti, talivaṉum taliviyum uṭaṉpōy mīṇṭavaḻi, avarkaḷi varavēṟṟu makiḻumāṟu tōḻi kūṟal.	(to. po. 39. pārati)
vaḻiyil, talivaṉum taliviyum koṇṭuḷḷa kātaliṉ uṇmi iṉmiyi ārāyntu kaṇṭōrit tāykkuk kuṟippiṭṭu, ataṉāl taṉ karuttiṟku iṇaṅki maṉam māṟi varutali nōkkit taliviyi mīṭṭaṟkuc cellāmal tāyi mīṭṭuk koḷḷumpōtum, allatu taliviyi mīṇṭum aḻippikka ēṟpāṭu ceyyumpōtum tōḻi kūṟal.	(to. po. 179 kuḻa.)
oṉṟit tōṉṟum tōḻi viṭuttaṟkaṇ kūṟal -
tāṉ eṉṟum talivi eṉṟum vēṟṟumiyillātu naṭpuṭaṉ paḻakiya tōḻi, talivaṉiyum taliviyiyum uṭaṉpōkkiṟku iṇaṅkacceytu, taliviyit taliva ṉuṭaṉ kūṭṭi uṭaṉpōkkiṟku viṭuttuḻit talivaṉukkut taliviyip pātukākkum kaṭamiyik kūṟi viṭuttaṟkaṇ kūṟal.
talivaṉ taliviyi uṭaṉaḻittuc cellātu talivi yi viṭuttuc cellumiṭattut tōḻi kūṟal. (naṟ. 12)  	(to. po. 39 nac.)
‘pāṅki vikiruḷ viṭuttal’ eṉpatu na. a. kiḷavi. (182)
`oṉṟiya uḷḷamoṭu uvantu avaḷ mīṭal' -
uṭaṉpōy talivaṉiyum taliviyiyum tēṭic ceṉṟa ceviliyi vaḻiyiṭik kaṇṭa cāṉṟōr teḷivuṟuttavē, avaḷ taliviyatu ceyki ēṟṟatē eṉṟa uḷḷattoṭu makiḻntu illam mīḷutal. (pāli naṭiyiyal) (vī. cō. 93 uri mēṟ.)
`oṉṟu nōyi maṟikka; iṉṟēl talivaṉukkut tūtu viṭuka' eṉṟa tōḻikkut talivi kūṟal -
a) “tōḻi! eṉṉāl innōyiṉi iṅkuḷḷār aṟiyāvaki maṟikkavum muṭiyavilli; nāṇattiṉāl talivaṉukkut tūtu vāyilākak kūṟavum muṭiyavilli. eṉ ceyvēṉ?” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	
ā) “tōḻi! eṉṉuḷḷattil kāmanōyum, ataṉi veḷip paṭuttit tūtu viṭuttalit taṭukkum nāṇamum, otta āṟṟaliyuṭiya iraṇṭu etirikaḷāka iruntu eṉṉit tuṉpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu. (kuṟaḷ 1162,63)

`oṉṟē vēṟē eṉṟirupāl' -
oruvaṉum oruttiyumāka illaṟam ceytuḻi avviruvari yum maṟupiṟappilum oṉṟuvittalum atiṉ vēṟākkalum ākiya iruvaki ūḻ. (to. po. 90 iḷa.)
talivaṉ talivi eṉṟa iruvarukkum ōriṭam vēṟṟiṭam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa iruvaki nilam. eṉavē, talivaṉum taliviyum ōriṭattavarātalum vevvēṟiṭattavarātalum kūṭum eṉpatu. (93 nac.)
ummip piṟappil kāmam nukarntār iruvariyum maṟu piṟappilum oṉṟuvittalum vēṟākkalum ākiya iruvaki ūḻ.	(i. vi. 487 uri)
`ōttiṉāṉa' eṉappaṭupavi -
aviyāvaṉa maṟikaḷi aṭippaṭiyākakkoṇ ṭeḻunta camaya nūl, ēraṇa nūl, vāṉiyal nūl, kōḷiyal nūl, eṇṇiyal nūl, maruttuva nūl, poruḷiyal nūl, araciyal nūl, paṭikkalap payiṟcikkuriya nūlkaḷ, ciṟpam ōviyam mutaliya kali nūlkaḷ, ilakkaṇanūlkaḷ mutaliyaṉa.	(to. akat. 33 ca. pāla.)
otaṟpiriviṉ tuṟikaḷ -
1) kalvinalam kūṟal 2) pirivuniṉivu urittal (3) kalakkam kaṇṭurittal (4) vāymoḻi kūṟit talimakaḷ varuntal eṉṉum nāṉkumām. (kōvi. 308-311)
ō
ōtaṟ pirivu -
ōtaṟpirivu kaṟpukkālattut talivaṟku nikaḻum pirivukaḷil oṉṟu. ippiriviṟkuriya kālavariyaṟi mūṉṟāṇṭiṉ mikutal kūṭātu. 	(to. po. 186 iḷa.)
illaṟam nikaḻttiṉār tuṟavaṟam nikaḻttum karuttiṉarāka vēṇṭutaliṉ, tuṟavaṟattik kūṟum vētāntam mutaliya kalvi, ataṟku variyaṟukkappaṭṭa āṇṭukaḷiyuṭiyatu. akkalviyellām atu eṉṟum nī eṉṟum āṉāy eṉṟum uḷḷa mūṉṟu patattik kaṭavātu.
ōtaṟpirivu antaṇar aracar vaṇikar uḻuvittuṇṇum vēḷāḷar eṉṟa nālvarukkum urittu.	(26 kuḻa.)
ōtaṟkup piriyum eṉpatu kaṟpataṟkup piriyum eṉpataṉṟu. paṇṭē kuravarkaḷāl kaṟpikkappaṭṭut talivaṉ aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭupēṟu paṟṟiya nūlkaḷik kaṟṟavaṉ. iṉi vēṟṟu nāṭukaḷilum avivallār uḷar eṉiṉ kāṇpal eṉṟum, vallārkku muṉṉiliyil taṉ aṟivik kāṭṭi mēmpaṭuttuval eṉṟum kūṟi ōtaṟkup pirivāṉ. ippirivu nāḷkaḷiyum tiṅkaḷiyum irutuvi yum elliyāka uṭiyatu.	(iṟi. a. 35,41)
ōtaṟpirivu mutaliya aintu pirivukaṭkum uriya kiḷavit tokikaḷiṉ vaki oṉpatāvaṉa -
	1. pirivu aṟivuṟuttal
talivaṉ piriya iruttalit tōḻi talivikku aṟivuṟuttal.
	2. pirivu uṭaṉpaṭāmi
talivaṉ pirintu celvataṟkut talivi uṭaṉpaṭāmi;
	3. pirivu uṭaṉpaṭuttal
talivaṉatu piriviṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyiṉik kūṟit tōḻi taliviyi uṭaṉpaṭac ceytal;
	4. uṭaṉpaṭutal
piriviṟkut talivi icital;
	5. pirivuḻik kalaṅkal
talivaṉ pirintaviṭattē talivi varuntal;
	

6. vaṉpuṟi
tōḻi taliviyi vaṟpuṟutti iṭittuc collip piriviṉip poṟukkumāṟu ceytal;
	7. vaṉpoṟi
maṉavuṟutiyuṭaṉ talivi piriviṉit tāṅkal;
	8. varuvaḻik kalaṅkal
talivaṉ tirumpi varum vaḻiyil taliviyi niṉintu varuntutal;
	9. vantuḻi makiḻcci
talivaṉ vantatum, taliviyum tōḻiyum  makiḻtal - eṉpaṉa.	(na. a. 209)
ōtimap peṭiyōṭu ūṭi urittal -
kaḷavu oḻukkam nikaḻumpōtu talivaṉ oruvaḻit taṇan tāṉāka, avaṉ piriviṉi āṟṟātu varuntum talivi, aṉṉap pēṭiyik kaṇṭu atu taṉakkup parinturi ceyyāta ceyali uṭkoṇṭu, ataṉoṭu kōpittuk kūṟuvatu.
e-ṭu :	“caṭakōpaṉuṭiya tirukkurukūriṉ poruninatit tuṟiyilē taṉ cēval taṉṉit taḻuvum ciṟakukaḷ ciṟitu pirintālum tuyaruṟum peṭi aṉṉam, tēṉ nirampiya kuvaḷip pūkkaḷip pōla naṭuira vilum kaṇṇīr nirampi eṉkaṇkaḷ tuyilāta ceytiyit tam tuṟivaḻiyē ceṉṟa eṉ taliva ṉukku urittu avaṉ pōkkiṉit taṭukka villiyē?” eṉṟa kūṟṟu.	(mā. a.pāṭal 198)
ōmpaṭutturittal -
uṭaṉpōkkiṟkut talivaṉ talivi iruvariyum oruppaṭuttiya tōḻi, taliviyi naḷḷiruḷil talivaṉiṭam oppaṭittu, “vētaneṟi tiriyiṉum, kaṭal vaṟṟiṉum, it taliviyiṭam nī aṉpu māṟāmal ivaḷip pātukāppā yāka” eṉṟu taliviyip pātukākkumāṟu talivaṉi vēṇṭutal.
itaṉip “pāṅki (talimakaḷit talimakaṟkuk) kiyaṭi koṭuttal” eṉṟum kūṟupa. 	(na. a. 182; i. vi. 536).
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉ pōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 213)
ōri -
irāci; ‘ōriyum nāḷum tuṟanta oḻukkam’		(to. po. 135 nac.) (ṭu)
ōriyum nāḷum tuṟanta oḻukkam -
kaḷavuk kālattil talivaṉum taliviyum tīya irāciyiṉ kaṇṇum tīya naṭcattirattiṉkaṇṇum tām naṭattum kaḷavoḻukkattit tuṟantu taṉittiruttal.	(to. po. 135 nac.)
ka
kaṭṭu -
