kuṟi collum kaṭṭuvicci, cuḷakil ciṟitaḷavu nelliṉi vāṅkikkoṇṭu annelliṉ oru piṭiyi eṭuttut tūvi avvāṟu tūvappaṭṭa nelliṉik kaṇakkup pārttu ataṉ aṭippaṭiyil teyva āvēcamuṟṟuk kūṟum coṟkaḷ. ivaḷ vēlaṉip pōṉṟu teyva aruḷ peṟṟavaḷātalāl mukkālamum uṇarntu collum āṟṟaluṭiyavaḷeṉṟu ivaḷi makkaḷ karutiṉar. kaḷavuk kālattut talivaṉ aṭikkaṭip pirintu pōtalāl talivikku uṭal meliya, atu teyvattāṉ ēṟpaṭṭa vēṟupāṭō eṉṟu aṟivataṟkuc cevili kaṭṭuvicciyi aḻittu avaḷ vāyilākak kaṭṭuk kaṇṭu avaḷ kūṟiya paṭi talivi nalaṉ karutit teyva vaḻipāṭu nikaḻttuvatu vaḻakkam.  	(to. po. 115 nac.)
‘kārār kuḻaṟkoṇṭi kaṭṭuvicci kaṭṭēṟic
cīrār cuḷakiṉ cilanel piṭitteṟiyā
vērā vitirvitirā meycilirāk kimōvāp
pērā yiramuṭiyāṉ eṉṟāḷ; peyarttēyum
kārār tirumēṉi kāṭṭiṉāḷ; kiyatuvum
cīrār valampuriyē eṉṟāḷ; tiruttuḻāyt
tārār naṟumāli kaṭṭurittāḷ’  (ciṟiya. 20-26)
kaṭṭuc collutal -
kuṟicollutal.
kaṭṭuri kēṭṭal -
kaḷavu iṭiyīṭṭāl talivikku vanta uṭalmeliviṉ uṇmikkāraṇatti aṟiyāta ceviliyum naṟṟāyum ūriluḷḷa mutiya peṇṭiri aḻittut talivi uṭal nalam peṟaṟkuc ceyya vēṇṭuvaṉa paṟṟi avarkaḷ karutti viṉavutal. (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 92 urimēṟ.)
kaṭṭuvitti -
kaṭṭuvicci (kuṟicolpavaḷ) 	(kōvi. 285 koḷu)
kaṭṭuvitti kūṟal -
kaḷavuk kālattil talivaṉ varivuiṭivittup poruḷvayiṉ piriya, avaṉ pirivāl talivi uṭalum uḷḷamum meliya, ataṉ uṇmik kāraṇam aṟiyāta tāy kaṭṭuvittiyi (kuṟicolpavaḷ) aḻittut talivi yatu uṭal naliviṉ kāraṇatti viṉava, kuṟi colpavaḷ uṇmiyik kaṇṭu colliviṭuvāḷō eṉṟu tōḻi kalakkamuṟṟu niṟpa, talivaṉ taliviyarik kūṭṭuvitta teyvam maṟṟavarkku ikkaḷavoḻukkam pulaṉākātapaṭi, avaḷ parappiya nelliṉkaṇ murukaṉuṭiya vaṭivattik kāṭṭa, ataṉik kaṇṭa kaṭṭuvitti “itaṉi ellīrum kāṇuṅkaḷ. ittalivikku murukaṉāl vanta nōy tavirap piṟitoru tīṅku iṉṟu” eṉa neṟkuṟi kāṭṭik kūṟutal.
itu ‘variporuṭpirital’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 285).
kaṭṭuvitti talivikku nōy pērāyira muṭiya tirumālāl ēṟpaṭṭatu eṉṟu uṇmiyik kūṟum tiṟamum uṇṭu. ataṉit tirumaṅkiyāḻvār aruḷiya ciṟiyatirumaṭalil kāṇka. 	(kaṇṇi : 20-26)
kaṭṭu vippittal -
kaḷavuk kālattil talivaṉ taliviyip pirintu tirumaṇat tiṟkuriya poruḷ tēṭivarac ceṉṟāṉāka, avaṉpirivu kuṟittut talivi varuntiyataṉāl avaḷ maṉamum uṭalum meliya, nōyatu kāraṇattiṉi uḷḷavāṟu aṟiyāta cevilittāy taliviyatu uṭal meliviṉ kāraṇatti naṉṟāka aṟintukoḷḷak kaṭṭuvitti eṉṉum kuṟi colpavaḷi aḻittu avaḷ vāyilākak kaṭṭuk kāṇa muṟpaṭṭatu.
itu ‘variporuṭ pirital’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 283)
kaṭaliṭi vittut tuyar aṟivittal -
iravukkuṟiyil talivaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākat tōḻi, talivi iravil paṭum tuṉpattik “kaṭalē! talivaṉuṭiya pirivit tāṅka māṭṭāta talivi vāṭṭamaṭiya nī iṭiviṭātu olittuk koṇṭiruttal takkatō?” eṉṟu kaṭaloṭu viṉavuvāḷ pōlat talivaṉukku aṟivittal.
iḵtu ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  (kōvi. 173)
kaṭaloṭu pulattal -
talivaṉ taṉ ceyal kuṟittut taliviyi oru nāḷē pirintu oruvaḻit taṇantāṉāka, avvorunāṭpiriviṉi yum āṟṟāta talivi, kaṭaṟkariyi aṭintu kaṭalip pārttut taṉ nāyakaṉ varum nēram paṟṟi viṉava, atu taṉakku vāy tiṟantu maṟumāṟṟam oṉṟum kūṟātataṉi nōkki, “eṉ vaḷiyalkaḷik kaḻalac ceyta eṉ talivaṉ tiṟampaṟṟi yāṉ viṉava, nī vāy tiṟavātiruppatu ēṉ?” eṉap piṉṉum akkaṭaloṭu pulantu kūṟutal.
iḵtu ‘oruvaḻit taṇattal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 183)
kaṭaloṭu varavu kēṭṭal -
talivaṉ mīṇṭu varutal paṟṟit talivi kaṭali viṉavutal. “kaṭalmalliyil kariyiṭattu mōtum kaṭalē! eṉṉit taṉ uṭalākavum, tām avvuṭampiṉi viṭṭup piriyāta uyirākavum kūṟi aṉpu pārāṭṭiya eṉ talivar poruḷ tēṭap pirintār; iṉṉum mīṇṭu vantilar. avar pirintu ceṉṟapōtu mīṇṭu varum nāḷ paṟṟi niṉṉiṭam ētēṉum kūṟicceṉṟa tuṇṭāyiṉ, ataṉi eṉakkut terivippā yāka” eṉṟu talivi kaṭali vēṇṭutal.
itu “poruḷvayiṉ pirital” eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(mā. a. pāṭal. 759)
kaṭaṟṟeyvam -
varuṇaṉ (cilap.7 : 5 pāṭal)	(ṭu)
“kaṭitiṉ vantīr” eṉum īṉṟāṭkut talivaṉ kūṟal -
“virivil tirumpi viṭṭīrkaḷē!” eṉṟu makiḻnturitta cevilikku talivaṉ kūṟutal.
“malikaḷum āṟukaḷum koṭiya kāṭum cēymiyilaṉṟi mikka aṇmiyil uḷḷaṉa eṉṟu kūṟum vakiyil eṉṉi ittuṇik kaṭitiṉ iṅkuk koṇṭuvantavi, taliviyiṉ iṭikkaṇ uḷḷa kuḻaviyum ivaḷatu neṭṭuyirppum kiyāl tuṭittum nillāmal perukiya ivaḷatu kaṇṇīrumē!” eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu, “ivaṟṟiṉ niṉivāl piriviṉi āṟṟātu virivil mīṇṭu vantēṉ” eṉpatu karuttu.
itu ‘poruḷvayiṉ pirital’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.  	(ampikā. 557)
kaṭinakar ceṉṟa cevili naṟṟāykku urittal -
talivi talivaṉōṭu illaṟam nikaḻtti varum māṭcimi kāṇa avvillattuc ceṉṟu kaṇṭu mīṇṭa cevili naṟṟāykku avvillaṟac ciṟappiṉi viyantu kūṟutal.
“niṉmakaḷ piriyiṭṭa ceṟinta tayirit taṉ kāntaḷ mel viralālē picintu nekiḻvuṭiyatākki, avviralkaḷā lēyē naḻuvaluṟṟa taṉ puṭiviyi iṟuka uṭuttik koṇṭu, camiyal ceyyumpōtu tāḷitap puki taṉ kuvaḷiyuṇkaṇkaḷil vantu cūḻavum ataṉip poruṭ paṭuttātu tāṉ tuḻāvip puḷikkuḻampu aṭṭāḷ. avaḷ aṭṭa aṭicilik kaṇavaṉ iṉitu eṉṟu kūṟi uṇṇā niṟpavē, avaḷatu oḷipaṭitta mukam nuṇṇitiṉ muṟuval koṇṭu makiḻntatu. itukāṇ ammaṉimāṭci!” (kuṟun. 167) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa cevili kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 53 nac.)
kaṭināṭkaṅkul -
nāḷ kaṭikkaṅkul; orunāḷ muṭivataṟkuriya iṟutip pakutiyākiya iravu iruḷ. itu vikaṟiyiṉ iṟutip pakuti yākum. 	(cilap. 10 : 3) (ṭu)
kaṭippiṭi -
āṇmakaṉukkuriya nāṟpaṇpukaḷuḷ oṉṟu; koṇṭa poruḷ maṟavāmi. kāmam mikumpōtu ēṉiya mūṉṟuṭaṉ ippaṇpum puṉalōṭumvaḻip pul cāyntāṟ pōlac cāyntu piṉ veḷippaṭum.	(iṟi. kaḷa. 1 uri)
kaṭiyar -
uḻavarākiya marutanilamakkaḷ.  	(na. a.) (ṭu)
kaṇ aḻaku keṭa nī aḻutal kūṭātu’ eṉṟu coṉṉa tōḻikkut talivi kūṟiyatu -
“tōḻi! eṉ uḷḷam eṉ talivaṉ irukkum iṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟuviṭṭatu. appaṭit tāmum pōka iyalavilliyē eṉṟu eṉ kaṇkaḷ aḻukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟi amitiyuṟac ceytal eṅṅaṉam iyalum?” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.  			(kuṟaḷ 1170)
kaṇṭavar kūṟal -
talivaṉ taliviyip pirintu parattiyar cērikkaṇ cella, apparattiyar avaṉic cuṟṟivaḷittukkoṇṭu āravāritta ceytiyit teruvilē kaṇṭavarkaḷ, “ik kāṭciyit talivi kāṇiṉ eṉṉākumō?” eṉṟu tammuḷ kūṟal.
itu ‘parattiyiṟpirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 352)
kaṇṭavar makiḻtal -
uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ talivaṉum taliviyum vaḻivaruttam tīrntu kāṭṭiṭi iṉpuṟṟuc celvati avvaḻiyē vantavarkaḷ kaṇṭu makiḻntu kūṟutal.
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 219)
kaṇṭu kaṇ civattal -
peruntiṇit talivi taṉ talivaṉ parattiyarpāl niṉṟum vantami aṟintu ciṉantu ūṭutal.
talivaṉ taliviyi neruṅkit taḻuva vantapōtu, avaṉ mārpilum mukattilum avaṉ parattiyoṭu kūṭivantami kāṭṭum aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ iruttalik kaṇṭa talivi ciṉam koḷḷutal.  	(pu. ve. mā. 16-11)
kaṇṭu ki cōrtal -
talivi kāmam mikuntu tuyaruṟutalik kaṇṭa tōḻi kiyaṟutal.
“taliviyiṉ vaḷikaḷ kaḻaṉṟaṉa; kaṇkaḷ uṟakkam nīttaṉa. iṉi avaḷnili yātākumō?” eṉpatu pōlat tōḻi varuntutal.
iḵtu irupāṟ peruntiṇikkaṇ nikaḻvatoru kūṟṟu. (pu. ve. mā. 17-5)
kaṇṭōr ayirttal -
uṭaṉpōkkil talivaṉum taliviyum iṇintu celvatik kaṇṭavarkaḷ, iruvariṭiyē irunta kātaṟ ciṟappiṉik kaṇṭu viyantu, “ivarkaḷ vāṉulakattuk kātalarkaḷō!” eṉṟu aiyuṟutal.
“ivar yārō? tirumakaḷ eṉṟu ivaḷit tuṇitaṟku ivaḷ kiyil centāmari illi. ivaṉit tirumāl eṉṟu tuṇivōmāyiṉ, ivaṉ mārpil maṟu illiyē” (ampikā.377) eṉṟāṟ pōlat talivaṉtaliviyaric curattiṭik kaṇṭōr aiyuṟṟuk kūṟutal.
itu variviyaluḷ kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭiṟkuriya ‘uṭaṉ pōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 			(na. a. 182; i. vi. 536 - 15)
kaṇṭōr irakkam -
taliviyiṉ pirivāl mikka tuyaruṟṟup pulampum naṟṟāy cevili pāṅkiyar ākiyōrik kaṇṭa ayalār tāmum tuyaruṟṟuk kūṟutal.
“talivi eṅkaḷi aṉpoṭu pārttu iṉcoṟkūṟi maṟivāka iṅkuḷḷa poruḷkaḷiyellām viṭuttut talivaṉ tuṇiyē peritu eṉac ceṉṟuviṭṭāḷē’ eṉṟu tōḻiyar nontum kaṇṇīr ukuttum varuntu kiṉṟaṉar. avaḷ viṭuttuc ceṉṟa pantiṉiyum kaḻaṅkiṉi yum kaṇṭu naṟṟāy vāṭukiṟāḷ” (tañci.kō.339) eṉṟāṟ pōlak kaṇṭōr iraṅkik kūṟuvatu.
itu variviyaluḷ ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 187)
kaṇṭōr iraṅkal -
pāliyil talivaṉuṭaṉ vanta taliviyik kaṇṭavarkaḷ avaḷatu meṉmikku iraṅkik kūṟal.
“ippeṇ mika iḷiyaḷāka uḷḷāḷ. taṉ kātaṟkaṇava ṉatu naṭpik kātalittu naṭattaṟkariya ikkoṭiya pāliyil naṭantu varum ippeṇṇip peṟṟa tāy evvāṟu ivaḷip pirintu iṉṉum uyirōṭu irukki ṟāḷō?” (ampikā. 380) eṉṟāṟpōlak kaṇṭōr iraṅkik kūṟutal.
itu variviyaluḷ kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭil ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(i. vi. 536. uri)

kaṇṭōr kātaliṉ vilakkal -
iṭiccurattē uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ iṇintu ceṉṟa talivaṉ taliviyarik kaṇṭavarkaḷ, avviruvariṭamum aṉpu koṇṭu, iravu varum nēramātaliṉ mēlum vaḻi naṭakka vēṇṭā eṉṟum, arukēyuḷḷa tampāṭiyil taṅkit tām aḷikkum māṉtaciyum pālum cuvittu iravu taṅkiyiruntu viṭiyaṟkāli cellalām eṉṟum iṉṉō raṉṉa kūṟit taṭuttal.	(tañci. kō. 320)
itu variviyaluḷ kaḷaveḷippāṭṭil ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 182)
kaṇṭōr tamarit teruṭṭutal -
uṭaṉpōkkuc ceṉṟa talivaṉ taliviyarip piṉ toṭarntu ciṉattuṭaṉ ceṉṟa taliviyiṉ cuṟṟattāri vaḻiyil kaṇṭōr ciṉam taṇittut teḷivuṟuttal.
“ittalivaṉ kulapperumiyum aḻakum naṟkuṇaṅkaḷum koṇṭavaṉ; num peṇṇāṉa taliviyum ciṟanta kaṟpoḻukkattil tavaṟātu taliniṉṟu oḻukukiṉṟavaḷ. nīṅkaḷum veṟṟiyum villāṇmiyum uṭiyavartām. ivvāṟiruppa, yām yātu pukalvōm? (ātaliṉ, pōr ceytu taliviyi mīṭkak karututal takātu)” eṉṟa kūṟṟu.
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku iṭiyīṭu’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(ampikā. 424)
kaṇṭōr teruṭṭal -
 uṭaṉpōkiya talivaṉiyum taliviyiyum piṉ tēṭic ceṉṟa cevilikku iṭiccurattuk kaṇṭōr urittu uḷḷattit teḷivittu mīṇṭu cellumāṟu kūṟutal. (pālinaṭiyiyal)  	(vī. cō. 93 urimēṟ.)


kaṇṭōr pati aṇimi cāṟṟal -
uṭaṉpōkkil cellum talivaṉiyum taliviyiyum kaṇṭamakkaḷ, talivaṉatu ūr mikka aṇmiyilēyē uḷḷatu eṉṟum. kavaliyiṉṟip pōkalām eṉṟum kūṟutal.
itu variviyaluḷ, kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭil, ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	 (na. a. 182)
‘nakar aṇimi kūṟal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa. 	(kōvi. 221)
kaṇṭōr makiḻtal -
talivaṉ talivi iruvariyum curattiṭik kaṇṭōr akkāṭci kuṟittu makiḻtal.
 “talivaṟku muṉṉē naṭantu cella ippeṇ nāṇukiṟāḷ. ivaḷatu naṭiyaḻakit tāṉ kaṇṭu makiḻa, iccelvaṉ ivaḷ piṉṉē naṭattaliyē virumpukiṟāṉ. iṟukap potinta aṉpuṭaṉ ippāli nilattē iruvarum neṭuntolivu kaṭantu vantuḷḷaṉar” (ampikā.378) eṉṟāṟpōlak kaṇṭōr makiḻntu  kūṟal.
itu variviyaluḷ, kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭil ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(i. vi. 536 uri)
kaṇṭōr moḻital kaṇṭatu -
iṭiccurattil kaṇṭōr talivi uṭaṉpōkkiṉkaṇ naṭimelintu acintataṉik kaṇṭapōtum, avaḷiṭic ‘cērntaṉi cel’ eṉṉātu, talivaṉoṭu kūṭi iruvarāka irukkumvaḻiyē talivaṉiṭam kūṟṟu nikaḻttuvār eṉpatu ‘kaṇṭatu’ eṉpataṉāl koḷḷappaṭum.  (to. cey. 193 nac.)
kaṇṇayanturittal -
tāṉ virumpum talivi iṉṉāḷ eṉpataṉit tōḻikku aṟivuṟutta viḻinta talivaṉ, attaliviyiṉ uṟuppu nalaṉkaḷi ellām kūṟiyatōṭu amiyātu, taṉakku aṉṟu tōḻiyik kāṭṭiya taliviyiṉ kaṇṇaḻakiṉip piṉṉum nayantu (virumpip) pukaḻnturittal.
itaṉip piṟar ‘iṟiyōṉ iṟivitaṉmi .iyampal’ eṉpa.	(na. a. 144; i. vi. 509-5)
itu cēṭpaṭi eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  (kōvi. 109)
kaṇṇē alamaral -
 talivaṉ taliviyi mutaṟkaṇ etirppaṭumpōtu itaṟku muṉ kaṇṭaṟiyāta āṭavaṉ vaṭiviṉit tāṉ taṉittu niṉṟa niliyil taṉ arukē kaṇṭa accattāl, avaḷ kaṇka ḷiṭiyē piṟanta taṭumāṟṟam.
ikkaṇkaḷ taṭumāṟum ceyal, “ivaḷ tēvarulakattavaḷ allaḷ, māṉuṭamakaḷē” eṉṟu talivaṉ tuṇitaṟku utavum karuvikaḷuḷ oṉṟu. 	(to. po. 95 nac.)
kaṇpaṭi peṟātu kaṅkul nōtal -
talivaṉ taliviyip pirintu uṟakkam koḷḷāmal iravil tuyaruṟutal. itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, iyaṟkip puṇarcci nikaḻnta piṉṉar, ‘pirivuḻik kalaṅkal’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatu oru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 133)
kaṇ putikka varuntal -
pāṅkaṟkūṭṭattukkaṇ talivaṉ taliviyip palavāṟu pukaḻa, avaḷ appukaḻccikku nāṇit taṉ kaṇkaḷip pottikkoṇṭu oru koṭiyic cārntu niṟka, avaṉ avaḷiṭam, “eṉṉi varuttuvaṉa niṉkaṇkaḷ māttiramē alla; niṉmēṉi muḻutumē eṉṉi varuttukiṉṟatu!” eṉṟāṟpōlak kūṟal.
itaṉi iyaṟkip puṇarccikkaṇ ‘iṭiyūṟukiḷattal’ eṉṉum kiḷaviyākavum koḷpa. 	 (na. a. 127; i.vi. 495)
itu ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 43)
kaṇputi nāṇam mītūral -
uṭaṉpōkkil talivaṉ taṉṉip pukaḻtalik kēṭṭa talivi nāṇam mikuntu taṉ kikaḷāl kaṇkaḷip pottik koḷḷutal.
“iṭiccurattē kurāmalarum marāvum cērttu yāṉē toṭuttu niṉ kūntalil cūṭṭi aṇi ceykiṟēṉ. eṉ uyir aṉiyāy! ittavappēṟu vēṟu yārukku uṇṭu?” eṉṟu talivaṉ kūṟiyatik kēṭṭu nāṇ mikunta talivi taṉkaṇkaḷip putittuk koṇṭāḷ.  (itu kavikkūṟṟu)
iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(ampikā. 376)
kaṇ vituppaḻital -
talivaṉ pirintuceṉṟavaḻit taliviyiṉuṭiya kaṇkaḷ talivaṉik kāṇpataṟku virivataṉāl varuttutal.
vituppu - virivu (kuṟaḷ. ati. 118 tōṟṟu. parimē.)
kaṇiyeṉa urittal -
vēṅki pūttami kūṟutal.
kaḷavuk kālattup pakaṟkuṟikkaṇ talivaṉik kūṭi makiḻnta talimakaḷiṭam, “kaṇi eṉṟa peyariyuṭiya vēṅki, tāṉ pūkkum kālam tiṉimuṟṟum kālamākavē, tāṉ pūttalic ceytu tiṉi muṟṟiyatit terivittut tiṉikkatirkaḷik  koyyacceytu namtiṉikkāvalik keṭukkac cōtiṭam pārttu nāḷkūṟuvārip pōlac ceytuviṭṭatu!” eṉṟu iṉippakaṟkuṟikku vāyppillāc ceytiyit tōḻi kūṟutal.	(kōvi. 138)
itu ’tōḻiyiṟkūṭṭap pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ka. kā. pak. 86.)
kantaruva maṇam -
 talivaṉum taliviyum tāmē etirpaṭṭuk kūṭum kūṭṭam. 		(ṭu)
kantaruva mārkkam aintu -
mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal pāli eṉṉum aintiṇi kaḷiyum tamakku aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu avaṟṟoṭu porunti varum yāḻōr kūṭṭatti ottuk kaḷavil toṭaṅkik karaṇamoṭu puṇarntu kaṟpoḻukkam naṭattu taṟkuriya aivaki nilattut tirumaṇaṅkaḷ; pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkil pālikkut taṉinilam iṉṟēṉum, ulakiyalil pāli nilamum aṅku vāḻvārkkut tirumaṇamum uḷa ākaliṉ, pāli koḷḷappaṭṭatu. paṭavē, aivaki nilattu vāḻvār tirumaṇamum paṟṟic ceyyuḷ iyaṟṟappaṭum.  (to. po. 106 nac)
nilam eṉpatu iṭam. otta kāmamākik karupporuḷoṭum puṇarnta kantaruvaneṟi iṭavakiyāl aintuvakippaṭum. aviyāvaṉa kaḷavum uṭaṉpōkkum iṟkiḻattiyum kāmakkiḻattiyum kātaṟparattiyum eṉaccollappaṭṭa aivakik kūṭṭam.	(104 iḷa.)
kampi miṉṉāḷ vāḻttal -
valampuri kiḻatti vāḻttal	(na. a. 170; cāmi. 107)
‘kayantali tōṉṟiya kāmar neyyaṇi, nayanta kiḻavaṉi neñcu puṇuṟīi, naḷiyiṉ nīkkiya iḷivarunili’kkaṇ talivi kūṟal -
yāṉikkaṉṟu pōlum putalvaṉ piṟattalāṉ uḷatākiya viruppam tarum neyyaṇiyik kāṇavirumpiya talivaṉi neñcu puṇpaṭap pēcit taṉṉik kūṭātu nīkkiya iḷivanta nilikkaṇ talivi kūṟutal.
itu talivi putalvaṟ payanta kālattut talivaṉpirivu paṟṟik kūṟiyatu. (kayantali - melliya taliyiyuṭiya putalvaṉ)
“karumpiṉi naṭṭa pāttiyil tāmē - tōṉṟit taḻitta āmpaṟpūkkaḷ vaṇṭukaḷatu paciyiṉip pōkkum perum puṉal ūra! putalvaṉi īṉṟa emmēṉiyit taḻuvaṟka. taḻuviṉ, atu pālpaṭutal mutaliyavaṟṟāl niṉmārpiṉ eḻilic citippatu ākum.” (aiṅ. 65) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu. 	(to. po. 147 nac.)

‘karaṇattiṉ amintu muṭinta kāli, neñcu taḷi aviḻnta puṇarcci’kkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟṟu -
ācāṉik koṇṭu tirumaṇaccaṭaṅkiṉi muṭittapiṉ, iyaṟkip puṇarcci mutaliyavaṟṟiṉpiṉ kaḷavoḻukkam iṭiyīṭupaṭṭatāl vēṭki taṇiyātu tirumaṇam muṭiyum vari iruvariṭamum kaṭṭuṇṭa neñcu kaṭṭu viṭappaṭutal. (to. po. 144. iḷa.)
tirumaṇaccaṭaṅkiṉi muṭittu mutal mūṉṟu iravu kūṭṭamiṉṟi nāṉkām nāḷ pakalelli muṭinta kālattu, mūṉṟu nāḷ kūṭṭam  iṉmiyāl nikaḻnta maṉakkuṟi tīrak kūṭiya kūṭṭattukkaṇ talivaṉ kūṟutal.  	(146 nac.)
“innilavulakamum peṟutaṟkariya tēvarulakamum ākiya iraṇṭum, kuvaḷip pūppōṉṟa miyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷiyum poṉpōṉṟa mēṉiyiyum māṇvari alkuliyumuṭiya ikkuṟumakaḷuṭiya kūṭṭatti mēvum  innāḷōṭu cīr ovvā!” (kuṟun. 101) eṉa neñcutaḷi aviḻnta puṇarccik- kaṇ talivaṉ kūṟṟu nikaḻntavāṟu.
karaṇam (1) -
karaṇam eṉpatu tirumaṇattiṉpōtu nikaḻum vēḷvic caṭaṅkām. ippeyar, tīyi muṉṉiṭṭuc ceyyappaṭuvatāl vaṭamoḻip peyarāy aminta ‘aknu karaṇam’ eṉpataṉ tokuttalākum. ikkaraṇattiṉpōtu talivi yiṉ peṟṟōrāvatu uṟaviṉarāvatu taliviyit talivaṟ kuk koṭuppa avaṉ ēṟṟukkoḷvāṉ. taliviyiṉ uṟaviṉar talivaṉ avaḷi maṇattaṟku iciyātavaḻit talivaṉ taliviyi aḻittuc ceṉṟu vēṟṟūril karaṇatti muṭittu avaḷi maṇappāṉ. karaṇam nālvaki varuṇattārkkum uṇṭu. itu neṭuṅkālamāka nikaḻntu varuvatu. toṭakkattil kaḷavu nikaḻnta piṉ talivaṉum taliviyum tāmākavē kaṟpu vāḻkkiyit toṭaṅki naṭatti vantaṉar eṉavum, poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṉṉark karaṇam vaṟpuṟuttappaṭṭatu eṉavum kūṟupa.
poy - oruvaṉ oruttiyoṭu kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻttip piṉ illi eṉpatu; vaḻu - oruttiyoṭu kaḷaviṉkaṇ oḻukik kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭapiṉ cilakālam illaṟam naṭattiya oruvaṉ avaḷik kiviṭṭu viṭutal pōlvaṉa. (to. po. 143 - 145 nac.)
karaṇamāvatu -
karaṇamāvatu otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum illaṟak kiḻami pūṇṭoḻukum takavum urimiyum peṟa, mantiraṅkaḷoṭu mutumoḻikaḷiyum kūṟiyum kūṟuvittum, piṟa maṅkalaviṉi kaḷic ceytum ceyvittum talimakkaḷik kūṭṭuvikkum vitiyām. avi nūlneṟiyōṭu ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉiyum tērntuṇarnta aiyarkaḷāṉ vakuttamikkap peṟṟavi. vatuvic caṭaṅku eṉpār iḷampūraṇar. vēḷviccaṭaṅku eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.  	(to. kaṟ. 1 ca. pāla)
karantuṟi kiḷavi -
uḷḷak kuṟippi maṟittuc collum moḻi.
karantu uṟi kiḷaviyāl talivaṉ tōḻiyi irantu kuṟiyuṟuvāṉ.  	(kōvi. koḷu. 50)
karavu nāṭṭam -
maṟintirunta ceytiyi āyntu aṟital
tōḻiyi matiyuṭampaṭukka niṉinta talivaṉ avaḷ taṉit tiruntapōtu ūr mutaliya viṉaviyatōṭu, avaḷ talivi yoṭu cērntirunta poḻutu taḻiyum kaṇṇiyum ēnti vantu ūr peyar keṭutiyōṭu oḻintaṉavum viṉaviyavaḻi, talivi avaṉmītu pārvi celuttutaliyum talivaṉ taṉṉoṭu pēciyavāṟē taliviyi aṭikkaṭi nōkkutali yum kaṇṭa tōḻi, “ittalivaṉukkum nam talivikkum iṉpamum tuṉpamum oṉṟākat tōṉṟumāṟu, kākkiyiṉ iru kaṇṇukkum oru kaṇmaṇiyē amintiruppatu pōla, iruvar uṭaliṉkaṇṇum ōruyirē uḷḷatu” (kōvi. 71) eṉṟu avarkaṇ maṟintirunta ceytiyi nāṭi uṇartal.
itu ‘tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭap pakaṟkuṟi’ eṉṟa tokutiyic cārnta kūṟṟukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.  	(ka. kā. pak. 54)
karuttaṟin taḻuta kaṇṇīr tuṭittal -
talivaṉ kaṟpukkālattil poruḷ tēṭap piriyalām eṉṟu eṇṇaṅkoṇṭa taṉ puṟappāṭṭiṟkuc ciṉṉāḷ mutaṟkoṇṭu taliviyiṭam taṇṇaḷiyi mikutiyākac ceytuvara, attaṇṇaḷiyi uṭkoṇṭu talivaṉ taṉṉip piriyappōkiṉṟamiyit talivi uṇarntu kaṇṇīr viṭat toṭaṅkat talivaṉ akkaṇṇīrit tuṭittu avaḷukku āṟutal aḷittatu.
e-ṭu. “tirumālatu tiruvaraṅkam pōṉṟa aḻakiya mukattāy! niṉ kaṇ ākiya vēl imiyākiya uṟiyi nīkki veḷippaṭṭut tuṉpattic ceyyum pūcaliṉ mēlum yāṉ mika varuntumpaṭi kaluḻvataṟkuk kāraṇam eṉ?” eṉṉum talivaṉ kūṟṟu.   	(mā. a. pāṭal. 248)
karuttaṟivittal -
matiyuṭampaṭuttaṟkaṇ talivaṉ taliviyum tōḻiyum kūṭiyirunta iṭatti aṭuttu vēḻam kaṭamāṉ pati vaḻi iṭi mutaliya viṉaviyavaḻi, avarkaḷ maṟumoḻi ētum koṭātu iruntatu kaṇṭu , aṅṅaṉam viṉavutali viṭut tut tāṉ kiyil vittirunta taḻiyāṭiyi avarkaḷ virumpiṉāl tāṉ makiḻvoṭu koṭuppatākak kūṟit taṉ karuttiṉi aṟivittal.
itaṉi ‘urittatu uriyātu karuttaṟivittal’ eṉpa. (i. vi. 507)
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ’ matiyuṭam paṭuttal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 58)
karuttupporuḷ -
kāṭcipporuḷākātu, maṉattāṟ karutappaṭṭa poruḷ.  (to. po. 1 nac. uri) (ṭu)
karutturi -
 iḵtu akapporuḷ uri irupattēḻaṉuḷ oṉṟu 	(vī. cō. 90)
 karuttu eṉpatu aṟital, aṟiyāmi, aiyuṟal eṉa mūvakip paṭum. avitām mīṇṭum, aṟintu aṟiyāmi, aṟintavi aiyuṟal, aṟiyātu aṟital, aiyuṟṟilāmi eṉa nāṉkākap pakuttuk koḷḷappaṭum.  	(vī. cō. 96 urimēṟ.)
karupporuḷ -
mutaṟporuḷākiya nilattiyum kālattiyum oṭṭi mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal pāli eṉṉum aivaki nilattum tōṉṟum poruḷkaḷ.
paramporuḷi iṉṉa peyarāl iṉṉa vaṭivil ivvāṟu vaḻipaṭal vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇam avvan nilattiyum kālattiyum oṭṭi avvan nilattu vāḻvār uḷḷattē tōṉṟutaliṉ, teyvamum karupporuḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟā yiṟṟu. avvan nilattuk karupporuḷ piṟanilattum mayaṅki varutal kūṭum; āyiṉ teyvam avvan nilattiṟkē uriyatu.  eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, mulli nilattut teyvamākiya māyōṉ kuṟiñci mutaliya piṟanilattut teyvamākac ceyyuḷ ceytal kūṭātu. uḷḷuṟi uvamam kūṟumpōtum teyvam nīṅka lāṉa ēṉiya karupporuḷkaḷik koṇṭē ceytal vēṇṭum.  	(to. po. 5, 47 nac.)
teyvam, uṇavu, vilaṅku, maram, paṟavi, paṟi, toḻil, yāḻ eṉpaṉavum pū, nīr, ūr, tiṇitoṟum marīiya peyar, tiṇi nilippeyar eṉpaṉavum karupporuḷkaḷām.  			(18, 19, 20. nac)
aṭiyārkku nallārum nampiyakapporuḷ āciriyarum muṟiyē koṭiyiyum paṇṇiyum karupporuḷākak koṇṭaṉar.	(cilap.. patika uri. na. a. 19)
karupporuḷ (2) -
kuṟiñci mutaliya aintiṇikaḷiṉ mutaṟporuḷākiya nilam poḻutu eṉṟa ivaṟṟic cārntu annilattut tōṉṟum teyvam, uyar makkaḷ, iḻimakkaḷ, paṟavi, vilaṅku, ūr, nīr, pū, maram, uṇavu, paṟi, yāḻ, paṇ, toḻil eṉṉum patiṉāṉkum avvan nilak karupporuḷ eṉappaṭum.		(na. a. 19)
karupporuḷkaḷuḷ teyvam uḷḷuṟikōṭaṟku nilam ākāmi -
aḵṟiṇip poruḷkaḷuḷ nilamum poḻutum allātaṉa ellām karupporuḷāka vakukkappeṟṟamiyāl teyvam karupporuḷāka amivatāyiṟṟu. tattuvaṅkaḷiṉ uruvaka māka amittukkoṇṭa teyvaṅkaḷ iruvakit tiṇi kaḷuḷ aṭaṅkāmiyiṉ āciriyar avaṟṟi ‘uyar tiṇik kaṇ cārttik kūṟiṉār. ammuṟiyāṉ teyvattiṟku makkaḷiṉ oḻukalāṟukaḷi ēṟṟik kūṟaliṉ teyvam uḷḷuṟi kōṭaṟku nilam ākātāyiṟṟu.
teyvam cuṭṭiya peyarkaḷ “uyartiṇi maruṅkiṉ pāl pirintu iciyāta viṭattē, aḵṟiṇiyāy aḵṟiṇi muṭipē koḷḷum” eṉṟār ātaliṉ, avvaḻi avi aḵṟiṇip poruḷāyk karupporuḷuḷ aṭaṅkum; uḷḷuṟi kūṟaṟku nilaṉāka amiyum. ātaliṉ ataṉi vilakka vēṇṭiṟṟu.	(to. akat. 49 ca. pāla)
karupporuḷ mayaṅkutal -
mulli kuṟiñci marutam neytal pāli eṉṟa aivaki nilattuk karupporuḷkaḷākiya teyvam nīṅkalāṉa ēṉiyavi tattamakku uriyavākak kūṟappaṭṭa nilattoṭum kālattoṭum  naṭavāmal piṟanilattoṭum kālattoṭum mayaṅkivantāl avi vanta nilattiṟku uriya karupporuḷka ḷākavē koḷḷappaṭum.
marutattuppūk kuṟiñcikkaṇ vantāl, kuṟiñci nilappūvēyām. 				(kali.52)
marutattuppūp pālikkaṇ vantāl, pālinilap pūvēyām. (kali.3)
kuṟiñcikkuriya mayil pālikkaṇ vantāl, pālinilap 				paṟaviyēyām.  (kali.27) 			(to. po.19 nac)
karumpaṇi -
peṇpālartam tōḷmārpukaḷil cantaṉakkuḻampu mutaliya vaṟṟāl karumpiṉ vaṭivāka eḻutappaṭum kōlam;
aṉṟutāṉ īrttakarum(pu) aṇivāṭa’ (kali, 131-29)
peykarumpu īrkkavum vallaṉ’ (kali. 143-32)
peṇpālār tōḷmārpukaḷil tōḻiyarē aṉṟi, avaravarkkuriya āṭavar ivvoppaṉi ceytalē ciṟappu.
karintataṟku iraṅkal -
talivaṉ varivu vēṇṭi viṭat talivitamar varivu maṟuttavaḻi, akkūṟṟiṉi aṟintu talivi varuttutal 
(kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal) (vī. cō. 92. urimēṟ.)
kalvi nalam kūṟal -
kaṟpukkālattil talivaṉ ōtaṟkup piriyak karutip pāṅki talivi ivarkaḷiṭam, kaṟṟavarkaḷiṉ kalvicciṟappiṉi eṭuttuk kūṟutal.
itaṉik ‘kalvip pirivu talivaṉ pāṅkikku aṟivuṟuttal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.  	(i. vi. 558)
iḵtu ōtaṟpirivu eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (kōvi. 308)
kalakkaṅ kaṇṭurittal -
kaṟpukkālattil talivaṉ ōtaṟkup piriyat tiṭṭamiṭṭu acceytiyit talivikkuk kūṟa, atu kēṭṭa talivi uṟṟa kalakkattik kaṇṭa tōḻi, “talivar kalvi kaṟṟali virumpiṉār eṉṟa collē ivaḷceviyil kāycciya vēl pōlac ceṉṟaṭintu tuyaram uṟuvikkiṉṟatē! iṉi maṟṟa pirivukaḷi ivaḷ eṅṅaṉam āṟṟuvāḷ?” eṉṟu taṉṉuḷḷē kūṟiyatu.
iḵtu ‘ōtaṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (kōvi. 310)
kalakka muṟṟu niṟṟal -
talivaṉ kaḷavukkālattē variviṭi vittup poruḷ. vayiṉ pirintāṉāka, appirivāl taliviyiṉ uḷḷamum uṭalum meliyat  taliviyatu meliviṉ uṇmik kāraṇatti uḷḷavāṟu aṟiyāta cevili ataṉi aṟiyak kuṟicollum kaṭṭuvittiyi aḻittuk kaṭṭu vippik kavē, ” ikkaṭṭuvitti nam kaḷavoḻukkam muḻutiyum veḷippaṭutti nammi varuntacceytu ayalār aṉṟu collāta paḻiyiyum veḷippaṭac colli em kuṭikku mācu kaṟpippāḷēl, yāṉ yātu ceyval?” eṉṟu talivi maṉam kalaṅki niṟṟal.
itu ‘variporuṭ pirital’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 284)
kalaṅki moḻital (2) -
pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭattil talivaṉukkuk kuṟinērnta tōḻi taliviyiṭam talivaṉ paṭum tuyaranilikkut tāṉ kalaṅkiya taṉmiyi viḷakkik kuṟi nayakkumāṟu vēṇṭal. situ ‘tōḻi maṟuttaṟku arumi māṭṭal’ eṉavum (na.a. 148) kūṟappaṭum] (kuṟiñci - naṭiyiyal.)(vī.cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
kalanta poḻutum kāṭciyum -
kalanta poḻutu - talivaṉ taliviyik kaṇṇuṟṟavaḻi avaḷ kuṟippiṉi aṟiyum vari talivaṉ uḷḷattil avaḷip paṟṟiya eṇṇam irukkum kālam.
kāṭci - taliviyik kāṇṭal.
ivi kikkiḷikkaṇ nikaḻum uripporuḷ.  (to. po. 18 iḷa.)
kalanta poḻutu - iyaṟkip puṇarcci nikaḻnta kālam; kāṭci talivi taṉṉi virumpukiṉṟāḷō eṉṟu avaḷ kuṟippiṉi avaḷkaṇkaḷāl aṟital. iviyiraṇṭum vēṉiṟkālattu nikaḻvaṉa. vēṉili aṭutta kārkālattu maḻi toṭaṅkutalāṉ talivi poḻilil viḷiyāṭa varutaṟku vāyppu illi; ātaliṉ avaḷi etirppaṭṭuk kūṭutaṟku vāyppu illiyām. ākavē, iyaṟkip puṇarcci vēṉiṟ kālattu nikaḻum eṉṟār. 	(16. nac.)
kalanta poḻutu - talivaṉ talivi iruvariṉ mutal cantippu. kāṭci - iruvarum kaṇkaḷāl uḷḷakkuṟippi aṟivittuk koḷḷutal. ivviraṇṭum enta oru nilattiṟkum urimi yākātu ellā nilaṅkaḷukkum potuvāṉa uripporuḷ kaḷām. 	(akat. 16 pārati.)

kalantuṭaṉ varuvōrk kaṇṭu viṉātal -
uṭaṉpōkkil ceṉṟa taliviyit tēṭikkoṇṭu curattiṭic ceṉṟa cevili, aṉpuṭaṉ oṉṟiyiṇintu varum kaṉṉiyiyum kāḷiyiyum kaṇṭu viṉavutal.
“kātalarkāḷ! nummip pōlavē aṉpiṉāl oṉṟiya pēti oruttiyum kāḷiyoruvaṉum ivvaḻiyē cel vatik kaṇṭīrō?” (tañci. kō. 346) eṉṟāṟpōṉṟa cevili viṉā.
‘puṇarntuṭaṉ varuvōrip porunti viṉāval’ eṉṉum tirukkōviyār  (244)
itu variviyaluḷ, ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.  	(na. a. 188)
kalantuṭaṉ varuvōr ceviliyip pulampal tēṟṟal -
uṭaṉpōkkil ceṉṟa taliviyit tēṭikkoṇṭu curattiṭic ceṉṟa cevili, aṅku oṉṟiya aṉpuṭaṉ naṭantu varum kātalaṉ kātali ākiya iruvariyum viṉaviya pōtu, aṉṉār cevilikku āṟutal kūṟutal.
avar iruvarum kūṟuvatu oru ciṟappuṭiyatu. āṭavaṉ tāṉ talivaṉik kaṇṭataṉiyum, avaḷ tāṉ taliviyik kaṇṭataṉiyum taṉittaṉiyē kūṟuvar; ataṉ piṉṉar “niṉ makaḷum avaḷ kātalaṉum ippāliyi nīnti nalamē tam ūr elliyi eytiyiruppar. iṉic ceṉṟu avarkaḷik kāṇṭal iyalātu” (kōvi. 247) eṉṟāṟpōṉṟu āṟutalum kūṟuvar. 	(na. a. 188)
kalantuḻi makiḻtal -
iyaṟkip puṇarcci nikaḻnta annāḷ taliviyoṭu kūṭi iṉpuṟṟa piṉ talivaṉ taṉ maṉattuḷ makiḻntu kūṟik koḷḷutal.
‘kalavi urittal’ eṉṉum tirukkōviyār. (8)
iḵtu ‘iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉṉum tokutiyatu oru pakuti yākiya teyvap puṇarccikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	 (na. a. 125)
kalappu -
kāmam cāṉṟa talivaṉtaliviyariṉ meyyuṟu puṇarcci.
‘kantiruvar kaṇṭa kalappu’ to.po. 92 nac. uri. mēṟ.
eṉpuḻik ‘kalappu’ ipporuṭṭāyiṟṟu.
kalavi iṉpam kūṟal -
tirumaṇam nikaḻntapiṉ talivi illaṟam nikaḻttum talivaṉmaṉikkuc ceṉṟu mīṇṭa cevili naṟṟāyiṭam, “talivaṉ talivi eṉṉum iruvarkātalum iṉpa veḷḷattiṭi aḻuntap pukukiṉṟatōr uyir ōr uṭampāl tuyttal ārāmiyāl iraṇṭu uṭampu koṇṭu avviṉpa veḷḷattiṭik kiṭantu tiḷittataṉōṭu okkum; avviṉpaveḷḷam oru kālattum vaṟṟuvatum muṟṟuvatum ceyyātu” eṉṟu kūṟutal.
itu ‘maṇam ciṟappurittal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 307)
kalavi urittal -
iyaṟkippuṇarccikkaṇ talivaṉ taliviyik kūṭi iṉpuṟṟa ceytiyik kūṟal.
itu ‘kalantuḻi makiḻtal’ eṉavumpaṭum  (na. a. 125)
iḵtu ‘iyaṟkip puṇarcci’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kū ṟṟu.	(kōvi. 8)
kalavi karutip pulattal (1) -
parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉiṭam pulavi tīrntu iṉpuṟak kūṭi muyaṅkiya talivi, talivaṉ taṉakkuc ceyta taṇṇaḷiyi niṉittu, “ivvāṟu talivaṉ parattiyaruk kum aruḷuvāṉē!” eṉṟu karutip porumi aḻutu piṉṉum avaṉoṭu pulavi koḷḷutal.
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu; kavik kūṟṟāka nikaḻvatu.  			(kōvi. 366)
kalavi karutip pulattal (2) -
parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉ taṉ ceyalāl ūṭi niṉṟa taliviyiṉ ūṭalit tīrkka avaḷ nutaliyum tōḷiyum varuṭik karuṇi ceytu niṟpat talivi, ‘aiya! ittakiya niṉatu pārāṭṭutali yāṅkaḷ akkālattu vēṇṭi niṉṟatu uṇmiyē. iṉṟu nī em illiṟkuk karuṇi kūrntu vanta icceyaloṉṟē amiyum; vēṟu karuṇi oṉṟum emakkuc ceytal vēṇṭā” eṉṟu talivaṉatu kalavi taṉakku māttiramaṉṟip piṟarkkum kiṭṭiya ceyti kaṇṭu vekuṇṭu kūṟiyatu.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi . 392)
kalaviyiṭattu ūṭal -
parattiyiṟ pirintu vanta talivaṉ, vāyil palavum maṟukkappaṭa, iṟutiyil putalvaṉiyē vāyilākap peṟṟut taliviyiṉ pulaviyip pōkki avaḷoṭu paḷḷiyiṭat tāṉāka, taṉṉik kūṭutali virumpiya avaṉit talivi vekuḷa, atu kaṇṭa avaṉ avaḷ cīṟaṭikaḷit taṉmuṭimēl cūṭa, avaṉuṭiya vañcakak ceyalkaḷik kaṇṭu avaḷ kaṇṇīr ukuttāḷ. icceyalik kaṇṭa akampuku vāyilar tamakkuḷ itu paṟṟi uriyāṭikkoṇṭa tuṟi itu.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 397)
kalaviyiṉ makiḻtal -
talivaṉ taliviyoṭu kūṭi iṉpuṟal.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉum tokutikkaṇ nikaḻum kūṟṟu. kaḷaviṟ puṇarcci yāvaṟṟiṉum icceyti iṭam peṟum.	(na. a. 137)
kalaṉ kaṇṭu kaḷittal -
tammāl piriyappaṭṭār niṉivākavē varuntiyirukkum pōtu avar aṇinta aṇikalaṉkaḷuḷ yātāṉum oṉṟu tamakku vaḻaṅkappaṭavē, aṇikalaṉik kaṇṭa aḷavilēyē tammāl piriyappaṭṭavari mīṇṭum kaṇṭu makiḻvatu pōlvatoru makiḻcciyi eytutal eṉṉum pāṭāṇic cārnta potuviyal tuṟi.
e-ṭu :	irākavaṉ koṭutta kaṇiyāḻiyic cumantu aṉumaṉ kaṭalikkaṭantu cītiyi ilaṅkiyil kaṇṭu akkaṇiyāḻiyi avaḷiṭam cērppitta aḷavil avaḷukkut taṉ neñciṉip pōl uyirum taḷirttatu eṉṟal pōlvaṉa. 			(mā. a.pāṭal. 429)
kaluḻtaliṉ pāṅki kaṇṇīr tuṭittal
tōḻi varuntiya taliviyiṉ kaṇṇīrit tuṭittal.
kaḷavuk kālattil pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟi ivaṟṟiṉ iṭiyīṭu kaḷāl talivaṉik kāṇum poḻutiṉum kāṇāp poḻutu mikutalāl, talivi āṟṟāmi mikku varuntiya vaḻit tōḻi,
“pintoṭi! niṉ kattūri maṇattiṟku ācippaṭṭu malit teyva moṉṟu niṉṉit tīṇṭiviṭṭatā? tāy nī tiṉik kāvalukkup pōtal vēṇṭā eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭāḷā? nī kaṇṇīr vaṭippataṉ kāraṇam eṉṉa?” (tiruppatik. 345) eṉṟu taliviyi viṉavutal.
iḵtu ‘aṟattoṭu nili’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu. 	(mā. akap. 72)
kavarporuḷ nāṭṭam -
talivikkut talivaṉoṭu kūṭṭamuṇmiyit tōḻi tāṉ pēcum pēcciṭip pala karuttukkaḷip putiporu ḷāka vittup pēci ārāytal. aviyāvaṉa :
“piṟitoḻuka” eṉṟalum, “ampu paṭṭataṉāl puṇ ṇoṭu vanta yāṉiyik kaṇṭēṉ” eṉṟalum, “talivaṉ oruvaṉ taṉ perumikku ēlāta ciṟu coṟkaḷi eṉṉiṭam kūṟi ētō kuṟi vēṇṭi niṟkiṟāṉ; avaṉi nīyum kāṇṭal vēṇṭum” eṉṟalum, “attalivaṉ eṉṉi etirpārā vakiyil taḻuvikkoṇṭu taṉ viruppatti veḷiyiṭavum, yāṉ maṟuttu niṉṟēṉ” eṉṟalum, ivi pōlvaṉa piṟavum ākiya ceytikaḷi meyyum poyyum kalantu kūṟuvaṉa ‘palvēṟu kavarporuḷ’ ām.
talivikkuk kuṟṟēval ceyyum niliyaḷākiya tōḻi taliviyiṭam nērāka viṉavi avaḷ talivaṉoṭu koṇ ṭuḷḷa toṭarpiṉip paṟṟi aṟital iyalāmiyiṉ, taṉ aṟivip payaṉpaṭutti iṅṅaṉam viṉavi aṟivāḷ.	(to. po. 114 nac. uri)
kavaliyilēṉ eṉak kaḻaṟiya mulli -
mullip potuviyaṟpāla tuṟikaḷuḷ ‘mulli’ kāṇka. 		(pu. ve. mā. 13 - 1) (i. vi. 619 - 34)
kavaṉṟurittal (1) -
iyaṟkip puṇarccikkuppiṉ taṉṉic cārnta talivaṉ uḷḷatti naṉkuṇarntu uriyāṭiya pāṅkaṉ, “ittali vaṉuḷḷam itaṟku muṉ illātavāṟu kalaṅkukiṉṟatē” eṉṟu kavaliyuṟṟuk kūṟal.
itaṉik ‘kiḻavōṟ paḻittal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa. 	(na. a. 137, i.vi. 505)
itu ‘pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 24)
kavaṉṟurittal (2) -
talivi talivaṉuṭaṉ uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻtta, uṭaṉ pōkkiṉi aṟinta cevili, “nēṟṟu eṉṉoṭu cirittup pēci eṉṉit taḻuviyavaḷ ciṟiya upāyaṅkaḷ ceytaṉa ellām, iṉṟu ikkoṭiya kāṭṭiṉik kaṭakka vēṇṭip pōlum! itaṉi appoḻutē aṟintilēṉē!” eṉṟu taliviyatu niliyi eṇṇik kavaliyaṭintu collu tal.
 iḵtu ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 227)
kavi -
‘kaviyāṉavaṉ ilakkaṇam’ kāṇka.
kaviṉ aḻivu urittal (1) -
iravukkuṟiyil kūṭip pirinta talivaṉ mīṇṭum vārātatāl vāṭimelinta talivi, tuyarattāl taṉ mēṉiyaḻaku kulintatit tōḻiyiṭam kūṟal.
“tōḻi! eṉ paṇṭit tīviṉiyāl eṉ aḻaku, eṉakkum avarukkum payaṉpaṭātu pōmāṟu pacali pāytalāl, ivvāṟu nāḷum kuṟintu varukiṟatu. avar tanta kāma iṉpatti mīṭṭum nīkkikkoḷḷum upāyatti aṟiya muṟpiṟappil tavam ceyyātupōyviṭṭōmē!” (ampikā. 245) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘varital vēṭki’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 164)
kaviṉ aḻivu urittal (2) -
tōḻi talivaṉiṭam taliviyatu aḻaku pirivuttuyarāl kulintuviṭṭati eṭuttuk kūṟal.
“taliva! ivaḷ mēṉiyiṉ māntaḷir vaṇṇam maḻi poḻiyum iraviṉkaṇ pūtta pīrmalar pōṉṟuḷḷatu; kuvaḷi pōṉṟa kaṇkaḷō, nīr ēṟṟa ceṅkaḻunīrmalar pōla uḷa; ivi yellām niṉṉāl viḷintaṉavē” (tañci. kō. 247) eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ, ‘varivu kaṭātal’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. (na.a. 166)
kaviṉuṟa iruvarum kaṭavuṭ parāval -
uṭaṉpōya talivaṉ talivi ākiya iruvarum mīṇṭu tam illattiṟku varumāṟu cevili teyvatti vēṇṭal.
talivi talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉpōkkuc ceṉṟakāli avaḷit tēṭip pāliyil neṭuntūram ceṉṟu mīṇṭu vanta cevili, avarkaḷ talivaṉ uṟaviṉartam ūriṉi aṭinta ceytiyi naṟṟāyiṭam kūṟi, avarkaḷ viriviṉ mīṇṭu varuvataṟku aruḷpuriyumāṟu tam kulateyvatti vēṇṭutal.
 itu ‘cevili piṉ tēṭic ceṉṟu mīṭal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(mā. aka. 88)
kaḻaṅkiṭṭurittal -
kaḻaṟcikkāyāṟ kuṟiyaṟintu collutal.  (to. po. 1 14 nac. uri.) (ṭu)
kaḻaṅku -
kaḷavuk kālattut talivaṉ aṭikkaṭip pirintu celvatāl, talivikku maṉattil ēṟpaṭum vētaṉipaṟṟi uṭal meliya ammeliviṉ uṇmik kāraṇatti aṟiyāta cevili, “uṇmik kāraṇam teyvaaruḷ peṟṟa kaṭṭuviccikkē pulappaṭakkūṭum” eṉṟu avaḷi aḻittu avaḷukkuc ciṟappucceyya, avaḷ kaḻaṟcik kāykaḷi eṭuttut tariyil parappik kaṇakkup pārttut taṉ teyva āvēcattāl, talivikkut teyvattāl uṭal melivu ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa tākak kūṟi, am meliviṉip pōkkat teyva vaḻipāṭu ceyyumāṟu kūṟal.	(to. po. 115 nac.)
kaḻaṟṟetir maṟuttal -
pāṅkaṉuṭiya uṟutimoḻiyit talivaṉ kēḷātu maṟuttal eṉṉum kiḷavi. 	(kōvi. 23)
kaḻaṟṟetir maṟi -
talivaṉ pāṅkaṉuṭiya uṟutimoḻiyik kēḷātu maṟuttuc colluñ col.	(iṟi. a. 3)
kaḻaṟimoḻital -
 pāṅkiyiṟ kūṭṭattiṉkaṇ talivaṉukkuk kuṟi nērnta tōḻi taliviyi kuṟi nayappikka muyaṉṟavaḻit talivi avaḷ kūṟṟil īṭupaṭāmal aṟiyāḷ pōṉṟu kuṟiyāḷ kuṟittapōtu, tōḻi avaḷi vekuṇṭu kūṟutal. (kuṟiñci naṭiyiyal)	(vī. cō. 92 uri mēṟ.)
kaḻaṟutal -
iṭitturittal; aḵtāvatu aṉpuṭiyārmāṭṭut tīyaṉa kaṇṭāl, aṉpu kuṟiyāta coṟkaḷāl, attīyavaṟṟik kaṭiyumāṟu vaṟpuṟuttiyurittal. (iṟi. a. 3 uri.)
kaḻikkarip pulampal -
pirinta talivaṉi niṉintu talivi kaṭaṟkariyiliruntu koṇṭu  taṉiyē iraṅkuki.
e-ṭu :	camuttira vilācam eṉṉum pirapantam.	(ṭu)
kaḻintatu nikaḻttal -
talivi taṉ ūṭal taṇiyāmal nīṭṭittavaḻit talivaṉ avaḷatu paḻiya aṉpiṉi eṇṇi varuntutal.
“uṉ peṟṟōr varunta eṉṉuṭaṉ nī uṭaṉpōkkiṟku oruppaṭṭu vantatiyum, pālinilattu makkaḷillattē māṉtōl paḷḷiyil uṟaṅkiyatiyum, iṭiccurattil niṉ cuṟṟattār vantapōtu nāṉ oḷintukoṇṭatiyum nāṉ ekkālattum maṟakkalāṟṟēṉ. āyiṉ niṉakku ivi yellām maṟantuviṭṭāṟ pōla niṉ iyalpē vēṟupaṭu kiṉṟatu” eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ampikā. 507)
kaḻipaṭar -
mikka niṉippu; talivaṉit talivi kāṇum poḻutiṉum kāṇāp poḻutu neṭiyatākum kaḷavuk kālattē eṟpāṭṭu nērattil avaḷ avaṉpirivukku mikavum varunti avaṉ niṉippākavē iruttal.	(iṟi. a. 30 uri)
kaḻiyoṭu pulampal -
talivi uṭaṉpōṉatu aṟintu curattiṭi avaḷit tēṭic ceṉṟa cevili, akkāṭṭiṭi mūṅkilkaḷ veṭittu muttutirntiruppatik kaṇṭu akkaḻikaḷi nōkki, “eṉ tuyaram nōkki nīṅkaḷ kaṇtōṟum muttam ukukkiṉṟīr kaḷē!”
(kaṇ - kaṇu; muttam - kaṇṇīrttuḷi pōṉṟa muttu) eṉṟu viṉavutal.
itu ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(tiruvārūrk. 406)
kaḷḷappuṇarcci -
peṟṟōraṟiyāmal talimakkaḷ tāmē kūṭuṅ kūṭṭam. 		(na. a. 34.)
kaḷḷiyam puṟavuṭaṉ uḷḷi ucāval -
pāliyiluḷḷa puṟāvoṭu cevili pēcutal.
talivaṉōṭu uṭaṉceṉṟa taliviyit tēṭip pāli nilattil ceṉṟa cevili aṅku oru puṟāviṉik kaṇṭu, “puṟāvē! eṅkaḷ malināṭṭu ūri viṭuttup pāliyiṉ veppattip poruṭpaṭuttātu ayalāṉ oruvaṉpiṉ iruḷil puṟappaṭṭu vanta kaḷḷattaṉamuṭiya eṉmakaḷpāl nī aṉpu vittu avaḷit taṭukkāmal iruntuviṭṭāyē!” (tiruppatik. 429) eṉṟu pulamputal.
itu ‘cevili piṉ tēṭic ceṉṟu mīṭal’ eṉṉum toku tikkaṇ amintatoru kūṟṟu.	(mā. aka. 88)
kaḷam -
kaḷamāvatu kaṭṭum kaḻaṅkum iṭṭu urikkum iṭamum veṟiyāṭṭu iṭamumām.	(to.po. 144. nac. uri)
kaḷam cuṭṭuk kiḷavi -
talivaṉip pakaṟkuṟikkaṇṇum iravukkuṟikkaṇṇum iṉṉa iṭattiṟku varuka eṉṟu karutik kūṟum col.
talivi tāṉ ceṉṟu talivaṉik kūṭutaṟkuriya iṭattit tāṉē uṇarvāḷ ātaliṉ, talivaṉ kūṟiya coṟkaḷik kaṭantu talivi ceyaṟpaṭuvatu aṟam aṉmiyiṉ, avaṉ virumpiyavāṟu pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟikkuriya iṭaṅkaḷik kuṟippiṭṭuk kūṟum poṟupput taliviyuṭiyatēyām.
talivaṉukkup pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟi iṭaṅkaḷit talivi veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟāmal kuṟippākavō, talivaṉ ciṟippuṟattāṉākavō, tōḻiyiṉ vāyilākavō kuṟippiṭuvāḷ. talivaṉ kaḷam cuṭṭumāyiṉ akkaḷa voḻukkam viriviṉ puṟattārkkup pulaṉākik kuṭip piṟappu mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkuk kēṭu viḷikkum ātaliṉ kuṟiyiṭam kūṟum poṟupput taliviyiṉuṭiyatēyākum.			(to. po. 120 nac.)
kaḷam peṟak kāṭṭal -
kāppu mikutiyāṉum kātal mikutiyāṉum tamar varivu maṟuttalāṉum talivi āṟṟāḷāyavaḻi, “iḵtu eṟṟiṉāṉ āyiṟṟu?” eṉṟu cevili aṟivari viṉava, avar kaḷattip peṟāniṟkat taliviyi avarkku veḷippaṭak kāṭṭu miṭattut tōḻi kūṟṟu nikaḻum.
kaḷamar -
1) maruta nilamakkaḷ; ‘karuṅki viṉiñarum kaḷamaruṅ kūṭi’			(cilap. 10 : 125)
aṭimikaḷ; ‘kaḷamar katirmaṇi cālēkam cempoṉ’ 			(pu.ve.mā. 3 : 15)
kaḷaviṭit talivaṉ vaḷikoṇṭu vantu koṭuttuḻi, “paṇṭi vaḷi pōlāvāy, melintuḻi nīṅkā nalaṉuṭiyavō ivi?” eṉat taliviyatu melivu collit tōḻi varivu kaṭāyatu.
“kaṭaṟkari maṇalilē muttukkaḷ iruḷ nīṅkumāṟu oḷivīcum tuṟiyiyuṭiya neytalnilat talivaṉātaliṉ, ivaṭku varukiṉṟa tīṅkik kaṭitiṉ nīkki varintu koḷḷa vēṇṭiya nī ippoḻutu talivikkuk koṭuttirukkum caṅku vaḷiyalkaḷ nallaviyāka eppoḻutum nekiḻā tirukkumā? aṉṟi, talivi, uṭal meliyiṉ tāmum kaḻaṉṟu viṭumā?” eṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.	(aiṅ. 193)
kaḷaviṭit tōḻi talivaṉiṭam taliviyiṉ kaṟpu mikutiyum āṟṟāmiyum ivvoḻukku alarākiṉṟamiyum kūṟi varivu kaṭāyatu -
“veṟpa! niṉ piriviṉāl taṉ nōy mikuntavaḻiyum, niṉṉip piṟar muṉṉar ‘nilitta maṉamuṭiya paṇpu cāṉṟavaṉ allaṉ’ eṉṟu yāṉum cēriyiṉuḷḷārum āyattārum paḻi kūṟuvōm eṉṟu añcit taṉ nōyiṉi eṅkaḷ ellārkkum kuṟippāṉum kūṟāmal talivi maṟittuviṭṭāḷ” eṉṟāṟ pōlat tōḻi taliviyatu kaṟpumikuti kūṟi varivu kaṭātal.	(kali : 44)
kaḷaviṭip piriyum nāḷ
kaḷavukkālattil talivi mātantōṟum eytaṟpāla pūppu eytiyavaḻi mūṉṟu nāḷkaḷum kūṭṭamiṉṟikkaḻiyum. pūppu nikaḻāta niliyilum, talivaṉ taṉ toḻil karuti vēṟṟup pulattut taṅkutal nikaḻak kūṭumāyiṉ, appoḻutu oru nāḷō iraṇṭu nāḷō kūṭṭamiṉṟik kaḻitalum kūṭum. mēlum alla kuṟippaṭṭu, oru nāḷum iru nāḷum iṭiyī ṭātalum uṇṭu. 	(to. po. 122 nac.)
pūppu nikaḻvatu kaṟpuk kālattiltāṉ eṉṟa karuttuṭiyatu iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷ. eṉavē, annūlukkut tīṇṭā nāḷākiya munnāṭ pirivu kaḷavuk kālattu iṉṟu; ēṉiya pirivukaḷē uḷa.  	(iṟi. a.44 uri)
kaḷaviṭi vēntaṟku uṟṟuḻip pirinta talivaṉ varivu malintami -
kaḷaviṭi aracaṉukkup pōriṭi utavaṟkup pirinta talivaṉ virivil mīṇṭu taliviyi maṇattaṟkuriya ceyalkaḷi mēṟkoṇṭāṉāka, avi kaṇṭa tōḻi, “koṭiyiṭiyāy! polampūṇāy! talivaṉ aracaṉukku utavi mīṇṭu vantu viṭṭāṉ. iṉi nī niṉ kāntaḷ pōṉṟa melviraṟkikaḷāl avaṉit taḻuvi makiḻtaṟkaṇ iḻukkiṉṟu” eṉṟāṟpōlak kūṟutal. 	(to. po. 141 nac.)
kaḷaviyal kiḷavittoki patiṉēḻāvaṉa -
1. iyaṟkip puṇarcci, 2. vaṉpuṟi, 3. teḷivu, 4. pirivuḻi makiḻcci, 5. pirivukkalaṅkal, 6. iṭantalippāṭu, 7. pāṅkaṟ kūṭṭam, 8. pāṅki matiuṭaṉpāṭu, 9. pāṅkiyiṟkūṭṭam, 10. a) pakaṟkuṟi ā) oru cār pakaṟkuṟi, 11. pakaṟkuṟi iṭiyīṭu, 12. iravukkuṟi, 13. iravukkuṟi iṭiyīṭu, 14. varital vēṭki, 15. varivu kaṭātal, 16. oruvaḻittaṇattal, 17. variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirital eṉpaṉavām.	(na. a. 123)
kaḷavil talivaṉ karutātaṉa -
kaḷavukkālattil talivaṉ nilavum iruḷum pakivarum paṟṟit taliviyik kuṟikkaṇ etirppaṭa vārātu taṅkutal, varum vaḻiyiṉ arumi niṉintu varukiyit tavirtal, taliviyik kāṇamuṭiyātu pōmpōtu maṉam calittuk koḷḷutal, tāṉ taliviyik kāṇavarum vaḻiyil pāmpum vilaṅkum pōlvaṉa naliyum eṉṟu añcutal, tāṉ taliviyik kāṇa varutaṟkaṇ iṭiyūṟukaḷ uḷavāmō eṉṟu karutit taṉṉiṭattēyē taṅkutal - eṉṉumivi. kaḷavil talivaṉ karutātaṉavām.
‘āṟu iṉṉāmiyum ūṟum accamum, taṉṉi aḻitalum kiḻavōṟku illi’	(iṟi. a. 31, to. po. 136 nac.)
kaḷavil nikaḻum piriviṉ vaki -
iṭṭuppirivum arumiceytu ayarttalumākiya 1. oruvaḻittaṇattal, 2. variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ pirital eṉak kaḷavukkālattukkuriya pirivu iruvakippaṭum.   (na. a. 39)
kaḷavilum kaṟpilum alar -
kaḷavuk kālattum kaṟpuk kālattum talivaṉ ceyalkaḷ            ūrārāl eṭuttuk kūṟip paḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu taliviyum tōḻiyum kūṟal.
kaḷavil alar paṟṟit taliviyum tōḻiyum kūṟutalāl kaḷaviṟ kūṭṭam nikaḻātupōm.
kaṟpil alar paṟṟit taliviyum tōḻiyum kūṟutalāl talivaṉukkup piriviṉmi piṟakkum.
e-ṭu :	“talivaṉik kaṇṭu iṉpuṟṟatō orunāḷtāṉ; āyiṉ, cantiraṉip pāmpu maṟikkum kirakaṇam ūravar palarālum eṭuttuk kūṟappaṭuvatu pōla, yāṉ talivaṉiṭam koṇṭuḷḷa toṭarpu ūravar palarālum paḻi tūṟṟappaṭukiṟatu” (kuṟaḷ 1146) eṉṟa talivikūṟṟu kaḷavuk kālattu alar paṟṟiyatu.
“talivaṉ poruḷ mutaliya karutip pirintu ceṉṟatāl yāṉpaṭum tuyariṉi ivvūravar yāṅṅaṉam aṟintaṉar?” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu kaṟpuk kālattu alar paṟṟiyatu. (kuṟun. 140.)	(to. po. 162. nac.)
alar piṟattalāṉ, talivaṟkum talivikkum kāmam ciṟakkum. kaḷavu alar ākiyavaḻi iṭiyīṭṭiṟku añciya ac cattāṉ talivaṉ talivi iruvarukkum kāmam ciṟatta lum, kaṟpiṉuḷ parattimiyāṉ alar tōṉṟiyavaḻik kāmam ciṟattalum, avaḷ varuntum eṉṟu talivaṟkuk kāmam ciṟattalum, talivaṉpiriviṉkaṇ talivikkuk kāmam ciṟattalum nikaḻum.
“ūriluḷḷār kūṟum alar eruvāka, aṉṉi col nīrākak kāmanōy vaḷarkiṟatu” (kuṟaḷ 1147) “alar kūṟik kāmatti oḻippōm eṉṟu karututal neyyi ūṟṟi eriyum neruppi avikka muyalvataṉōṭu okkum” (kuṟaḷ 1148) eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa talivi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 163. nac.)
kaḷaviṟkuriya oḻukalāṟukaḷ nikaḻumiṭam -
talimakkaḷ oruvarkkoruvar tam nāṭṭaṅkaḷāṉ uritta kuṟippuri tām karutiya kāmak kūṭṭattiṉit tiripiṉṟik koḷḷumāyiṉ, avviṭam kaḷaviṟakuriya oḻukalāṟukaḷ nikaḻum eṉṟu kūṟuvar pulavar. (iṭam - cantippil) (to. kaḷa. 6 ca.pāla.)


kaḷaviṟpuṇarcci nāṉku -
iyaṟkip puṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu, pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam (pāṅkoṭu taḻāal), pāṅkiyiṟkūṭṭam (-tōḻiyiṟ puṇarppu) eṉak kaḷaviṟ puṇarcci nāṉku vakippaṭum.  (to. po. 498 pērā.,  na. a. 27)
kaḷaviṉ aṟuvaki -
iyaṟkippuṇarcci, pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam; tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭattup pakaṟkuṟi, iravukkuṟi, varivu kaṭātal, uṭaṉpōkku valittal eṉpaṉa.  	(ta. ne. vi. 14 uri; ka.kā. 23)
kaḷaviṉvaḻi vanta maṇam aintu -
1.	kaḷavu veḷippaṭāmuṉ, aḵtāvatu talivi aṟattoṭu niṟpataṉmuṉ talivaṉ taliviyit taṉṉūrkku aḻittuc ceṉṟu maṇantukoḷḷutal.
2.	kaḷavoḻukkam ūrār aṟiya alarāyavaḻit talivaṉ taliviyi uṭaṉkoṇṭu pōy varintu koḷ ḷutal;
3.	avvāṟu talivaṉ uṭaṉkoṇṭu pōyavaḻic ceviliyō taliviyiṉ cuṟṟattārō piṉceṉṟu iruvariyum aḻittu vantu maṇam ceyvittal;
4.	uṭaṉpōṉa taliviyiṉ peṟṟōr talivaṉ ūrkkuc ceṉṟu maṇam ceyvittal;
5.	kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭapiṉ taliviyiṉ peṟṟōr uṭaṉpaṭṭut talivikku maṇam muṭittal - eṉpaṉa.			(to. po. 191 kuḻa.)
kaḷaviṉil kūṟṟukkuriyār -
pārppāṉ, pāṅkaṉ, tōḻi, cevili, kiḻavaṉ, kiḻatti               eṉṉum aṟuvarum. kūṟṟueṉpatu ceyyuḷ uṟuppukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.  	(to. po. 501 pērā.)
kaḷaviṉuḷ kiḻavōṟku illaṉa -
kaḷavuk kālattut talivaṉ tāṉ varum vaḻi ēṟṟu uṭittu iḻivu uṭittu, iḻukku uṭittu, kal uṭittu, muḷ uṭittu eṉṟō, tāṉ varum vaḻiyil puliyāṉum yāṉi yāṉum cūraramakaḷirāṉum kaḷvarāṉum karaṭiyāṉum iṭiyūṟuṭittu eṉṟō añcutalum, iṅṅaṉam varutalāṉ taṉakkut tuṉpam aṭuttavaḻi, “eṉakku ākāta tōr oḻukkatti mēṟkoṇṭēṉē!” eṉat taṉṉi neñciṉāṉ nōtalum eṉṉum ivi koḷḷāṉ. ivvaccaṅkaḷ talivikkum tōḻikkumē nikaḻum. (iṟi. a. 31)
kaḷaviṉuḷ tavircci -
1. 	kaḷavuk kālattuḷ talivaṉ talimakaṭ puṇarātu iṭiyiṭum 			iṭiyīṭu.
2.	kaḷavuk kālattē talivaṉ tamiyaṉākal taṉmi nīṅkutal.
1.	a)	tāy tuñcāmi, nāy tuñcāmi, ūr tuñcāmi, kāvalar kaṭukukal, nilavu veḷippaṭutal, kūki kuḻa ṟal, kōḻi kural kāṭṭal ivi perukiyavaḻiyum kaḷavup puṇarcci iṭi yīṭupaṭum. kuṟiyallataṉik kuṟiyāka mayaṅkutalāṉum cilanāḷ iṭiyīṭu paṭum. talivaṉ variviṭivittup poruḷvayiṉ pirinta kālattum taliviyiṉ kaḷavukkūṭṭam palanāḷ iṭiyīṭupaṭum.  	(iṟi.a.cū. 16)
	ā)	kaḷaviṉuḷ talivaṉ taṉ uṇarviṉaṉ allaṉāy iruttaliṉ, vaḻiyiṉatu arumi accam eṉpaṉavum, kāval mikuti mutaliyaṉavum avaṉip puṇarcciyi iṭiyīṭu ceyyum vaki taṭuppaṉa illi. (33)
3. kaḷavoḻukkattil taṅkutal; aḵtāvatu talivaṉum taliviyum kaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻttum kālam. aḵtu iraṇṭu tiṅkaḷ ellikku akappaṭṭatu. (32)
kaḷaviṉuḷ tuṟavu -
kaḷavu nikaḻttum kālattil talivaṉ taliviyi nīṅki yiruttal. aḵtu oṉṟiraṇṭu nāḷkaḷ taliviyip pirintu  talivaṉ vēṟṟuc ceyalkaḷil īṭupaṭum oruvaḻittaṇat talum, iraṇṭu tiṅkaḷ ellikkuṭpaṭṭup pala nāḷkaḷ taliviyip pirintu avaḷi maṇattaṟku vēṇṭum poruḷ tēṭuvataṟkāka vēṟṟūr cellum variviṭi vittup poruḷvayiṉ piritalum ām. iṭṭuppirivu eṉavum, arumiceytu ayarttal eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa piṉ talivaṉ ippirivukaḷi nikaḻttiṉ, avi kaṟpuk kālattiṉ pirivukaḷākak karutappaṭum.
kaḷaviṟku irutiṅkaḷ aḷavu eṉṟa kāla variyaṟi kūṟap paṭṭatu.	(iṟi. a. 25)
kaḷavu -
kaḷavāvatu piṟarkkuriya poruḷi avar aṟiyāvakiyāl kippaṟṟutal. akapporuḷiṟ kaḷavākiya iḵtu aṉṉa taṉṟu. ottārkkum mikkārkkum potuvākiya kaṉṉi yarit tamar koṭuppak koḷḷātu, icciyiṉāl tamari maṟittup puṇarntu piṉṉum aṟanili vaḻāmal niṟṟalāṉ aṟam eṉṟu pōṟṟappaṭum. akattiṇiyiṉ oḻukalāṟu iraṇṭaṉuḷ mutalāvatākak koḷḷappaṭum oḻukkam ituvām	(to. po. 89 tōṟṟu. iḷa.)
kaḷavuk kālattil talivaṉ cākkāṭṭiyum virumpi ēṟpatākak kūṟal -
“eṉ kēḷir! vāḻi! eṉatu neñciṉic ciṟikkoṇṭa peruntōḷ kuṟumakaḷatu ciṟu meṉmēṉiyi orunāḷ nāṉ muyaṅkappeṟuvēṉāyiṉ, ataṉ piṉṉar arināḷ uyirvāḻtalum yāṉ vēṇṭēṉmaṉ!” (kuṟun. 280) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.  	(to. po. 100 nac. uri)
kaḷavuk kālattil talivi cākkāṭṭiyum virumpi ēṟpatākak kūṟal -
“mānīrc cērppa! eṉakku immi nīṅki maṟupiṟappu nēriṉum, nīyē eṉ kaṇavaṉ ātaṟkaṭavi; niṉ maṉattiṟ kicinta kātali yāṉ ākuka!” (kuṟun. 49) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(to. po. 100 nac. uri.)
kaḷavuppuṇarcci nikaḻum iṭam -
illattiṉ elliyik kaṭanta cōlikaḷil oṉṟaṟku mēṟpaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷit tam kūṭṭattiṟku ēṟṟa iṭamāka amittukkoṇṭu talivaṉum taliviyum 1) pakaṟ pōtiṟ kūṭum pakaṟkuṟiyum, 2) illattiṉ ellikkuḷ kaṭṭaṭaṅ kaḷiṉ puṟattē ōriṭattiyē tērntu puṇarccikku ēṟṟa iṭamākak koṇṭu iruvarum kūṭum iravuk kuṟiyum eṉa, kaḷavup puṇarcci nikaḻumiṭam iru vakittām. talivaṉum taliviyum piṟaraṟiyātu taṉiyiṭattē etirppaṭṭuk kūṭutal kaḷavuppuṇarcciyām.	(na. a. 37, 38)
kaḷavu vīṭu payattal -
makkaḷuḷ taliyāyiṉār, peṇṇiṉpattāl cuṟṟat toṭarcci uṇṭākak koli kaḷavu vekuḷi cerukku māṉam mutaliya kuṟṟam nikaḻum eṉṟu kāmattiṉ nīṅkuvar; iṭiyāyiṉār veḷivaṉappuṭiya uṭaliṉ uṭpuṟa iḻivi uṇarntu kāmattiṉ nīṅkuvar; kaṭiyāyiṉār ettiṟattālum kāmattiṉ nīṅkār. avarkaṭku ittakiya  talivaṉ taliviyariṉ kātal aṟavāḻkkiyi eṭuttuk kūṟa, anniliyi avarkaḷ tāmum eyta virumpuvar; ataṟkut tavam ceyya vēṇṭum eṉpati uṇarntu attavattiṉkaṇ muyalvar. aṅṅaṉam muyalvōriṭam piṟappup piṇimūppuc cākkāṭuṭiya immaṉita iṉpatti ṉum paṉmaṭaṅku mēmpaṭṭa vīṭṭiṉpatti viritturip piṉ, immaṉita iṉpattil paṟṟiṉi viṭuttu vīṭupēṟu eyta muyalvar. ātaliṉ, avarkaḷi vañcittuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu naṉṉeṟikkaṇ niṟuttum ikkaḷaviṉpam vīṭu pēṟaṭiya vaḻi ceyyum eṉpatu.	(iṟi. a. 1. uri)
kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭapiṉ varital -
1. taliviyuṭaṉ uṭaṉpōkkucceṉṟu pōṉa iṭattil koṭuppōr iṉṟit taliviyi maṇattal, 2. talivi yoṭu mīṇṭu tammūrukku vantu taṉ maṉiyiṉkaṇ avaḷi varital, 3. avaḷmaṉiyiṉkaṇ avaḷi varital, 4. taliviyōṭu uṭaṉpōṉapōtu iṭiyē uṭaṉpōk kut talivi tamarāl taṭippaṭuttappaṭa, avarkaḷi vaḻipaṭṭut taliviyi maṇattal eṉak kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭa piṉ talivaṉ taliviyi varital nāṉku vakippaṭum. (na. a. 44)
kaḷavu veḷippaṭāmuṉ varital -
1) iyaṟkip puṇarcci nikaḻttiya piṉṉarō, iṭantalip pāṭu nikaḻttiya piṉṉarō, pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam nikaḻttiya piṉṉarō, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭam cilanāḷ nikaḻttiya piṉṉa rō, ikkaḷavoḻukkatti nīṭṭippatu taṉakkum talivik kum maṉattuyaram viḷippatākum eṉṟu talivaṉ teḷivu eytit tirumaṇattiṟku ēṟpāṭu ceytalum, 2) ikkaḷa voḻukkatti virivil viṭuttut taliviyi maṇantu kaṟpu illaṟam nikaḻttutalē takkatu eṉṟu pāṅkaṉālō tōḻiyālō vaṟpuṟuttit terivikkappaṭṭut talivaṉ taliviyi maṇattalum eṉak kaḷavu veḷippaṭā muṉṉar varital iruvakippaṭum.	(na. a. 43)
kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭiṟkuriya kiḷavi mūṉṟu
1.	uṭaṉpōkku - talivaṉ taliviyit taṉṉūrkku uṭaṉ aḻittuc cēṟal;
2.	kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi - talivi talivaṉuṭiya urimiyāyk kaṟpoṭu kūṭiyiruttali, ayalār viraviya cēriyiṉar palarum aṟital. (kavvi - alarveḷippāṭu);
3.	mīṭci - putalviyit tēṭic ceṉṟa cevili mīṇṭu varutal; uṭaṉpōṉa talivaṉtaliviyar mīṇṭu varutalum ām.
kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭiṉ naṟṟāy cevili iruvarum aiyuṟṟu aṟital -
“nam kulattiṟku otta talivaṉi aṟintu kūṭāta aṟivuṭi yavaḷ ivaḷ” eṉṟu naṟṟāyum ceviliyum aiyamuṟṟu, ataṉi antaṇar mutaliya uyarntōriṭattē ucāvi, atuvum muṟimiyē eṉṟu avarkaḷ kūṟat tām teḷivar; talivaṉ kulattiṉatu uyarcciyi aṟinta viṭattē iṅṅaṉam ivaḷ kūṭutal muṟiyeṉṟu av vuyarntōrik kēṭṭu aiyuṟavu nīṅkit teḷivar eṉṟavāṟu.	(to. po. 117. nac.)
kaḷavuṟi kiḷavi -
1. kaḷavuk kālattil kūṟiya uṟutimoḻi; aḵtāvatu “niṉṉiṟ piriyēṉ; piriyiṉ āṟṟēṉāvēṉ” eṉpatu.
2. kaḷavu uṟinta kālattukkuriya col; aḵtāvatu aṟattoṭunili.	(iṟi. a. 51)
kaḷaveṉappaṭuvatu -
ulakattu kaḷavāyiṉa ellām ki kuṟippavum kaṇ cūlavum kaḻuvēṟṟavum paṭṭup paḻiyum pāvamum ākki narakam mutaliya tīkkatikaḷil uykkum. āyiṉ, otta talivaṉum taliviyum oru poḻilakattut tāmē koṭuppārum aṭuppārum iṉṟi etirppaṭṭuk kūṭip piṉ karaṇamoṭu puṇarntu vāḻum kaṟpoḻukka illaṟattukku vaḻivakukkum kaḷavo ḻukkam, mēṉmakkaḷāl pukaḻappaṭṭu ñāṉa oḻukkattōṭu otta iyalpiṟṟu ākalāṉum, paḻipāvam iṉmiyāṉum ‘eṉappaṭuvatu’ eṉa vicēṭikkappaṭṭatu.	(iṟi. a. 1 uri)
kaḷavoḻukkattiṟkuriya uṇarvukaḷ, avaṟṟiṟkuriya talimakkaḷuṭiya kiḷavikaḷ -
meytoṭṭuppayiṟal, poypārāṭṭal maṟintu avaṟ kāṇṭal, taṟkāṭṭuṟutal pōlvaṉa ‘vēṭki’ paṟṟiyaṉa.
nīṭu niṉintiraṅkal, peṭṭa vāyil peṟṟa iravu valiyuṟuttal, kāṇāvakiyiṟ poḻutu naṉi ikattal pōlvaṉa ‘orutali uḷḷutal’ paṟṟiyaṉa.
taṇṭātirattal, pirintavaḻikkalaṅkal, kiyaṟu tōḻi kaṇṇīr tuṭittal pōlvaṉa ‘melital’ paṟṟiyaṉa.
niṟpavi niṉii nikaḻpavi urittal, iṭṭup pirivu iraṅkal pōlvaṉa ‘ākkam ceppal’ paṟṟiyaṉa.
nīṭu niṉin tiraṅkal, peṭṭa vāyil peṟṟu iravu valiyuṟuttal, kāṇāvakiyiṟ poḻutu naṉi ikattal pōlvaṉa ‘orutali uḷḷutal’ paṟṟiyaṉa.
pirinta vaḻikkalaṅkal, taṇṭātirattal, kiyaṟu tōḻi kaṇṇīr tuṭittal pōlvaṉa ‘melital’ paṟṟiyaṉa.
taṇṭātirattal, kēṭum pīṭum kūṟal, maṉippaṭṭuk kalaṅkic citintavaḻit tōḻikku niṉittal cāṉṟa arumi uyirttal pōlvaṉa ‘nāṇuvari iṟattal’ paṟṟiyaṉa.
paṇpiṟ peyarttal, aṉpuṟṟu nakutal, varivutali varutal, kaḷavaṟivuṟutal pōlvaṉa ‘maṟattal’ paṟṟiyaṉa.
col avaṭcārttip pulliyavaki, vēṭkiyiṉ mayaṅkik kiyaṟutal, nontu teḷivoḻittal pōlvaṉa ‘mayakkam’ paṟṟiyaṉa.
maṭalmāk kūṟal, aḻivu talivanta cinti pōlvaṉa ‘cākkāṭu’ paṟṟiyaṉa.	(to. kaḷa. 9 ca. pāla.)
kaḷavoḻukkam -
piṟappu, tiru mutaliyavaṟṟāl mēmpaṭṭa talivaṉ taṉakku ottavaḷō caṟṟut tāḻntavaḷō ākiya taliviyi viti vayattāl taṉittu oru poḻilil kaṇṭu avaḷoṭu piṟar aṟiyāvakiyil cila nāḷkaḷ pakalilum cila nāḷkaḷ iravilumākak kūṭi oḻukum oḻukkam.
talivaṉ taliviyi avaḷpeṟṟōr maṇam ceytu koṭuppa muṟiyāka maṇantu kūṭātu, talivaṉum talivi yumāka maṟinta uḷḷattōṭu etirppaṭṭuk kūṭutaliṉ kaḷavu eṉappaṭṭatu. piṟarāl aṟiyappaṭāta poruḷiyuṭiya vētatti ‘maṟinūl’ eṉpatu pōlap piṟarāl aṟiyap paṭātu nikaḻnta puṇarcci kaḷaveṉappaṭṭatu; piṟar aṟiyāta vāṟu maṟittuk kūṭaliṉ ‘māyappuṇarcci’ eṉavumpaṭum. koṭuppārum aṭuppārum iṉṟit talivaṉum talivi yum tāmē etirppaṭṭup puṇartaliṉ itu kantaruva maṇat tōṭu otta maṇamākum. kantaruvarukkuk kaṟpu iṉṟi ami yavum peṟum; āyiṉ kaṟpu iṉṟik kaḷavē amiyātu. kaḷavoḻukkam cilanāḷ kaḻittuk karaṇamoṭu puṇarum kaṟpoḻukka mākum.	(to. po. 92 nac.)
kaḷavoḻukkamāvatu otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyumākiya tali makkaḷ, tattamakkuriyōr karaṇamoṭu puṇarttuk koṭuppak koṇṭu iṇiyāmal, uyarnta pālatu āṇiyāl tāmē oruvari oruvar etirppaṭṭut tammuḷ kūṭi ak kūṭṭattit tamar aṟiyātavāṟu maṟittoḻukum oḻukkamām. itaṉi maṟi eṉṟum arumaṟi eṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvar.
īṇṭuk kaḷaveṉṟatu, tam oḻukalāṟṟiṉit tāyarum tam aiyarum aṟiyātavāṟu maṟittalākiya avvaḷavēyām. ākavē, piṟarkkuriya poruḷi avaraṟiyātu vañcittuk kōṭalākiya kaḷavu vēṟu, itu vēṟu eṉpatu pōtarum. ‘uṟṟārkkuriyar poṟṟoṭimakaḷir’ ātalāṉum piṉṉark kaṟpoṭu niṟivuṟutalāṉum iḵtu aṟaneṟiyātal peṟappaṭum.	(to. kaḷa. pāyiram ca. pāla)
kaṟpāvatu uyiriṉum ciṟantatākak koṇṭoḻukum kulamaka ḷiratu maṉattiṇmi. kaṟpu eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyar īṇṭu ōr oḻukalāṟṟiṉi uṇarttum paṇpuppeyarāy maṉiyaṟa oḻukkamākap peṟṟōrāl vakuttōtappaṭṭa neṟiyiṉik kuṟikkōḷāka ēṟṟu ataṉiṉiṉṟum vaḻuvātoḻukum maṉat tiṇmiyik kuṟittu niṉṟatu. atu koṇ ṭāṉiyē teyvamākak koṇṭu avaṟku ākkamum pukaḻum eytat taṉ uyir mutaliyavaṟṟiyum tantoḻukum ceyalārnta taliviyatu paṇpākum. attaku kaṟpuṭiya maṉiviyip pēṇi avaḷvaḻi oḻukum oḻukkam talivaṟku aṟaṉākum.	(to. kaṟ. pāyiram. ca. pāla)
kaṟpiyal kiḷavi ēḻu -
ilvāḻkki, parattiyar pirivu, ōtaṟpirivu, kāvaṟpirivu, tūtiṟpirivu, tuṇivayiṟ pirivu, poruḷvayiṟ pirivu eṉpaṉa.	(na. a. 201)
kaṟpil paruvaṅkaṇṭu āṟṟāḷākiya talivi pācaṟic ceyti kēṭṭu varuntiyatu -
“nam talivarō aracaṉukku utavap pirintuḷḷār. avarukkuk kārkālat toṭakkattilāvatu nam illattiṟku mīṭal vēṇṭum eṉṉum āci irukkum. āyiṉum, aracaṉō pakivar paṭiyi aṭiyoṭu vēṟalik karutik kārkālattum taṉ pācaṟiyil taṅkiyuḷḷāṉ. innili yil ikkārkālattil nāṉ ceyalaṟṟu varuntat talivar eṉmāṭṭu vantu taṅkuvataṟku vāyppu ilatāyiṟṟē!” (aiṅ. 451) eṉṟu talivi pācaṟic ceytiyik kēṭṭu varuntiya vāṟu. (itu mulliyuṭ pāli; pirivu mulliyuḷ muṭivuṭiyatāyiṟṟu). (to. po. 9 nac.)
kaṟpiṟpuṇarcci vaki -
kaṟpu eṉṟa oḻukkattiṟ puṇarum puṇarcci, kuravariṟ puṇarcci eṉavum vāyiliṟ kūṭṭam eṉavum iruvakippaṭum.
kuravariṟ puṇarcci - kuravarkaḷ taliviyit talivaṉukku maṇam ceytu vikka iruvarum kūṭum kūṭṭam.
vāyiliṟ puṇarcci - talivaṉ taliviyariṭi nikaḻum ūṭali vāyilāvār tīrttu vikka iruvarum kūṭum kūṭṭam.	(na. a. 56)
kaṟpiṉ ākkattu niṟṟal -
uṭaṉpōkku aṟinta piṉṉarc cevili, tōḻiyoṭu matiyuṭampaṭṭut taliviyatu kaṟpu mikutiyē karuti uvanta uvakiyōṭu avaḷip piṉ toṭarātu, avaḷ ceyal ēṟṟatē eṉṟu karutiyiruttal.	(to. po. 115 nac.)
kaṟpiṉ iru vaki -
kaḷaviṉvaḻi vanta kaṟpu eṉavum, kaḷaviṉvaḻi vārāk kaṟpu eṉavum kaṟpu iruvakippaṭum. 	(na. a. 55)
kaṟpiṉil kūṟṟukkuriyār -
pāṇaṉ, kūttaṉ, viṟali, paratti, aṟivar, kaṇṭōr, pārppāṉ, pāṅkaṉ, tōḻi, cevili, kiḻavaṉ, kiḻatti eṉṟa paṉṉiruvarum kaṟpiṉkaṇ kūṟṟukkuriyār. kūṟṟu eṉpatu ceyyuḷ uṟuppukkaḷuḷ oṉṟu.  	(to. po. 502 pērā.)
kaṟpiṉuḷ tuṟavu -
illaṟam nikaḻttum kaṟpuk kālattil talivaṉ talivi yic cila karumam nōkkip pirintu cellum pirivukaḷ vilakkappaṭa māṭṭā; ittuṇik kālam eṉṟu kālavari yaṟiyum ceyyappaṭamāṭṭā.
kaṟpoḻukkattiṟkuk kālavariyaṟi iṉmiyiṉ, ataṉkaṇ nikaḻum pirivukaḷukku eṇṇikki variyaṟiyum kāla variyum kūṟutal vēṇṭā eṉpatu iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷ karuttu.	(cū. 34)
āyiṉum ōrāṇṭukku uṭpaṭṭu nāḷum tiṅkaḷum iru tuvum variyaṟuttup piriyum eṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum. (41)
ōtaṟpirivukku mūṉṟu yāṇṭu elliyum, ēṉiya pirivu kaḷukku ōryāṇṭu elliyum tolkāppiyam (po. 188, 189, 190) mutaliya piṟa nūlkaḷ kūṟum.
kaṟpu -
talivikkut talivaṉi viṭac ciṟanta teyvam illi eṉavum, avaḷ avaṉiṭam iṉṉavāṟu naṭantu koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉavum taliviyiṉ peṟṟōrkaḷ avaṭkuk kaṟpikkiṉṟaṉar. antaṇar cāṉṟōr muṉivar tēvar ākiyōr tiṟattu iṉṉavāṟu naṭantukoḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu talivaṉ talivikkuk kaṟpikkiṟāṉ.
peṟṟōrum kaṇavaṉum kaṟpitta ceytikaḷi uṭkoṇṭu avaṟṟi vaḻuvātu piṉpaṟṟi vāḻum taliviyiṉ oḻukkam kaṟpu eṉappaṭum.
talivaṉum kaḷavukkālattil nāḷ mutaliyavaṟṟip paṟṟi niṉiyāmal taliviyoṭu kūṭiyatu pōlātu, vētaṅ kaḷilum caṭaṅkukaḷilum kūṟappaṭṭa ciṟappilakkaṇaṅkaḷik kaṭippiṭittup piṉ tuṟavunili eytum vari illaṟattiṟ kuriya kaṭamikaḷi maṉattiṟ kaṟpittukkoṇṭu tavaṟātu ceyaṟpaṭutaliṉ talivaṉukkum kaṟpu uriyatāyiṟṟu.
kaṟpāvatu taliviyi avaḷ peṟṟōr vēḷvic caṭaṅ kōṭu maṇam ceytu koṭuppat talivaṉ avaḷi maṇam ceytukoṇṭu vāḻtalām. vēḷvic caṭaṅkil taliva ṉiṭam, “ivaḷi iṉṉavāṟu pātukāppāyāka” eṉavum, taliviyiṭam, “ivaṉukku nī iṉṉavāṟu kuṟṟēval ceytu oḻukuka” eṉavum aṅkiyaṅkaṭavuḷ aṟikariyāka mantira vakiyāṉ kaṟpikkappaṭutaliṉ vēḷvic caṭaṅkukkum kaṟpu eṉpatu peyarāyiṟṟu. 	(to. po. 142 nac.)
kaṟpu eṉpatu pūppu. atu kaṟpuk kālattaṉṟik kaḷavuk kālattu nikaḻāmiyiṉ pūppiṉiyum kaṟpu eṉṟu colliṉār eṉpatu. 	(iṟi. a. 44 uri.)
kaṟpāvatu uyiriṉum ciṟantatākak koṇṭoḻukum kulamakaḷiratu maṉattiṇmi. kaṟpu eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyar īṇṭu ōr oḻukalāṟṟiṉi uṇarttum paṇpup peyarāy maṉiyaṟa oḻukkamākap peṟṟōrāṉ vakukkappaṭṭa neṟiyiṉik kuṟikkōḷāka ēṟṟu ataṉiṉṟum vaḻuvā toḻukum maṉattiṇmiyik kuṟittu niṉṟatu. atu koṇṭāṉiyē teyvamākak koṇṭu avaṟku ākkamum pukaḻum eytat taṉ uyir mutaliyavaṟṟiyum tantoḻukum ceyalārnta taliviyatu paṇpākum. attaku kaṟpuṭiya maṉiviyip pēṇi avaḷvaḻi oḻukum oḻukkam talivaṟku aṟaṉākum. (to. kaṟ. pāyiram ca. pāla.)
kaṟpu aṟivittal -
talivi talivaṉōṭu illaṟam nikaḻttum maṉikkaṇ ceṉṟu mīṇṭa cevili naṟṟāyiṭam, “niṉ makaḷ taṉ kaṇavaṉiyaṉṟi vēṟoru teyvatti vaṇaṅkāḷ. avaḷ kaṇavaṉ, pakivariṭam tiṟi koḷḷac ceṉṟālum tiṟi koṇṭu vantu taṉ illattil ivaḷoṭu vikuvāṉaṉṟi vēṟevviṭattum taṅkāṉ. avarkaḷ iyalpu ītu” eṉṟu taliviyiṉ kaṟpu māṭciyi eṭutturittal.
itu tirukkōviyāril ‘maṇañciṟappurittal’ eṉṟa tokutikkaṇṇatoru kiḷavi.	(kōvi. 304)
kaṟpukkālattut taliviyiṉ ceyalkaḷ -
kaṟpukkālattil talivi pūppeytiyavaḻi, acceytiyi avaḷ cevvaṇi aṇinta cēṭi vāyilākak kāmakkiḻatti yatu illattil irukkum talivaṉukkut terivippāḷ.
talivaṉ taliviyik kuṟittuk kāmakkiḻattiyi nīṅki varavē, atu poṟātu paḻitūṟṟum avaḷit talivi ēcuvāḷ; talivaṉatu puṟattoḻukkam kuṟittu avaṉiyum iṭitturippāḷ; talivaṉ maṇanta iraṇṭām maṉi viyi avaṉōṭu etirkoṇṭaḻittu upacarippāḷ; avaḷiyum ēṉiya tuṇivariyum taṉ pakkal koṇṭu parattiyi ēcuvāḷ; kaṇavaṉōṭu ūrppuṟattē puṟappaṭ ṭuc cōliyum vayalum aruviyum maliyum kāṭum kaṇṭu makiḻntu viḷiyāṭuvāḷ; āṟṟilum ōṭiyilum kuḷatti lum avaṉoṭu kūṭi viḷiyāṭuvāḷ.	(na. a. 94)
kaṟpukkālap piriviṉuḷ talivi talivaṉiṭam, “emmiyum uṭaṉ koṇṭu ceṉmiṉ” eṉṟatu -
‘mariā maraṟceṭiyic cuvikkumāṟu māri vaṟaṅkūravē, malikaḷ ōṅkiya aruñcura neṭuvaḻikkaṇ celvōr āṟali kaḷvarāl ampu eyyappaṭavē avarkaḷ tammuṭal curuṅki uḷḷēyuḷḷa nīr vaṟṟippōka, pularntu vāṭum avar nāviṟkut taṇṇīr peṟāta taṭumāṟṟaruntuyaratti avartam kaṇṇīr naṉittup pōkkumpaṭiyāṉa kaṭumiyi yuṭiyaṉa kāṭukaḷ’ eṉṟu kūṟuvīrēl, eṉṉi aṟiyā mal yāṉ iṟantupaṭuvēṉ eṉṉum uṇmiyi aṟiyamāṭ ṭāmal nīvir kūṟuvaṉa iṉiya nīrmiyuṭiyaṉa alla; tuṉpattiṟkut tuṇiyāka emmiyum aḻittup pōka nīvir karutuvīrāyiṉ atuvaṉṟi emakku iṉpam taravallatu vēṟuṇṭō?” (kali. 6) eṉṉum talivi kūṟṟu. 			(to. po. 45 nac.)
‘kaṟpuṭi maṉiviyik kāmakkiḻattiyar naṟkuṇam ilaḷ eṉa nakitturiyāṭal’ -
taliviyik kāmakkiḻattiyar paṇpaṟṟavaḷ eṉṟu iḻittuk kūṟal.
kāmakkiḻattiyar taliviyip paṟṟi, “nam talivaṉiṭam pallāṇṭu iṉpam tuyttu avaṉip pōṉṟa arumi yāṉa māṇikkatti makaṉākap peṟṟa piṉṉum amiti yuṟāmal, talivaṉ ematu toṭarpu koṇṭuḷḷamiyi niṉittu niṉittu ‘eṉ taṅki’ eṉṟu eṅkaḷ peyaric collum pōtē aḻat toṭaṅkukiṉṟāḷē! ivaḷ kaṟpum illaṟam naṭattu muṟiyum naṉṟāka uḷḷaṉa!” (tiruppatik : 506) eṉṟu kuṟi kūṟutal.
iḵtu ‘uṇarvatōṭu uṇarā ūṭaṟku’riya tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(mā. aka. 140)
kaṟpu nalaṉurittal -
talivaṉi uṭaṉpōkkiṟku uṭaṉpaṭuttiya tōḻi taliviyiṭam vantu, “makaḷirkku nāṇam uyiriṉum ciṟantatu; annāṇattiṉum kaṟpē ciṟantatu” (tañci. kō. 311) eṉṟu, avaḷ uṭaṉpōkkiṉit tuṇiyumāṟu nāṇattik kīḻppaṭuttik kaṟpiṉ ciṟappi mēmpaṭuttik kūṟutal.
itaṉik ‘kaṟpu mēmpāṭu pāṅki pukaṟal’ eṉpa.	(na. a. 182)
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṟa tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 204)
kaṟpu nilikku iraṅkal -
tōḻi aṟattoṭu niṟpak kēṭṭa cevili, uṭaṉpōkku nikaḻttiya taliviyi niṉittu, “ivaḷ ceyvatu aṟa māyiṉum ivaḷ paruvattukku itu takātu; iṉi ivaḷukku aṟamāvatu avaṉi vaḻipaṭuvatallatu piṟitilli” eṉṟu cāṉṟavar vakutta kaṟpu makaḷiriṭam nikaḻttum ceyal kuṟittu maṉam nōtal.
itaṉik ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi’ eṉṉum kiḷavikkaṇ, ‘cevili iṉiyal eṉpōrkku etiraḻintu moḻital’ eṉpataṉkaṇ aṭakkupa. 	(na. a. 184, i. vi. 538)
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘uṭaṉpōkku’ eṉṟa tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 226)
kaṟpup payanta aṟputam urittal -
cevili naṟṟāykkuk kaṟpiṉ pērāṟṟalik kūṟal.
“nam taliviyiṉ kaṟpu aruntatiyiṉ kaṟpiṉiyum viñci yuḷḷatu. nāṭṭi viṭṭup pirintu pōyiṉum, talivaṉ ūrum kaḷiṟu māliyil taṉ maṉik kaṭṭuttaṟiyilaṉṟi vēṟṟiṭattil niṉṟu taṅkātu” (kōvi 305) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa cevili kūṟṟu.
iḵtu ‘ilvāḻkki’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘kaṟpup payappu urittal’ eṉṉum (305) 	(i. vi. 552 uri)
kaṟpup payappu urittal -
talivi talivaṉōṭu illaṟam naṭattum maṉikkaṇ ceṉṟu mīṇṭacevili, “talivi talivaṉiyoḻiya vēṟṟut teyvatti vaṇaṅkātatāl, avaṉ celuttum yāṉiyum vēṟṟu viṉi karuti veḷiyē celliṉum, talivaṉillattē mīṇṭu taṉ kaṭṭuttaṟikku vantē ōyvu koḷḷum. taliviyiṉ kaṟpu aruntatiyiṉ kaṟpiṉi nikarppatu” eṉṟu taliviyatu kaṟpuc ceyyum nalaṉkaḷi eṭuttuk kūṟutal.
itaṉi ‘ilvāḻkki’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇ ‘kaṟpup payanta aṟputam kūṟal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.  (i.vi. 552 uri)
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘maṇam ciṟappurittal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 305)
kaṟpumēmpāṭu pāṅki pukaṟal -
uṭaṉpōkkukku oruppaṭṭa talivi tāṉ nāṇatti iḻakka nērntami paṟṟi varuntiyapōtu, tōḻi kaṟpiṉ uyarvi avaḷukku eṭuttuc collutal.
“eṉ arumittalivi! uyiriṉum nāṇam ciṟappuṭiyatē. āyiṉ annāṇamum kaṟpik kākka vēṇṭumiṭattē attuṇic ciṟappuṭiyatu ākātu”. (tañci. kō. 311) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa tōḻi kūṟṟu.
‘kaṟpu nalaṉ urittal’ eṉṉum tirukkōviyār.(na. a. 182)
kaṟpuvaḻippaṭṭavaḷ parattiyi ēttal -
illaṟam naṭattum talivi talivaṉuṭiya muṉṉiliyil parattiyip pukaḻvatu avaḷ uḷḷattu ūṭali veḷip paṭuttuvatām. parattiyip pukaḻavē talivaṉiṭam kāta liṉmi kāṭṭi vaḻuvāyiṉum, talivi uḷḷattil ūṭa loṭu kūṟutalāṉ amiyum eṉpatu.  (to. po. 233 nac.)
kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi -
aḵtāvatu talivi kuṭimip paṇpāṉ kaṟpiṉ ākkat tukkaṇ ūṉṟi niṟpataṉik kaṇṭōr tammuḷum, talivi tamaroṭum kūṟum urikaḷāl eḻum āravāram.  			(to. akat. 43 ca. pāla)
kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi vaki aintu -
kaḷavu veḷippāṭṭiṟkuriya kiḷavi mūṉṟaṉuḷ iraṇṭā vatu ‘kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta kavvi.’ ataṉ vakikaḷ aintāvaṉa:
1.	cevili pulampal - taṉ makaḷ avaḷ kātalaṉuṭaṉ pōyviṭṭati aṟintu cevili pulamputal.
2.	naṟṟāy pulampal - avvāṟē naṟṟāyum pulamputal.
3.	(kaval) maṉi maruṭci - naṟṟāy maṉikkaṇ iruntavāṟē mika varuntutal.
4.	kaṇṭōr irakkam - taliviyiṉ tōḻiyarum tāyarum varuntutalik kaṇṭa ayalār iraṅkik kūṟutal.
5.	cevili piṉ tēṭic cēṟal - cevili taliviyit tēṭik koṇṭu curattil pōtal.	(na. a. 183)
kaṉavil kaṇṭu iraṅkal -
talivaṉik kaṉavil kaṇṭu piṉ kaṇ viḻitta talivi taṉatu varuttattit tōḻikkuk kūṟal.
paratti yiṭattāṉākiya talivaṉit talivi kaṉavil taṉṉiṭam varakkaṇṭu avaṉip pulaviyāl taḻuvātu viṭuttuk kaṇviḻittuk kaṉavilum avaṉit taḻuvum vāyp piṉi iḻanta taṉ avala nilikku varunti ataṉit tōḻiyiṭam kūṟal.
itaṉit tirukkōviyār ‘kaṉaviḻantu urittal’ eṉṉum (355)
itu ‘parattiyar pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatākiya kūṟṟu. iḵtu uṇartta uṇarum ūṭal. 	(i. vi. 554 uri)
kaṉaviṉ araṟṟal (1) -
kaṟpukkālattu ōtal mutaliya ceyal karutip pirinta talivaṉ tāṉ mīṇṭuvaruvatākak kūṟiya paruvam vantum taṉceyal muṟṟup peṟātapōtu, taliviniṉivōṭu uṟaṅki avaḷik kaṉavil kaṇṭu kaṇviḻittu avaṉik kāṇāmi yāl peritum varuntik kūṟutal. (mulli naṭiyiyal)    (vī. cō. 94 uri mēṟ.)
kaṉaviṉ araṟṟal (2) -
kaṉavil tōṉṟiya kātalaṉ maṟintuviṭṭamiyāl talivi pulamputal.
uṟakkamiṉṟi varuntiya talivi ayarcciyāl ciṟitē kaṇmūṭiya ciṟupōtil kaṉavukaṇṭu, atil kāḷiyuṭaṉ kūṭi makiḻntāḷ; viḻippuṟṟatum tāṉ taṉiyē kiṭappati aṟintatum avaḷ tuyaram iru maṭaṅkākiyatu; ākavē ciṉantu, “kayavaṉē! naṉavil vantu iṉpam tārāta nī kaṉavil vantu kūṭi eṉṉi ēṉ tuṉpuṟuttukiṉṟāy?” eṉpatu pōṉṟa kūṟṟu.
itu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyuḷ peṇpāṟkūṟṟuk kikkiḷiyil oru kiḷavi.	(pu. ve. mā. 15-9)
kaṉaviṉ araṟṟaliṉ pakkam -
kaṉavilāvatu kātalaṉik kāṇavēṇṭumeṉṟu talivi virumputalum ittuṟiyēyām.
“eṉ kātalaṉ vēṟṟumātaroṭu kūṭiṉāṉ eṉṟu kūṟi avaṉic ciṉantu ūṭa, avaṉ eṉ ūṭal taṇikka virumpi eṉ kāliṟ paṇiya, ittakiya kaṉavu varukaveṉa vēṇṭu kiṉṟēṉ” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.	(pu. ve. mā. 15-10)
kaṉavu avaḷ urittal -
talivaṉik kāṇum poḻutiṉ kāṇāp poḻutu kaḷavuk kālattu mikutiyum iruttalāṉ talivi varunti aritiṉ tuyileytiyavaḻi avaṉik kaṉavil kaṇṭu viḻittu avaṉ aṉmiyiṉ varuntum varuttattit tōḻi talivaṉiṭam kūṟutal. (neytal naṭiyiyal)  (vī.cō. 96 urimēṟ.)
itu “kaṉavu nalipurittal’ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum.  (na. a. 164, 166; i. vi. 521, 523)
kaṉavu aḻivu -
kaṉavu nalivu urittal.	(cāmi. 102)
kaṉavu iḻanturittal -
parattiyiṟ pirinta talivaṉuṭiya koṭumiyi niṉittu vāṭiya talivi kaṉavil avaṉikkaṇṭu taḻuvikkoḷḷa niṉittuk kikaḷi uyarttiya aḷavil kaṇ viḻittuk kaṉavilum avaḷik kūṭum vāyppu iḻanta tuyarattit tōḻiyiṭam kūṟutal.
itaṉik ‘kaṉavil kaṇṭu iraṅkal’ eṉṟu kūṟupa.
	(i. vi. 554 uri)
itu ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 355)
kaṉavu nalipu urittal (1) -
iravukkuṟi vantu pōṉapiṉ vārāta talivaṉik kaṉavil kaṇṭu tāṉ tuyaruṟṟatit talivi tōḻikkuk kūṟal. kaṉavu nalivu - kaṉaviṉāl nalintami.
“tōḻi! nēṟṟiravu talivaṉuṭaṉ kūṭi iṉpuṟuvatākak kaṉākkaṇṭu makiḻntēṉ; āyiṉ viḻittatum atu poy yāṉatāl peritum tuṉpamuṟṟēṉ” (tañci. kō. 215) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu kaḷaviyalil ‘varital vēṭki’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. ilakkaṇaviḷakkattum ikkiḷavi nikaḻkiṟatu.  (521) 	(na. a. 164)

kaṉavu nalipu urittal (2) -
talivi kaṉavil talivaṉuṭaṉ kūṭiyatākak kaṇṭu piṉ avaṉik kāṇātu varuntutalik kūṟutal (kaṉavu talivi yit tuṉpuṟuttalit tōḻi talivaṟkuk kūṟutal)
itu kaḷaviyaluḷ varivukaṭātal eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(na. a. 166, i. vi. 523)
kāṭṭalākāp poruḷ -
oppu uru veṟuppu kaṟpu ēr eḻil cāyal nāṇ maṭaṉ nōy vēṭki nukarvu eṉpaṉavum, oḷi aḷi kāytal aṉpu aḻukkāṟu poṟi niṟi aṟivu mutaliyaṉavum neñcāl uṇarntu koḷḷakkūṭiya poruḷkaḷē aṉṟi, ulakiyal vaḻakkāl oruvarkkoruvar kaṭpulaṉākak kāṭṭutal iyalātavi.	(to. po. 247 nac.)
oppu - ‘otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum’ eṉṟavaḻi, avvop pumi maṉuṇarvāṉ uṇarvataṉṟi mey vēṟupāṭu paṟṟip poṟiyāṉ uṇaralākātu. ‘tantiyi oppar makkaḷ’ eṉpatum atu. oppu eṉpatu vaṭivu oppumiyoṭu paṇpu oppumiyum iyintatu ātaliṉ, atu kāṭṭalākāp poruḷāyiṟṟu.	(nac.)
uru - accam. atu nutal viyarttal pōlvaṉavaṟṟāṉ aṉṟip piḻampu paṟṟi uṇaralākātu.  (nac.)
veṟuppu - ceṟivu. aḵtu aṭakkam kuṟittu niṉṟatu. ‘avar aṭakkamuṭiyar’ eṉṟavaḻi, atu maṉattiṉāṉ uṇarak kiṭantatu.  (243 iḷa.)
atu makkaṭkuṇamāy iciyātatoru maṉa nikaḻcci yātaliṉ poṟiyāṉ uṇaralākātu  (nac.)
kaṟpu - makaḷirkku māntarmāṭṭu nikaḻum maṉanikaḻcci. taṉ kaṇavaṉit teyvameṉṟu uṇarvatoru mēṟkōḷ.(nac.)
ēr - taḷiriṉkaṇ tōṉṟuvatoru polivu pōla, ellā uṟuppiṉum oppakkiṭantu kaṇṭārkku iṉpam taruvatoru niṟa vēṟupāṭu. aḵtu ellā vaṇṇattukkum potuvāta liṉ vaṇṇam aṉṟāyiṟṟu.  (iḷa.)
ēr - eḻucci; aḵtu eḻukiṉṟa nilimiyeṉa etirkālamē kuṟittu niṟkum.  (nac.)
eḻil - aḻaku. atu mikkum kuṟintum nīṭiyum kuṟukiyum nērākiyum uyarntum melitākiyum valitākiyum uḷḷa uṟuppukkaḷ avvaḷaviṟ kuṟiyāmal amintavaḻi varuva tōr aḻaku. ‘ivaḷ aḻakiyaḷ’ eṉṟavaḻi, aḻakiṉip pirittuk kāṭṭalākātu maṉattālēyē uṇarappaṭum.  (iḷa.)
eḻilāvatu aṅṅaṉam vaḷarntaminta paruvattum itu vaḷarntu muṭintatu eṉpataṉṟi iṉṉum vaḷarum eṉpatu pōṉṟu kāṭṭutal.  (nac.)
cāyal - meṉmi. atu nāyum paṉṟiyum pōlātu, mayilum kuyilum pōlvatoru taṉmi.  (iḷa.)
aimpoṟiyāṉ nukarum meṉmi.  (nac.)
‘mayil cāyal makaḷ vēṇṭiya’(pu. ve. mā. 6-29), ‘cāyal mārpu naṉiyalit taṉṟē’ (patiṟ. 16 : 20) ivi oḷiyāṉum ūṟṟāṉum piṟanta meṉmi uṇarttiṉa. ‘amirtu aṉṉa cāyal’ (cīvaka. 8) eṉpatu kaṭku iṉitākiya meṉmi uṇarttavē, pala meṉmiyum aṭaṅkiṉavām. ākavē, cāyal eṉpatu mey vāy kaṇ mūkkuc cevi eṉṟa aim poṟiyāṉ nukarum meṉmiyi aṭakkiṉatu.			(to. co. 325 nac.)
nāṇ - periyōr oḻukkattukku māṟāyiṉa ceyyāmik kuriya maṉappaṇpu.  (iḷa.)
ceyyattakātaṉavaṟṟiṉkaṇ uḷḷam oṭuṅkutal.  (nac.)
maṭaṉ - peṇṭirkku uḷḷatōr iyalpu. aḵtu uṟṟu ṇarntu nōkkātu kēṭṭavaṟṟāl uṇarum uṇarcci.  (iḷa.)
koḷuttakkoṇṭu koṇṭatu viṭāmi.  (nac.)
nōy - tuṉpam, “ivaḷ tuṉpamuṟṟāḷ” eṉṟavaḻi, aḵtu ettaṉmiyatu eṉṟārkkuk kāṭṭalākātu.  (iḷa.) nōy - nōtal  (nac.)
vēṭki - yātāṉum oṉṟip peṟal vēṇṭum eṉṉum maṉa nikaḻcci. “ivaḷ vēṭkiyuṭiyāḷ” eṉṟavaḻi, aḵtu ettaṉmittu eṉṟārkkuk kāṭṭalākātu.  (iḷa.)
poruḷmēl tōṉṟum paṟṟuḷḷam; ataṉip peṟal vēṇṭum eṉṟumēṉmēl nikaḻum āci ‘avā’ ākum.  	(to. e. 288 nac.)
nukarvu - iṉpattuṉpaṅkaḷi nukarum nukarcci  (nac.)
oḷi - veḷḷimi (-aṟivu mutirāmi) yiṉmi.
aḷi - aṉpukāraṇattāl tōṉṟum aruḷ.
kāytal - vekuḷi
aṉpu - maṉiviyarkaṇṇum, tāytanti putalvar mutaliya cuṟṟattiṉkaṇṇum maṉamakiḻcci nikaḻttip piṇippittu niṟkum nēyam.
aḻukkāṟu - piṟarcelvam mutaliyavaṟṟik kāṇa maṉam poṟātiruttal.
poṟi - piṟar ceyta tīmiyip poṟuttal : ‘poṟi yeṉappaṭuvatu pōṟṟārip poṟuttal’  (kali. 133)
niṟi - maṟi piṟar aṟiyāmal oḻukutal; ‘niṟiyeṉappaṭu vatu maṟipiṟar aṟiyāmi’  (kali. 133)
aṟivu - nallataṉ naṉmiyum tīyataṉ tīmiyum uḷḷavā ṟuṇartal.  (nac.)	((247. nac.)
kāṭṭalākāp poruṇmiyuṭiyavi -
maṉattāṟ koṇṭuṇarvataṉṟik kaṇṇāṟ kāṇumāṟu kāṭṭamuṭiyāta poruṇmiyuṭiyavi: oppu, uru, veṟuppu, kaṟpu, ēr (eḻucci), eḻil, cāyal, nāṇ, maṭaṉ, nōy, vēṭki, nukarvu - eṉpaṉa.
uru eṉṟatu, arimā mutaliyavaṟṟik kaṇṭu añcum accam pōlāmal, aṉpu kāraṇamāka uḷḷattē tōṉṟum uṭku eṉṉum uṇarvu ām.
veṟuppu eṉṟatu, maṟi piṟar aṟiyāmal aṭakkum uḷḷattiṉ ceṟivām.
kaṟpu eṉṟatu, ‘koṇṭāṉiṟ ciṟantatoru teyvam illi’ eṉṉum pūṭkiyām.
eḻil eṉṟatu, avvapparuvattē paruva vaṉappiṉic ciṟappittu niṟkum polivām.
nāṇ eṉṟatu, ceyyavum pēcavum eṇṇavum takātaṉa vaṟṟiṉkaṇ cārāmal taṉṉip pēṇik koḷḷum paṇpām.
nōy eṉṟatu, piṟarkkup pulappaṭa nillāta neñca naliviṉi.
nukarcci eṉṟatu, uvattalum muṉitalumiṉṟip parupporu ḷum nuṇporuḷum ākiyavaṟṟip poṟikaḷāṉum maṉattāṉum tuykkum uyirkkuṇam ām.  			(to. poruḷiyal 52 ca. pāla.)
kāṭci -
talivaṉ taliviyik kāṇpatu. iḵtu akapporuṭ kikkiḷip pakutikkaṇ mutaṟ ceyti; kōvikaḷiṉ mutalāvatu kūṟṟu.
tōṟṟam naṭi mutaliyavaṟṟāl kāṇpōr yāvariyum kavarum vaṉappu mikka taliviyit talivaṉ kaṇṭu viyantu niṟṟal.	(na. a. 119)
ikkiḷavi puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyuḷ āṇpāṟ kūṟṟuk kikkiḷiyiṉkaṇṇum nikaḻkiṟatu. 14-1.
kāṭuṟi ulakam -
maram ceṭi koṭikaḷum pul ceṟinta pakutiyum mikka kāṭṭup pakuti; māyōṉāl kākkappaṭutaṟkuriya mulli nilam. ulakam eṉpatu, innilavulakam muḻutiyum kuṟiyāmal ataṉ oru pakuti nilattiyum kuṟikkum.   (to. po. 5 nac; cīvaka. 2606 nac.)
kāṇṭal -
taliviyorutti taṉpāl kātal koḷḷāta talivaṉ oruvaṉik kāmattoṭu pārttal.
“nāṉ ivvaḻakaṉik kaṇṭu kātal mikakkoṇṭu, ūrārtam ēcutaliyum poruṭpaṭuttātu ivaṉ arukē ceṉṟu niṉṟakāliyum, ivaṉ eṉṉi ēṟiṭṭum pārttilaṉē!” eṉat tuyaruṭaṉ moḻiyum talivi kūṟṟu.
itu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyuḷ peṇpāṟkūṟṟuk kikkiḷiyil orukiḷavi.	 (pu. ve. mā. 15-1)
kāṇṭal valittal -
kikkiḷit talivi kāmam mikkut talivaṉik kaṇṭē ātal vēṇṭum eṉṟu uṟuti pūṇutal.
“iravil uṟaṅkāmal eṉṉi varuttum kaṇkaḷ evvāṟē ṉum eṉ kātalaṉik kāṇattāṉ vēṇṭum eṉa eṉṉit tūṇṭukiṉṟaṉa” eṉpatu pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
itu puṟapporuḷ veṇpāmāliyuḷ peṇpāṟkūṟṟuk kikkiḷik kaṇṇatoru kiḷavi. 	(pu. ve. mā. 15-6)
kāṇānili urittal -
poruḷvayiṉ pirintu mīṇṭa talivaṉ taliviyiṭam piriviṉkaṇ tāṉ avaḷi mukakkaṇṇāl kāṇāta tuyaraniliyik kūṟutal.
“eṉ uyiraṉiyāy! nī varuntumāṟu niṉṉi yāṉ akkoṭuñcurattiṉkaṇṇum eṉ maṉattakattē potintu koḷḷāta nēramē illi; āyiṉum eṉ uḷḷattuḷ ḷēyē irunta nī maṟantu viṭṭāyō? eṉ ūṉuṭampiṟ kuḷḷē niṉṉi oḷittuk koṇṭāyō? nāṉ eṉ mukakkaṇkaḷāl appōtellām niṉṉik kāṇamuṭin tilatē! ippoḻutu eṅkō vāṉattuniṉṟu vantāṟpōla eṉ kaṇmuṉ vantu tōṉṟukiṟāy!” eṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟu.
itu ‘poruḷvayiṉ pirivu’ eṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.	(ampikā. 559)
kātal kaṭṭurittal -
talivi talivaṉi maṇantu avaṉatu illattil kuṭumpam naṭatti varutalik kaṇṭuvanta cevili naṟṟāyiṭam, “tali vaṉ nam makaḷatu iṭiyiṉ noymiyi uṇarntu avaḷmāṭṭuk kātalāl avaḷ kūntalil viṭumalarkaḷi aṇitalēyaṉṟi neṟṟiyil poṭṭiṭṭālum avaḷiṭik kup pāram mikum eṉṟu tilakam aṇitaliyum ceyyāṉ. iḵtu avaṉatu kātal nili” eṉṟu avaṉatu kātal mikutiyik kuṟittal.
itu ‘maṇam ciṟappurittal’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇa toru kūṟṟu.	(kōvi. 303)
kātal paratti -
talimakaṉāl kātalikkappaṭṭa paratti.
talimakaṉ peruñcelvaṉ ātaliṉ avaṉ kuravarkaḷ avaṉukku meyppōkam oḻiya ēṉiya iṉpaṅkaṭku uriyarāka variyaṟuttu vitta parattiyarkaḷ. pakaṟpoḻu tiṉ mutaṟpattu nāḻikikaḷi aṟattiṉum, aṭuttup pattu nāḻikikaḷiyum poruḷiṉum kaḻittu, kaṭiyaṉa pattu nāḻikiyum taṉ iruppiṭam vantu avaṉ taliviyō ṭirukkak karutumpōtu, parattiyar avaṉ muṉ vantu tam āṭal pāṭal mutaliyavaṟṟāl avaṉi makiḻvuṟutta, apparattiyar payiṉṟa kalikaḷiṉ nuṭpam nōkkit talivaṉ avarkaḷpāl aṉpu celuttuvāṉ ātaliṉ, parattiyum talimakaṉāl kātalikkappaṭṭāḷ ātal uṇṭu eṉpatu.	(iṟi. a. 40 uri)
‘kātal peyarpu kār aṉṟu itu eṉat, tātaviḻ kōti tāṉē kūṟal’ -
kaṟpuk kālattut talivaṉ pirintu ceṉṟavaḻi avaṉ mīḷvatākak kuṟittuc ceṉṟa kārkālam toṭaṅkiṟṟāka, avaṉatu uṇmip paṇpiṭattuk koṇṭa viruppattāṉ, tāṉ muṉpu kārkālam vantuviṭṭatākak karutiya karuttu nīṅka, “ipparuvam kārkālam aṉṟu” eṉṟu talivi tāṉē taṉakkuḷ kūṟikkoḷḷutal.  (mulli naṭiyiyal) 			(vī. cō. 94 urimēṟ.)
“kātalar pirintatu aṇittāy irukkavum, nī āṟṟukiṉṟili” eṉṟa tōḻikkut talivi kūṟiyatu -
“eppoḻutum nikaḻvatu ippaṭittāṉ; avar aṅkē pirintu celvār, uṭaṉē eṉmēṉi iṅkē pacappu ūrntu viṭum. itaṉit tavirppatu yāṅkaṉam?” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
“viḷakkiṉatu cōrnta taṉmi pārttu neruṅkivarum iruḷē pōla, koṇkaṉatu muyakkattiṉatu melivu pārttu ippacappu neruṅki varum” eṉpatum atu.
“kātalarit taḻuvik kiṭanta nāṉ eṉṉiyum aṟiyātu ciṟitu acintu nakarntēṉ; ataṟkuḷ pacali eṉṉi aḷḷik koḷvatu pōla vantu paraviviṭṭatu! aṅṅaṉa māka ippirivi yāṉ āṟṟumāṟu eṉṉi?” eṉpatum atu.	(kuṟaḷ 1185, 1186, 1187)
“kātalari ivvūr iyaṟpaḻiyāmal avaratu koṭumiyi maṟittal vēṇṭum” eṉac coṉṉa tōḻikkut talivi kūṟiyatu -
“nam maṉattuḷḷa kāmanōyi nam kaṇkaḷē paṟiyaṟintu aṟivikkumpōtu, ūrār aṟiyātavāṟu ataṉi maṟit tal evvāṟu iyalum?” eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu. (kuṟaḷ 1180)
kātalaṉ pirivuḻik kaṇṭōr, “iṟivikkup pulavikku ētu ītām” eṉṟal -
talivaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintoḻukum niliyik kaṇṭavar ivaṉatu ivvoḻukkam talivi ūṭutalukkuk kāraṇa mākum eṉṟu kūṟutal.
“talivaṉ ulā vantapōtu parattiyar avaṉik kaṇṭu kikuvittu vaṇaṅkat talivaṉ aṅku avarmāṭṭu aruḷ ceytu ceyta kāriyaṅkaḷ talivikkup pulaviyi uṇṭāk kum” (ampikā. 447) eṉpatu pōṉṟa kaṇṭōr kūṟṟu.
itu kaṟpiyaluḷ, ‘parattiyiṟ pirivu’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu.
iḵtu ‘uṇartta uṇarum ūṭal’	(na. a. 205)
kātalaṉ mulivili viṭuttami pāṅki kātalikku uṇarttal -
taliviyi maṇantu koḷḷum viruppuṭaṉ talivaṉ paricap poruḷākap poṉṉum maṇiyum tantuḷḷa cey tiyit tōḻi talivikkuk kūṟutal.
‘nitivaravu kūṟāniṟṟal’ eṉṉum tirukkōviyār (298)
itu variviyaluḷ ‘varivu malital’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 174)
kātali naṟṟāy uḷamakiḻcci uḷḷal -
talivaṉ paricap poruḷākap poṉṉum maṇiyum koṭut tuḷḷa ceytiyik kēṭṭu makiḻnta talivi, taṉakku vatuvi puṇarum nili vantati aṟintu taṉṉuṭiya naṟṟāy aṭiyavirukkum makiḻcciyi niṉittup pārttal.
itu variviyaluḷ ‘varivu malital’ eṉṉum tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(na. a. 174)
kātaliṉ kaḷittal -
peruntiṇit talivi mikunta kāmattoṭu talivaṉit taḻuvi makiḻtal.
ūṭal tīrntu iruvarum muyaṅki iṉpuṟum vaṇṇam iravik kaḻitta talivi virivil vikaṟi vantuṟṟami kaṇṭu irāppoḻuti muṉintu kūṟum aḷaviṟkuk kātaliṉ kaḷittami.
itu puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māliyuḷ, peruntiṇik kaṇ peṇpāṟ kūṟṟil orukiḷavi. 	(pu. ve. mā. 16-12)
kātaṟparatti talimakaṭkup pāṅkāyiṉār kēṭpa urittatu -
“vāḷimīṉatu karukkoṇṭa peṇmīṉ utirnta māmpaḻat tik kavvit tiṉṉum kuṉṟūrukkuk kiḻakkiluḷḷa taṇṇiya periya kaṭal, talivi taṉ aṟiyāmiyāl pulakkum paṭiyākat talivaṉtiṟattu yāṅkaḷ naṭantukoḷvōmā yiṉ, emmi varuttuka!” eṉṟu kātaṟparatti kūṟal.			(kuṟun. 164)
kātaṟparattiyuṭaṉ puṉalāṭiya talimakaṉ, tōḻiyi vāyil vēṇṭa, avaḷ, puṉalāṭiyavāṟu kūṟi vāyil maṟuttatu -
“viyiyiṉ orucār, keṇṭi pōṉṟa kaṇ matu nukarcciyālum puṉalāṭalālum pulaviyālum niṟam civappa vum, kūntaliṉ vīḻum pūkkaḷiṉiṉṟu tēṉ tuḷippavum, nīr viḷiyāṭum kātaṟparattiyi nī taḻuviṉāy. ataṉāl niṉ mārpiṟ pūciya kattūrik kuḻampu aḻintatum aḻiyātatu māyiṟṟu. taliva! attōṟṟam tēṉ cōrntu vīḻum mali pōṉṟatu” eṉṟu kūṟit tōḻi vāyil maṟuttal. 	(pari. 16)
kātaṟpāṅkaṉ -
talivaṉukku uṟṟa naṇpaṉ. ‘maṉṉavaṉ ṟaṉakku, nīṅkāk kātaṟ pāṅka ṉātaliṉ’ (maṇi. 28 : 125)	(ṭu)
kātaṟṟōḻi -
taliviyiṉ aṉpukkuriya pāṅki. (kōvi. 50 ava.)	(ṭu)
kāntaruvam -
kāntaruvamaṇam. ‘yāḻōr kūṭṭam’ kāṇka. kantaruvar tammuḷ nērntavaḻik kūṭit tīrntavaḻi maṟakkum maṇam ākiya itu, “piriyātu uṭaṉ vāḻtal, iṉṟēl tariyātu uyir nīttal’ eṉṉum aintiṇik kaḷaviṉ vēṟupaṭṭatu.			(po. 92. na)

kāppiṉuḷ vēṇṭum oḻukkam -
aṉpu aṟam iṉpam ivaṟṟi niṉittu varuntāmal, nāṇattik kiviṭṭut tamarukkup paricapporuḷ koṭukka ēṟpāṭu ceyyat talivaṉ poruḷvayiṉ piritalit taliviyum tōḻiyum karuti mēṟkoḷḷum oḻukkam taliviyi iṟceṟittalāl kāval mikutippaṭṭavaḻi nikaḻtal.	(to.po. 211 iḷa)
talivaṉkaṇ nikaḻum aṉpum kuṭippiṟantōr oḻukum aṟaṉum tamakku iṉṟiyamiyāta iṉpamum nāṇamum akaṉṟa oḻukalāṟu kāvalmikutiyāṉ talivikku varuttam nikaḻntaviṭattu uḷatām ātaliṉ, tōḻi paricap poruḷ paṟṟit talivaṉiṭam kuṟippiṭutalit taliviyum uṭaṉ paṭuvāḷ.	(215 nac.)
kāppuc ciṟimikka kiyaṟu kiḷavi (1) -
talivi taṉatu illiṉ puṟattē pōka muṭiyātu kāval mikka viṭattē ceyalaṟṟup paṟavikaḷiṭamum vilaṅku mutaliyavaṟṟiṭamum pulampik kūṟutal.
“ciṟu veḷḷāṅkurukē! kaḻaṉi nallūr makiḻnarām eṉ talivarkku eṉatu iḻi nekiḻum tuṉpatti itukāṟum collātōy! emmūrkkaṇ oṇtuṟi nīrit tuḻāvic ciṉikkeḷiṟṟu mīṉkaḷi ārntu avarūrkkup peyarki ṟāy. eṉpāl attakiya aṉpuṭiyāy āvāyō? aṉṟip perumaṟatikoṇṭu akaṉṟu viṭuvāyō?” (naṟ. 70) eṉṟāṟpōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟu.
‘maṉippaṭṭuk kalaṅki’ eṉṟataṉāṉ, ikkūṟṟut taḻuvik koḷḷappaṭṭatu.	(to. po. 111 nac. uri)
kāppuc ciṟi mikka kiyaṟukiḷavi (2) -
kaḷavoḻukkattil talivaṉ iravukkuṟiyiṭit talivi yik kūṭivanta nāḷkaḷil cilanāḷ, iravukkuṟiyiṭik kāmam mikkut talivaṉi etirppaṭa virumpi niṉṟa talivi, “tāy tuñcāmi, kāvalar kaṭukutal mutalāṉa iṭiyīṭu kaḷi ellām nīntit talivar oruvāṟu vantārāyiṉum innāy iravellām tūṅkātu kurikkiṉṟamiyiṉ, nām avari etirppaṭutal aritāyiṟṟu” eṉṟu kāppuc ciṟi mikku varuntutal.
iḵtu ‘iravukkuṟi’ eṉṉum tokutikkaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 175)
kāppuṭitteṉṟu maṟuttal -
taliviyiṉ icivit tōḻi vāyilākap peṟalām eṉṟu talivaṉ karutik kiyuṟiyoṭu katiravaṉ maṟiyum vari puṉattiṭi niṟpat tōḻi talivaṉiṭam, “ittiṉik kolli kāvaluṭiyatu; em tamiyaṉmār iṅku variṉ niṉakku iṭiyūṟu nērum; yām kālam tāḻttātu illam mīḷvōm; nī nāḷi varuka” eṉṟāṟpōlak kūṟik kiyuṟi ēṟka maṟuttal.
itaṉip ‘pāṅki añci accuṟuttal’ eṉṟum kūṟupa.
	(na. a. 144, i. vi. 509-16)
itu tirukkōviyāruḷ ‘cēṭpaṭi’ eṉṟa tokutik kaṇṇatoru kūṟṟu. 	(kōvi. 98)
kāppu vaḻuvutaliṉālākiya kuṟṟam kāṭṭal -
iravukkuṟiyil kāval mikutiyik kaṭantu talivaṉ varutalāl nikaḻum kuṟṟaṅkaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭit talivi yum tōḻiyum avaṉi iravukkuṟi vilakkutal.
“taliva! nī pakalil tīrtta māṭutaṟkēṟpa īrattāl taṇṇi tākiya āṭiyi uṭiyiyāy, iravil kaḷavoḻukkat tiṟkēṟṟa iyalpiṉi uṭiyiyāy, varintu kōṭalit tavirntu, taliviyiṉuṭiya kuṟiyiṭam nōkki varuṅkāl, oḷitikaḻum koḷḷikkaṭṭi, kavaṇkal, vil ivaṟṟoṭu tiṉi kāppōr kaḷiṟu vantatākak kūṟi āravāram ceyvar” (kali. 52) eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa. 	(to. po. 210 nac.)
kāma iṭiyīṭṭil ‘aṟivum pulaṉum vēṟupaṭa niṟīi, uvama vāyiṟ paṭuttal’ -
talivaṉum taliviyum piriviṉkaṇ kāmanukarccikku iṭiyūṟu vantavaḻi, maṉaaṟiviyum poṟiaṟiviyum vēṟupaṭa niṟutti, aḵṟiṇi irupāṟkaṇṇum uyar tiṇi muppālum uriyaṉavāka uvamam ceytaṟkup poruntumvaḻi avvuvamattiṉvaḻiyē cārttik kūṟutal.
e-ṭu :	‘kaṭalē! aṉṟilē! vēyṅkuḻalē! nīṅkaḷ taṉittu varuntum eṉṉip pōṉṟavarkaḷiṉ tuyaram kaṇṭu varuntukiṉṟīrō! allāl, emmip pōlak kātal ceytu akaṉṟavari niṉittu varuntukiṉṟīrō!” (kali. 129) eṉṟa talivi kūṟṟil, maṉaaṟiviyum poṟiaṟiviyum vēṟupaṭa niṟuttik kaṭal mutaliya aḵṟiṇip poruḷ kaḷiṭam uyartiṇip poruḷkaḷiṭam uriyāṭu māṟu pōla uriyāṭi avaṟṟit tamakku uvama mākak koṇṭurittami uṇarappaṭum. 			(to. po. 196 nac.)
uvamappeyarum uvamikkappaṭum peyarum toḻil paṇpu payaṉ eṉṟa poruḷ paṟṟi uvamam poruntumāṟu kūṟutal. 	(194 iḷa)
e-ṭu :
koṭikaḷit taliviyatu iṭieṉṟum
kāntaṭ pūkkaḷit taliviyiṉuṭiya kikaḷ eṉṟum,
māntaḷirit taliviniṟam eṉṟum,
karuviḷampūkkaḷi avaḷkaṇka ḷeṉṟum,
āṇmayili avaḷcāyal eṉṟum
talivaṉ kōṭaṟkaṇ, uvamavāyilpaṭutti aṟiyum aṟivi yum aṟiyappaṭum poruḷiyum vēṟupaṭa niṟutti, uvamam poruntiyavaḻik kūṟiyavāṟu.	(to.po. 196 nac.)
kāma iṭiyīṭṭil ‘avaravar uṟupiṇi tamapōl cērttal’ -
talivaṉum taliviyum pirivuk kālattil kāmanukarccikku iṭiyūṟu vantavaḻi, vārtti collā iyalpiṉi yuṭiya puḷḷum māvum tuyaramuṟṟaṉavākak karutikkoṇṭu attuyarattit tammip pōlak kāmam paṟṟiya tuyaramākak kaṟpaṉiceytu kūṟutal.
e-ṭu :
“pāytiri pāṭuōvāp parappunīrp paṉikkaṭal!...
... empōlak, kātalcey(tu) akaṉṟāri uṭiyiyō nī!”
“maṉṟuirum peṇṇi maṭalcēr aṉṟil!...
empōla, iṉtuṇip pirintāri uṭiyiyōnī?”
“paṉi iruḷ cūḻtarap pitalañ ciṟukuḻal!...
empōla, iṉiyaceytu akaṉṟāri uṭiyiyō nī’ (kali. 129)
	(to. po. 196 nac.)
kāma iṭiyīṭṭil kaṉavu urittātal -
talivaṉukkum talivikkum pirivuttuṉpattāl kāmattiṟku iṭiyūṟu vantavaḻi, avarkaḷ kaṉavu kāṇṭalum urittu.
iṅṅaṉamē uṭaṉpōkkil talivi pirintavaḻit tāy kaṉavu kāṇṭalum urittu.
talivi kaṉavu kāṇṭal kali. 128 ām pāṭalilum,
talivaṉ kaṉavu kāṇṭal akanā. 39 ām pāṭalilum,
cevili kaṉavu kāṇṭal akanā. 55 ām pāṭalilum kāṇka.
	(to.po. 197, 198 nac.)
kāma iṭiyīṭṭil, ‘collā marapiṉavaṟṟoṭu keḻīic, ceyyā marapiṉ toḻiṟpaṭuttu aṭakku’tal -
 talivaṉum taliviyum piriviṉāl kāmanukarccikku iṭiyūṟu vantavaḻi, vārtti collum iyalpiṉa allāta puḷḷum māvum mutaliyavaṟṟoṭu kūṭi avi ceyya iyalāta toḻilkaḷi avaṟṟiṉmēl ēṟṟik kūṟal. avi neñcam, nāri, vaṇṭu mutaliyavaṟṟit tūtu viṭutal pōlvaṉa.	(to. po. 196 nac.)
kāma iṭiyīṭṭil neñciṉi uṇarvuṭiyatu pōlak kūṟal
talivaṉum taliviyum pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟi eṉpaṉa piḻikkumpōtum, ēṉiya pirivukaḷiṉpōtum avarkaḷ tattam neñcaṅkaḷ taṅkaḷip pōla uṇarvuṭiyaṉavākak kaṟpaṉi ceytu avaṟṟuṭaṉ kūṟal.
“neñcamē! nī talivaṉi aṇukumpōtu ikkaṇkaḷi yum uṭaṉkoṇṭu celvāyāka. ivi talivaṉik kāṇṭal viruppattāl eṉṉit tuṉpuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa” (kuṟaḷ. 1244) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟum,
 “piṉṉē niṉṟu eṉṉic celuttum neñcamē! yāṉ pōkak kūṭiya pālivaḻiyil taliviyiṉ uruveḷit tōṟṟam eṉṉi varuttumpōtu uṉ coṟkaḷ eṉ tuyarip pōkkumō?” (akanā. 3) eṉṟāṟpōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟum koḷka.	(to. po. 196 nac.)
kāma iṭiyīṭṭil neñciṉi uṟuppuṭiyatu pōlak kūṟal -
talivaṉukkum talivikkum pakaṟkuṟi iravukkuṟi piḻikkum pōtum, ēṉiya pirivukaḷiṉpōtum avarkaḷ tattam neñcaṅkaḷukku vaṭivam kaṟpittu avi uṟuppukkaḷi yuṭiyaṉa pōlak kūṟutal.
e-ṭu :	“taliviyiṉ meṉtōḷkaḷip peṟum naci yāl ceṉṟa eṉ neñcu taliviyiṉ kūntalit tīṇṭi avaḷit tōyntatuvō?” (akanā. 9) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟum,
“tōḻi! eṉ neñcam eṉṉoṭum niṉṉoṭum kalantu ārāyāmal talivaṉ iravukkuṟikku vantu tirumpumpōtu iravil kuḻikaḷil avaṉkālkaḷ taṭumāṟātapaṭi tāṅku taṟku avaṉip piṉtoṭarntatō?” (akanā. 128) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟum ām.	(to. po. 196 nac.)


kāma iṭiyīṭṭil neñciṉi maṟutturippatu pōlak kūṟal -
kaḷavilum kaṟpilum piriviṉāṉ kāmam iṭiyīṭupaṭṭa kālattut talivaṉum taliviyum tattam neñcaṅkaḷit tam viruppatti maṟutturippaṉa pōlak kaṟpaṉi ceytu kūṟal.
e-ṭu : “eṉṉip poruḷ tēṭac celuttum neñcamē! pāliyil iṭinilattil taliviyiṉ uru veḷit tōṟṟam kaṇṭu varuntum eṉṉi uṉcoṟkaḷ varuttam tīrac ceyyumā?” (akanā. 3) eṉṟāṟ pōṉṟa talivaṉ kūṟṟum,
“tōḻi! parattiyarit tōyntuvanta talivaṉiṭam ūṭutal vēṇṭum eṉṟa eṇṇamuṭiyēṉ nāṉ. āṉāl koḷkiyillāta eṉ neñcu avaṉik kūṭuvēṉ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu” (kali. 67) eṉṟāṟpōṉṟa talivi kūṟṟum muṟiyē neñciṉi maṟutturippataṟkum, neñcu tammi maṟuttu urippataṟkum ām. 	 (to.po. 196 nac.)
kāma iṭiyīṭṭil pālkeḻu kiḷavikkuriyār -
kāmanukarcciyiṉkaṇ talivaṉukkum talivikkum iṭiyūṟu vantavaḻi, attākkutalāṉ vēṟupaṭutal tōḻi cevili naṟṟāy pāṅkaṉ eṉṟa nālvarukkum urittu. talivi talivaṉ ivaroṭu muṟiyē piriviṉṟi iyinta tōḻi, pāṅkaṉ eṉpavari avviṭiyūṟu mikutiyum tākkātu; avarkaḷ iṭiyūṟṟip pōkkum vaḻi nāṭupavar āvar.
pālkeḻu kiḷavi - ilakkaṇattiṉ pakkac col; ilak kaṇam aṉṟu āyiṉum ilakkaṇam pōla amittuk koḷḷutal.	(to. po. 199, 200. nac.)
